~Assallamu alaikum masoya LEEMA,masoya littafin LEEMA na sake dawo maku da wani sabon littafi Mai cike da tsantsan tausayi,wahala,q'uncin rayuwa,yaudara,soyayya,nadama,son zuciya da sauran su,duba da rayuwa ta yau da kullum yasa na zakulo wannan labari  wldomin ya zama darasi ga al'umma NIMA'YA CE yasha banban da sauran littafan dana rubuta a baya da fatan zaku karbe shi kuma ku fahimce ni ,,naso fara littafin INDO A SOKOTO,Amma Allah bai nufa ba saboda wasu dalilai,Asha karatu lafiya~

NIMA 'YA CE LOADING..



~🅿1-2~




    Kyakkyawar yarinya ce wace ba zata wuce shekaru tara ba ke tafe cikin uniform d'in kwalli primary school dake cikin garin Kano,daga ganin yanayin tafiyar ta mai karatu zai san ta kwaso yunwa banda rana da ake kwala wa hanunta rike da jakar buhu wanda ke dauke da littafai biyu kacal sai pencil, ƙafan ta kuma silifas ne wanda ya cinye alamar ya sha wahala matuƙa a ƙafar wace ke sanye dashi,tafiya mai tsayi ya kai ta aunguwar tukuntawa ,da sallama ta shiga gidan nasu wanda aka fi sani da "GIDAN KAZO NAZO" ,amma babu wanda ya damu da amsa sallamar nata  wanda idan da sabo ta saba,daga masu shan sigari,yan caca,Yan daudu sai d'an agunda mai gurmi yana nashi aikin a gefe wasu daga cikin mazauna gidan na cashewa yayin da abokan dadiron su na masu ruwan kuɗi, taja wani dogon tsaki wanda ya zame mata jiki a duk lokacin data dawo daga makaranta abinda take fara cin karo dashi kenan a cikin  gidan nasu,haka ta rinka ratsa mutane har ta kai d'aki mai number goma sha huɗu wanda shine nasu,da sallama ta d'aga labulan d'akin amma sai me!

Wata farar mace  mai matsakaiciyar jiki,kyakyawa zaune daram saman cinyar wani jibgegen mutum irin manyan alhazan nan ne"tsawa ta daka mata,don uban ki bana hana ki shigo wa d'akin nan idan  ina tare da baƙi ba..?,don tsabar iskanci hadda wani sallama kamar abun kirki!

Alhajin ya shafo fuskar ta,habba babbar yarinya menene na b'ata ranki akan wanan,ita kuma yarinyar daga kika samo ta..?,cike da barikan ci take magana tare da ƙara lafe wa a jikin Alhaji Habu'uhmm alhaji na kada ka damu da ita nan makwabta take kayan wanki aka aiko ta ɗauka kada ka damu,washe baki yayi ahhh na fahimta.

Baby dake rak'ube jikin kofar d'akin ta shiga sharar kwalla jin an kira ta da ar aiki mai zuwa kwasar wanki,nan ta duke saboda yunwa dake barazanar tsinke mata hanji,magajiya ta fito daga banɗaki hankalin ta na kan waya da take dannawa da alama sak'o take karanta taci karo da ƙafar mutum,cike da masifa ta d'aga ido karaf cikin na baby'matsiya ciya haihuwar asara mai baƙin hali yau kuma mugunta naki a kaina ya sauka..?,banza baby tayi da ita,dama nasan ba zaki amsa ba ai miskilan banza kawai,dama ke nake jira yau baki kyara mun d'aki ba kika fita yana can yana jiran ki.

A wahalce ta d'aga dara darar idanun ta uwar masu gida(suna da kowa ke kiran magajiya dashi kenan a gidan)yanzu na dawo daga schol ko abinci ban ci ba bare nayi sallah kuma koda na fita kina tare da baƙo,wanan keta shafa haihuwar asara har wani sallah za kiyi idan banda kinibibi wa zai amshi sallar a gidan karuwai..!, Nayi gaba idan kinga dama kya biyo baya,jiki a sanyaya baby ta Mike said d'aki Mai number ɗaya wanda shine mallakin magajiya kuma shi ɗaya ne ciki da palour a cikin gidan malale da ties a hanzarce ta kya kamar yadda ta sama ta kunna turaren wuta tare da jawo d'akin,gefe ta koma ta dungule tana tunanin rayuwar ta,ita dai taso wa tayi taga kanta a gidan  kullum da safe sai ta gyara dakin uwar masu gida,uban masu gida(shugaban y'an daudu),sai dakin da take kwana (watau d'akin  ammie),kullum safe sai ta wanke toilet din gida wanda shine kwara guda ɗaya tal,snan ta  kai ma uwar masu gida ruwan wanka,uban masu gida da ammie,duk wanda ya tashi ita yake aike sayan sigari,goro,cingam,da sauran kayan ciye ciye kullum safe mutanen  gidan wanda kowa zaman kanshi yake zai ci gaba da harkokin shi babu ruwan wani da wani a hankali  zuwa rana ko yamma gidan zai shiru kowa ya kama hanya zuwa neman abinda zai tallafi kanshi kuma ya biya uwar masu gida kudin haya.
Akwai ranar da ta samu ammie lokacin tana cikin farin ciki ta roke ta alfarma tana so a saka ta a Makarantar boko,ranar tasha ɗan banzan duka sai da wasu suka tausaya mata a gidan,daga baya kome ta gani da safe ta gefa mata Naira dubu wai taje ta  shiga makarantar amma ta sani wallahi ko Naira ta ba zata sake bayarwa ba idan ta gaji da wahala ta bar zuwa,cike da zumudi ta fito amma bata san ta inda zata fara ba ta hadu da wasu yara da alamar makaranta zasu,tana ta binsu a baya har ɗan agunda primary school da tambaya aka nuna mata ofishin shugaban makaranta,ta sanar da ita abinda ya kawo ta dariya sosai shugaban makaranta tayi tana mamaki yarinya wacce ba zata wuce shekaru takwas ba wai tazo shiga makaranta ce maka tayi ta dawo tare da iyayenta,ai nan ta saka kuka tana faɗin bata san iyayenta ba taso wa tayi taga kanta a gidan kazo nazo dake hotoro,kome head mistress ta gani tace  menene sunan ki..?,Baby..!,Baby ai ba suna bane,tace nima haka na taso naji ana kira na'ok sunan baban ki fa..?,nima ban sani ba sai in za'a saka baby ammie sunan wacce nake wurinta,ok yayi kyau yanzu zaki bada kudin PTA Naira dari biyar,sai ɗinkin kayan makaranta da takadu za'a saka ki aji ɗaya,ta ciro Naira duba ɗaya da ammie ta bata tare da miƙa ma head mistress rubuta sunanta akayi cikin wani register tare da cika wani da form,waya ta d'aga ta Kira wani mallami ba'a ɗauki lokaci ba ya shigo, cikin harshen turanci tace mall.sani ga sabuwar d'aliba ajin ka nake so a saka ta primary 1c,ga Form nan na cika komi sai a rubuta sunanta a cikin register,ta kalli baby ki tafi gida sai ki fara zuwa gobe idan ya nuna maki ajin yanzu,GODIA sosai baby tayi cikin harshen turanci head mistress ta umurci Mallam Sani da yabi baby a  baya ya ga gidan da zata kuma ya samu mata information akanta kafin goben.

Baby bayan taga ajinta ta kamo hanyar gida cike da murna Mallam Sani na biye da ita har ta shiga,ya samu wurin mai shago ya tambaya nan ko aka faɗa masu komi a kanta tausayi sosai ta bashi tun a nan ya buga ma hajia Balki waya itama ta tausaya mata kuma ta kudiri niyar taimaka mata sosai.

Da safe bayan ta gama aikin da take yi a gidan ta kama hanyar makaranta uniform da takadu hajia Balki ta bata tare da mata alkawarin taimaka mata iya karfin ta,cikin nasara ta fara karatun tana gane wa sosai babban kalubalen data fara fuskanta shine yadda ɗalibai suka mayar da ita abun tsokana a cikin makarantar suna kyamar ta bata da halin zama inda suke sai an tashi a bar mata wurin,idan cikin aji ne bamai zama tare da ita,wata rana da abun ya dame ta take tambayar wata  zainab ne tayi masu suka tsane ta a Makarantar sai cewa tayi  ai an faɗa masu bata da Baba,Kuma mamanta karuwa ce,tana zama a gidan karuwai don haka itama karuwa ce idan suna magana da ita zasu zama karuwai,maimakon ranta ya ɓaci suka ga tsantsar farin ciki dauke a fuskarta tana faɗin da gaske ammie itace mamata..?,bata tsaya an tashi ba ta isa gida dai dai ammie tayi wani new catch young alaji ta Fado d'akin a guje ta rungume ammie tana hakki,da gaske ammie kece mama ta..?,cike da mamaki saurayin ammie ke kallon ammie da baby,ITA kuma ammie tsantsar tsoro ne a fuskarta,tsaki yaja ya fita daga d'akin,wawan mari ammie ta wanke baby dashi tare da haɗa kanta da bango tana bugawa da ƙarfi,tayi wurgi da ita wace tana kwallo da ita shegeya haihuwar asara yau sai kin bar gidan nan wallahi don na taimaka ina zama dake zaki yanke mun hanyar arziki a bariki!!!

Yan matan gidan,da yan daudu sai gulmar ammie suke suna dariya ƙasa ƙasa domin haushi take basu,Magaji da taga ammie na ƙoƙarin yin kisa ta janye ta zuwa dakin ta,haba ammie kin san fa kina da mahassada sosai a gidan nan menene na tara wa kanki jama'a akan wanan shegeyar yarinyar,kawai ki manta da ita shawara ɗaya zan baki daga yau ki hana ta zuwa makarantar shike nan an huta.

Tun daga ranar ammie ta hana baby zuwa makaranta kullum itace shara,wankin under Wears na ammie,gashi bata bata abinci isashe kamar lokacin farko,shiru shiru hajia Balki taga an share sati biyu baby bata  zuwa makaranta ta tura Mallam Sani gidan ya gano ko lafia,nan magajiya da ammie suka ci mai mutunci,barazana ya masu akan zai kai karar su wurin hukumar kula da hakkin dan Adam shine fa suka tsorata ammie tace gobe zata ci gaba da zuwa,shine baby ta samu damar komawa schl yara kuma basu fasa zagi da hantarar ta ba saboda haka nema ta kama kanta kullum a ƙasa take zama sai mallami sun shigo ta hau benci har ta samu nasarar cin Jarabawar shiga primary 2,da yake tana da kokari sosai ta iya rubutu da karatu gwrgwado hajia Balki tace a maida ta primary 3 saboda shekarunta tara kenan,komi ya taso na makaranta hajia Balki ke mata har lokacin da ta haɗu da hatsari mota Allah ya karbi abun shi,rankwashi da aka zabga mata ne ya dawo da ita hayacin ta don ubanki shiga ciki kici abinci ki gyara d'akin cewar ammie,jiki a hanzarta ta dauki sauran take away na tuwon semo da miyar egusi ta tutura sanan ta gabatar da azahar da la'asar ga magrib ya gabato,babban katifar ta fara gyarawa sanan ta kwashe tarkacen kayan ammie data cire ta share carpet din ta goge kayan kallo dai dai an kira sallah ta gabatar duk da bata san kome ake faɗa cikin sallar ba amma bata bari ya wuce ta tun lokacin da mallama mai IRK ta faɗa masu wanda baya sallah makomar shi wutar jahannama ta rike sallah a kullum addu'a take Allah ya kawo masu sauyin rayuwa ya shiryi ammie duk da ƙarancin shekarunta tasan sakon Allah ake aikatawa agidan,mallama na faɗa masu caca haramun ne,shan giya ko taba haramun ne,keb'an cewa da namiji haramun ne,shiru shiru ammie bata dawo ba ta dawo kofar dakin ta zauna tana kallon yadda ko wace macce ta ɗan ɗasa kwalliya tana kai kawo sannu a hankali har gidan yayi tsit alamar masu kwanan gida sun tafi,sai yan tsiraru suka rage muryan uwar masu gida ya dawo da ita daga karatun wasikar jaki da take yi,ke ga wata ammie zata yi magana dake,da sallama ta amsa,tsawa ta daka mata ba na hana ki mun sallama ba..?,shegiya nayi tafiya ban san ranar dawowa ba idan gari ya waye duk aikin da kika san kina yi kici gaba,sanan ki rinka amso abinci take away ɗaya a rana wurin iya Bose har in dawo, kayan wanki na rabe zai zo ya amsa,kafin tayi magana uwar masu gida ta amshe wayarta yayi hajjaju farar mace alkabar mata kada a mata ayo mana tsaraban Dubai,baby kowa ganin irin kwalliya da ammie tayi da zata fita tasan bana dawowa yau bane ashe har wata ƙasa ta tafi,Allah kare mata ammie.

Kullum da safe zata tashi tayi aikin data saba sanan ta wuce schl,sai ta dawo take amso aminci taci rabi saura taci kafin ta kwanta,a ko wani lokaci bata da tunani da ya wuce su wanene iyayen ta..?,sun mutu ne ko suna raye..?,menene dalilin zan ta gidan karuwai..?,wacece ammie kuma me suka haɗa..?,wurin wa zata samu amsar wanan tambayoyi ...?,tabbas wurin ammie amma ta ya zata tunkaro ta da wanan magana bata manta dukan da tasha a hannun ammie ba ranar data tsaya itace mahaifiyar ta!!!

~masu karatu ku kasance tare da LEEMA a sannu zata zakulo maku amsar wanan tambayoyi~



_Halimatus sadiya Muhammad_

~🅿3-4~

     *DUBAI UNITED ARAB*

     Rose hotel tower Dubai ammie sanye da Tommy hilifiger push up bra navy blue kwance cikin jacuzzi,idanunta rufe cikin cavalli sunglasses  ta tufke gashin kanta kamar irin Black American's d'in nan tayi zurfi cikin tunani,ji tayi an rungumota ta baya tare da bata light peack a neck nata,a sanyaye ta shafo fuskar alaji habu tana faɗin sugar boi nayi missing naka fa..!,miss U too my honey pot meeting din ne bamu samu kamalawa yau ba dole sai zuwa gobe za ayi concluding komi,murmushi ta sakar mashi wanda ke rikitar dashi tana kokarin fitowa daga jacuzzi d'in,kallonta yake sama da ƙasa cuz  kayan sun bi jikinta komi nata a bayane,suman tsaye alaji yayi yana kallon ammie wace kullum kwanan duniya rikita mashi ƙwaƙwalwa take yi,iska ta hura mai a kunne tare da kashe ido ɗaya,shima ya cije lips nashi na ƙasa hanun ta daya ta sagala a wuyan shi, ɗaya kuma ta rike waist nashi tare da haɗe bakin su wuri ɗaya tana bashi hot french kissies.

Bayan komi ya lafa har sunyi wanka alaji na sanye da blue big and tall knee length drawstring waist band cargo shorts cotton,ammie sanye da red hoodie ,idanunta rufe cikin wayfarer black sunglasses,kafafunta Kuma sanye da Black aldo aleni heels  ta rufe kanta da hat sai tashin kamshi take,murmushi mai kayatarwa alaji ya sakar mata ta sakala hanunta cikin nashi suna saukowa a hankali suka shiga taxi sai wokyo noodle bar suka yi lunch  daga nan suka wuce ocean blue and pool bar basu jima ba suka wuce Jumeirah beach tana makale da alaji kamar za'a kwace mata shi suna shan soyayya sai zuwa dare suka biya fish hunt seafood ya mata take away suka dawo masauki,wanka suka yi tare aka ci gaba da sheke  aya(Allah ya shirya babu sallah babu sallati).


 *NIGERIA,KANO STATE,KANO TUKUNTAWA AREA*


   Baby CE zaune  bakin kofar d'akin su abun duniya ya isheta yau satin ammie ɗaya kenan da tafi ya  zasu fara zana promotion exams na zuwa primary four har yanzu bata biya Naira ɗari biyar na  exams fee ba  tana da tabbacin babu wanda zai yadda ta rubuta jarabawar shiga sabon aji ba tare da ta biya kuɗin nan ba, tunda hajia Balki ta rasu aka canja head master mugu wanda babu abinda ya saka a gaba sai shegen son kuɗi..!;jiki a sanyaye ta nufi rumfar iya Bose amso abinci saboda karfe biyu ta wuce,wani irin harara iya bose ta bita dashi shegen yaro I will not give u anything today  ba kai ne nazo yi ma abinci a arewa  no food for u today until  dat prostitute  pay me my money; da yake baby na fahimtar turanci kaɗan kaɗan amma bata iya mayar wa kuma ta gane inda maganar iya Bose ya dosa,ta duka har ƙasa Madam kiyi hakuri Allah idan ammie ta dawo nasan zata biya ki kuɗin ki,,,tsaki taja taci gaba da attending customer's nata,wani mutum dake zaune yana cin abinci ta bashi tausayi ya dauki sauran abincin da yake ci ya miƙa mata,tana ta godiya ta zube a wani leda data gani a ƙasa ta shek'a zuwa gida,da yamma ma nan taje ta zauna wanda yaji tausayin ta idan yaci ya rage ya miƙa mata ko idan an kai wurin wanke wanke ta kwashe daga haka har ta fara taya masu aikin wurin wanke wanke da miƙa abinci ana bata abinci kamar yadda ake basu.

 Ranar da za'a fara exams office  din head master ta nufa ko zai tausaya mata a bari ta rubuta exams din duk da ba yau bane karo na farko data fara zuwa rokon shi ya bari tayi jarabawar amma fafur yaƙi amince..!,tayi  Sa'a shi ɗaya ne a office d'in,tayi sallama tare da gaisuwa, haɗe rai yayi yana faɗin menene kuma..?, Har ƙasa takai tana r rok'on daya ɗaga mata k'afa ta rubuta jarabawar   idan ammie ta dawo zata kawo kuɗin, ya rik'o suman kanta yana lashe labe tare da faɗin  mai zai hana ki biya mun buƙatar zan baki daman rubuta jarabawar kyauta,cikin zumudi tace Mallam ko menene buƙatar naka zan biya idan har zan samu rubuta jarabawar shiga aji hudu(abunka da yarinya ƙarama bata fahimci inda ya dosa ba),wandon shi ya tube yana kokarin fito da jijiyar shi(penis)"tace lahaulah menene haka mallam..?,yace  kayan daɗi yarinya abu mai sauki zan saki domin nasan babu komi ta tatare da jikin  ki,jijiyata zaki sha  har sai na kawo!..,kuka ta fashe dashi tana faɗin mallama mai IRK tace haramun ne wasa da maza ko taɓa jikin maza..,tsaki yaja munafukar  yarinya kada ki tara mun jama'a don Allah makira ina da labarin uwar ki k'aruwa ce tare kuke a gidan karuwai a hotoro menene baki sani ba idan har ba zaki yi yadda nace ba wallahi ba zaki rubuta jarabawar nan ba sai kin biya,sanan idan naji wanan magana ya fito sai na kashe ki har lahira..!,hannu biyu ta saka ta rufe bakin ta wallahi na tuba mallam bazan faɗa ma kowa ba.

 Mike wa yayi ya rufo Kofar yana fadin
Oyah zo nan  yarinya yana jawo hanunta jiki a sanyaye ta matsa kusa dashi ya tura mata jijiyar shi(penis)cikin baki yana zungura mata sai nishi take sama sama kamar zata mutu cuz abun ya cika mata baki gashi bata san yadda za tayi ba da yaga wahalar banza yake ya haɗe cinyoyinta ya matse jijiyar yana zungurawa har yayi releasing sanan ya bata kyale ta goge cinyar ta daya b'ata da sperm Yana murmushin keta tare cika receipt ya bata shike nan dake za'a rubuta jarabawa yarinya yi sauri ki tafi aji dakin nuna wanan za'a bari ki rubuta,haka ta rubuta jarabawar jiki babu kwari ga ciwon kai da jiki daya addabe ta,bayan sun gama ta nufi gida saboda paper ɗaya zasu rubuta a kullum har kwana bakwai.

Yau ma kamar kullum gidan cike yake da jama'a sai dai na yau yasha ban ban da kullum cuz dambe ake sosai da bak'ar ashana da wata yar beuty wanda aka basu fili suna ta d'irkan dambe ɗan agunda na ƙada gurmi,uwar masu gida da sauran yan daudu na cashewa..!,tsaki taja ta rinka rab'awa cikin jama'a har ta isa dakin tare da ɗauko ɗan makuli wurin da suka ajewa ta buɗe tana ƙoƙarin cire yakusashen uniform d'in ta uwar masu gida ta shigo yar duniya ga kayan ammie can na wanki rabe ya kawo mazza ki kwaso ki shirya mata cikin akwatita ta kusa dawo wa, daɗi ne ya rufe ta sosai jin ammie zata dawo ta huta da rayuwar yunwa wanda idan ba wanke wanke tayiwa iya Bose ba ranar da yunwa zata kwana.

Bayan ta gama shirya kayan ta canja cikin wani ɗan riga iya guiwa da karamin hijab nata wanda ya kuɗe ta rufo d'akin zata rumfar iya Bose taji yar beuty na faɗin wallahi bak'ar ashana sai na nuna maki ke karamar yar bariki ce kwata kwata yaushe kika fito yawon barikin da har zaki mun snatching...!,baby cikin ranta tace shegu yan iska nan suka fi auki kullum basu da magana sai saurayi,idan kaga tonon asiri da cin mutunci akan saurayi ne har ta isa rumfar a Sa'a an tara jibgin wanke wanke halira na faman dambe da plate da manyan tukwane saboda tsabar yawan su ko ganinta ba kayi ta nume ciki,ahhh halira ina saudi. ..?,baby gwara da Allah ya kawo ki wallah saudi ba lafi tana fama da ciyon mara,gasu abigel suna kai abinci,Allah ya bata lafiya tace a takaice tare da kama mata wanke wanken har suka kamala sanan aka basu abinci tayi zaman ta anan tacinye bata son kuma wa cikin gidan saboda fitinar su tasan har yanzu wutan bata mutu ba.


*DUBAI AZURE BEACH*

  Alaji na gani zaune saman steps din da ake takawa a shiga cikin swimming pool Yana amsa waya wanda daga gani important call me,ammie na kwance tsakanin cinyoyin shi sanye cikin swimsuit,idanunta rufe cikin carasie blue sunglasses tana seping shakemilk hankalin ta kwance,bayan yayi hanging call din ta ƙara shigewa jikin shi sugar boi ya kamata mu tafi shopping fa tunda ka kamala abinda ya kawo ka sai a waiwayi gida uwar gida da yara na kewar ka(onething da ammie a bariki bata son shiga hakkin kowa),shi yasa nake kara son ki wallahi honey pot anya ko mun  koma Nigeria zan iya nisa dake koda na kwana ɗaya ne .?,ya zama dole alaji a sauke hakkin uwar gida da yara,thank u honey pot yanzu mu koma masauki mu shirya sai mu tafi shopping ɗin, swimsuit din t cire ta mayar da tank top  ɗin ta tare da  sakale hanunta a waist nashi suka nufi Ibn battuta mall ta jidi kayan sawa son ranta ya biya da credit card nashi,suka wuce Dubai outlet mall inda ta jidi shoes and  bags,sai Dubai fountain and Burj khalifa dake down tower ta kwashi gwala gwalai daga Nan  tace ya kamata su shiga festival city mall ayuwa uwar gida da yara sayayya,bai Mu'sa ba ya sakar mata kuɗi ta jiɗar wa matar shi kaya da yara  sosai, shopping ɗin da suka yi ya kira waya masukin su aka turo motar rose hotel tower  ta kwashe kayan zuwa masauki su,suka nufi arabian tea House cafe tayi order na black coffee daga nan suka nufi sunken Garden kasan cewa gobe zasu bar ƙasar basu suka dawo masauki ba sai around 11:30pm na dare dai dai an gama shirya masu kayan su a babban travelling bag biyu nata dana alaji,ranar wani irin mahaukacin soyayya ta gwada mashi mai tsayawa a rai bayan komi ya lafa sun yi wanka tana rungume a kirjin shi ya kira sunanta ammie a sanyaye,ta amsa,maganar gaskiya tunda nake yawon bariki ban taɓa cin karo da mace mai class wacce ta haɗa komi irin ki ba bana jin zan iya rabuwa dake idan har kin amince zan aure ki saboda ba zan iya rabuwa dake ba kuma inada matukar kishi bazan lamunci sharing baki da ko wane da namiji ba..!,murmushi tayi nagode sosai alaji na maganar gaskiya bani da niyyar aure kwata kwata yaushe na fara barikan cin,me na tara a bariki,banyi suna a bariki ba,bani da mota ko gidan kaina,bani da wani abu dana ajiye wanda zan kira nawa koda nabar bariki gaskiya alaji bazan iya aure ba.

Uhmmn kina da masaniyar cewa rayuwar da muke aikatawa ba rayuwa bace mai ɓulle wa,musamman ma ke a matsayin ki na d'iya mace,idan har zaki aure Ni zan saya maki mota na alfarma,gida na alfarma zan baki ko million NAWA kike so ki fara business idan sune damuwar ki,alaji mubar maganar bata da amfani saboda baka san asali na ba, baka san kowa nawa ba haka baka san komi akai na ba aure ba zai yiwo tsakanin mu ba!

Ammie kenan wanan ba matsala bace domin nasan KEMA Y'ACE KAMAR KOWA,ba daga sama kika fado ba,walau iyayen ki sun rasu ne ko halin rayuwa ya fito dake daga gida;kuka ta fashe dashi alaji na tabbata idan har kaji labari na ba zaka iya aure na ba domin yana da muni sosai..!,ammie babu abinda yafi muni a wurin Ya mace kamar tabar gaban iyayenta ta shiga bariki wanda a nan zata yi zina,sata,caca,shaye shaye da kuma cikin shege a karshe ta kwaso cututukan zamani,kinga kuwa U Are at risk domin cikin duka bayansa na lissafa maybe biyu zuwa uku ne baki aikatawa kuma nace naji na gani!!

Ita dai kukanta take sosai saboda ya tuno mata da abubuwa da dama da suka faru da ita a rayuwarta,pls no more tears honey pot mu kwanta zan baki lokaci koyi shawara zuwa nan da 1week,haka suka kwanta bata yi bacci kirki ba.


*WANENE ALAJI HABU*?

   Alhaji  Abubakar maka(alaji Habu)wanda ba zai wuce shekaru sittin a duniya(60yrs) Dogo ne sosai baƙi mai girman jiki ga uban tumbi wanda Turawa kewa lakabi da (irin su ake kira da gient),amma fa yana da kyau'n fuska sosai wanda hakan ya boye shekarun shi,saboda huta da kuɗi idan ka ganshi zaka yi tunanin bai wuce shekaru arba'in ba Cikakken bakane ne gaba da baya iyayen shi yan asalin Dala L.G ne, yayi karatu tun daga matakin primary har master degree Wanda ya riƙe muƙamai iri iri na gwamnati kuma yana kasuwan ci sosai a nan Nigeria har kasashen ketare yanzu haka ma harkan kasuwan cin nashi ya kai shi dubai,Yana zaune a lamid'o crescent tare da matar shi ɗaya wacce auren soyayya suka yi hajia mariya  wayayar mace  cikakkiyar yar boko likitar mata ce (gynecology)tana aiki a teaching hospital kano,Allah ya albarkaci auren su da yara uku"Faiza dake Bayero University kano UG2,saratu tana Kano capital secondary School ss3,sai auta Usman dake  first Grade secondary School jss2..!,Babu abinda hajia mariya ta raga mijinta  dashi fannin kyautatwa amma idanun shi na wurin matan waje,ko wane ƙasa zashi sai an mashi special order  na Yar rakiya daga wurin Uwar masu gida wanan tafiyar ne uwar masu gida ta haɗa shi da ammie kuma yaji daɗin kasan cewa da ita sosai har yana tunanin auren ta.


 Misalin ƙarfe biyar na yamma jirgin su ya sauko Nigeria Murtala Muhammad international airport Kano dama yawa driver waya already  ado da motoci biyu wurin wani dankareren Benz E550 formatic taga sun nufa  yaba first dama ya amshi travelling bag din ya saka a buth rada mata yayi a  kunne ga tukuici ki nan yana nuna mata motar,ai tsalle ta daka ta rungume shi a wurin da yake dan duniya ne bai damu da wani driver ba,alaji zan baka tukuici wannan kyauta in a special way,driver ya umurta daya kai ta gida shima ya shiga dayar motar zuwa gida.

Tun a hanya ta tsegunta ma uwar masu gida,suna isowa ya bude mata motar tare da fitar da bag nata,ai sauran matan suka yi chaaa ana kallon ikon Allah ammie cikin daleliyar mota  tayi fresh da ita sanye cikin long coat,wani ɗan daudu da suke mugun shiri ya shiga mata kirari yana Jan bag nata zuwa ciki nan take sauran matan suka fito tare da uwar masu gida wace ke rangada gud'a tana sanar da ɗan agunda a ba ammie taken ta,nan ya fara rero waka yana wasa ammie suna taka rawa,sauran matan na dariyar yake amma na ciki na ciki, uban masu gida ya fito yana fadin gaskiya uwar masu gida ba'a kyauta mana ba ko wane lokaci alaji habu ya nemi order sai a rinka haɗa shi irin su ammie bayan gani da mutane na baki taɓa samu cikin order ba..!, take kai uban masu gida ka fiya azarbabi alaji habu baya harka da jinsin ka,kasan idan an samu naku ina bada order sai in baƙin ciki kake wa ammie,"dariya ya sheke ahayeee ras ammie ai tamu ce rainon mu bama yan haka da ita,kowa yaga mota aka sanya albarka tace da driver ya tafi da motar idan zata fita zata neme shi don babu wurin parking inda suke.

Baby na gefe rak'ube jikin gina tana jin duk abinda ke faruwa,ammie ta murde mata kunne da karfi muguwa yar baƙin ciki kina ganina amma ba ko sannu da zuwa bare murna,baki ji dadin dawo wana bane..?,naji ammie ya hanya..?,ban sani ba haɗa mun ruwan wanka yanzu.


Bayan tayi wanka ta canja cikin sheath dress ɗaya daga cikin kayan data dawo dasu,Baby tace akwai abinda kike so ammie..?,No tace,ammie mun gama jarabawar shiga aji hudu kuma nayi kokari sosai tana murmushi...,tsaki taja tare da daka mata tsawa keee bana son iskanci daga dawo wana zaki tasa Ni gaba da surutan banza tashi ki bani wuri!

Rabe ya ja mata akwatin zuwa palour'n uwar masu gida,nan  ta baje palour'n uwar masu suka kulle kofa, aka buɗe jakka uwar masu gida tashiga zaro idanu ganin kaya masu kyau da tsada,ammie ta ware mata nata tsarabar,ta cire ma sauran yan gidan kowa da kowa amma banda Baby wacce ko suturar kwarai bata dasu.

Ammie gaskiya alaji na sonki tunda nake haɗa shi da yan mata ban taɓa samun Wacce  ta dawo da abun arziki kamar ki ba hadda tsaraban mota yar nan..!,kedai bari uwar masu gida wai aure na yake so,me kika ce mai..?,cewa nayi ban shirya aure ba..!,amma baki da wayau ammie shekara da shekaru kina cikin bariki kina wahala Allah ya kawo lokacin da zaki huta amma kina shirme kawia ki amince idan kunyi aure da kwana biyu kin wawuri dukiya ki kashe banza kawai., Tafasa kawai suka yi,Uwar masu gida shi yasa nake mugun sunyi uwanta  baya goya marayu,yanzu ya zanyi da Baby kin San ba zan iya zaman aure da ita ba ko..?,itama ki sheke shegiya ta mutu har lahira babu mai tuhumar ki cikin dare ki damka masu Baushe gawanta a salwantar!!!

Basu zame ko inaba sai wani hadaden gida a Shagari Quarters,da hanzari driver ya bude mata gidan baya ta fito yana directing bata har palour'n gidan,alaji habu zaune saman 3seater daga shi sai three quater Babu ko riga sai uban tumbi yana murmushi irin nasu na gogaggun yan bariki welcome my honey pot..!,a yangance ta ke taku har inda yake ta haye saman cinyar shi tare da bashi deap kiss a baki na kusan 2mnt tana shan tongue nashi kafin nan ta zare harshen ta kanta kwance saman chest nashi alaji na nayi kewar ka da yawa fa,rungumo ta ya ƙara yi da kyau me too honey pot nan take ta rikita Mai lissafi suka shiga aikata shashancin su har zuwa wani lokaci suka yi wanka tare,kwance suke saman family size bed ya shafo fuskarta ammie..!

Dama da ya kyara ainihin sunanta magana mai muhimmanci zai mata;jiya da taimakon Allah na samu bacci saboda so da kaunar ki sun adabi zuciyata har uwar gida bata gane kai ne ba saboda halin kewar ki da nake ciki,na yanke shawarar mallaka Maki wanan gida nawa a matsayin nayi har abada takadu na palour zan kara lauyana ki saka hannu a gaban shi ayi komi;bata san lokacin data ƙara kankame shi tana kissing nashi ta ko ina ba GODIA nike alaji wanan makeken gida Ni ɗaya dama kuwa nazo maka da babban albishir cewa na amince zan aure ka!

Kuka ta fashe dashi domin kwata kwata bata son tunowa da rayuwar ta na baya shekaru goma kenan rabonta da iyayenta..!,ki bar kuka komi yayi tsanani maganin shi Allah ina so muyi tsaftataciyar rayuwar aure ne kuma hakan bazai samu ba har sai kin daure kin sanar dani labarin ki!

*LABARIN AMMIE*

    Iyayena yan asalin karamar hukumar kumbotso ne dake jahar kano,mahaifina Mallam Aminu ya kasance wanzami ne wanda gado yayi tun iyaye da kakanni bashi da wata sana'a data wuce wanzan ci yana fa matan aure uku;inna lami itace babba wacce auren saurayi da budurwa suka yi Allah bai bata haihuwa ba bayan wasu shekaru mahaifina ya auri mama ladi suna zaune lafiya  itama shiru kusan shekaru biyar ko batan wata bata taɓa yi ba sai aka ba mahaifina shawara ya kara aure inda ya haifi umma ta mai suna jummai watanni goma cif ta ta haifi ɗa namiji murna wurin Mallam da sauran matan gidan ba'a magana duk da ba wani ƙarfi ne dashi ba amma iya gwargwado anyi shagali ranar suna yaci sunan mallam(Aminu) ana kiran shi da mallam,rainon shi ya koma hannun inna lami iyaka shan nono ke kawo shi wurin umma bayan wattani shida sai ga wani ciki a jikin umma,murna wurin Mallam ba'a magana ga cikin  yazo da laulayi matuka 'hankalin kowa ya tashi da irin laulayi da take yi,inna lami ta bada shawara a cire shi a nono zata haɗa mai abincin yara na gargajiya zai murmure,haka kuwa akayi inna lami na kula da Mallam Karami,mama ladi na kula da umma har ciki ya shiga watanni bakwai lokacin ta murmure suka ci gaba da harkokin su gwanin sha'awa lokacin haihuwa ta sake samun namiji yaci sunan mahaifinta Aliyu (ana kiran shi hydar) mama ladi tace itama ta samu nata hydar ya koma d'akin ta komi ita ke mashi, lokaci na tafiya mai gari ya naɗa Mallam  sauratan baraden kumbotso saboda yana ɗaya daga cikin dattijawan kauyen aka bashi kyautar gona madaidaici ya fara noma a hankali har Allah ya sanya mashi albarka ciki ana samun amfanin gona sosai har ya fara gwada noman rani cikin ikon Allah Aka samu amfanin gona,rayuwar gidan mu gwanin sha'awa saboda idan ba sani kayi bazaka taba cewa kishiyoyi bane,ga Mallam Karami da hydar sun taso kaman yan biyu kamar yadda na samu labari wurin umma sai da suka shekara goma a duniya ta samu ciki na lokacin kuma inna lami tayi fama ciwon daji na mak'oshi(throat cancer),ciwo sai gaba yake kullum har  ya kai bata iya magana,aka shigo da ita cikin garin Kano babban asibiti likita ya tabbatar wa Mallam idan ba aiki akayi mata ba bazai warke haka nan ba kuma kudin aiki kusan million ɗaya da rabi ne,Mallam ya dawo yayi buga buga duk inda zai samu rancen kudi ya haɗa amma babu a karshe ya sayar da gona kin shi manya guda biyu akan dubu ɗari biyu(lokacin kuɗi nada daraja),wasu kiwo na mama ladi da umma na duka sun bayar anata buga buga da kyar aka haɗa dubu ɗari biyar za'a kai a fara aiki kafin a samu saura cikin ikon Allah koda Mallam ya isa rai yayi halin sa nan Mallam ya fadi ƙasa sumamme shima sai da yasa ƙarin ruwa jin shiru yayi yawa mai gari ya tadda mutane biyu abi bayan shi lokacin da suka isa mumunar labari ya riske su babu bata lokaci suka samu hyan akori kura aka dauko gawan inna lami zuwa gida,liman ya bada umurni ayi saurin mata sutura a kaushe ta,bayanin tashin hankali dasu mama ladi suka shiga ba'a magana musamman Mallam karami da take uwa a gare shi domin a wanan lokaci bashi da masaniyar mama ta haife shi,haka aka shirya inna lami Mallam na zubda hawaye aka kaita gidan ta na gaskia sanan aka ci gaba da amsar gaisuwa har kwana uku sanan jama'a suka dauke ƙafa aka bar Mallam da iyalan shi cikin jimami gidan ya koma shiru kamar babu kowa saboda rashin walwala,mama ladi ta yanke shawarar Mallam karami ya dawo d'akin ta saboda cikin umma yayi nauyi,Mallam kam yana cikin wani hali saboda duk cikin matan shi yafi son Inna lami bashi da tamkar ta matar rufi asiri tun bashi samun ko sisi suke tare har Allah ya buda mai bata taɓa nuna ɓacin ranta ba shekarun su ashirin da aure Allah ya amshi ranta tana da shekaru talatin da shida a duniya,sauran kuɗin daya rage a hanun shi ya biya bashin mutane yayi wa matan shi alkawarin biyan su kiwon su da ya sayar ko wacce tace ta yafe har lahira,sauran ƙaramin gona daya rage mallam ya amshi takin gwamnati bashi ya fara noma kullum tare da yaran shi yake zuwa suna taimaka mashi;Bayan watanni biyu Allah ya sauki umma lafiya ta samu d'iya mace ranar ansha kuka a gidan domin tunawa da inna lami wace ta taɓa cewa jumma gaskia idan kika sauka lafiya sunan surukar mu za'a saka duk da bata raye ayi mata takwara kinga mu Allah bai bamu haihuwa ba amma zuwan ki ko wacce ta samu d'a wanda zata yi alfahari dashi,idan Allah ya saukeki lafiya ammie ta zama taki...!,ranar  suna kuwa akayi mun huɗu ba da sunan  mahaifiyar mallam "Hadiza"ana kirana da ammie,na taso cikin so da kauna,kulawa da tsantsan kyautatawa daga wurin iyayena da yan uwana musamman ya'ya Mallam yana matukar ji dani,shine goyona da an gama mun wanka wani lokaci ko ina kuka da kyar mama ke amsana a wurin  yaya Mallam, ya hydar haushi abin yake bashi yana faɗin zaka shagwaba yar mutane domin kai ba ɗan gidan nan bane riƙon ka mama ladi keyi ranshi ya ɓaci sosai da jin maganar hydar ranar kuwa ya samu umma yana tambayan ta da gaske ne shi ba dan shidan bane riƙon shi ake yi...?,abunka da ɗan fari tana matukar jin kunyar shi tace eh haka ne Allah yayiwa mahaifiyar ka inna lami rasuwa yanzu baka da kamar Mallam da mama ladi jiki a sanyaye ya samu mallam a  hanyar shi ta dawo wa daga rumfar mai gari ya tambaye shi da gaske bashi bane mahaifin shi..?,yace waya faɗa mashi wanan magana..!,yace umma ce"yace duk abinda ta faɗa maka gaskia ne bamu muka haife ka ba Allah ya amshi mahaifiyarka (Mallam ya dan ganta maganar umma da kunyar ɗan fari ne),ranar ya kebi a dakin su(wanda d'akin inna lami aka kyara musu nan suke zama),yana cikin kuka ya hydar ya shigo yana tambayar shi musababbin kukan nashi..?,yace ai akan maganar daya faɗa mashi dazu ne umma da Mallam sun tabbatar mashi  iyayen shi basu da rai riƙon shi ake yi a gidan,ya hydar yace ai na faɗa maka saboda sam bakayi kama da yan gidan nan ba kowa ya Fika kyau da hasken fata yanzu dai kayi hakuri muci gaba da masu addu'a kawai;tun daga lokacin yaya Mallam ya dauki kanshi amatsayin maraya abinda ke damun shi daya nuna kulawa sosai akan ammie sai hydar yata gwale shi ko a gaban wanene yana nuna zan shagwaba mashi yar uwa,wata rana da ranshi ya ɓaci yakai kara wurin mama ladi dariya kawai tayi saboda a ganinta yaranta ke cin su ta bashi hakuri;inada shekaru uku umma ta sake samun wani cikin mai matukar laulayi fiye da cikin ya hydar sai kulawar mu duka ta koma wurin mama ladi tunda asuba take tashi tayi aikin gida da bin kari su Mallam su tafi gona lokacin tare dani yaya Mallam ke zuwa ya goyani suna aiki,idan sun dawo tare muke zaga gari yawo da dare muka mu tafi makarantar dare na allo tare tun ya hydar na masifa da faɗa har ya hakura ganin Allah ya haɗa jini na da yaya Mallam sosai shi kuwa Bama shan inuwa guda ko kaɗan komi nashi faɗa da zafin rai kuma baya dariya kullum fuskar nan tashi a haɗe kamar an dauro hadari;haka muke rayuwa cikin rufi asiri umma na fama da laulayi ko ruwa tasha sai amai har ciki yakai watani bakwai aka yanke shawarar zuwa asibiti babba cikin gari,bayan anyi mata hoto(scanning)aka gane cikin yan biyu ne amma basu kwanta dai dai ba(breech presentation) ɗaya da ƙafa, ɗaya da hannu likita ya bada magunguna da za'ayi amfani dasu da shawarar wari akan da taji nakuda a kawo ta asibiti ta haihu; suka dawo gida taci gaba da jinya har lokacin haihuwa yazo aka koma hospital tun safe take nakuda har yamma haihuwa bai zina aka yanke shawarar aiki za'ayi mata,Mallam ya saka hannu aka shigar da ita da taimakon Allah aka ciro yara biyu namiji da mace duka babu rai gashi ta zubar da jini sosai dole akayi mata ƙarin jini likita yaba Mallam shawara akan umma tayi amfani da dubarun tazarar haihuwa(family planning) har na wani lokaci mahaifan ta ya huta kafin ta dauki wani cikin yana ba Mallam bayani akan irin dubarun da ka dasu(methods),babu bata lokaci Mallam ya zaɓi rubbe domin ta samu isasshen hutu,Mallam ya dawo da yaran gida aka kau dasu ya dauki mama ladi damu duka muka tafi ganin umma da jiki wanda sai da tayi sati daya Ka sallame ta taci gaba da jinya a gida tunda lokacin haihuwa ya tsaya bata sake samun wani cikin ba;lokaci na tafiya ranaku,watanni da shekaru na shude wa shak'uwa mai ƙarfi na kara shiga tsakani na da yaya Mallam,inada shekaru shida yaba Mallam shawara a saka Ni a makarantar boko tunda ina zuwa na allo na yamma kuma muna zuwa na dare tare dasu,da kyar da sud'in goshi Mallam ya amince domin bashi da buri da ya wuce ina samu wadataciyar ilimin addini ya aurar dani ga miji na gari,murna wurina da yaya ba'a magana shi da kanshi ya kai Ni model primary school Nan cikin ƙauyen mu aka bani aji ɗaya"da yake  yaya Mallam yana zuwa yaƙi da jahilci(adult education) asabar da Lahadi wanda akeyi anan  makaranta,ya hydar kam yafi son noma da kasuwanci duk da shekarun su sha shida amma yana zuwa cin kasuwar wasu ƙauyuka nan makwabta;na fara karatuna a Sa'a ina mayar da hankali sosai bani da aboki sai yaya Mallam idan na dawo shike mun bitar karatu na har na allo akwana a tashi na samu nasarar shiga aji biyu lokacin yaya mallam na zangon karshe a nasu karatun wanda dama shekaru uku daka kamala zaka samu damar zuwa makarantar gaba da primary na cikin garin kano Allah yasa yaya Mallam na ɗaya daga cikin wa'yanda suka ci common entrance exams d'in da ayi masu Government day secondary School kano ,lokacin mallam ya fara shirye shiryen tafiyar yaya mallam makaranta ya haɗa shi da aminin shi dan kasuwa  dake zaune a sheka cikin Kano yana kasuwancin a yan kaba na kayan miya idan su Mallam sun cire amfanin gona ya sari kayan lambu ya kai kasuwa har ALLAH ya sanya mashi albarka a ciki ya gina gidan kanshi sheka ya koma da iyalin shi;ranar da yaya Mallam zai tafi da kuka muka rabu tun daga lokacin na koma shiru shiru bani da abokin hira sai mama ladi da umma sai in Mallam na gida domin ba ƙaramin wahala ya hydar yake bani ba!!!

nadi muka sha kwalliya akayi girke girke iri iri na zamana sai rumfar mai gari ga makida na gargajiya suna kadawa aka naɗa ya hydar kusan su shida kowa da nashi sarautar Hakimi ya bashi kyautar yar shi Asiya sadaki kawai ake buƙata a saka lokacin aure murna wurin mallam ba'a magana haka taro ya watse..!,da dare ɗan mai anguwa ya aiko yana kirana Kofar gida ai kuwa yaya Mallam ya fita yana kumar bada hakuri akan yaya Mallam bai kyauta ba kuma zai tsawata mashi,da Mallam ya dawo da maganar gida yaya Mallam ya hasala ya shiga haɗa kayan mu wai kano zamu koma ba zan sake zuwa hutu ba sai na kammala karatu(Quruciya dangin hauka)na biye mashi muka nufi kano gidan Mallam saminu na karkare hutuna koda je su yaya mallam ya fito ta neco ya lashe komi hadda "B"guda biyu yake sanar dani yanzu son Neman gafarar yaya Mallam domin Mallam ya sanar dashi komi akan shine mahaifin su  kuma umma ta haife su duba rikon su kawai inna lami da mama ladi su kayi ranar yayi kuka saboda abubuwan da ya rinƙa yiwa babban wannan shi yaya Mallam yana goranta mashi iyayen shi sun rasu...!,  ai kuwa kamar jiki ake su iso kano ciwon marar ammie ya tashi bai zame da ita ko ina ba Sai asibiti inda aka duba ta likita yace bai kamata ko wane lokaci ana mata ƙarin ruwa da allurai ba gwara a bari tayi endouring pain din nan,yaya Mallam yace yanzu ba wani abu da za'a mata yanar irin matan..?,yace ai yawan ci mace mai kananun shekaru haka take fama wasu har sai sunyi aure an fara shigar su(sex)yake ragewa with time Kuma da ta samu ciki shike nan ya wuce,yace ya gode pain killer Kawai ya basu suka dawo gida tayi ta fama gwaggo larai na tausaya mata har tayi kwana bakwai da take yi ta gama,shi kam yaya Mallam bashi da tunani sai abinda likita ya sanar dashi idan ba shigar (sex)ammie akayi ba ciwon bazai ragu ba Allah yasan tunda aka haifi ammie ya kamu da ciwon sonta bashi da buri da ya wuce ya mallake ta amatsayin matar shi uwar ya'yan shi shine hikimar da yasa ya hanata sauraran kowa har sai ta kamala Secondary schl dai dai har service yayi lokacin takai shekaru sha takwai sai suyi auren su,yanzu haka zai zuba idanu tayi ta shan wahala gaskiya gwara ya shigewa ko sau ɗaya ne ta rage jin azaba ai babu mai ganewa tunda shine zai aureta...!(RASHIN SANI YAFI DARE DUHU),,,,haka kuwa akayi ranar da dare Mallam saminu na wurin bada karatu,gwaggo larai kamar tayi bacci domin da tayi sallar isha'i take bacci yaran ta kuwa duk suna makaranta yaya mallam ya kira Ni zuwa d'akin su cikin natsuwar ya sanar dani abinda likita yace da kuma kudirin shi...!,cikin jimami nace yanzu yaya mallam kana ganin babu wanda zai gane...?,yace a'a saboda yana matukar sona yayi alƙawarin babu wanda zai san wanan magana kuma yasan idan mallam ya samu labari muna don juna zai farin ciki da hakan,babu wani bata lokaci na biye wa daɗin baki irin na da namiji,son zuciya da rudin shaidan RANAR ASABAR  ASHIRIN GA WATAR MARIS SHEKARA ...,MISALIN KARFE TARA NA DARE YAYA MALLAM YA SAMU NASARAR KAWAR DA BUDURCINA ALAJI HABU!!!!

Kuka mai ƙarfi ta fashe dashi tana buga kanta da karfen gado,shima cikin hawaye jikin shi ya matukar sanyin yake ƙoƙarin rike ta amma ya kasa...

Sai da ya bari tayi mai isarta yaci gaba da sallati yana istigfari wanan kwaba da mai yayi kama....?,muryan ta yaji tana faɗin nasan tabbas duk namijin da yaji wanan labari nawa ba zai taɓa aure Na ba shine dalilin da yasa  a duniya ban taɓa kawo ma kaina zanyi aure ba har in koma ga mahaliccin na!!!

Shiiii ammie wanan dalili ba shi zai hana Ni auren ki ba domin labarin ki ya zama iznah a gare Ni da sauran al'umma baƙi ɗaya ki daure ki karkare wanan labari INSHA ALLAH wahalar ki ta yanke a duniya.

Cike da kwarin gwuiwa taci gaba....,nasha kuka a daren na azaba shima harda hawaye da kyar ya lalab'ani a cewar shi mallam saminu ya kusa shigowa a rufe gida in tashi in tsaftace jikina insha paracetamol in kwanta,,,haka kuwa akayi da kyar na samu bacci da safe na tashi da zazzabi(ashe tun a daren nan na samu ciki bamu sani ba tunda kwana ɗaya kenan da gama al'ada ta),hankalin ya Mallam ya tashi sosai aka mun allurai a chemist duk da tafiyata ta canja gwaggo larai ta barshi a rashin karfin jiki ne sanadiyar zazzaɓi dana tashi dashi tun daga ranar yaya Mallam ya koma wani irin jarababbe kullum da dare sai ya shige Ni kafin yayi wanka ya tafi masallaci wurin ɗaukar karatu,idan Mallam saminu yayi magana me yasa yake zuwa latti yace sai ya gama karatun jamb yake fitowa har aka samu tsawon sati ɗaya muna Abu ɗaya tun ina jin zafi bana sakin jiki har na fara jin daɗin abun na fishi zak'e wa har hutu ya ƙare gidan babu wanda ya fahimci komi duk da anga mun ƙara shak'uwa sosai tare muke yin komi aka barshi akan shakuwa ce na jini,ranar da zan koma makaranta haka muka rabu da alkawaruruka masu dama akan wanan abu zai kasance sirrin mu babu wanda zamu sanar wa duk runtsi da wuya muna tare har mutuwa...!, Mun shiga jss2 lokacin su headgirl sun tafi na fara shan wahala kamar yadda sauran ɗalibai ke sha saboda haushi na kawai  jira ake headgirl ta wuce a Fara cuɗani lokacin nayi baƙi na rame  wahala har wata ya ƙara aka shiga sabon wata bangaren al'ada ba"nisa keyi tsakiyar  wata"...,shiru har wani wata lokacin kuwa na fara laulayi komi naci sai amai ga kasalance sai faman bacci bana tabuka komi a aji aka kira wayar mallam saminu ai kuwa ya tadda yaya Mallam yazo a duba Ni aka bani permission zuwa gida har na samu sauƙin koda muka nufi asibiti gwajin farko ya nuna ina ɗauke da juna biyu na wattani biyu...!,duk da shekaru na sha uku a lokacin nasan ciki ba abun wasa bane kuma ba ƙaramin abun kunya na aikata ba ɗaukar ciki ba tare da aure ba,idan aka samu labari a ƙauyen mu nasan na gama yawo domin sai mun sha surutai ana iya faɗin saboda haka ne yaya Mallam ya hana Ni aure da sauraren kowa don yamun ciki...!,maganar yaya Mallam ya dawo dani daga dogon tunanin da nake yi,yanzu dai kibar kuka mu tafi gida tunda na samu makarantar horar da sojoji dake Kaduna zan wuce  kafin nan zan sanar da Mallam a ɗauka mana aure da mun dawo Kano zan nemi aminta cen likita a zubar da ciki sai a akara daura mana aure kici gaba da karatu domin wanan Magana sirri ne a tsakanin mu minti alkawari duk runtsi babu mai sani!!!!

~🅿11-12~



   
  🅱an kawo komi a zuciyata ba muka sallami gwaggo larai zamu kumbotso lokacin Mallam saminu na kasuwa tayi mana Allah kiyaye hanya, tafiyar ya kasan ce wani irin domin har muka isa kowa da abubuwan da zuciyar shi ke sak'a mashi a nawa bangare roƙon Allah nake kada iyayena su fahimci halin da nake ciki while yaya Mallam yana tunanin wani likita zai gani a cire cikin dake jiki na...!,Da sallama na fara shiga gidan na tsinkayi muryan mama larai tana maraba da yan makaranta ammie'n babanta"murmushi na saki a kasalance na rungume ta sai kuka wiwi  ,tace jumma fito kiga ja'irar yarinya....,umma ce ta fito daga rumfar Mallam da alama abinci ta kai masa tace ai kun fi kusa in banda shagwaba daga shigowa sai kuka ina mai sunan mallam din..?,muryan shi suka tsanko yana faɗin umma a bita asannu rashin lafiya tayi shine aka bata hutun kwana biyu har ta warware,mama larai ta ƙara ɗago ni daga jikin ta  ai ko ta faɗa sosai tayi baƙi yanzu debi ruwa kiyi wanka kizo ga hura can wanda umman ki dama wa Mallam ki diba za kifi jin daɗin shi,sum sum ta dauki bukiti ta cika sai ban daki,gaisawa suka ƙara yi da yaya Mallam yana masu bayani masassarar cizon sauro (malaria) ke damun ta....,koda na fito yaya Mallam bai gidan na kimtsa jikina tare da neman wuri na kwanta dakin mama ladi babu yadda bata yi dani nasha huran ba amma fafur naƙi gudun kada nayi wata aman su gane halin da nake ciki ga yunwa na adaba na,tsakar gida ta fito suna magana da umma tana ce mata taga yadda kirjina(breast) suka ciko kuwa kamar wata babban mace,basar wa umma tayi tana faɗin bakya gajia da zance yaya ladi tana yan aikace aikacen ta amma fa hankalinta bai kwanta da wanan rashin lafiya na ammie ba har Mallam ya dawo shima sai da yayi magana akan yadda ta rame tayi baƙi yaya Mallam ya nuna rashin lafiya ce kawai,,,,Da dare Mallam ya tara mu duka hadda ya hydar saboda ya faɗa masu akwai muhimmiyar magana da zai yi damu kowa ya halaraa hadda su umma ya fara buɗe taro da addu'a yana ƙara miƙa godia ga Allah daya basu zuri'a dayiba masu tarbiya musamman yadda yaya Mallam ya riƙe yan uwan shi hannu biyu ya nuna koda bayan ran su zai zumunci kuma zai kula dasu,abinda yasa na tara ku domin sanar daku wani boyayen lamari da babu wanda ya sani cikin ku dama hydar na sanar dashi lokacin kuna Kano,duba da halin rayuwa babu wanda ya san gawan fari  naga ya kamata kusan wanan sirri (yaya Mallam murna ya cika shi a tunanin shi yana ce ko za'a yi maganar haɗa shi aure da ammie ne wanda dana shi ya kawo su),Mallam yace da farko dai  zan fara gode wa mata na da suka haɗa kai babu wani banbanci wurin kula da tarbiyyar ku duka,Allah ya jikan marigayi ya innar ku lami wace ta rike mai suna na kamar itace ta haife shi bayan rasuwan ta riƙon ka ya dawo hanun naman ku ladi,Abinda nake so ku sani dukan ku Ayau shine inna lami da mama ladi basu suka haife ku ba"ya nuna  umma ga mahaifiyar ku nan umman ku jummai itace ta haife ku duka(wani irin zufa ne ya keto ma yaya mallam,ita kuma ammie har ta fara hawaye),tabbas shekaru goma sha biyar da auren Matana biyu babu wace ta haihu sai zuwan umman ku ta fara haihuwar ka Aminu mai suna na,sai Aliyu hydar sai ke Hadiza ammie,saboda zaman lafiya dake tsakanin su yasa ta mallaka ma abokan zamanta ku halak malak(yana nuna yaya Mallam da ya hydar) kuka wiwi ammie ta fara tana faɗin na shiga uku na lalace umma ,wayyo umma na ki mun rai,Mallam kace mafarki nake yi....!cike da mamaki kowa yake kallon na saboda basu gane inda maganata dusa ba,yaya Mallam dake zub da hawayen baƙin ciki yaga idan ba takamun burki yayi ba zan tona mana asiri ya riko hannu na zuwa waje yana fadin dama zazzabin ciwon sauro idan yama mutun yawa har sambatu yake saka wa likita ya tabbatar mana da haka(Lol),Mallam yace ashha kai ta daki ta kwanta kota samu bacci....,turjewa ta fara yi tana faɗin ka kyale Ni,ka sake ni sai na sanar da Mallam komi,ai yana jin haka ya cicibe ta sai saman gadon umma yana rufe mata baki da hanuwan shi duka biyu"baki da hankali ne ko kin manta da alkawarin da muka ɗauka komin runtsi babu wanda zai ji wanan magana...?,mugu azzalumi ashe kasan ciki ɗaya muka fito ka yaudare Ni yanzu gani dauke da cikin yaya zai haifa ma kaina K'ani...!,wani wawan mari ya wanke ta dashi shiru wallahi ko jikin ki ya gaya maki yanzu da iskan ci idan nasan ciki ɗaya muka fito zan fara wahalar da kai na ne aka ki...?,zan kashe kaina in raya kine....?,zan bata lokaci,dukiya da kuma zubar da girma na a kanki ne..!,bara ki faɗa maki abunda baki sani ba wallahi duk wani hidima dana maki tun kina ƙarama saboda in mallaki zuciyar kine,in hana idanun ki kallon ko wane da namiji aduniya sai Ni a karshe ina mallake ki a matsayin matar aure ashe duka na banza ne..!!!, Ko kin k'i kokin so gobe zamu bar garin nan zuwa Kano ayi gaggawar zubar da cikin nan na rantse da girman Allah wallahi tallahi idan kika sake wani a gidan nan yasan halin da kike ciki ko abinda muka aikata sai na kashe ki har lahira kada ki mance da girman alkawari kuma kin ɗauka duk runtsi babu wanda zai san tsakanin mu!!!

Mamaki,haushi, baƙin ciki da wani tsanar yaya mallam ne ya taso mun ban taɓa sanan mugu azzalumi bane sai yau,ai gwara ya hydar dukan abinda yake mun da dalili domin kare ni da martaba ta na dauke shi makiyi ashe shine babban masoyina...!,barina yaya Mallam yayi ya koma wurin su Mallam yana sanar dasu ai na su bacci,ya hydar ya taso tare da rik'o hanuwan yaya mallam yana nan gafara akan abubuwan daya aikata mashi cikin rashin sani a baya,daure wa yayi ya nuna mashi ai babu komi ya wuce haka dai taro ya watse a daren ranar yaya Mallam bai runtsa ba yaci kuka har ya gode Allah,haka nima duk da ƙarancin shekaru na wallahi na razana matuka da abinda muka aikata wanan banan suna da muka jawa zuri'ar mu har ina...?,Da safe babu yadda ba'a ba ya bari in kara murmurewa yace ai jarabawa zamu fara dama sallama yazo masu cikin satin zai wuce makaranta a Kaduna dama an gama komi rashin lafiyar ammie ya tsayar dashi....!,bata sona da kuka ta rabo dasu Mallam sai rarashin ta mama ladi take har suka iso kano bata saurara da kukan ba,ko gida bai bari mun  biya ba ya nufi wani Chemist ko ina ya san yaron mai chemist oho Amma da alama sun  yi sabo sosai"yace in zauna waje yana zuwa ya shiga cikin Chemist din ya ma yaronta bayani cewa a makarantar kwana nake wani mallami ya yaudare Ni yamun ciki kwata kwata shekaruna sha uku  saura wata biyu inda shiga sha hudu...!yaron da bazai wuce sa'ar yaya Mallam ba ya leko tare da ƙare mun kallo ya koma ciki naji yana  faɗin subhanallah amma ya cuce ta  wanan ai child abuse ne mesa ba'a sanar da hukumar makaranta ba ta dau mataki akai..?,yace ai zai zama tonon asiri a wurina da zuri'ar mu baki ɗaya shi yasa yake so a zubar kafin yayi kwari duka duka watannin cikin biyu ne bai shiga na uku ba..!,yace gaskiya wanan ƙaramar yarinya ce kuma aikin nada matuƙar hatsari da za kubi shawara na kawai a bari ta haife  abunta Allah kadai yasan baiwar da yayiwa abinda ke cikinta...hasala yaya Mallam yayi cikin daga murya yace kai Mallam ba wa'azi nazo kayi mun ba ina da ilimin addini sosai kuma na Fika sanin illar abinda za'a aikata idan ta rasa ranta sai me..?,da wanan abun kunya ai gwara ta rasa ranta kowa ma ya huta wallahi,, rik'o kafadan shi yayi abokina kayi hakuri ba nufi na bata maka rai ba ai Ni mai ma k'aunar mu aiki kyauta ne,yanzu dai bari zan baka list din wasu magunguna da zaka sayo da allurai Babu shi anan  shi kawai muke buƙata daka dawo zan mata aiki,godia yayi Tare Da amsar list din ya fita ko kallon inda nake bai yi ba...,mutumin yace in shigo mana in jira yaya Mallam har ya dawo nace tau,san wani farin kujera na zauna sai naga ya mayar da kofa ya rufe yana ƙoƙarin cire kaya...,nace lafia dai Mallam..?,yace haba yan kan wata nasan ke yar hannu ce testing zaki bani kafin ya'yan ki ya dawo yana ƙoƙarin cire wando ai ban san lokacin dana dauko wani karfe a gefena na kwada Mai a kai ba tare da bangaje shi na buɗe kofar da kyar domin bai faɗi ba yana rike dakai da hannu ɗaya yana lalibe ne har na samu nasarar buɗe kofar na rinƙa gudu ban san inda nake shiga ba mutane sai kallona suke ana kaucewa ana bani hanya da rana tsaka ban tsaya ko ina ba sai GIDAN KAZO NAZO wanda shima gudun ne ya isheni na wahala kuma naga alamar akwai mutane da dama a kofar gidan shi yasa na shiga don tsorata da rayuwata!!!

Kuka yaci karfin ta sosai hadda majina ta koma looo ta faɗa saman gadon tana birgima kamar wace ta kama farfadiya...!,alaji habu kuwa imagining irin zalunci na yaya Mallam da ƙarfin hali yake yi wai yar uwanka uwa ɗaya uba ɗaya kace koya mutu baka da asara,gwara ta mutu da ashirin ka ya ton!!!

Daga kwance tace alaji habu uwar masu gida ta amshe Ni hanu biyu duk da bata san daga ina mike ba kuma har yau ban sanar da ita labarina ba sanan bata taɓa matsamun sai na sanar da ita ba,na zauna a dakin ta duk da ta fuskanci inada ciki kuma tamun tayin azubar da cikin na nuna mata ina son haihuwar abuna dole ya zama izna ga mutane mutane masu mugun hali irin yaya Mallam,da kuma iyaye masu ƙarancin wayewar kai da ilimi  irin namu" taci gaba da kula dani har cikina yakai haihu na wahala sosai domin kwana nayi nakuda aka kaini asibiti sai da akamun ƙari da kyar na haihu inada shekaru sha hudu na haifi baby girl Mai Kama da yaya mallam sak hatta baƙin fatar ta nashi ne sai yanayin jikina data dauko,ranar suna ta nemi dana bada suna da nake so a saka nace baby,duk da tayi mamakin haka nasan bata son takura mun ne tana tausayi na yan daudu suka yanke tunkiya aka rada ma yarinya suna baby tun da na haife ta ban taba shayar da ita ba,ban taba daukar ta ba saboda tsabar baƙin ciki ko ganinta Bana son yi Uwar masu gida ta fida kuɗi aka sayo madaran jarirai dashi babu ta rayu wani kulawa da hidima nata uwar masu gida ce kuma nice na nuna mata inada sha'awar irin rayuwar da akeyi a gidan babu bata lokaci ta daurani akan hanya,lokacin da aka yi mata sarautar uwar masu gida ta koma baban d'aki tabar mana nata nida  baby shi yasa bani da kamarta har yau  alaji habu kaji labarina!


Ajiyar zuciya ya sauke tabbas ammie kinga babban jarabawa a rayuwar ki amma da lokacin da kika bar chemist din nan gidan Mallam saminu kika koma ko ƙauyen ku...

Katse shi tayi alaji bazan iya tunkarar kowa nawa da wanan babban abun kunyar ba na gwammace na ƙari rayuwata a bariki da iyayena kuji ko suga wanan mugun abu.

Amma ai ba laifin baby bane ƙaddara ce kamata yayi ki jata ajiki tunda har kika iya zama da cikinta na watanni tara kuma kika juri azaba wurin haihuwarta...

Kuka taci gaba dayi kada ka manta rabon haihuwarta yasa na baro mahaifata da iyayena da duk wani buri nawa nason yin karatu dole na bar shi shekaru goma kenan rabona na kowa nawa mai zai hana bazan tsane ta ba wallahi bazan taɓa son baby ba idan da nabi shawarar uwar masu gida ai da tuni na sheke ta ta zama tarihi!

Naji yanzu abinda nake so dake ki shirya mu tafi kauyen ku Ni da kaina zan sanar da iyayen ki komi nasan zasu fahimta shi kuma yaya mallam idan har yana raye wallahi wallahi wallahi nayi alkawarin yadda ya saka ki kuka ya zama sanadiyar lalacewa tarbiyar ki sai shima ya dandana kwatanta kwacin baƙin cikin da kika dandana!!!

~🅿13-14~




  🅱ayan iya tunkarar iyayena da wanan tashin hankali ba bayan shekaruna goma rabo na da su rana tsaka in Koma da baƙin labari...!

Ammie ya kamata ki fahimci inda maganar ta dosa dole ki koma gaban iyayen ki domin tabbatar da tsarkin ki kafin muyi aure wanda sai son nemi sanan dalilin da yasa kika bar su a baya wanda na tabbata yaya Mallam bazai iya sanar dasu ba.

Alaji habu wallahi bazan iya ba sai dai mu hakura da juna kawai naci gaba da barikin har Allah ya dauki rayuwata ta"taci gaba da kuka....

Ok na fahimta yanzu inaso zaki zauna a gidan nan tare da wata dattijuwa da zan haɗa ku har ki kamala istibra'i sanan a ɗauka mana aure amma da sharadin zamu tafi ga iyayen ki bayan mun yi aure a warware komi ki nemi gafarar su domin iyaye ba abun wasa bane kowa na buri ya rabu dasu lafia...,sanan zaki bani address na Mallam saminu zan tafi har gidan  domin neman yaya Mallam lokaci yayi da zai d'and'ana kud'ar shi!!!


Amma alaji ya za ayi da baby Allah yasan ba zan iya ci gaba da zama da ita ba cuz zan iya zama ajalin ta!...,ki kwantar da hankalin ki dana samu yaya Mallam baby zata bar wurin ki har abada lokaci yayi da zai d'and'ana kwatankwacin azaba,radadi da kunya da kika fuskanta a rayuwa.

Wanan ma shawara ce mai kyau Allah yasa a dace amin"yanzu kiyi wanka ki gyara jikin ki driver zai dawo tare da tsohuwar zata rinka zama dake abinci komi idan kina so zata maki Kuma zan baki kudade ki shiga kasuwa ki saya kayan sawa na mutunci da hijabai saboda komi bana so ki dauka a cikin kayan ki dake can gidan,baby taci gaba da zama tare da uwar masu gida har in kamala bin cike na...

Katse shi tayi uwar masu gida na iya kashe baby har lahira inaso zan tafi inyi sallama dasu daga nan zan dauko baby ta zauna nan har lokacin da zamu samu yaya Mallam "...hakan ma yayi,Nagode sosai alaji Allah ya kara budi Amin ya fita jiki a sanyaye!

Driver ta saka ya kai ta can gidan nasu wanda yake cike da mutane kamar kullum dakin uwar masu gida ta shiga ta sanar da ita alaji habu ya mallaka mata gidan kanta can zata koma da zama kafun ayi auren(amma bata sanar da ita yadda suka yi ba gudun tonon asirin ta),murna ta taya ta"yanzu ya za kiyi da Baby....?,wanan ba matsala bace yanzu da ita zan tafi in damka masu baushe A halaka ta kawai,ahm bazan dauki komi a cikin kayana na gidan nan ba sai gwala gwalai da na sayo a Dubai sauran kayan na bar maki halak malak domin kin mun komi a duniya uwar masu gida tana kuka ..!,bari kuka ammie zama na dake baki taɓa bata mun rai ba anyi zama na amana da daɗi babu daɗi kin kyautata mun lokaci ne yazo da zaki huta idan har kina da ra'ayin zaman aure yi daure ki rungumi auren ki domin na tabbata alaji habu masoyin kine na gaskia,koni da nike cikin wanan hali kaddarar rayuwa ya sanya Ni cikin wanan mumunan rayuwa ina fata ALLAH ya kawo mun karshe wanan zama...

Riko hanun uwar masu gida tayi dake faman zub da kwalla mai zai hana mu koma gidan alaji habu tare ki zauna dani har Allah ya kawo maki miji kiyi aure...?,Hakan ba zan yiwo ba ammie Allah ne ya kawo karshen zaman ki a wanan gida muma ki taya mu da adu'a kada Allah ya dauki ran mu cikin wanan kazama rayuwa...!,kuka suke tare da rungume juna can ammie tace shike nan uwar masu gida na shirya yin zan aure saboda Allah KEMA ina fatan Allah ya kawo maki karshen wanan rayuwa kuma idan kina da wani buƙata na rayuwa idan har baifi ƙarfi na ba kai tsaye ki sanar dani zan taimaka "suka ci gaba da tattaunawa har alert na 200k ya shigo wayanta tace zan shiga kasuwa ki shirya mu tafi tare daga nan ki duba gidan jiki ba kwari suka shiga d'akin ammie lokacin baby na zaune gefe ta rafka tagumi jiki na rawa ta mike sannu da zuwa ammie...!,uwar harara ta rafka mata tare wuce ta ,ƙaramin akwati mai dauke da gwala gwalai  sai ta buɗe akwatin kayanta  ta zabe wasu tsadadun  English Wears cikin tsaraban  Dubai (domin tasan alaji habu na mugun sun ƙanana  kaya kuma ba Lallai bane ta same su a nan Nigeria  irin wanda ta sayo ɗin),wani jakan fata ta dauko kome ke ciki oho mai ta haɗa dasu cikin kayan da take so da dai sauran abubuwa masu muhimmanci  ,,ta kali uwar masu gida"ga sauran kayan idan kun dawo ki zabi wanda kike so sauran kiba yaranki...,gyada kai tayi domin tana ganin abun kamar almara yau ammie zata tafi ta barta,ammie tace da baby"ke tashi muje idan kinga dama,jiki a sanyaye ta biyo bayan su ,uwar masu gida ta tara sauran yaran gidan wanda basu fita ba tayi masu bayani ammie zata bar gidan domin ta samu ci gaba a rayuwa yanzu ta mallaki gida na kanta...!,kowa sai taya ta murna yake Saboda zaman ta gidan bata shiga harkan da bai shafe ta ba tsakanin ta da kowa sai girmamawa da mutunci duk da kowa yawancin yaran gidan ta riga su zuwa har bakin mota suka rata ta,ta shiga gidan baya tare da uwar masu gida baby na gaba bayan an saka kayan a buth!

Bank suka fara biyawa ta cire adadin kuɗi da take da bukata suka nufi kasuwa,baby da driver na cikin mota sai tunani take ina zasu koma taji ana taya ammie murna ta bar gidan,ko yaya makomar karatun ta...?

Kaya suka jida tun sanan  atamfofi,less,shadda,yadi,material,hijab, manyan gyalen da sauran kayan bukata na mata m...,sanan taba uwar masu gida dama ta zaɓi abubuwan da take so suka biya shagon tela ta bada dunki sanan suka nufi gida dama alaji ya dame ta da waya ganin ta dad'e bata dawo ba kuma yana son komawa gida.

Tun daga harabar gidan da ya sha interlock uwar masu gida ta fara santin gidan har ciki babban palour ne mai dauke da kayan kallo na gani na fadi sai set na furnitures, da kuma d'akin bacci na baƙi a gefe, dianing area na  sai open kitchen idan an taka bene za'a haura ɗaku nan bacci biyu ko wanne da toilet barakallah,Yar dattijuwa da ba zata haura shekaru arba'in ba zaune saman carpet tana kwasan gaisuwa...,alaji ya nuna ammie ga matar gidan na ta dawo zata maki bayanin komi Ni zan tafi Allah ya baku HAKURIN zama suka amsa Amin!

Har harabar gidan ta rako shi tana sanye da ɗaya daga cikin hijab din da suka saya iya guiwa mai hannu ya matukar mata kyau,alaji habu na ƙara yaba kyau'n ta cikin zuciyar shi...!,wanan kallo da alaji..?

Murmushi yayi naga kinyi kyau ni"""zan koma gida ana jira na ga ba'baa mairo nan mace ce mai amana da gaskia duk da ban sanar da ita komi game dake ba a matsayin matata kike kamar yadda na faɗa mata auren ki zanyi yan uwanki ba nan suke ba sai ki kula ku zauna lafiya kada kiyi abinda zai saka ta zargi wani abu tsakanin ki da baby Ina so ki nuna mata kulawa har in kamala bincike da nake yi akan yaya Mallam insha Allah!

Nagode sosai insha Allah zanyi yadda kace bazan baka kunya ba kuma inaso driver ya koya mun mota saboda nafi ra'ayin tuki da kaina idan kaga babu damuwa...?,wanan ba matsala bane idan kin shirya sai ku fara zan mashi magana daga driver har Mai gadin gidan nan suka daga cikin aminta tun yara na da muka jima tare ki kwantar da hankalin ki,ok Allah ya tsare amin.


Ciki ta dawo suka ƙara gaisawa da ba'baa mairo tace kamar yadda alaji ya sanar dake zamu zauna tare INSHA Allah za kiji daɗin zama tare dani aikin ba wani mai yawa bane shara da girki ne sai wanke wanke wanda shima ba kullum ba tunda Ni abinci ba damuna yayi ba sai don ke,yarinya da mai gadi""wani kofa ta nuna mata  wanan shine dakin ki komi na ciki..,Nagode hajia insha Allah zaki same Ni mai kulawa da amana ta mike ta nufi d'akin,baby ta kala ga abinci can aje ki dauko plate a kitchen ki diba""sum sum ta Kama hanya,uwar masu gida tace ammie shike nan yanzu zaki bani kewa wallahi na saba dake ina jin daɗin zama dake...!,murmushi tayi rayuwa kenan uwar masu gida suka ci gaba da hira har yamma sanan uwar masu gida ta koma!

Da dare ammie tace Baby ta shiga dayan d'akin ta kwanta,ita kam tasha soyayya a waya da alaji habu sanan tayi shirin bacci amma ta kasa baccin sai tunano rayuwarta take wanda a ko wane lokaci take kallo kaman film,yau gashi Allah ya kawo ta wani matsayi na rayuwa wanda bata taɓa tunani zata taka ba, sanadin bariki Allah ya buda bata hanya na arziki,ko cikin wani hali iyayen ta suke oho...!.

A makare ta tashi domin bata yi bacci da wuri ba a hanzarce ta shiga wanka ta gabatar da sallar asuba wanda ta manta rabonta da yin shi,ta jima tana azkar kafin ta shirya cikin dogon riga ta fito palour ko ina tsaf an gyara sai kamshi ke tashi taji kwaramniya a kitchen ba'baa mairo ne tare da baby suna aiki gaisawa suka yi ta ""tace hajia me zaki karya dashi...?,kome ku ka yi ba'baa mairo banda Matsala,ta dawo palour ta zauna tare da kunna tv ta kamo islam channel Qira'a ke tashi daga bakin Abu bakr ash-shatri cikin suratul nisa'i volume ta ƙara ta runtsa idanu karatun na shigar ta a hankali taji wani natsuwa da kwanciyar hankali na shigar ta har bata san lokacin da bacci yayi gaba da ita ba""ganin haka ba'baa mairo ta shirya break fast din cikin foodflask saman dianing  ta gyara kitchen din,ta zuba ma baby nata suka koma d'akin ba'baa mairo suna karyawa....

Wayan Alaji ga ya tashe ta daga baccin dataa samu suka gaisa ya tambaya babu wani matsala dai ..?,tace Babu tana son zuwa saloon ne kawai,yace kada ta jima a can domin baya so tana yawan fita shima yanzu zai kama hanya zuwa dorayi gidan Mallam saminu""wani irin faduwan gaba taji ya ziyarce ta ta kashe wayan kawai,koda ta duba dianing chips and omelet ne sai ruwan shayi,bata wani ci da yawa ba saboda hankalinta a tashe yake ta haura sama ta watsa ruwa tare canja shigar ta ta sauko baby,ba...by!

Ma'am ta fito tare da ba'baa mairo shirya mu fita, ba'baa mairo tace Barka da tashi hajia..?,Barka dai ba'baa ya bak'unta..?,da sauki ai na samu abokiyar hira baby na ɗebe min kewa sai labarin makarantar su take bani..!,a razane ammie ta kalli baby tare da rike hannu ta sai sama ta bude dakin tare da jefata saman gado dan ubanki wani labari kike bata ..?
Tsiro tsuro tayi Allah ammie na schl ne kawai, rik'o kunenta tayi na rantse idan kika sake na samu labari kin sanar da ita wani abu Dangane da rayuwar mu sai na yanka ki shegiya wace bata san arziki ba ,cikin kayanta ta ciro wani hookie ta mika ma baby sanya wanan riga yanzu mu fita yar iska mai shegen baki ina jiranki palour,,,,jiki na rawa ta cire tsuman dake jikinta ya saka wanda ammie ta bata cikin ikon Allah ya mata chif har ƙasa ya kai mata ta fito da cinyayen silifas dinta"""ba'baa mairo tace yar gida na fita za'a yi a dawo lafiya,shiru tayi sanin halin ammie,,ai hajia me za'a dafa na rana..?,kome kike ra'ayi bakin ku dasu driver kada kisa damu bazan dawo da wuri ba,a dawo lafiya.

Gaskia sukayi da driver baby na gaba ta shiga gidan baya  suka nufi well care shopping mall  ta zaba ma baby kaya tsadadu masu kyau kusan kala goma English wears,veil,panties da kayan wanka na yara sai huluna da takalma uku ta dauki wani jakka madaidaici aka zuba ciki ta biya,,,,daga nan suka nufi alpen store ta kwashi kayan kwalliya nata da turaruka suka nufi gyara mata beuty saloon tace driver ya tafi idan sun gama zata kira shi"""gashin baby daya kai sati Uku bai samu kitso ba tace a tsefe kitson a wanke mata ayi wani,itama tsefe na attach akayi ta shiga dryer sai zuwa biyu suka gama alaji habu ya isheta da waya har yanzu bata dawo gida ba(haushi ya kamata domin kwata kwata 11:40am suka bar gidan amma ya isheta da waya),koda ta fito driver na waje suka biya shopRite ta saya kwalamar ta sanan suka dawo gida lokacin ba'baa mairo har ta gama tuwon semo da miyar kubewa na rana zuwa dare bayan sun gaisa tayi wanka ta gabatar da sallah ta kira baby ta kwashi kayanta sanan ta bata cake da ice cream ta dauki nata ta mika wa ba'baa mairo nata,bayan ta gama ne suka dawo palour duka suna kallo har la'asar kowa ta tashi zuwa Sallah bayan ta idar alaji habu yace zai shigo akan maganar yaya Mallam an jima""ai kuwa hankalinta bai ƙara kwanci ba sai tunani kala kala take fama dashi har yamma bata da sukuni,koda ya iso a harabar gidan suka zauna saman fararen kujeru tana sanye da dogon riga da hijab Baki.


Ammie kamar yadda na sanar dake zan tafi aunguwar sheka neman gidan Mallam saminu na tafi wanda da kyar na gano gidan saboda an canja mai fasali cikin ikon Allah na samu babban danshi kabiru,sanin kanki ne niba boyayen mutum bane a jahar kano da kewaye dalilin haka ya bani dama muka yi magana na fahimta kuma gaskia da gaskia....!, ɗago jajayen idanunta tayi wanda ya cika da hawaye tana kallon shi""kin fara ko,baki da aiki sai kuka kamar wace bata yadda da ƙaddara ba.,,,share hawayen tayi ta kwara dukar da kanta!

Ya faɗa mun kamar yadda ya samu labari wurin mahaifin shi lokacin da labarin mahaifiyar su ya riske shi  yana makaranta!,,kana nufin gwaggo larai ta rasu...?,ta rasu tafiyar ki bada jimawa ba Allah ya ɗauki ranta yanzu haka gidan daga kabiru sai matar shi,,kuka taci gaba dayi""ran shi ya ɓaci na faɗa maku bana son kuka ko in tafi ne ..?,girgiza kai tayi

Kabiru yace Mallam saminu ya bashi labari ranar da ku'ka dawo kano ganin yaya Mallam suka yi shi ɗaya,gwaggo larai take tambaya ya bata ganki ba ko jikin ne yasa baki dawo ba...?,yace ai ya sauke ki makaranta shike nan ba'a sake tayar da magana ba washe gari ya shirya ya wuce Kaduna inda ya samu gurbin karatu a makarantar hurar da sojoji shiru shiru babu labarin shi,kema babu labarin ki an bincika makaranta suka tabbatar baki dawo ba tun hutun da aka baki nan hankalin kowa ya tashi har ƙauye anata cigiyar ki shiru har gwaggo larai ta rasu, bayan ya samu wanan labari da kanshi musamman ya tafi kadunar ya sanar da yaya Mallam shima ya rufe hankalin shi ya tashi yana ta kuka gashi babu daman ya biyo shi saboda yanayin karatun nasu Kabiru ya amshi number da za'a samu yaya Mallam don sanar dashi halin da gida ke ciki ya dawo ya sanar da su Mallam komi aka ci gaba da rokon ALLAH lokacin kuma Mallam ya kamu da ciwon zuciya aka dawo dashi nan Kano babban asibiti bayan dogon jinya Rai yayi halin sa....

Wani irin ihu ta saki tare fadiwa ƙasa sumamiya,ba'baaa mairooooo ya kwala kira....
Ta fito a guje yace ta kawo ruwa da sauri,a hanzarce da dauko pure water aka zuba ma ammie tayi ajiyar zuciya idanunta shame shame da hawaye yace ba'baa mairo ta kamata zuwa ciki bata jin daɗi ne""rike ta tayi har zuwa daki tana mata sannu shi kuma ya zauna palour yaa ƙara tausaya mata ,,,tace da ba'baa mairo tace da alaji tana son ganin shi ..,jiki ba kwari ya shiga d'akin tana zaune saman gado ya nemi wuri nesa da ita ya zauna ""alaji da gaske mahaifina ya rasu...?,wallahi nasan ba komi ya kashe shi ba sai baƙin cikin nan da yaya Mallam ya kunsa mun har yayi sanadin barina daga gida...!,kada kice haka ammie lokacin shine yayi kowa baya wuce lokacin shi ki kwantar da hankalin kici gaba da yi masu adu'a kina istigfari domin nan rahamar Ubangiji Allah yasan dalilin da yasa ya ɗauki ran Mallam ba tare da yaga wanan babban abun kunyan ba a duniya,na samu labari mama ladi ta koma kauyen su,ita kuma umma hydar da matar shi suna kula da ita sosai,yanzu maganar nan da nake miki Kabir ya tabbatar mun da yaya Mallam na nan zuwa garin nan cikin satin nan domin ya kamala karatu har anyi posting din shi Lagos sai dai har yanzu bai yi aure ba ko ƙauyen ma baya son zuwa,,na bashi number waya ya tabbatar zai kira Ni idan yaya Mallam ya iso zai kira Ni a wanan lokaci zan aiwatar da nufi na akan yaya Mallam.

Shiru tayi tana gyada kai ki zauna cikin shiri ko wane lokaci zan iya kiran ki,gyada kai tayi kawai ranar ko kofar d'aki bata fito ba har garin Allah ya waye, ba'baa mairo tahuta zuwa dubawa d'akin a rufe yake""baƙin cikin ammie ɗaya yadda mahaifinta ya rasu ba tare da ta nemi gafarar shi ba duk da mata da burin sanar da kowa nata abinda ya shiga tsakanin ta da yaya mallam!


Bayan kwana alaji yayi mata waya akan ta shirya  ita da baby da dare zai aiwatar da kudirin shi,cike da faduwar gaba ta yini da dare da kanta ta shirya ma baby kayanta cikin jakan data saya mata ta sallami ba'baa mairo akan zata mayar da baby  gidan iyayen ta dama  hutu tazo yi wurin ta...,tace Allah sarki baby yarinyar kirki mun saba sai wani lokaci,da suka fi tace ammie ina zaki kai Ni,don Allah kada ki mayar dani gidan can ina son zama tare dake na hakura da karatun idan saboda haka zaki mayar dani wurin Uwar masu gida...!,tsawa ta daka mata keeee bana son suturun banza gidan uban ki zan mayar dake nasan za kiyi farin ciki da hakan ko ..?,shiru tai kawai har alaji habu ya iso cikin 406 din shi navy blue Mai baƙin glass shi da kanshi ke driving har unguwar sheka wanda ko Shekara ɗari tayi ba zata manta da wanan hanya ba duk da gidan samu gyare gyare sosai suna Parker was ta fara kuka yace zaki fara ko...?,U have to be strong plz,gyada kai tayi ya daga waya ya kira kabiru ai kuwa tare da yaya Mallam suka fito duk da babu wadatacen haske a kofar gidan bai hana ta gane yaya Mallam da ya Kabir ba ,yaya Mallam sanye cikin shirt da jeans trouser har wani shining yake yi saboda hutu kwanciyar hankali"""alaji ya fito rike da hanun baby,bayan sun gaisa ya kalli yaya Mallam wanda ko ba'a faɗi ba yasan cikin mahaifin baby Koma yayan ammie...!,daure wa yayi ya kira ainihin sunan shi;,Aminu wurin ka nazo domin kusan shekaru goma kenan ina nan ka Allah bai ƙaddara saduwar mu ba sai yau nasan ba zaka mance yarinyar daka yaudara shekaru goma da suka wuce ba ka dirka mata ciki a wanan gidan ka gudu ka barta,ya Turo mashi baby wanan itace ajiyar ka na wanan shekaru goman an haifa maka ita,an raine ta cikin kulawa yau kulawarsa zai dawo karkashin ka idan kaga dama ka sauke hakkin dake kanka,idan kaga dama ka kashe ta kamar yadda kayi niyyar a farko ruwan ka,mota ya bude ya dauko jakar baby ya she gaban su baby ga papan ki nan yau Allah ya nufa zaki gana dashi daga yau kulawar  ki ya dawo karkashin shi...!,tunda yake bayani jikin yaya Mallam rawa yake kamar mazari burin shi ɗaya kada wanan mutumin ya tona mai asiri a duniya gaban kabir,,,na gama nawa cewar alaji habu ya juya wurin mota ya rankwafo dai dai wurin ammie yace kina da magana ne..?,kuka ta fashe dashi tana Kada kai ya shiga ya ja motar....
