      A guje Alaji habu yaja motar yana sharara gudu saboda yadda ranshi ya ɓaci akan abinda ammie tayi sun shirya itace zata damk'a wa yaya Mallam baby amma ta ƙasa sai wani kukan banza da take damun shi dashi tunda sauka isa wurin har suka isa gida bai ce da ita k'ala ba haka itama ta fita ko sallama ba suyi ba ta shige wanta ciki ranta a ɓace bata ko saurari ba'baa mairo dake mata sannu da zuwa ba direct tun nata ta wuce ta faɗa saman gado tana dirzan kuka mai tsuma zuciya saboda ganin yaya Mallam ayau ya tuno mata da abubuwa da dama wanda take kokarin shafe su cikin babin rayuwar ta,wani irin tsanar shi ta ƙara ji yana shiga ta wanda tayi imani ko wuk'a aka bata a wurin tana iya kashe yaya Mallam har lahira idan har hakan zai rage radadi da take ji cikin zuciyarta wanda shine sila rugujewan rayuwarta!!!


Kabir ke tambayar yaya Mallam da gaske ne maganganun da wanan alajin ya fadi yanzu...?,wace yarinya ce yayi wa ciki shekaru goma da suka wuce...?,ashe akwai sirrin shi da zai boye mashi a matsayin shi da dan uwa kuma aminin shi...?

Cikin kakkausar murya irin nasu na sojoji yace"luk KB wanan abu ƙaddara ce wanda bai wuce fadawa akan ko wane ɗan adam Allah yasan wanan abu Daya faru yana cikin kadarar rayuwata bazan so kowa yasan wanan sirrin ba gudun tashin hankali da zubewar mutuncin gidan mu shi yasa na boye ka gafarce ni yanzu haka zan kai wanan yarinya ajiya ne wani wuri har in gama nazari akan ta.....

Katse shi yayi baka da wata hujja na boye mun wanan ƙaddara idan har a matsayin kadarra ka ɗauke shi,gwara tun lokaci bai kure ba ka koma gida ka nemi gafarar iyayen ka idan ma saboda shine kayi kaura baka zuwa kumbotso tun lokaci bai kure ba ka sanar dasu abinda kake boyewa domin tabbas jiki na yana bani akwai wani babban al'amari game da wanan yarinya da aka kawo,mallam ya rasu yana mai baƙin cikin rashin ammie wacce har yau bamu san tana a mace ko tana raye bane,ga baƙin cikin kashin ka a kusa dashi wanda sai bayan rasuwar shi kaje kayi kwana biyu har yau baka sake taka ƙafar ka ba,kenan idan hydar bashi da rai haka zaka bar mama ladi da umma su wulakanta a duniya..?,Wallahi ban baka shawarar kai wanan yarinya gidan kowa ba!!

Luk KB Bana son shishigi cikin lalura na wallahi da nasan dalilin kiran kenan da bazan taɓa tasowa daga Lagos zuwa Kano ba,ga ayyuka can suna jira na akan banzan maganar ka na bata lokaci a banza,iyayen ka ko nawa..?,ce maka nayi bana kyautata masu...?,sirri ne bazan sanar daki ko menene ba,yarinya kuwa babu wanda ya isa ya hana Ni kai ta inda nike so domin jini tace sai anjima Mallam!!!

Aminu..!,Aminu...!!,Ami....nu!!!

Ko waige yana rike da hanun baby,hannu ɗaya rike da jakarta wacce duk hiran da suke a kunnen ta abunka da mai ƙaramin ƙwaƙwalwa ta rasa gane inda maganar nasu ya dosa kenan wannan shine papan ta wanda ya haife ta...?,gashi ammie tace ba itace maman ta ba,kenan wacece maman ta..?

Adaidaita suka shiga taji yace Mallam nan kurna zaka kai mu,adaidaita yaja suka kama hanya jugum har suka isa lungu lungu ne sai kwata har bakin wani gida ya biya suka fito waya ya shiru daga aljihun wandon shi yayi dianing wani number "yes Ina kofar gida sai ya kashe....,wani mutum siriri fari tasa mai ƙaramin jiki ya fito har ƙasa ya kai yana gaida yaya Mallam da harshen turanci yace ya tashi mana ya bashi hannu suka gaisa"ya koma harshen yar banci suka ci gaba da magana yana nuna baby da alama akanta suke magana(Ni dai ban gane komi ba don baji nake ba Lol),sai gyda kai mutumin da aka kira da taufeeq yake yi naga yaya Mallam ya ciro bandir na ɗari biyar biyar ya bashi suka yi sallama ko kallon baby Yaya Mallam bai yiba ya juya fuskan nan a murtuk'e ya wuce...!,mutumin ya amshi jakar baby tare da rik'o hanunta suka shiga ciki.

Gida ne wanda da gani na haya ne d'akuna sun kai biyar ciki a jere tsakar gidan duk ya fafashe babu haske a tsakar gida kowa na sha'anin gaban shi Da alama mazauna gidan ba yan gari bane aiki ya kawo su ,tafiya suka yi har kifar wani daki madaidaici ya shiga da sallama wata mace mai kimanin shekaru ashirin da biyar k'atuwa ta amsa da fara'a amma ganin shi riƙe da yarinya yasa ta canza fuska,babu komi cikin dakin sai katifa madaidaici da akwatuna uku na kayan su"ya zaunar da baby a gefen katifan kayanta na gefe,matan ta mike tsaye tana masifa da harshen yar banci,naji mijin ya kirata da shukura cikin natsuwa yana mata bayani da yar banci tare da zaro kuɗin da yaya Mallam ya bashi yana nuna mata,ai kuwa ta warce kuɗin tana yashan haƙora a dole taga kuɗi...!,kallon baby tayi omoge yaya sunan ka...?,ta amsa da baby,ah Baby yaro mai chau papan ka tace zaka zauna damu for a while kafun tazo hutu dama Ni bata haifu ba ina so yaro sosai from now on call me mama me,shey u understand English..?tace yes mama me,,Gud girl"mijin da ya fita jin Hausa yace baby ki kwantar da hankalin ki papan ki zai koma wurin aiki gobe zaki zauna damu ki ɗauke mu kaman iyayen ki nida matata shukura Yoruba wan osun ne tare da papan ki muka yi training a Kaduna da yake Ni police ne kuma kurtu aka ajeni Ni nan Kano ina gadin gidan senata night duty Nike yi yanzu zan wuce aiki idan kina son WANI abu ki fada ma shukura...,,tace tau kawai !
Shirya wa yayi a hanzarce ya musu sallama ya tafi,shukura ko an samu duniya sai rawa take tana raira waka da Yoruba nci tana kirga kuɗin wanda dubu hamsin ne chif"kallon baby tayi u wan  take ur bath ne..?
tace no,kayi beshi yanzu zan dawo tana daura zani saman skin tight trouser dake jikin ta da wani bingileliyar riga kai yasha kitson attach ta fita da kudin....,kallon d'akin baby ta k'ara Yi wayam daga ledar ƙasa sai katifa  da fankan sama tayi  imani ko d'akin su na tukuntawa yafi wanan sosai gefen katifan ta kwanta tare da rumtse idanu tana tunanin rayuwar ta shike nan haka zata ƙare daga wanan gidan zuwa wan can gidan..?,menene tayi wa iyayenta da basa son ta shin wacece ammie a wurin ta...?,shi kuma papan ta menene dalilin shi na daure mata fuska hada kawo ajiyar ta kada dai shima banzatar da ita zai yi kamar yadda uwarta ta batta hanun ammie...?

Alamar buɗe kofa taji da muryan shukura mai kama da gangan tsafi;,,baby u don sleep..?,shiru taji ba amsa ta kuna wutan d'akin tare da bajewa a ƙasa ta bude ledan kazan ta da babban kwalin hollandia youghurt apple flavour ta shiga yaga tana korawa da hollandia tana wake waken ta har ta cinye tas ta shiga latsar waya"baby dai na kallon ta da gefen ido har bata san lokacin da bacci yayi awon gaba da ita sai da taji bugun kofa da asuba ashe taufeeq mijin shukura ne ya dawo daga duty da kyar shukura ta buɗe tana masifa yake tambaya tayi sallah kuwa...?,tsaki taja ta koma bacci bubuga baby yayi ta tashi tayi sallah,babu musu ta tashi ya bata buta tana tambaya ina toilet tayi tsarki yace tayi a poo  kawai (ashe gidan babu toilet  kowaa leda ko poo take bayan gida,sai bathroom guda ɗaya wanda aka zagaye da kwano tun da asuba ake layin wanka a gidan),tana gama alwala ta dawo ciki hijab din ta sabo ta fitar cikin kayan da ammie ta saya mata ya shinfida mata sallaya ta gabatar da sallah,bayan ta gama ne ta jima tana adu'oi kamar yadda ta saba har ta kammala tayi zugum wurin..., taufeeq ne yake ta dawo ta kwanta mana..!,tace bata jin Bacci,gashi yana son kwanciya da matar shi babu hali daure wa yayi ya koma Masallaci dake wajen gidan ya kwanta nan da nan Bacci ya kwashe shi...

Baby na zaune har shukura ta tashi bacci tace da baby ta duba kofar d'akin akwai kwanoni da tukunya ta ɗauki bucket rijiya na kofar gida ta debo ruwa ta wanke mata zata haɗa breakfast,Babu musu baby ta tashi da babban bucket na karfe sai a lokacin ta ƙara ganin gidan da kyau akwai ma yara ashe,kowa nata zirga zirgan shi wata yarinya da ba zata wuce sa'ar taba  daga ita sai pant Babu  takalmi ko ɗan kwali a kanta dana  tsufa ko tsagar kitso baka iya gani sai wasa da kasa take;baby tace don Allah ina ake diban ruwa..?,ta kalli baby sama da ƙasa  tana murguda baki,ke kuma daga ina..?,tace jiya papa na ya kawo Ni wurin su aunty shukura zan zauna har ya samu hutu....,katse baby tayi chaf duk faɗin garin nan ya rasa wurin wanda zai kawo ki sai muguwan matar nan shukura...?,lalai na tausaya maki yarinya muje dai ba don halinta ba,shiru baby tayi yarinya nata zuba tana faɗa mata halayen shukura wacce har dambe tana yi da mijinta salihi wanda babu ruwan shi da shiga tsabgar kowa,gata da gulma,karya kwadayi da son abun duniya...!,har suka isa rigiyar kwakwara safiya bata bar zuba ba ashe kowa da guga yake zuwa basu zo dashi ba da yake yan aunguwar sun san rashin kunyar safiya aka ki ara masu guga sai da baby ta ruki wani mutum ya bata da sauri ta cika aka daura ta suka kama hanya tana ci gaba da bata labarin yan gidan nan ta fahimci ƙabila uku ke gidan(baban su   safiya ba kane ne kuma shine mai gidan rasuwa yayi,matar shi abu banufa ce yaranta  hudu yan mata biyu sai namiji da safiya sune masu gidan,aunty grace wace yar makarnta ce tana zaman kanta,maman Daniel itama zaman kanta take da yaronta ɗaya sai su taufeeq), har suka iso gidan ta samu maman daniel,aunty shukura da yaya abu (maman su safiya) zaune tsakar gidan suna gulman aunty grace,tsaki Safiyya taja tana faɗin daga gani guntu ya sauka..!,baby tace menene guntu kuma..?,gulma mana ai da kin ga sun taru kofar abu(maman su) guntu ya sauka munafukai kawai"abu tace ke Safiya ina kika tafi tunda safe dan uwanki ya wuce makaranta ..?,baby na raka rijiya,tace oho (dama shukura ta basu labarin baby),zaman da akayi wurin zugata ake kada ta samar ma baby fuska taci ubanta ita bada haihuwa ba akawo mata raino waya sani ko shegiya ce...

Tare da safiyya suka yi wanke wanke tana zuba mata surutai suka gama ta share dakin ta gyara sai ga taufeeq ya shigo ta gaishe shi,ya tambaya taci abinci kuwa..?,tace a'a..

Shukura ya kwala wa kira Wacce ke zaune rashe rashe suna gulma,tsaki taja tana murguda baki har ta iso tayi tsaye a kan shi,da turanci ya tambaye ta ina abun kari..?,tace bata yi ba!

Bai damu da kuɗaɗen dake wurinta ba ya dauki jug ya nufi wurin mai shayi ya sayo da bread suka yi breakfast lokacin 9:30am yace baby ta shiga tayi wanka,babu musu ta ɗauki bucket ta koma rijiyar ta debo ruwa ta zo tayi wanka (dama da komi nata tazo),ta shirya cikin wani dogon riga yar kanti...

Yace da baby primary nawa kika tsaya..?,tace 4 zata shiga,yace ok yanzu yasan ba za'a dauke ta ba sai da transfer daga schl din data fara ko wani evidence da zai nuna ta taɓa karatu a wani wurin amma zai waya da oga aminu (yaya Mallam),yaji yana dadana waya can ya amsa cike da girmamawa yana mashi bayani da turanci,sai cewa yaya Mallam yayi ai ba makaranta yace su saka ta ba don haka babu ruwan su da karatun ta ya kashe wayan!

 shukura da tunda yake wayan take kallon shi daɗi ya cika ta domin ta samu helper ,jiki a sanyaye ya kashe wayan amma yasan tabbats something so wrong some where...

Fita yayi zuwa yan buga buga shi,yana fita shukura ta saka baby dahuwar shinkafa da murhun gawayi (charcoal),duk da bata taɓa girki ba amma zaman ta da ba'baa mairo tana kallon yadda take abubuwa yasa ta gane komi cikin natsuwa da taimakon Allah ta dafa white rice Dama shukura nada stew aka diga mata ɗan kaɗan a plate ta lashe,zuwa karfe biyu taufeeq ya dawo yaci abinci ya kwanta..

Bayan baby tayi sallah ta nufi wurin Safiyya dake shirin fita tallan Pure water lokaci duk yaran gidan sun dawo daga makaranta (babban budurwan mai shekaru sha shida sunan ta amina jss 3 take,sai fati mai shekaru sha uku jss1A,Ibro mai shekaru sha biyu primary 6,sai Safiyya mai shekaru goma wace ke primary 3 amma sai taga dama take zuwa),ganin tayi duka sun shirya Amina da  fati   sun dauki tallan dafafan gyada,Ibro da Safiyya kuma Pure water zasu fita,tace Safiyya ina zaku..?,ahhh baby cikin babban makaranta talla(BAYERO UNIVERSITY KANO),yanzu sai yaushe zaku dawo..?,sai yamma suka fita abun su....

Zama tayi gefe shiru tana tunanin ammie domin tayi matukar kewar ta .


Tunda ammie ta dawo gida take fama da zazzabi na kewar baby (bahaushe yace sabo turkin wawa),ita kam bata yadda kewar baby ya saka ta zazzaɓi ba illah tunawa da rasuwar mahaifinta ta kusan sati ɗaya tana fama da zazzabi hada ƙarin ruwa ba'baa mairo ke kula da ita yan gidan can nasu duk sun zo duba ta Musamman uwar masu gida nan take yini har ta warware alaji habu ya saki bakin aljihun shi sosai.

Yaya Mallam tunda ya koma hankalin shi ya kasa kwanciya babu abinda yake tunani sai cin mutunci da tonon asiri da ammie ta jawo mashi,ya rasa gane menene alakar ta da babban mutum kamar alaji habu har yake bugun gaba da zai kare mata hakkin ta idan har yana raye,yana barazanar ruguza farin cikin shi ta hanyar dawo da abinda ammie ta haifa hanun shi"ashe labarin da mai chemist ya Sanar dashi lokacin daya dawo sayo magani gaskiya ne wai ammie ta zazzage shi yayi kokarin dakatar da ita ta kwada mai karfe ta gudu,ashe da gangan tayi don ta Saɓa alkawarin da suka ɗauka a farko...???

Ya dau alwashin sai ya mayar da rayuwar baby abun tausayi a duniya sai ammie ta gwamaci zubar da cin nan tayi da haihuwar baby!!!

Ya abu ba wai karatun boko tayi ba amma zama  bariki yasa ta na jin turanci kuma tana iya juya harshe,kema shukura kina da matsala me akayi akayi wata baby da har zata hana ki jin daɗi rayuwar ki ko taufeeq dake auren ki bai  zama matsala ba bare wata baby"kawo kunne kiji wani plan da nake dashi akan baby,kuskus suka yi,shukura ta shek'e da dariya tana faɗin "oshe wo abu..!,oshe wo abu..!! ,Suka tafa  ta hade  girar sama da ƙasa tare da faɗin"1,2,3 Acton Baby........

Shukura ta leka d'akin common ka fita kazo ka debo ruwa a rijiya yanzu..!,,jiki a sabule ta fito tare da ɗaukan bucket na karfe ta nufi rijiya tana sharar kwala haka taje mutane sun yi yawa wurin saboda lokacin rani ne sauran rijiyoyi na kusa sun kafe dole sai an jira ruwan ya taru a rijiyar kowa ya saka guga ya diba gashi bata da guga,gefe ta zauna tayi tagumi tana kallon kowa yayi ready suna kira asaka kowa ya diba...

A sabule ta fito Safiyya ta rike hanunta tana nuna mata shukura da ta fito cikin kwaliyya tana rufe daki haka ta wuce su cikin jeans trouser da hookie shirt rike da purse kai babu dankwali fuskan  ta yasha make up sai kamshi take zuba wa ta wuce su sai nuna ta ake cikin gidan ana gulma...

Safiyya tace yanzu haka wurin ɗan iskan nan Bobby zata ko..?,baby tace Ni ban san wani Bobby ba..!,oh na manta ai wani saurayin shukura ne tunda suka dawo gidan nan idan mijin ta ya tafi duty na dare Bobby yazo su kwana tare ko taje dakin shi...

Baby ta katse ta shi Bobby wane ne..?,wani ɗan makaranta ne ya kama haya nan kusa damu shine suke soyayya da kuma taufeeq ya sani amma baya magana,zan faɗa maki amanatul amana wata rana da dare dani da Ibro muka leka ta window shukura muka ganta tsirara da Bobby ya hau saman jikinta yana mata wani abu tana ihu tana jin daɗi wai shine Ibro yace ai cinta yake yi(🙆🏼😳🙈)

Jikin baby yayi sanyi,Safiyya tace ni dai amana na baki idan kika faɗa ma taufeeq wuta bal bal Kuma sai kin biya Ni tuwon da kika ci yau...!, ƙaramin tsaki baby taja ta Koma ciki shiru tana tunanin rayuwa shike nan ita kuma haka rayuwar ta zai ci gaba da tafiya babu wani ci gaba..?,daga wanan bala'i sai wanan!!

Yaran gidan ko su Amina da fati suna kofar gida wurin hira da samarin su,Ibro da Safiyya kuma suna ta window maman Daniel suna leken wani romantic nollywood Film da take kallo mai cike da Shashanci har ta hango su ta rufe window ta,suka koma kofar gida suka leken yankin su dake makure jikin gini maza na lagudan jikin su cikin duhu sai zuwa karshe goma sha ɗaya suka shigo gida lokacin su Safiyya suka dawo ciki da gudu ko wane  ya shige d'akin nan da shukura tace baby ta Koma ashe nan yaran ke kwana saboda ya abu tayi wani aure bazawarin ta na zuwa mata kwana,,fati ta kunna wayar ta lenovo kirar China ta gada fitina nan taga baby kwance inda take kwanciya dundu ta dirka wa baby" a zabure ta mike waya kawo ki d'akin nan..?,Safiyya tace ai ya abu(maman su)tace ta dawo nan da kwana,oh ta matsa can gefen Ibro akwai wuri mana amma ta kwanta wurina,Safiyya tace Baby Koma gefen can akwai wuri nan katifa muke sawa Ibro yana fitsarin kwance shine yake kwanci ya saman tabarma ta shek'e da dariya;,baby ta mirgina gefe dama ba jin daɗin baccin take ba saboda bashi da banbanci da kwanciya saman tsakuwa..

Suka shinfida yaleluwar katifan da kodaden zuwa gadon suka kwanta su fati da Amina na ci gaba da hira da samarin su a waya,Safiyya sarkin bacci har tayi bacci abunta,gefe kuma baby ta sa kanta wurin kafafun ibro tana ta tunani bacci yaƙi zuwa can taji Ibro ya daura mata ƙafafuwan shi a kanta sai ta ƙara matsawa gefe can taji shaaaaaa"ya zuba fitsari wurin fuskarta haushi ne ya kamata ta koma gefe ta zauna tana sharar kwalla har asuba bata samu bacci ba...

Cikin su babu wanda ya tashi yayi sallah ta fito bucket ta gani da guga a gefe na ya abu ta dauka ta nufi rijiya cikin Sa'a babu taro da yawa ta dibo bucket ɗaya ta shiga wanka ta daura alwala tayi sallah tana zaune gefe har ta fara jin hayaniyar mutane alamar kowa ya tashi yana harkokin gaban shi haka Safiyya ce ta bata ruwan koko tasha!

Sai zuwa karfe goma shukura ta dawo ta bude daki ta kwalo ma baby Kira,da sauri ta isa d'akin ta gaishe da shukura,,a wulakan ce ta amsa tace tayi saurin haɗa wuta a murhun gawayi ta girka ma Bobby abinci zai zo amsa idan ya dawo schl ,ta amsa da tau cike da kasala ta haɗa wutan ta shiga gyara kayan abinci gashi Babu isashe ruwa a gidan haka ta rinka wahala har ta kamala ta zuba a foodflask masu kyau wanda ko taufeeq bata taɓa ganin an bashi abinci a ciki ba,wani ikon Allah don tsabar zalunci irin na shukura ko dandano bata  bari baby tayi ba tace da baby ta dauki babban kula ta bi Safiyya wani gidan ruwa kusa ta dauko leda biyu masu sanyi ta bisu talla...!,a razane baby ta kalle ta mama me,ban taba zuwa talla ba tunda aka haife Ni don Allah kiyo hakuri ki bani ko wane irin aiki ne banda talla..!,wani mahaukacin mari ta wanke baby dashi tana huci baby b'ata lokaci da ɗauki kulan ta fice da gudu tana kuka sai da fitsari ya zubo mata a wando...

Safiyya ta kalle ta menene kuma..?,cikin kuka ta faɗa mata sai data gama kwasar dariyar ta tace nasan wacce ta bada wanan shawara wallahi ya abu ce hegiya maman mu,amma baby zaki iya kuwa..?,wurin da muke zuwa talla akwai nisa fa Kuma masu ruwa sun yi yawa wani lokaci Ni kam ɗakunan samari(Male hostel) nake kai taka su samun hannun a wando(vaginal) a biyani kuɗin ruwan,saboda wanda bai sayar ba uhmmn ranar ya abu sai ta kusan kashe ka da duka ga rashin abinci, shine ko su fati ai suna kai gyada d'akin maza ayi sadaka a biya kuɗin..!,shiru ya biyo baya Safiyya tace jira in SAKA riga mu fita tare.

Gidan ruwa suka fara zuwa suka ɗauki ruwan leda biyu aka zuba ma baby a kula amma kamar an daura mata buhun shinkafa saboda bata saba daukan abu mai nauyi ba sai wurin shukura tafiya mai nisa ya kai su Bayero University baby sai nishi take sama sama saboda wahala Safiyya sai dariya take shek'e wa dashi domin ita sun saba ai kuwa direct male hostel ta nufa ,baby kam dama tace a sauke ta wurin gate ta aje kulan ta zauna tana hutawa,zuwa minti ashirin Safiyya ta fito da kula Empty Babu ruwa tana ya mutsa fuska Baby tace menene kuma ina ruwan..?

Ke dai wani gabjejen kato ya saye ruwan ya shigar sani d'akin su sai zungurawa mun abun fitsarin shi yake a gabana ina jin ciwo amma bai bari ba shege sai da yaga dama ya bani kuɗin"lokacin babu ta tuno da abinda head Master ya taba mata akan kuɗin exams Nan take ta fara hawaye gudu suka ji daga bayan su security ya biyo wasu almajirai da suka hauro ta katanga suka shigo suna wa ɗalibai sata shine aka biyo su wani ya faɗa kan kular ruwan baby ki make tas tas ya tatake su shima security daya biyo almajirin ya ƙara take sauran ruwan ji kake tamm ya zame kasa ai kuwa a guje Safiyya taja hanun baby suka bar makarantar domin tasan idan har ya kama su kashin su ya bushe sai da suka yi nisa baby tace gaskiya Ni komawa sanyi in ɗauko kulan mutane gashi an faɗa ruwan ko ɗaya ban sayar ba me zan ce da shukura na shiga Uku ta fara kuka...

Wallahi idan kika koma babu ruwana kulle ki zai a dakin masu gadi sai gobe ki dawo gida gwara kiyo hakuri muje ana shukura hakuri kawai,,shiru tayi na wani ɗan lokaci suka ci gaba da tafiya har gida tana kuka suna isa Safiyya tace "asiri mato nike in ka gane da kai nike guntu ya sauka",dama matan gidan da sun ji haka sun gane me ake nufi wani gulma mai zafi aka samo kowa ya halara ana sauraron Safiyya,ta kali shukura wallahi sai kin biya zan sauke wanan guntun idan ba haka ba shike nan kuma guntun ya shaafi kowa cikin gidan nan..!,sanin halin Safiyya ba zata taɓa fadi ba sai an biya yasa shukura ta bata naira hamsin,tace saura maman daniel,aunty grace da ya abu domin ba'a kyauta zan sauke wanan guntun ba...!,tsaki suka ja kowa na don jin gulma kuma basu son bada kuɗi dole suka biya hadda uwarta nan take ta zayane masu abinda ya faru da baby a makaranta...

Bata ida ba shukura ta kurma ihu a dole tayi asarar kula da ɗari biyu na ruwa ta kamo baby dake rak'ube a zaure ta shiga kwallo da ita har kofar d'akin su tana duka gamu da kafa tana zage zage ruban barkono ta bude ta kamo hanun baby ta daga pillow sai ga sabon razor baby na shure shure tana ihu shukura ta tsatsaga mata yatsun hannu ta zuba dake barko nu tana ƙara kallo da ita a a gigice ta balle dakatar dakin tana ihun kiran ammie ammie ammie zan mutu kizo ki tafi dani wallahi ba zan iya zama a nan ba zata kashe Ni na tuba Bata san inda take saka kafarta ba har tabar cikin gidan aguje tana ratsa lunguna...


 """Hankalin ammie ya kwanta yanzu bata da wata Matsala sai taci ta kwanta sai sallah suna zama lafiya da ba'baa mairo itace ke daɓe mata kewar baby idan ka ga yadda ta koma ta ƙara kiba da haske alamar biyu ya nuna a jikin ta,uwar masu gida na kawo mata ziyara akai akai ta koma da alheri sosai..,shima alaji habu ba'a barshi a baya ba yana kula da dukkan wasu bukatun ammie baya yawan zuwa gidan saboda zuciyar shi na raya mashi abubuwa da dama baya iya ɗauke idanun shi a kanta idan har ya biyewa son zuciya  zai iya afka mata a koma gidan juya shi yasa bai damu da zuwa gidan ba sai waya.


""A Lagos kuwa yaya Mallam ya gama haɗa dukan wani plan akan baby so yake aiki ya rage mashi ya aiwatar da abinda yayi niyya akan baby,da zaran ya samu Lokaci cikin satin nan zai shiga kano"waya ya daga ya kira taufeeq bayan sun gaisa yake shaida mashi idan ya samu dama zai shigo kano,yace shima yana wurin wani aiki sai karshen sati zai koma kano suka yi sallama.



~OH JAMA'A KO MENENE KUDIRIN YAYA MALLAM AKAN BABY???~


_Halimatu Sadiya muhammed_

~🅿19-20~


   Gudu sosai baby keyi bata san inda take jefa ƙafafuwan ta ba sai saman titi ji kake k'eeeeeeee an take burki...!,wata mace ta fito tana faɗin subhanallah ta nufi baby a hanzarce yarinya daga ina haka,baki ji ciwo ba ko...?,a razane baby KE juye juye nan ta gane  baby ba cikin natsuwa take ba ga hanunta da aka kama aka tsatsaga da razor sai zugi yake mata ta koma kamar sabuwar kamun hauka rungume ta matar tayi tana shafa bayanta a hankali tana tofa mata adu'oi har ta natsu,mutane sai kallon su ake ta rik'o ɗaya'n hanun mai lAFIA ta shiga da ita mota suke nufi nearby private clinic ta tambayi health recorder Ina doctor..?,yace sai sun saya card 500 naira kafin suga nurse on duty, Babu musu ta cire 1k ta bayar aka tambayi baby sunan ta..?,tace Baby kawai,matan ta ce rubuta haka nan ta bada shekaru da address wanda taga ya dace aka nuna masu wani office wata nurse ce zaune  tana aiki suka gaisa ta nuna mata hanun baby tana mata bayanin tsautsayi ya ritsa da ita tazo giftawa Allah yasa dai bata ji rauni ba sai wanda ke hanunta wanda bata san yadda ta same shi ba...!


Kallon baby tayi da kyau ta duba hanun tace wanan wane irin zalunci ne yanzu haka ba wurin iyayen ta take ba ko yarinya..?,gyada kai tayi tana faɗin papaw na ya kai Ni gidan aunty shukura ya wuce Lagos shine dana fasa mata ruwa da kula tamun haka,nurse ɗin ta kalli matar" hajia kinji ko na sani wanda yasan zafin haihuwa bazai taɓa wulakanta dan wani ba yanzu bari ayi dressing wound din ya kamata mu nemi iyayenta gaskiya tana haɗa kayan aiki suna tattaunawa da hajia Balki baby dai na faman kukan ta sai da aka kira wata nurse ta rike ta aka fara mata lidocaine injection sanan aka fara wanke wounds d'in duk da alurar da aka yi mata na kashe zafi sai data sha kukanta aka wanke tas sanan aka naɗa hanun da bandage,sai  pain killer a aka bata hajia ta biya komi,nurse tace Baby kina zuwa schl..?,tace eh da nike wurin ammie amma yanzu gidan aunty shukura Bana zuwa..

Yanzu kina da number ammie ko zaki gane gidan ta a mayar dake can..?,tace banda number nata,gidan mu na farko na sani ba zan gane wanda ta koma yanzu ba gidan  aunty shukura kawai zan iya gane wa,tace shike nan hajia ki kai ta can amma ya kamata ki tsorata matar nan fa kada ta kashe yarinyar mutane "ah haba kada ki damu aiki na ne ai(hajia Balki barrister ce),motar suka shiga baby na mata kwatance har gidan lokacin da suka shiga matan gidan anyi chirko chirko ana gulman shukura zata kashe yarinyar mutane hadda adu'a suke Allah yasa iyayen yarinya su kuleta sai ganin Baby suka yi tare da wata hamshakiyar hajia sun shigo"cikin sakin fuska hajia Balki tayi sallama tana gaishe su ko wace gulma na cinta suka amsa ,a tunanin su itace mahaifiyar baby da Yan sanda tazo kama shukura,,,baby na nuna mata hanya har dakin shukura dake kwasar bacci hankali kwance yamma tayi bata ga diyar mutane ba ko a jikin ta.

Sallama hajia Balki ke kwada wa cike da masifa shukura ta tashi sai tayi tuzali da hajia Balki ai nan jiki na rawa ta fito tana gada wurin zama har ƙasa ta kai tana kwasar gaisuwa...,hajia Balki tace  ina mijin ki...?,tana kame kame tace tayi tafiya ne",ok zauna aunty shukura ita kuma tana riko hanun baby"shukura shawara zan baki a matsayina mace yar uwanki kuma lauya mai aiki da qungiyar kare hakkin mata da kana nan yara(women right),hasashe na ya zama gaskiya ba kece mahaifiyar baby ba,Allah ne ya haɗa ku zan so kiji tsoron Allah ki rike ta da amana ɗa na kowa ne ba lallai ɗan daka haifa shine zai duba ka ba,Allah kaɗai yasan abinda zata Zama nan gaba,wanan abinda da kika mata ayau ya zama child abuse Wanda za'a hukunta ki a akai amma me..?,ina baki shawara da ki kiyaye gaba saboda abun duniya Pure water na ɗari biyu kula da bai wuce 2k ba zaki ɗauki makami(razor) ki tsaga yatsun yarinya ɗaya, ɗaya har biyar for goodness sake,Babu zuciya ne a kirjin ki...?,ko babu imani ne a tatare dake bayan duka data sha bai isa ba hadda amfani da makami..!,,that is  beyond reasonable doubt gaskiya,is too much for her at her age wanan ya zama domestic violance zan baki Shawara guda ɗaya mai muhimmanci idan har kin san ba zaki iya riƙe ta ba KINDLY take her back to her parents ,idan lallai sai kin zauna da ita dole kada ki sake koda wasa ki sake daura mata talla...!,kada ki sake dukanta domin ITAMA Y'ACE kamar kowa kasancewa ba kece kika haife ta ba that will not be an excuse for u to maltreat her madam shukura yanzu irin firgice wa  da tayi ba don Allah ya kiyaye ba wa yasan idan zata shige..?,ta haye saman titi da wani mara imanin ya kad'e ta taji rauni kota rasa ranta ya gudu sai ya kenan...?,Na hada ki da girman Allah ki mayar da ita wurin iyayen ta kawai cuz zan sake dawo wa gidan nan wurin mijin ki idan na samu labarin sun yi mata wani abu zan sa adaure  mun ku wallahi,Ta mike tsaye ga ledan magungunan ta nan yanzu a bata abinci taxi sai ta sha magani ta kwanta tana bukatar hutu kada a tashe ta sai ta tashi da kanta...!

Ok hajia ba za sake ba wanan ma ba plan nawa bane ya Abu yace ayi mata shi,i promise such mistake will never happen again I swear madam,hajia Balki na rike da hanun baby tace atau yanzu dai is left for u ,shekaru na goma da aure ko miscarriage ban taba samu ba kin samu fine girl from no where kina maltreating nata,zan dawo da mijina idan bakwa sonta ku bani..,tana magana tana tafiya rike da hanun baby,shukura na bin su a baya har wurin mota tana godia ""shafa kan baby tayi kiyi hakuri kinji baby Allah na tare da mai hakuri insha Allah za kiga riban hakuri sai na sake zuwa insha ALLAH,baby na sharan hawaye tace Nagode jiki a sanyaye hajia Balki taja motar ta kamar kada ta rabu da baby Allah ya daura mata son baby...

Suna dawo wa cikin gidan aka shek'e da dariya ana nuna shukyra,masu Allah ƙara nayi,masu dama tafiya akayi da ita suka fadi..,ita dai bata tanka ba domin akwai mugun tsoro tayi daki"BA GONI SAI ALLAH SPECIAL BREAD ta dauko ta zuba stew da ice fish a plate ta aje ma baby a gabanta bata ce kala ba ta fice,baby kam ta shiga ci saboda tun ruwan koko da Safiyya ta bata da safe bata sake cin komi ba gashi yanzu pass 5:0pm,sai da taci kusan rabin bread ɗin sanan ta kira da ruwa tasha magani ta kwanta ai kuwa sai bacci....

Shukura na zaune kofar d'aki abun duniya ya isheta har magrib baby Bata TASHI ba ,fita tayi ta sayo masu amala da miyar yauki lokacin baby ta tashi da tsamin jiki sai hawaye take  saboda zugin da hanun ta ke mata,shukura ta shigo"sorry baby oya come lemme bath u ,baby ta girgiza kai domin a tsorace take da shukura ok come and  brush yr teeth before u eat again,a hankali ta bude baki"i want to pray,cike da mamaki take kallon baby ga hannu nade da plasta,jiki a kukuje tana kiran sallah za tayi,ai kuwa bata hana ta ba a hankali ta tashi shukura na zuba mata ruwa tana alwala da hannu ɗaya har ta gama ,aka shinfida mata sallaya ta zauna ta gabatar da salolin da ake Binta tayi adu'a ma iyayenta kamar yadda ta Saba sanan taci amala kadan tasha magani nan ya buge ta sai bacci kamar saboda tsoro ko tsakar gida shukura bata fita hira ba bare wurin Bobby.

Tun daga ranar shukura ta saukaka ma baby kulawa na musamman take bata,abinci akai akai,itace ke mata wanka,ta wanke kayan baby duka ta goge ta mata kitson rubber  Mai kyau komi ta samu baby ce har mijinta ya dawo daga Katsina lokacin hanun bai gama cikewa ba amma suna zuwa dressing a wani Chemist kusa dasu"tambayar duniya shukura taki sanar dashi abinda ya samu baby,itama baby daya tambaya tace babu komi gajia yayi ya kyale su cike da zumudi ya faɗa ma baby papan ta zai zo karshen sati su tafi tare",ai kuwa ta shiga murna sosai  tana farin ciki zata rabu da wahala.

Yanzu alhamdulillah babu abinda baby ta rasa gidan taufeeq sai ilimi domin babu boko babu islamiyya,data gama kama ma shukura aiki taci gaba da zaman banza saboda bata da abokiyar hira sai Safiyya wace rabin labarin ta gulman yan gidan ne!




"""Yaya Mallam ba shirin zuwa Kano ne wani aiki ya taso musu na gaggawa kuma yana ɗaya daga cikin wa'yanda aka zaba zuwa  dajin nan wanda zasu shafe watanni a ciki,waya ya Daga ya kira taufeeq yana ƙara bashi hakuri akan ya kara rike mashi baby har ya dawo zai Turo mashi wasu kuɗaɗe ya ƙara wurin hidimar baby,,,godia sosai yayi ma Yaya Mallam irin kulawar da yake dasu akan baby,fatan alheri yayi ma yaya Mallam da adu'a samun nasara akan abokan gaba suka kashe wayan"abinda keba taufeeq mamaki tunda oga aminu ke kira bai taɓa tambayar lafiya baby ba ko yace a bata waya suyi magana!

shukura kam tun tana ɗari ɗari kada hajia Balki ta dawo gidan mijinta yasan zalunci da tayi ma baby har aka kwashe wata ɗaya bata dawo ba ta saki ranta idan muguntan ya tashi mata tasa babu aikin abinci da diban ruwa wani lokaci hadda wankin under Wears nata, boxer da singlet na taufeeq.


"""Alahi habu ya samu kawu shi da maganar ammie bai boye mashi komi ba akan rayuwar ta sanan ya sanar dashi kuɗi rin auren ta da yake dashi,"kawu idrisu yaji daɗin wanan kudirin na alaji habu ko babu komi zai ceto rayuwar ta daga halaka ta hanyar raya Sunnah,ya nuna mashi ya kamata suje ga iyayenta kamar yadda ta fadi tunda umman ta nada rai a daura aure can,alaji yace yafi so kawu idrisu ya zama waliyin ammie a daura auren daga baya zasu tafi ga iyayenta,yace Allah mana jagora...,tun daga lokacin aka fara shirye shiryen auren ba wani shagali dai za ayi ba amma alaji ya saki kudi saboda saura wata ɗaya ta kamala istibra'i a d'aura aure,tare da uwar masu gida suka haɗa lefe daidai yada take so,ga gyaran jiki tana shan shi hadda na hauka komi saya ake ana durama ciki,ana cusa ma gaba saboda alaji habu.

Lokacin da alaji habu yaje ma matar shi hajia mariya da maganar aure ko a jikin ta domin tasan babu abinda ta rasa a gidan mijinta kuma ba wuri ɗaya zasu zauna ba menene matsalar ta,fatan alheri kawai tayi mashi.


Rana bata karya sai dai ita d'iya taji kunya ranar juma'a bayan sallar juma'a aka daura auren HADIZA AMINU(AMMIE) DA ALHAJI ABUBAKAR MAGA(ALAJI HABU)akan sadaki Naira dubu hamsin,bayan daurin Aure aka gabatar da walima na cin abinci a gidan gonan shi...,can gidan kuwa yan daudu da yan bariki sun cika ana ta shagali sai kida ke tashi ana cashewa ita kam ba'baa mairo sai mamaki take ina ammie ta samo wanan zugan tsiyar,amarya tasha kwala na kece raini ta zuba gwala gwalai sai sheki take ana ta shagali har dare lokacin daga amarya sai ba'baa mairo wacce ta ƙara gyara gidan,Alaji babu ya shigo da sallama hanun shi riƙe da leda, ba'baa mairo na kitchen tana wanke plates da suka b'ata suka gaisa ya haura sama amarya na zaune saman gado cikin wani boyl less Mai ɗaukan ido tasha kwaliyya sosai da sallama ya shiga d'akin sai fashe wa tayi da kuka sosai kamar wace aka aiko ma sakon mutuwa...!!!

~🅿21-22~



    A hankali yake takawa har zuwa inda take ya zauna saman bed leda dake hanun shi ya aje saman chest of drawer sanan ya rungumo ta yana tapping bayan ta har tayi shiru tana sauke ajiyar zuciya"yau rana ce na farin ciki ba kuka ba yanzu ki tashi muyi alwala mu gabatar da nafila raka'a biyu domin miƙa godiya ga Allah subhanahu wata'ala da ya nuna mana wanan rana....,jiki a sanyaye ta yaye mayafin dake saman kanta ya rik'o hanunta har toilet sai daya fara alwala sanan ya bata wuri tayi koda ta fito har ya shimfida babban darduma yana jiran ta,hijab ta ciro cikin wardrobe tabi bayan shi ya tadda sallah a sujjada na karshe sun kai kusan mintuna goma kowa karon Allah bukatun shi kafin daga bisani ya sallame ya haɗa hanayen shi biyu ya shiga kwararo adu'oi tana amsa wa da Amin har suka shafa ya fuskance ta tare da mata tambayoyi akan addini..?,Alhamdulillah abinda ya shafi shika shikan musulunci,tsarki,alwala da sallah ta bashi amsa dai dai sai ɗan abinda ba'a rasa ba,mikewa yayi ya nufi kofa ya fito har palour ya rufe kofar ya shiga kitchen ya dauko plate da cups ya dawo Da sallama hannu ya mika ya dauko leda da ya shigo da ita gasasun kaji guda biyu ne sai hollandia youghurt ya kalleta amarya bismillah..,"cike da jin nauyin shi tace na koshi kaci kawai,ya kwaikwayi yadda tayi magana shima ya koshi da taga da gaske ba ci zai ba ta saka hannu yana bata a baki tana bashi har suka yi rabin ɗaya ta nuna da gaske a koshe take ya kwashe kayan ya nufi fredge dasu,toilet suka koma a tare suka yi wanka da brush suka fito ko wane daure da towel ta haye can karshen gadon ta kwanta tana karantu adu'a kwanciya bacci,shi kuma yana tsaye yana fesa turaruka  har ya kamala ya hawo gadon tare da jawo ta jikin shi yana shafa bayanta a hankali cikin kwarewa tun tana noke wa har ta saki jiki komi ya wakana...

Sun kai wani lokaci kafin su samu natsuwa sanan yayi kissing forehead nata bacci yayi gaba da su sai da asuba suka farka,a gurguje yayi wanka ya nufi masallaci ya bata wuri tayi wanka ta fara gabatar da nafila kafin farilla ta jima sosai cikin sujjada tana adu'oi Allah ya basu zama lafiya da zuri'a dayiba,Allah ya jikan mahaifinta ya bata ikon ganawa da umman ta kafin ta koma ga mahaliccin ta....,koda ya shigo tana karatun Alkur'ani mai girma wuri ya samu ya kwanta har ta kamala ta aje komi cikin girmamawa ta gaishe shi,rik'o ta yayi zuwa jikin shi"amarya bakya laifi koda kin kashe dan masu gida..,fari tayi da idanu wace ni fatana Allah ya albarkaci auren mu alaji na,ya amsa da Amin ta ƙara shigewa jikin shi ba'a ɗauki lokaci ba bacci ya kwashe su.

Kiraye kirayen wayan Alaji da ake ta yi akai akai ya tashe su daga bacci tsaki yaja ya mika hannu ya dauko wayan yadda yake amsa wayan zai nuna maka wanda ake wayar da ita tana da matukar muhimmanci,jiki a sanyaye ammie ta shige toilet tayi wanka koda ta fito yabar gidan..., jiki ba kwari ya shirya cikin super wax babban  duk da ba make up tayi ba amma taya kyau sosai ta sauko lokacin ba'baa mairo tana kitchen suka gaisa ta kama mata har suka kamala breakfast suka shirya a table ta dawo palour shiru alaji bai dawo ba sai zuwa 11:30am  taji tsayuwar motar shi da hanzari ta fito har wurin mota ta tarbe shi ya fito tare da ware mata hanayen shi ta shige jikin shi ya rungume ta tsam yana shaƙar kamshin jikin ta"honey pot u luk stoney,thank U tace suka nufi ciki yana kame kame wai uwar gida ce ta kira shi yara sun damu basu Ganshi a gidan ba shine yaje ya gansu.

Murmushi tayi kawai duk da ranta ya ɓaci,muje break fast na jiran ka...,aush sowie da baki jiraani ba nayi breakfast tare dasu shine ma dalilin da yasa ban dawo da wuri ba"har ga Allah bata ji dad'i ba amma ya ta iya haka zata hakura tunda mijin wata ta aura,ruwan tea kawai ta iya sha ya kwala mata kira ta haɗa mai ruwan wanka babu musu ta taya shi har ya shirya cikin yadi navy blue Mai laushi yace zai tafi can gidan kawu idrisu baƙin da suka zo daga ƙauye yau zasu juya zai sallame su.

Har wurin mota ta raka shi ta bude ya shiga ta zauna saman laps dinshi ta sagala hanayen ta duka biyu a wuyan shi sanan ta haɗe bakin su wuri ɗaya tana kissing dinshi sai da tayi mai isanta tace a dawo lafiya Allah tsare hanya plz don't stay long,jikin shi a mace yace insha Allah I will be back soon yaja motar tana tsaye har ya fita ta sauke nanauyar ajiyar zuciya ta dawo ciki zuwa d'akin ba'baa mairo da sallama ta shiga...,"Barka da rana hajia..?,Barka dai ba'baa y gajiyan aiki..?,babu gajiya hajia kome za'a girka na rana..?,ki huta kawai ba'baa anjima zan girka sai idan kina bukatar wani abu ki girka..,hira suke yi suka ji sallama alamar sun yi baƙi.

Fitowa suka yi ba'baa mairo ta buɗe kofa sai ga mata kusan shida da yara ƙanana daga gani yan ƙauye ne,bayan sun gama shigowa ne driver ya gaishe ta Yana mata bayani alaji ne yace a kawo su suga wuri kafin su koma,tace ok shi alajin yana ina..?,ya wuce gidan hajia(uwar gidan shi),ciki ta dawo suka gaisa da baƙin suna mata bayani yan uwan ne su, murmushi tayi ai driver ya min bayani bismillah ku zauna mana, ba'baa mairo ta dauko masu drinks da snacks,ammie ta dauko foodflask cikin kayan breakfast din su na peepe soup din kifi danye ta aje masu yaran sai wasa suke a Parlor'n wasu har san Centre tables bata ce dasu komi ba ba'baa mairo taba umurni ta daura shinkafa shiga kicaniyar haɗa miya har aka kira sallah alaji bai dawo ba lokacin ta kamala miya ta nuna ma baƙin dakin da baby tayi zama su shiga suyi sallah,ta koma dakin ta tayi wanka ta gabatar da sallah,a gagauce ta shirya cikin geznah skyblue ta zuba gwala gwalai ta fito suka gabatar da baƙi da abinci da drinks sai yaba mata suke tana ki suna faɗin tafi hajia mariya mutunci da kulawa har suka ga driver yace su fito alaji yace motar da zai mayar dasu ya iso...,ta basu kyautar sabulai da turaruka masu sauƙin kuɗi ta raka su har wurin mota suka tafi.

Alaji habu bai dawo gidan ba sai bayan sallah isha'i yace ta shirya zasu fita..,babu musu ta saka Arabian gown marron Wanda yasha stones tayi Rolling veil nashi basu tsaya ko ina ba sai bakin gate din wani babban gida a unguwar lamid'o crescent yayi horn Mai gadi ya buɗe masu girma da faɗin gidan ya ninka nata sau uku riko hanunta yayi har babban palour na gidan yace ta zauna yana zuwa ya haura sama....,"furnitures set biyu ke palour'n saboda girman shi wall paper ne zagaye da bangon palour'n ga set na kayan kallo masu kyau da tsada,sai sanyin Ac da kamshi ke tashi daga gefe tafka tafkan window size hotunan hajia mariya ne rungume da yaranta,wani tare da alaji,wasu kuma ita ɗaya nan ta fahimci gidan uwar gidan shi suka zo nan take gaban ta ya yanke ya fadi ganin irin kyau da tsari na hajia mariya har kusan mintuna talatin basu fito ba har ta fara kosawa da zaman can taji alamar saukowa da hayaniyar mutane koda ta duba,alaji habu ne rungume da yaran shi,hajia mariya na take masu baya cikin shiga na alfarma less din dake jikin ta yakai dubu hamsin banda gwala gwalai tun daga kunen,wuya har hannu zasu kai dubu ɗari biyu sai sheki take nan take ammie ta daburce domin hajia mariya tayi mata zarrah sosai wurin kyau,tsari da kuma wayewa har suka karaso  kallon su take suka zauna saman three seater yaran su na rungume a jikin su.

Jiki a sanyaye ta gaishe da hajia..,lafiya kawai tace ta ci gaba da hira da mijinta cikin harshen turanci yaran ma babu wanda yabi ta kanta sai usaman Karami yace da ita "Hello,ta amsa da hey suka ci gaba da zuba ma daddy'n su shagwaba ammie ko yar kallo ta koma har zuwa 9:30pm ta mike tare da faɗin sai da safen ku....

Babu wace ta kalle ta bare su amsa mata,alaji ya kalli hajia me yasa kika yi haka bayan tun a sama munyi dake zaku gaisa...?,tsaki taja saboda me zan gaisa da ita bayan ta aure mun miji tana neman nisanta ka da yaran ka wallahi ba zan taɓa sonta ba ta zauna can gidanta nima ina gida na period...,haba hajia why do u hate her dat much..?,bcuz she's a prostitute pretending to be innocent!!


Yaran ya kala yaga sun zuba mashi idanu yace dasu zai tafi sai gobe nan take usman ya saka mai kuka zai bishi...,faiza da yake itace babba tace U Are not going anywhere shut up,Sarah tace Mummy I hate day bitch and I don't want to see her near us again...Yan hanun yaranta taji suka haye sama alaji na tsaye yana kallon ikon Allah kamar ba itace hajia mariya dakata nuna bata da matsala da auren shi ba lokaci ɗaya duk ta birkita mashi lisaffi duk yadda akayi zugata akayi,jiki ba kwari ya fito.

Ammie na duke ƙasa ta haɗa kai da guiwa tana kuka riko hanunta yayi ta kwace da ƙarfi kada ka sake tabani mugu azzalumi kawai,adalci kenan tunda muka tashi bacci baka zauna ba kana hanyar gidan nan bai isheka ba sai daka dauko Ni aka ci mutumci na hankalin ka ya kwanta,wallahi da nasan haka kake da ban aure ka ba....

Katse ta yayi"enough ammie Dame zanji..?,wani irin cin mutunci kike magana dukan ki akayi ko zagi.. .?,shike nan tunda na auri sarauniyar mata bazan je ga iyalai na ba,for ur information bani da kamar su duk faɗin duniya U can go to hell ,Dama matsalar auren karuwa kenan wacce bata tashi gaban iyayenta ba ilimin addini da tarbiyya bai wa dace ta ba babu abinda ta sani sai kwana da maza,wallahi nayi mugun taimaka maki despite abubuwa masu muni da kika aikata a rayuwar ki na amince da auren ki tun ba'a kai ko ina ba zaki fara tayar mun da hankali,meke jikin ki Banda panko Babu komi ragowar kazamai yaja tsaki!!!

Ranta ba ƙaramin ɓaci yayi ba da jin kalaman alaji habu akanta cike da karfin hali tace" dama nasan rana makamancin wanan zata zo koba dade koba jima sai ka goranta mun,amma ka sani bani ce nace ka aure Ni ba ra'ayin kanka ne haka NIMA Y'ACE  kamar kowa kaddarar data ce kawai ta rabo Ni da iyayena badan bana sonsu ko basa sona ba na Barka lafiya alaji habu cikin zafin nama ta nufi hanyar gate Yana Kiran sunanta ko waiwayi bata yiba bare ta amsa har ta fita daga gidan....



"""'''shukura na zaune kofar maman Daniel suna gulman aunty grace kamar yadda suka saba taga shigowar mijinta yana amsa waya har ya shige d'akin tana zaune wurin gulma na cin ta...,baby na zaune kofar su Safiyya tana bata labarin makarantar boko su da zalunci da prefect keyi suka ji muryan taufeeq yana kiran baby...

Da sallama ta shiga d'akin yace ta kira shukura su zo tare....,tare suka shiga ya warware leda daya shigo dashi kaya ne na matasa dai dai jikin baby da takalma biyu.

Ya kalli shukura da baby yace yanzu na gama waya da papan ki yana cikin garin nan tun juya ashe har sun kamala aikin ya dawo lafiya yanzu hutu yake yi,yace a haɗa kayan ki gobe za kubi jirgin karfe goma na safe zuwa Lagos,yanzu zan dauki photon ki a waya na a cire ayi passport dashi...

Murna sosai shukura ta shiga yi domin tasan yaya Mallam yaba mijinta wasu kuɗaɗe kuma zata samu rabonta a ciki...,baby ta rasa farin ciki take yi ko baƙin ciki Ace watannin ta uku a gidan papanta bai taɓa waya da ita ba ko bada sakon gaisuwa a gare ta ba,haka ammie tunda suka damka ta hanun shi basu sake waiwayeb ta ba yau rana tsaka ace zata bishi har wani gari Lagos ko yaya zaman su zata kasance oho...!, taufeeq ya katse shirun nata yana faɗin shukura zata gyara mata kai ta tattara shirya kayanta shi zai fita.

Kuka ta fashe dashi tana fadin uncle Ni ba zan bi papa na ba wurin ka zan zaun...,ya katse ta zaki ji daɗin zama da papan ki domin mutum ne mai natsuwa da kulawa ki share hawaye shukyra zata taimaka maki ki gyara komi naki kinji...?,ta gyada kai ya share mata hawaye sanan ya dauke ta pics har uku da wayan shi ya fita.

Shukura haushi baby taji tana farin ciki Allah ya raba ta da wahala baby nason bijirewa,wurin wata mata kusa da gidan suka bada kayan baby ta wanke na kuɗi shukura ta tsefe mata kai ta mata wani kitson suna ta shirye shirye kafin kace me,shukura ta yaɗa labari cikin gida gobe baby zata wuce ta huta da wahala sai tayata murna ake,Safiyya tace ma baby nikam na tayaki murna zaki bar gidan nan saboda Ibro ya taɓa fada mun wai yadda yake samun yatsa cikin pant idan ina Bacci kema zai fara saka maki idan kika zo dakin mu da dare ko idan ya kama ki a lungu,Allah na sonki ibro bai saka maku katon yatsan shi ba da sai kin kuka Wallahi...!,shiru baby tayi tana sauraren ikon Allah ashe dama saboda haka Ibro ke yawan shishige mata musamman da dare idan an aikita yataa binta a baya!!

Washe gari tunda safe shukura da kanta tayi wa baby wanka ta haɗa masu tea suka yi breakfast ta k'ara gyara mata kayanta zuwa 9:40am tayi sallama da mutanen gidan domin yaya Mallam ya bugo ma taufeeq akan ya kawo ta airport Yana jiran su....

Shukura hadda kukan munafu nci suka shiga adaidaita har airport yaya Mallam na sanye cikin uniform d'in su na sojoji a gaba an rubuta A.mallam suka gaisa da taufeeq cikin girmamawa baby na gaishe shi amma ko kallo bata ishe shi ba yace da taufeeq su jira Bara yazo...

Suna zaune a wurin taufeeq na mamakin ikon Allah domin dai oga aminu yace mai baby yarinyar shine sun rabu da matar shi amma menene hujar shi na nuna mata tsana haka..?

Sai da aka fara announcing passenger idan suna kusa su matso jirgin su zai tashi sanan ya dauko rafar yan dubu dubu ya miƙa ma taufeeq yana ƙara mai godiya..., taufeeq ma godiya yayi mashi yana daga ma baby hannu hadda hawaye saboda ya shaku da ita tunda yake bai taba ganin yarinya mai hakuri da ibada irin baby ba duk da bata wuce 11yrs ba amma akwai kau da kai ga natsuwa.

Cikin jirgin baby na zaune kusa da yaya Mallam ya daura belt ya daura mata bai sake kallonta ba hankalin shi na kan wayan shi da yake kashewa.

Baby ta fara tunanin ya rayuwarta zata kasance tare da wanan mutumin da ko gaisuwa ta bai taɓa amsawa ba,shike nan ta rabu da ammie har a bada ba tare da ta amsa mata dumbin tambayoyin da take son mata ba!!

Yaya Mallam sai lokacin ya kare wa baby kallo kama take da shi sak haɗa baƙin fatan,idanu,hanci da baki nashi ne,yanayin jiki ne kawai na ammie baban tashin hankali yanzu idan ya isa mai zai fada wa abokan aikin shi,wacece baby a wuri shi bayan kowa yasan bai taba aure ba!!!

~🅿23-24~

Tunda Allah ya halicce ta bata taɓa cin sakwara ba sai ranar ta ɗan tsakura ta shanye miya tas baiwar Allah ta wanke kwano nin a  madaidaicin kitchen din nashi ta dawo palour ta zauna saman carpet tana kallon Film abunka da tsautsayi ta manta da warning din daya bata "banda zama a palour"!!!

Tsaki yaja idan zaka iya ka kashe Ni sai an jima ya ja hanun baby suka fita...




"""'''Alaji habu ya shiga motan shi a gurguje aka buɗe gate Koda ya fita har tayi  nisa da tafiya a ƙasa kasancewa Area din akwai ƙarancin abun hawa ya Parker a gaban ta"na haɗa ki da girman Allah ki shigo Mu tafi gida nasan ban kyauta maki ba insha Allah haka ba zata sake faruwa ba...!,banza tayi dashi tana kokarin canja hanya da yaga zata bata mai lokaci ya fito ya cicibeta ya saka sakin mota yayi locking yaja tana ta bugun kofa sai gudu yake da motar har suka isa gida lokacin 10:20pm a guje ta shige tun nata ta rufe...

*WANENE CAPTAIN SADIQ MANGAL*???


  Captain Sadiq mangal haifaffen garin Adamawa state ,yola ne iyayen shi fulanin asali ne tun daga kan kakani,ya taso cikin so da kauna da kuma kulawa na iyaye da yan kowa saboda shine last born Yana da yane maza biyu da mata biyu ko wane na zaune tare da iyalan shi,cikin Yola ya fara tun daga nursury, primary har secondary daya kamala ya tafi NDA KADUNA Wanda a Nan suka hadu da yaya mallam abota mai karfi ya shiga tsakanin su har suka kamala cikin Sa'a,lokacin  posting aka raba su yaya Mallam na Lagos aka tura Sadiq ibadan sai bayan yayi aure ne aka dawo dashi Lagos kwata kwata watannin su shida kenan da aure.

""Wurin da yayi jini aka wanke sanan aka shafa mata magani,nan take akayi mata allurai aka basu kwayoyin magunguna suka nufo gida,lokacin sadeeyah ta kamala girki da take yi har tayi wanka tana zaune cikin English Wears tana jiran dawowar mijinta.

~🅿27-28~

Yaya Mallam yace"saboda kai yau suna na ya ɓaci cikin barikin nan,a dalilin abinda ka aikata mutunci na ya zube a barikin nan.!,ka buɗe kunu wanka da kyau kaji abinda zan faɗa maka tunda ka mun shishigi cikin lamura na zan nuna maka kai ƙaramin ɗan iska ne,gata nan na kawo maka daga yau babu Ni babu ita kayi duk yadda kake so da ita tunda ban isa tayi laifi in hukunta ta ba Ni da d'iya ta a mun iko da ita,baka san komi a kanta ba,baka san lokacin da aka haife ta ba,baka san pains and agony da nayi going through cuz of her "Yana nuna baby a dalilin ta na rasa family na kowa nawa na zama mara ya na karfi da yaji wanan,wanna shegiya tsinaniyar yarinya wace ba zata gama da duniya lafiya ba wallahi yadda kika saka na aikata abun kunya a duniya kema sai Allah ya nufe ki da aikatawa sai kin haifa kin goya da yardan Allah!!!

Kai kuma na dawo kanka wallahi idan ka sake koda wasa ka tunkaro Ni da maganar wanan idiot d'in WALLAHI hukuma zata raba mu,ya juya fuuuuuu!!!!!


Baby CE tabi bayan yaya Mallam a guje..., Captain sadiq ya daka mata tsawa"where the hell are you going to.?

A razane ta dawo ƙasa ta zauna sai da tayi fitsari a wando saboda rude wa...

Ya ɗauki lokaci mai tsayi Kafin sadeeyah ta dawo hayacin ta bacci yayi gaba da ita,koda ya duba agogo 9:40pm daukan ta yayi zuwa bed room nasu ya gyara mata kwanciya,sanan ya dawo wurin baby dake dunkule gefe ɗaya,alama yayi mata da hannu da tazo...,cikin kad'uwa ta kara su wurin ta zauna ƙasa lokacin ya lura tayi fitsari a zaune fasa magana yayi"ga daki can ki dauki bag naki idan kin shiga ki fara yin wanka sai ki canja kaya ki kwanta.

Mikewa tayi ta kama hanya zuwa d'akin....,kwanciya yayi saman carpet Yana tunanin maganganun yaya Mallam domin sun kunshi wani sirri mai girma da yake boye wa,iya tsawon shekarun da suka kwashe dashi bai taɓa sanin kowa nashi ba abu ɗaya ya sani baban shi ya rasu baya ga wanan bai san asali ko wani dan uwa nashi ba idan har ya fahimci inda maganar ya dosa ciki yayi ma wata kenan ta haifa mai yarinya a dalilin haka ya rabu da iyayen shi bai sake waiwayen su ba,idan har hasashen shi ya zama gaskiya ba'a son ranshi ya taho da ita barikin ba shine dalilin da yasa yake kokarin boye ta kada kowa ya sani"murmushi yayi babu komi insha Allah zai rika baby ya mata gata kamar ko wace d'iya domin ITAMA Y'ACE CE  wace yasan nan gaba sai anyi alfahari da ita,wanka yayi sanan ya kwanta bacci.

Tunda suka tashi  sallar asuba ya lura da zazzaɓi ta tashi don haka bai fita Masjid ba a gida yayi sallah ya mata wanka tare da haɗa mata tea Tasha tare da magani sanan  ya gyara gidan,koda ya leka d'akin baby ta tashi tayi sallah yace ta dawo wurin sadeeyah zai fita...,ya shirya ya fita cikin barrack domin suna da aiki.

Ganin Baby yasa sadeeyah ya warware sosai ta tashi suka shiga kitchen tare suka haɗa abinda za su ci,bayan sun kamala tayi ma baby wanka itama tayi tare da cin kwaliyya suka dawo palour suna kallo"can tace wai ya sunan ki..?,tace baby!

Murmushi tayi baby ai ba suna bane,tace tunda aka haife Ni baby ake ce mun har schl ma da sunan nake amfani...,ok kin taɓa yin schl ne..?,eh a Kano lokacin ina wurin ammie na!

Yanzu ina ammie ta and me yasa ta bari kika dawo nan.?

Ammie na kano ta canja gida ne mai kyau tayi aure shine mijinta yace a mayar dani wurin papa na"lokacin ta fahimci lallai ba ta hanyar aure aka haifi baby ba shi yasa da mahaifiyar ta zata yi aure ta dawo da ita wurin Captain Aminu babu shakka shine mahaifinta kenan!!!

Amma menene dalilin shi na nuna mata kiyayya haka.?,wanan amsa a bakin mijinta za taji idan ya shigo...,tambayoyi ta shiga ma baby akan karatun da tayi,ta gane baby Nada kokari sosai ta fanin addini kuwa babu abinda ta sani!

Zuwa azuhur ta shirya abinci cikin basket ta d'aura abaya saman English Wears dake jikinta ta yane kanta da ƙaramin veil din shi...,baby ki zauna zan kai ma uncle abinci office yanzu zan dawo kin ji.?

Tace tau sai kin dawo aunty..,Koda sadeeyah ta Isa office din nasu suna fama da aiki ta gaisa da sauran abokan aikin mijin ta cikin wasa amma banda yaya Mallam wanda ko gaisuwa ta bai amsa ba bare ya biye wa shirmen su.

Bata wani bata lokaci ba ta aje mai abincin ta dawo gida saboda baby dake ita ɗaya a gida,koda ta shigo baby na zaune tana kallon cartoon Network inda ake Film ɗin"Sofia the first",sai dariya take kyalkyala ganin ta haka ya faranta ran sadeeyah sosai.

Sai zuwa yamma Captain sadiq ya dawo bayan yayi wanka ya ci abinci suka tattauna da sadeeyah akan yadda yaya Mallam ya mashi jiya kma ta bashi goyon baya ɗari bisa ɗari na su riƙe baby da amana har take sanar dashi ta fara karatu kuma she's  very brilliant ,sai dai fanin karatun addini bata da sani!

Yace what's ur suggestion now?,,fuskan shi ta shafa,honey tunda akwai schl cikin Barack and she can read and write Kawai ta shiga cikin yan primary6 suyi common entrance exmas tare kaga idan ta dawo zuwa 4pm ta rinka shiga masallaci tana daukan karatu tare da sauran yara.

Murmushi yayi saboda abinda ke zuciyar shi kenan,ok zuwa gobe zan yi komi ta fara zuwa insha Allah.

Abunka da akwai kuɗi ƙasa Captain sadiq yayi duk wani shige da fi ce cikin kwana aka sama ma baby admission a "COMMAND PRIMARY SCHOOL" da yake suke bada uniform da writting material yasa ba'a wani bata lokaci ba ta fara zuwa abunta..,ranar farko abun ya zame mata sabon abu tunda ta kwana biyu bata shiga class ba,sanan wanan private ne akan wanda tayi a kano government schol karatun akwai banbanci sosai amma a hankali take fahimta da ta dawo schl taci abinci tayi sallah sadeeyah ke mata lesson a gida zuwa 4pm ta shiga islamiyya idan ta dawo da dare Captain sadiq ke biya masu ita da sadeeyah.

Hankalin baby kwance bata da matsalar komi tana samun kulawa sosai wurin sadeeyah da Captain sadiq wanda ko wane lokaci daya dawo office Yana gida idan ba masjid ya fita ba sai in wani aiki ya kai shi cikin gari ko wani gari shine suke zaka su biyu abun su, sadeeyah bata da irin munafuncin nan na matan barrack Babu ruwan ta da shiga block ɗin kowa tana gidan ta bare azo a turke ta tayi gulma ko munafunci wanda aikin yawan cin matan barikin kenan da an tura mazajen su wani gari wurin aiki Sai a kafa chamber na gulma da munafunci akan turke turke har ayi faɗa.


Yaya mallam kuwa bai sake bi takan Captain sadiq ba bare baby Koda kuwa sun haɗu a office sallama kawai ke shiga tsakanin su tun Captain sadiq na damuwa har abun ya fice mai akai.


Yanzu baby ta zama fes da ita,taɓa mayar da hankali a karatu sosai aikin gida ma ba'a barta a baya ba tare suke yi da da sadeeyah har ta saba da mutane da dama cikin barrack din saboda tana da natsuwa.

Sadiq duk weekend zai tura baby gidan yaya Mallam ta gaishe shi amma da kuka take dawo wa saboda zagi da cin mutumci sai abinda ya manta!




"""Alaji habu bai zame ko ina ba sai gida ya tambayi ba'baa mairo tun wani lokaci ammie ta fita .?,tace ba'a jima ba ci gaba yayi da Safa da marwa shiru shiru bata dawo ba har dare.

Can wurin Uwar masu guba da kuwa bayan ta gama sauraren korafin ammie shawara ta bata da ta daddaka kwalban turare karami har ya zama gari kullum tana Barbada ma alaji habu cikin abinci ki abun sha har ya ƙare"slow poison ne a hankali zai rinka cacakan hanjin shi har su katse ya mutu cikin ruwan sanyi babu wanda zai tuhume ta tunda abun zai dauki lokaci kafin ya rasa ranshi...,cike da farin ciki suka dake kwalban ta dawo dashi gida dama yanzu ta iya driving da kanta take zuwa ko ina.


Tana sallama taji muryan alaji habu yana faɗin"ki koma gidan uwan da kika fito.."dama tasan za'a yi haka tunda ta shigo taga motar shi cikin gidan saboda lokuta da dama idan ya riga ta dawo wa gidan zargi iri iri babu wanda ba ya mata!

Ranta a b'ace ta bashi amsa babu inda zan tafi domin bani wani gida da ya wuce na aure na.  ,Aure!,ashe ke ɗin fa matar aure ce kike yawo kwararo kwararo gidan karuwai neman mazan banza kin raina ma mijin ki....

Dakata Alaji habu na gaji da wanan cin fuska naka,har tsawon wani lokaci zan zauna a gidan ka kana kunsa mun baƙin ciki kala kala,na safe dabam na dare dabam,shike nan kuma bani da halin fita sai zargi.???

Ba'baa mairo ce ta fito domin abun nasu ya koma bata haushi tun yana bata mamaki su kenan kullum cikin faɗa kamar ba auren soyayya suka yada ba!!!

Kallon ba'baa mairo yayi"ki faɗa mun tsakanin ki da Allah wace macen arziki zata rinƙa zuwa gidan karuwai da auren ta.?

Shiru tayi domin bata gane inda maganar ya dosa ba...,ammie tace na tafi sai kayi yadda kaga dama dani aure ba dole bane wallahi wanda kadararen auren naka na rashin yan ci!!!

Ni kike faɗa wa haka.?,an fadi idan ka cika haihuwar uwa.....

Ba'baa mairo ta rufe mata baki"a kul na hane ki da Daga harshe akan na mijin ki bare cin mutunci da tuzarci,me kike nufi da cikin iyayen shi.?,so kike ka hawa kanki tsinuwa da fushin Ubangiji.?

Kyale ta ba'baa mairo nima na gaji da zama da karuwa,mara asali da tarbiyya sallaman ta zan yi....

Idan kana yiwa darajar iyayen ka alaji kada ka yanke hukunci cikin fushi ayi hakuri,idan rai ya ɓaci kada hankali ya gushe,kai ne babba a kanta don darajar annabir rahama(S.A.W) ka tafi har zuciyar ka tafi sanyi"tana magana tana nuna mai hanyar waje!

Ammie ta tare kofa ba'baa mairo idan kinga ya bar gidan nan da takardar saki na,dole yau ka sawake mun wanan kadararen aure nashi....,kina jinta ki ba'baa mairo.?

Alaji idan har na isa da kai ka fice a gidan nan,jiki a sanyaye ya fita daga palour'n ya shiga motar shi tare da kifa kai da starin motar!

Ba'baa mairo ta fuskanci ammie da kyau"yau zan sanar dake wani sirri da baki taɓa sani ba wanda ya zama dole ki sani domin naga kina kokarin wuce gona da iri!!!

Cike da mamaki ammie ta ɗago idanu tana kallon ba'baa mairo wai yau ba'baa mairo mai aikin wanke wanke,shara da girki ke gaya mata bak'ar magana!



_Halimatus Sadiya Muhammed_

_Sweety smasher Ina taya ki murna fara sabon littafin ki mai suna"WANI AL'AMARIN..!,Allah yasa an fara a Sa'a ya bada ladan fadakar wa amin_





~🅿29-30~



     
      Ni maryma uwace ga Abubakar idan baki sani ba ki sani yau uwar shi tasha nono ta saki na kama uwar mu ɗaya uban mu ɗaya da mahaifiyar shi Marigayi ya hajia saratu!


Mamaki,tsoro da fargaba suka cika zuciyar ammie,idan har maganan da ba'baa mairo take faɗi gaskiya ne menene hikimar ta na zuwa gidan a matsayin yar aiki.???

Dafa kafadan ammie tayi,nasan abinda kike tunani sarai nazo gidan ki amatsayin yar aiki yanzu kuma nace Ni gwaggon mijin ki ce...,abinda ya faru lokacin da abubakar yazo wurina da maganar auren ki babue abinda aya boye mun akan rayuwar ki wanda na tausaya maki matuka domin kadarar ki babba ce dole sai akwai babba a tare da ku domin ɗauka ki akan hanya,shine dalilin da yasa na kaskantar da kaina a matsayin yar aikin ki amma yanzu na lura kina neman watsa mun ƙasa a idanu...

Kuka ta fashe dashi tana faɗin na tuba ba'baa mairo na bi Allah WALLAHI ba zan sake ba dama son zuciya ne da rudin shaidan amma ai shima yana da laifi ba'baa ba kya ganin yadda yake abubuwa cikin gidan nan idan ya fita sai ta mai daɗi yake dawo wa...,,kwantar da hankalin ki duk ina sane kuma makircin hajia mariya ne dama idanu na zuba inga iya gudun ruwan shi amma yanzu ya zama dole in tsawata mashi Kuma mu tashi tsaye da adu'a da neman taimako",Nagode Nagode Nagode ba'baa mairo Allah ya ƙara girma ya biya ki


A gaskiya kin tafka babban kuskure a rayuwar ki da har kika biyewa son zuciya tun lokacin da abun nan ya faru da gida kika koma ki haihu gaban iyayen ki,amma Quruciya da wauta ya saka kin shigo bariki kin HAIHU kin kasa kula da ita wallahi HADIZA idan kina son ganin dai dai a rayuwar auren ki gagauta neman gafarar iyayen ki,duk inda baby take ki sanar da ita gaskiya ko ke wace ce a wurin ta sanan ki bata hakuri!...,tana kai wa nan ta shige d'akin ta.

Kuka sosai ammie tayi domin tasan gaskiya ba'baa mairo ta faɗa mata tabbatas ya zama dole tafi ganin iyayenta bayan shekaru goma shq ɗaya da faruwar wannan lamari!..., d'akin ba'baa mairo ta shiga ta duka har ƙasa ba'baa ki yafe mun na dauki shawarar ki don Allah ki mashi magana mu tafi tare hadda ke gaba ɗaya domin samun sassauci a wurin MAHAIFIYATA duk da Allah ya dauki ran MALLAM,zan so muje tare da baby idan har tana cikin garin nan,,shike nan ki tafi ki watsa ruwa ki kwanta yanzu zan kira shi a waya da safe za muyi magana"gyada kai tayi.


Alaji HABU na barin gidan ya nufi wurin Uwar gida can ya kwana dama abinda take so kenan.


Da safe da zazzaɓi ammie ta tashi saboda kuka data sha duk da haka tunda asuba ta shiga kitchen ta haɗa breakfast Koda ba'baa mairo ta fito ammie ta kamala komi...,hajia me yasa baki bari nayi aiki na ba..?,murmushi tayi cikin jin kunya ba'baa matsayin uwa kike a wurin mu bai kyautu ki shiga kitchen Ina zaune ba ko a baya da haka ya faru rashin sani ne....,itama murmushi tayi ta nemi wuri ta zauna a palour cikin babban tray ammie ta haɗa mata breakfast nata"nan za kici ko akai daki.?,kawo nan muci tare d'iya ta...,cikin jin kunya ammie ta zauna suka yi breakfast tare da ba'baa mairo tana ƙara kwantar mata da hankali.


Zuwa 10am alaji habu ya shigo gidan da sallama d'akin ba'baa mairo ya fara shiga suka gaisa,ta gabatar mashi da shawara da suka yanke da ammie na zuwa kumbotso wurin iyayenta",yace ai dama tun kafin aure ya so su tafi bata amince ba,zai shiga yaji ta bakin ta ya haura sama lokacin ammie na gyaran  kaya cikin wardrob ya rungume ta yana shaƙar kamshin jikin ta"kiyi hakuri dani ban kyauta ba sharrin shaidan ne in Sha Allah haka ba zata sake faruwa ba..,sai daɗin baki yake mata har ta hakuri suka sha soyayyar su tun da safe(Lol).


Bayan komi ya lafa ya ƙara tuntubar ta da maganar zuwa gida ta nuna amincewar ta a sonta a tafi da baby,yace zai kira Kabir (babban ɗan Mallam saminu),yaji idan baby na cikin garin Kano.


Koda suka yada yawa Kabir ya sanar dashi duk yadda suka yi da yaya Mallam a ranar yanzu haka bashi da masaniyar  inda ya kai yarinyar ,,yace ya tura mai number Yaya Mallam zai waya dashi suka yi sallama!


Bai sanar da ita ba domin yafi son sai ya gama dukan binciken shi sanan ta sani.



Ba'a ɗauki lokaci ba number Yaya Mallam ya shigo wayar alaji habu yayi saving a lokacin ya kira shi har ta tsinke bai Daga ba....,ya sake kira bugu biyu ya ɗauka..alaji HABU bakin shi dauke da sallama yace ina magana da Captain Aminu ne..?,ya amsa da eh!

Nasan baka san mai magana ba,zan su ka bani Aron hankalin ka muyi magana ta fahimta idan zaka tuna watani biyar da suka wuce a Kano ranar juma'a da dare wani mutum yayi sallama da kai ya kawo maka yarinya"BABY",wace jinin ka ce.?

Huci yaya Mallam ya fara yi yana faɗin,ok na fahimta wulakanci da kamun a Kano bai ishe ka ba har sai ka ƙara kirana a waya don ka tozarta ni...!,wai ma menene haɗin ka da yarinyar da har zaka shiga maganar da baka san mafarin shi ba.?


Duk da ban san mafarin zancen ba,babu abinda ban sani ba tsakanin ka da ammie domin itace matar da mike aure yanzu !!!!,,..katse shi yayi kana nufin ammie tayi aure kuma tana cikin Kano.?

Tabbas ko ranar da na kawo baby tare muke da ita cikin mota shawara ɗaya zan baka yanzu duk abinda kake yi ka shi rya  mu haɗu kumbotso gobe kuma kazo da baby!

Wait wait are u ordering me?,,who am I to order u,shawara ce idan ka dauka ruwan ka,a word is enough for the wise,the word of the elders is the word of wisdom...,kit ya katse kiran!

Da mamaki yaya Mallam ke bin wayan da kallo yanzu kamar shi"CAPTAIN" wani banza ke gaya mun magana hada kashe mun waya.?,yanzu da gaske ammie yar uwan shi tayi aure tana cikin Kano all that while Allah bai taɓa haɗa su ba...,gida babu wanda ya kira shi tace an ga ammie bare ya samu labarin auren ta,,haka na nufin tunda tabar Chemist din nan bata koma goda ba kusan shekaru goma sha daya.!!,wurin wa ta zauna  tsawon shekarun nan???,,wurin wanan alajin kawai zai samu amsoshin nan...,amma idan sun haɗu mai zai faɗa mata bayan ya bata mata rayuwa,,yanzu idan ya biso kumbotso ai asirin shi ya gama tonuwa cikin duniya(niko nace yaya Mallam da kunyar lahira ai kwara na duniya),,da wani fuska zai kalli Captain sadiq yace yana son amsan baby ya kai ta gida!

Kai gaskiya babu inda zai tafi alkawari ya dauka ba zai bari wanan magana ya fito ba yanzu wanna ɗan iskan alajin nason tona masa asiri...,sake sake yake cikin ranshi har ya yanke shawara ɗaya ya canja layin waya kuma ba zai tafi kumbotso ba!


Da dare bayan Alaji habu ya dawo daga wurin harkokin shi ya sanar dasu yadda yayi da yaya Mallam, ba'baa mairo tace mai zai hana su bashi kwana biyu su gani ko zai kira idan har bai kira ba sai su shirya su tafi kawai"duka suka yi na'am da wanan shawara...



Shiru shiru yaya Mallam bai kira ba,alaji HABU ya kira wayar tashi a kashe haka ya hakura ya kyale shi da yake gidan hajia mariya yake da kwana shi yasa bai kira ammie ba har ya gama mata kwanan ta biyu ya dawo gidan ammie,, ba'baa mairo ta tambaye shi koya samu yaya Mallam.?,,ya sanar da ita yadda suka yi ..,tace a kyale shi kawai gobe su shirya su tafi.

Da kanta ta kira ammie ta faɗa mata gobe zasu tafi idan akwai abinda take son saya na tsaraba ta amshi kuɗi wurin mijinta ta shiga kasuwa yau.....,bata da wani kuzari tana zullumi haɗuwar ta da umma da ya hydar shi yasa koda ya bata kuɗi komi bata saya ba ta saka cikin handbag,,hakanta kwana jiki ba kwari duk da alaji habu yayi noticing yanayin ta sai bai ce da ita komi ba!

Da safe suka shirya tsaf ba'baa mairo tace ammie ta dibi kaya domin can zasu barta ko kwana biyu ne tayi da yan uwanta su gana...,jiki ba kwari ta diba piya biyu na dogayen riguna da hijab daya suka shiga mota"alaji habu yaja mota tana gefen shi, ba'baa mairo na gidan baya suka ɗauki hanyar kumbotso...


Koda suka isa an samu canje canje da kyar ta gane hanyar gidan nasu wanda ya koma ginin block yanzu an shafe da siminti amma ba'a yi penti ba,hawaye ya fara tarara a idanun ta saboda tunawa da mahaifin ta, ba'baa mairo ta riko hanunta"ki kwantar da hankalin kin domin abinda ya faru ba laifin ki bane ƙaddara ce..,share hawaye ta yi suka shiga da sallama,aka bar alaji habu cikin mota....,tsakar gidan yan mata biyu ne da wani yaro wanda ba zai wuce shekaru takwas ba suna cin abinci tare can daga cikin wani rumfar kuma wasu mata ne su uku saman tabarma suna hira ....,dayan ta amsa sallamar nasu tare da mikewa tsaye tana faɗin"maraba da baƙi sanun ku da zuwa ki iso nan yafi inuwa ke Hadiza dauko tabarta cikin dakin baban ku...,sai da gaban ammie ya bada dammmm,domin ba kowa b'ace take wanan magana ila nafisa matar ya hydar,yarinyar da aka kira da Hadiza sak ya hydar....,kan ammie a ƙasa suka karasa wurin da aka nuna masu ai nan take sauran matar suka mike a razane dayan tana faɗin"wa nake gani kamar ammie'n Mallam ƙarami.?,,sai lokacin ta daga ido ta gane mai maganar mama ladi ce, ɗaya dake kusa da ita kuma umman su ce "kuma mai ƙarfi ya kwashe ma ammie sai yi take babu kakautawa..m,yarinya ta dawo da tabarma ta shimfida ba'baa mairo ta riko hanun ammie suka zauna tace"ina yinin ku,mun sake ku lafiya.?

Nafisa ta amsa da lafiya lau sanun ku,sai ga yarinya ta dawo dauke da kwanon sha cike da ruwan randa Mai sanyi ta aje masu ta koma wurin cin abincin ta.

Mama ladi da umma dai suna tsaye ko wane da abinda yake sakawa cikin zuciyar shi..., ba'baa mairo tace da alama baku yi farin cikin dawo wanta ba..?,mama ladi tace mun fi kowa farin ciki domin shekaru da dama sun shude mu dade muna rokon Allah ya bayanar da ita cikin koshin lafiya Duk inda take har Allah ya ɗauki ran mahaifin su nayi wani auren yanzu haka ma nazo duba su ne Allah yasa da rabon zan ganta..,"ta nuna umma kin ga umman su tunda aka rasa wanan yarinya bata sake samun kwanciyar hankali ba yau ciwo gobe lafiya abinda ma ya kara tayar mata da hankali shine yadda baban wan su mai sunan mallam ba rabu da gida kwata kwata kusan shekaru takwas kenan rabon shi da ƙauyen nan yana can kudu wurin aikin soja,idan anyi magana yace aiki ya hana shi zuwa,sai godiyar Allah tunda dan uwan shi hydar na kula da ita sosai da matar shi nafisa...


Ba'baa mairo tace Allah ya jikan Mallam da rahama ya kyauta MAKWANCIN shi, Allah ya sanya wa hydar albarka ya shirya mashi zuri'a ku kuma Allah ya ƙara lafiya da nisan kwana shi kam wanan yaya Mallam Allah ya la'ance shi ya tozarta shi duniya da lahira Amin ammie ta amsa!!!

Umma da tunda suka shigo bata ce komi ba ta daga idanu ta kalli ammie da ba'baa mairo"saboda me za kiyi wa dana wanan mugun addu'ar.?,,saboda shine silar duk abinda ya faru da kowa kuma yake kan faruwar a yanzu"cewar ba'baa mairo...,zan so ku bani dama ayuwa mijin wanan yarinya iso ciki ku gaisa,sanan kuji komi a baƙin shi!

Mama ladi tace ammie dama kin yi aure ne ?..,ta gyada kai tana kallon umma wace fuskantar ke dauke da mamaki!!!

Cikin yaran dake cin abinci mama ladi tace "saidu sheka waje akwai bak'o ka shigo dashi ciki,,ke kuma nafisa kira mijin ki a waya domin shine namiji uba kuma babba yazo ayi komi a gaban shi yana shago...


Da sallama alaji habu ya shigo kanshi a ƙasa ya duka suka gaisa da mama ladi domin nafisa ta shiga daki ta kira mijinta,umma kam ko kallo basu isheta ba,,,,aka bashi kujera ya zauna.

Nafisa ta fito suka gaisa tace yace gashi nan tafe!,,,shiru wurin ya ɗauka kowa da abinda zuciyar shi ke kitsa mai,ammie dai kuka take sharba sosai.....,sallamar ya hydar ne ya saka kowa juya wa banda ammie,hannu ya mikawa alaji HABU suka gaisa,ya gaishe da ba'baa mairo koda suka haɗa ido da ammie a razane yaja da baya .!!!

Ammie dama kina raye shekaru da yawa ina kika shige baki waiwayi gida ba har Mallam ya koma ga Allah baki samu labari bane,menene ya faru dake haka?????

Mama ladi tace ka natsu hydar shine za suyi mana bayani yanzu domin wanda mutumi daka gani wai mijinta ne,wanan baiwar Allah gwaggon shi ce.....

Mamakin duniya ya kama ya hydar wai ammie tayi aure basu sani ba!!!

Nafisa ta gargada kan yaran ta uku suka shige daki domin maganar manya ne ba'a saka ta ciki ba!

Gyaran murya alaji habu yayi kamar yadda gwaggo na ta sanar daku Ni ke auren ammie,suna na Abubakar cikin Kano nake zama nan nake aiki, ƙaddara ya haɗa Ni da ammie har muka yi aure wanda naso mu fara zuwa nan wurin ku kafin ayi auren ta nuna rashin amince warta sai yanzu Allah baya ƙaddara,,zan so kuji komi Daga bakinta kamar yadda ta sanar dani kafin auren mu!

Ba'baa mairo ta shafa kan ammie dake faman kuka"Hadiza ki daure ki sanar da iyayen ki kaddarar data afka wa rayuwar ki kada ki biye komi na haɗa ki da girman Allah, faɗin gaskiya shi zai kawo fahimta da kuma gyara a tsakanin ki da iyayen ki da ɗan uwan ki.

Kuwa dai kallon su yake yaji wani ƙaddara ce wanan data hana ta zuwa gida shekaru da yawa...



Nanauyar ajiyar zuciya ta sauke,ya hydar mama ladi da ummana zan so ku yadda da abinda zan faɗa maku kuma ku yi mun adalci akan hukunci da zaku yanke a kai na ko yaya Mallam,,ranar da muka bar gidan nan da jiyar zamu Kano da yaya Mallam bamu tsaya ko ina ba sai Chemist domin a zubar da cikin dake jiki na!!!!

Sallati suka dauka dukan su banda alaji HABU da ba'baa mairo"ina kika samu ciki ammie.?,cewar umma.

Nan take ta kwashe komi ta sanar dasu tun daga lokacin daya fara manta da lalata har cikin sata samu zuwa alkawarin aure da suka yi har zuwan su gida da gano gaskiya ɗan wanta ne cikin su ɗaya zuwa guduwa da tayi har zuwa zaman ta gidan karuwai,haihuwarta da auren ta.!!!!

Kuka sosai mama ladi da ya hydar suke yi,ammie ma na kuka ita kuwa umma nan take ta fadi sumamiya!!!


""___ Idan kaga baby ba zaka gane ta ba domin baƙin ta har wani sheki yake yi gashi ta ƙara tsayi da girma jiki barakallah tana samun kulawa wurin Captain sadiq da sadeeyah,a makaranta ba'a barta a baya ba tana kokari sosai wurin mayar da hankali tunda karatun private school ba kaman na government bane,,,fanin islamiyya yanzu ta iya adu'oin iri iri,alwala,sallah da wanka duka sadeeyah na ƙara koya mata a gida...,wani lokaci suna fita cikin gari shopping ko park  su sha iska,aikin gida babu wanda bata iya ba daga girki,shara,gyaran daki dasu wanke wanke wanan ta jima da iya su tun a kano!

 
Suna haduwa da yaya mallam cikin barrack d'in Amma Bata ishe shi kallo ba bare ya amsa gaisuwa ta!!!

Cikin ikon Allah sadeeyah ta samu ciki murna wurin Captain sadiq da baby ba'a magana aka ci gaba da rainon shi cikin sauki komi tana ci babu wani dogon laulayi!
