  ""_______Ruwa aka zuba ba umma ta farfaɗo  idanun ta na kallon sama sai hawaye ke zirga ta kasa cewa komi.

Mama ladi tana jan majina tace,kaicon mu Allah wadaran naka ya lalace wanan abun kunya da mai yayi kama.?,ashe yasan laifin da ya aikata shine dalilin da yasa ya guje wa gida!,wayyo Mallam ka tafi kabar baya da ƙura wanan abun kunya ya wuce misali domin ko a zamanin mutanen farko ban taɓa jin makaman cin haka ya faru ba.....

Ya hydar duk'e wa yayi yana kuka sosai domin ya rasa abun cewa!

Ba'baa mairo tace,kuskure kam an riga da an tafka shi babba kuwa sai fatan Allah ya tsare gaba,abinda ya faru ba laifin kowa bane face ke umman su saboda son zuciya irin naku na mutanen karkara, ƙarancin ilimi da wayewar kai har wani kunyar ɗan fari zai sa ki budi baki ki faɗa ma ɗan cikin ki ba kece uwar shi ba,yanzu wa gari ya waya..?

Mama ladi tace,baiwar Allah banda cin fuska kuma azo har gidan mutum kuma a nemi gaya mai magana wanan abun ƙaddara ce kawai.!

Yoo ai ba cin fuska bane gaskiya ce zallan ta ki daina faɗin ƙaddara domin duka kuna da laifi da kuke boye masu gaskiya gashi yanzu wa yai ma kan wanshi ciki"shin jika ya saman maku ko d'iya ya haifa maku?,ita kuma kan wa ta haifa ma kanta ko d'iya?

Ammie kuka take dirja hada ajiyar zuciya domin kalaman ba'baa mairo sun yi tsauri da yawa a gare su, Alaji habu yace a bar wanan tashin tashinan abun da ya faru ya riga da ya faru wanda dama can haka Allah ya ƙaddara duk da ciki akwai son zuciya, ƙarancin ilimin da wayewar kai kamar yadda ba'baa mairo ta fadi amma babban mai laifi shine yaya Mallam saboda Allah ko ba ciki ɗaya suka fito ba ai bai kyautu ya aikata zina ba domin yana da ilimin addini,yasan haramun ne"Allah da kanshi yace kada mu kusanci zinah..,duk da zinan daya aikata zuciyar shi bata karaya ba sai ma ƙara kekashewa da tayi bayan yasan wanan yarinya yar uwan shi ce lokacin ya nemi salwanta rayukan ta,ta hanyar neman a zub da cikin wanda wanan ma wani babban zunubi ce na daban,a dalilin hakan ta faɗa muguwar rayuwa har Allah ya kawo karshen hakan,buri na ɗaya yanzu ku gafarta mata domin itama tayi kuskure data gane gaskiya bata sanar daku ba kuma bata dawo gare kuba ta biyewa son zuciya ta shiga duniya!


Ya hydar yace wanan gaskiya ne alhaji an tafka kuskure babba kamar yadda ka faɗa laifin yaya Mallam yafi yawa a cikin wanan barna yasan gaskiya kuma ya boye duk da yaga halin da iyayen mu suka shiga a dalilin rashin ammie har hakan ya jawo ma Mallam ciwon zuciya ya rasa ranshi,bai saka yaya Mallam yaji tausayin iyayen mu ya sanar dasu gaskiya ba,Nagode wa Allah da yasa Mallam baya raye bare yaji kuma yaga wanan mugun kwab'an da akayi a gidan shi...

Mama ladi tace,tabbas wanan Wata hikima ce daga ubangiji,ta riko hanun ammie"yanzu ina abinda kika haifa.?

Cikin kuka tace,yana wurin shi alaji ya kai mashi...,sallati tayi amma kunyi wauta salon yace can ya kashe ta burin shi ya cika shi mai gudun abun kunya.?

Alaji habu ya ƙara sunne kai ƙasa,a gafarce mu mama hikimar yin haka don zuciyar shi ya karaya ya dawo gare ku ya nemi gafara,amma kash haka bata samu ba domin na sanar dashi maganar zuwan mu kumbotso a karshe ma wayan shi bata shiga.

Ya hydar yace wallahi idan Allah ya haɗa idanu na dana yaya Mallam sai na zama ajalin shi, azzalumi mara imani kawai,ya saka iyayen mu cikin wani hali a dole shi ga ɗan boko ko..!!!

Mama ladi tace kul hydar ba tarbiyyar da muka baku kenan ba koshi da kaga ya aikata haka sharrin shaidan ne sai mu ci gaba da mashi adu'a har Allah ya karkato hankalin shi gida.


Umma da tunda suka zo bata ce komi ba sai yanzu ta buɗe baki tayi magana"tabbas yaya ladi na tafka kuskure babba wanan abu daya faru duka laifi na ne,domin har d'aki mai sunan Mallam ya same Ni yana tambaya da gaske ba nice na haife shi ba,na tabbatar mashi da mahaifiyar shi ta rasu riƙon shi kike yi ,ashe Bunda ke zuciyar shi da ban wayyo Ni jumma naci amanan Mallam a sanadiyan abunda na aikata jinin shi sun yi tarayya da juna har da rabo a tsakani,jinin shi tayi zaman kanta na shekaru da dama kaico na ashha na gurbata zuri'a nan gaba ɗaya"taci gaba da kuka sosai....


Ammie ta tashi ta shige jikinta tare da rungume ta tsam,itama ta rungume ta sosai suna kuka tace,umma ki gafarta min a dalilina kun zubar da hawaye babu adadi ban zama d'iya ta gari ba,na guje ku a lokacin da nafi kowa bukatar ku gudun ɓacin rai da b'anan suna da zan ja maku a wanan ƙauye ashe nabar baya da kura har mahaifina ya rasu da baƙin cikina wayyo umma idan baku yafe mun ba bazan taɓa ganin dai dai a rayuwata ba....,rufe mata baki tayi,kada ki sake faɗin haka ammie Mallam lokacin Shine yayi muma lokacin muke jira,ciwon shi daga Allah ne kuma abinda ya faru baki da wani laifi saboda a lokacin baki san ciwon kan ki ba,nikam na yafe maki duniya da lahira a kullum addu'a na Allah ya bayanar dake cikin koshin lafiya,ya kare ki a duk inda kike,,haka shima Mallam har ya koma ga Allah fatan alheri yake maku baki ɗaya.

Umma ta kalli ba'baa mairo da alaji HABU"a gaskiya kun yi namijin kokari wurin ceto rayuwar ta ga halaka sai fatan Allah ya biya ku ya saka da alheri.

Mama ladi tace wanan haka yake ammie Mallam har ranar da ya rasa ranshi albarka yake sanya maku duka bai riƙe ku da komi ba,mun yafe maki har Mai sunan mallam shima Allah ya karkato da hankalin shi zuwa gida ya ƙara shirya shi da abinda kika samu...,bayin Allah an gode sosai Allah ya biya ku!


Ya hydar ma godiya ya masu duk da har yanzu zuciyar shi a cunkushe take da baƙin cikin mugun abinda yaya Mallam ya aikata da baƙin fenti daya yaba wa zuri'arsu.



Mama ladi ta kwala wa nafisa kira,sai gata ta fito"ki daura masu girki azuhur ta kawo kai an bar su da yunwa.?

Alaji habu yace da ba'a wahalar da ita ba da anyi sallah zamu juya ne ,ita ammie zata maku kwana biyu tunda an jima ba'a hadu ba.

Umma taji daɗin hakan har cikin zuciyar ta domin tana son kebewa da d'iya nta,mama ladi tace hakan ma yayi dai dai hydar ko hura ne a samo masu ba zasu koma haka nan ba ko a hanya sa sha ko!

Ya gyad'a kai,da kanshi ya fita zuwa ɗan kasuwan garin ya samo masu fura mai kyau sakan hannu da nono sabon tatsa sai ƙanƙara guda biyu ya dawo gida,nafisa ta dame shi sosai ta kada ƙanƙara aka zuba masu cikin wani dogon jug da cups uku ta aje masu.

Ba'baa mairo suka ci gaba da hira tare da mama ladi tana basu lamarin yadda auren ammie ya kasance da irin zaman lafiya da suke yi a tare,umma dai jin su take amma zuciyar ta babu dad'i idan wanan labari ya fita cikin kauyen nan ai akwai aiki duk da yanzu ba kamar lokacin da bane an samu wayewan kai da canje canje.


Kiraye kirayen sallar azuhur ya saka suka tashi ya hydar da alaji habu suka fita Masallaci,ammie tayi alwala ta shige d'akin nafisa wanda ciki da palour ne babba yasha ledan ƙasa da kujeru irin nasu na ƙauye da kayan kallo,ciki kuma gadon katako ne babba, wardrob da mirror,nan ta aje jakanta ta gabatar da sallah ta jima sosai tana adu'oi.

Ba'baa mairo kuwa dakin umma ta shiga wanda shima babban palour ne mai dauke da dogon kujera ɗaya da gadon katako,ciki kuma gadon karfe mai runfa ne sai jere kwallaye (kayan da umma tazo aure da su),nan suka yi sallah suna tattaunawa irin nasu na manya.


Acan masallaci kuwa bayan sun fito yaya hydar ya gabatar da alaji habu wurin liman da mai gari a matsayin mijin ammie,daga nan suka dunguma sai rumfar mai gari suka ƙara tattaunawa aka jajanta ma juna akan rashin mallam,sauran aminan Mallam da suka rage da rai suka taya hydar farin cikin samun yar uwan shi a raye,,kuma suna addu'a shima yaya Mallam Allah dawo da hankalin shi gida.

Da alaji habu zai tafi ya basu address nashi da number waya sai damin kuɗi da ya aje masu,suna ta godiya ahanya ya dauke dubu ɗari ya miƙa ma ya hydar akan ya ɗauki dubu hamsin,ashirin  ashirin yaba umma da mama ladi,goma yaba matar shi domin tafiyar gagawa ne basu samu yin tsara ba a hanya ba",da kyar hydar ya amsa domin yana da gudun zuciya.

Da zasu tafi har bakin mota ammie ta raka su tana Daga masu hannu har ta bar ganin su!

Ya hydar ya fito da kuɗin ya ba kowa nashi,amma fafur umma taki amsa haka nafisa,umma tace ya rike tunda shike hidima da kowa yayi abinda ya kamata,,mama ladi kam ta amshi abunta tunda zuwa tayi kuma da yamma zata koma gidan mijinta.


Kasuwa ya hydar ya koma ya masu chefanai mai kyau ya kawo gida,ammie suka shiga aiki tare da nafisa...,take tambayar nafisa yanzu yaranta nawa..?

Murmushi tayi tace"bayan tafiyar ki da wata bakwai na haifi mai sunan ki hadiza itace babba(sa'ar baby Kenan,domin itama barin gidan ta da wata shida ta haihu),sai Rabi'ah( mai sunan mamata,sani da usman...,ajiyar zuciya tai Allah raya ya sanya masu albarka,nafisa ta amsa da amin suka ci gaba da aiki su a taɓa hira domin zabbi uku aka yanke suka tafasa da suka,sai tuwon shinkafa da miyar kuka wanda taji man shanu suka yi sai bayan la'asar suka Kamala ko wace ta shiga wanka suka yi Sallah.

Lokacin da mama ladi zata koma nasiha sosai mai ratsa jiki tayi wa ammie akan rayuwa da kuma zaman aure,ta ƙara jan hankalin umma akan abinda ya faru ya zama darasi gare su duka kada ta ɗauki zafi da ammie ko yaya Mallam domin ƙaddara ce,shima ya hydar ta ƙara kwantar mashi da hankali,da zata tafi ammie tayi mata alkawarin zata zo har dala wurin ta kafun ta koma kano suka rakata ta shiga mota.


Bayan sallar isha'i sun gama cin abinci wanda akwano ɗaya suka ci su uku"umma,ya hydar da ammie,yaran suka wuce islamiyya dare,nafisa ma tana shiga biyawa da dare wurin makwabciyar su.

Umma ta kalle su,dukan ku ba yara bane kowa ya mallaki hankalin kanshi zan su ku rike zumunci,ku zama masu yafiya da tsoron Allah a duk inda kuka kasance,ku riƙe ibada,amana da gaskiya"wanan abinda ya faru ku cire shi a ran ku kuskure ne an riga da an tafka ba'a bayar da hanun agogo baya adu'a shine takobin Mumini,zan su ko cire komi a zuciyar ku ko wanen ku  ya yafe ma ɗan uwan shi mu rungumi ƙaddara, Allah Ubangiji ya maku albarka tunda Allah ya ɗauki ran Mallam hydar ya dawo kusa dani da zama duk da ba wani ƙarfi ne dashi ba rufin asirin Allah ne har ya mallaki babban shago na kanshi yana sayar da kayan masarufi kuma yana noma har cikin  Kano yake shiga sarin kaya Allah bai nufa zaku haɗu ba,kinga shine ya gyara gidan nan ya mayar mana ciki da palour,gadon mahaifin ku bayan an cire wa yaya lami hakkin ta aka damk'a mai saura ya adana saboda hakkin kune duka tunda Allah ya kawo ki gobe da safe zamu wurin liman a ware hakkin ki a baki.

Ammie tace Nagode da kulawar ku duka akan mu,ya hydar Allah ya ƙara rufa asiri ya buda maka hanya na arziki a bisa hidima da kake yi da umma,zan so kaso na a barshi a hanun ka na mallaka maka shi babu abinda na rasa a rayuwa iya gwargwado mijina nada dukiya muna cikin rufin asiri bani da wani buri daya wuce in gama da duniya lafiya.

Yace na fahimta ammie,zan so ki amsa da hanun ki idan ma zaki yi kyauta ne daga baya kyayi domin hakkin ki ne...,umma tace haka ya kamata.

Tace ya hydar Nagode da takwara,murmushi yayi ai ko tayi gadon halin ki na don boko duk ta fisu ilimi yanzu haka aji ɗaya zata kwalejin (jss1),rabi na aji hudu,sani aji biyu Usman ba'a saka shi ba sai farkon zango.

Tace Allah raya mana so,ai shekarun ta ɗaya da baby Koda tabar wurina aji hudu zata shiga,duk suka yi shiru...!,can suka ji sallama alamar yaran da naman sun dawo,ya hydar yace lalai lokaci na tafiya har goma na dare tayi,umma ku kwana lafiya zan shige..,tace Allah bamu alheri,,ya kalli ammie gashi ba'a kawo wutan Nepa ba yau.!

Tace garin da sanyi sanyi ba kamar Kano ba,yace mu kwana lafiya tau.

Yaran ne suka shigo da sallama,tace maraba da yan makaranta,me aka koya maku yau.?
Suka shiga rero mata karatun abun sha'awa,tace madallah yanzu kuce kuyi fitsari mu kwanta...,umma ta shige kuryan daki ta haye gadon ta na karfi babba mai rumfa,rabi da Hadiza suka haye gadon palour'n,sani ya jawo katifan su shida usaman suka kwanta...,ammie ta tuna jakanta na dakin nafisa gashi suka rufe tana son saka kayan bacci zanin umma guda ɗaya ta daura bayan ta gabatar da shafa'i da wutri ta kwanta saman gadon tare da umma can wayarta ya fara ƙara kusa ta duba mijinta ne shaf ta manta dashi.

Sallama tayi ya amsa suka gaisa,yace watau yau gaji ga umma kin manta daki ko.?,murmushi tayi tare da kallon umma data rumtse idanu kamar mai bacci nan ko kunya ce ta rufe ta ana waya da suruki a kusa da ita"AFFUWA baby love wayar na cikin hand bag ne,ya kuka isa gida.?,lafiya klau gidan ya mun fadi sai kewar ki..,tace uhmm ka tafi wurin aunty ka kwana mana!,yace ai girkin kine"taso tace dafa da kake tsalake Ni ka tafi wurin ta kwana ranar girki na",tace kayi hakuri kwana biyu ne zan dawo,idan ba'baa mairo.?,gidan ta na sauke ta tace tayi kewan gidan ta tunda ta gama aaikita lalai sai na mayar da ita...,tace Allah sarki ba'baa kota jira in dawo...sun raba dare suna hira wanda duka akunnen umma har ta aje wayan ta gyara kwanciya...

Umma tace kina da abokiyar zama ne.?,tace eh umma amma ba guri ɗaya muke ba...,saboda me baku zauna wuri ɗaya ba.?,tambayar yazo mata a bazata!!

Ban san dalili ba umma tun farko shida kanshi ya raba...,ashe ba zaku taɓa fahimtar juna da zama lafiya da ita ba!!

Umma kamar kin san matsalar da nake fuskanta kenan a gidan aure na,matar nan nashi makira ce sosai tabi komi ta kanainaye bana sun kulawa gare shi kullum cikin zargi da faɗa...,shine nace ba zaku taɓa zama lafiya ba har sai kin zauna wurin ɗaya yanzu zan baki wani hikima ko sirri na zama da kishiya",kinga koda aka auro Ni mata biyu ke gidan nan nice cikin na uku muka zauna lafiya ba wai don basa don mijin su ko kishin shiga ba sai don fahimtar juna da muka yi ,sai gashi Allah ya bani kyauta wanda bai basu ba na haihu har ku uku cikin na hudun ne basu zo a raye ba yan biyu,,da ace da farko ya ake Ni wani wurin dabam zasu haɗe kai su rinka zargin yafi sona da kyautata mun saboda ji ɗaya ce zan fisu jin Dadi  aure da sake wa a gida ba musamman dana haihu zasu yi zargin komi nashi a kaina da yara na yake karewa,wanda wanan kishi da zargi zai saka su aikata koma menene domin karkato da hankalin mijin su a gare su ciki ya haɗa da"makirci,kissa,kis kisina,sharri,shirka,zafi da sauran su,, kin ga ba zamu taɓa samun jituwa da fahimtar juna ba sai tashin hankali,,,hikimar baki wanan labari shine ki nemi hanyar da zaman ki zai koma tare da yar uwan ki duk da nasan zai wuya saboda yana da arziki da gidaje,idan haka bata samu ba ki tabbatar a sati kin kai mata ziyara koda sau biyu ne ko uku,ki nuna mata mijinta bashi a gaban ki,yaranta su zama naki idan da hali ki kula dasu fiye da yadda take kula dasu,ki nasu a jiki fiye da yadda taja su a jiki,ki kyautata masu fiye da yadda take kyautata masu,,itama kima dukan abin dana lissafa kina ma yaran,ki kaskantar da kanki a gabanta,ki bata girman nan da Allah ya bata kuma take so,kada ki kuskure kiyi gidan mijin ku da ita ko ki fadi wani kyautatawa ko alheri da yake maki a gabanta"idan har kika riƙe wanan kin gama da ita babu sauran makirci da zargin juna zata saki jiki dake fiye da tunanin ki,shima zai so ki soyayya mai tsanani saboda zuciya tana son mai kyautata mata,,ki riƙe auren ki domin shine mutun ci da darajar ki idan kika saba wa mijin ki hadiza ban yafe maki ba duniya da lahira,wanan gwaggo tashi ta zama uwa a gare ki domin tayi maki halarci a rayuwa..., tabbas nasan abubuwa da dama zasu shige maki duhu a zamantakewar aure saboda baki tashi tare damu ba in Sha Allah kafin ki koma cikin kwana biyu zan koyar dake hikimomi na zaman aure cikin sauki.

Hawayen farin ciki ta shari tana murmushi,tabbas a duniya baka da kamar iyayen ka musamman uwa duk yadda wani zai soka ya faɗa maka gaskiya,ita uwa nata soyayyar na da ban ne haka kulawanta Allah ya ƙara nisan kwana umma na,ta amsa da amin(domin lokaci yayi da zata aje kunya ta tarairayi d'iya nta mace guda kwal a duniya).


Da safe bayan sun karya suka nufi gidan mai gari nan aka sake sabon gaisuwa da jajanta wa,aka kira liman yayi Kasafi aka cire wa ammie rabonta wanda ta damk'a ma ya hydar halak malak,daga nan suka zaga cikin gari dai ta gayar da mutane suka nufi kasuwa tare da ya hydar taga rumfar shi babban shago ginin block yasha kaya sai sam barka ta dawo gida Lokacin nafisa ta gama DAMBUN accha wanda shi tace tana so dama suka ci tana santi da dare kuma tuwon dasa taci da miyar danyen kubewa da man shanu,washe gari ranar da zata koma umma ta haɗa mata kayan kuka, dadawa,man gyada,man shanu,barkono,kayan kamshi,da sauran abubuwa masu na gida sai zabbi da aka siya mata guda hudu da zasu tafi dashi.


Umma da kanta ta dafa mata tantabaru yan shila da suka sha haku kuwa,sai haɗin kaza na musamman,ta zaunar da ita sai data cinye tas ta kowa da nonon raƙumi da yaji ridi,,,tace idan sun biya wurin yaya ladi zata amshi haɗin kumban dabino da tsumin baure da zata tafi dashi.
Zaunar da ita umma tayi tana ƙara bata shawara akan yadda zata gyara jikin ta a saukaka har alaji HABU ya iso wuraren azuhur lokacin ta shirya don sun yi waya tace ya sayo mata wayoyinsu kirata nokia kana na guda biyu da layin zain wanda shi yafi karfi a kauyen sai ƙaramin generator.

A palour'n umma ta sauke shi aka aje mai hura mai sanyi da tuwon shinkafa da yaji naman kaji,aje kunya yayi yaci ya koshi yana santi..,cikin wayoyin da yazo dasu taba umman ta ɗaya (dama ya hydar da nafisa suna da shi),tace ɗaya mama ladi zata kai wa, generator Kuma ta damk'a ma ya hydar domin basu wani sun wutan lantarki,aka sauke masu bugun shinkafa da kayan shayi...,umma nata saka albarka da zasu wuce tace ya hydar ya bata Hadiza su tafi tare tunda ba'baa mairo ta koma gidan ta sai a saka ta jss1 ɗin a can!

Yace gaskiya tayi hakuri ba za'a d'aura masu lalura ba,umma ma tace bai kamata ba sai dai su rinka zuwa mata hutu..,nan ta saka masu kuka wai basu yafe mata ba kenan..!,ya hydar yace dama kina nan da wannan shagwaba baki.?

Alaji habu yace tana nan dashi,ya'yan mu a taimaka a bamu Hadiza ta ɗebe mata kewa..,dole aka haɗa kayan Hadiza dameta tsalle zata koma kano da zama suka kama hanya da kuma ammie ta rabo da umma akan sai ta sake zuwa!

Dala suka fara zuwa wurin mama ladi wacce ta haɗa masu sha tara na arziki basu wani zauna ba suka kama hanyar Kano...



"""'A Lagos kuma abubuwan na tafiya yadda ya kamata domin yanzu baby an zama big girl saboda zaman Barrack yanzu bata cika yin hausa ba sai turanci cikin Sa'a suka zana jarabawar gama primary school aka masu graduation Wanda baby ta amshi kyaututtukan da dama,yanzu islamiyya kawai take zuwa,aikin gida duka ita keyi saboda cikin da sadeeyah ke fama dashi.

Akwana a tashi babu wuya jarabawar su baby ya fito taci gaba da zuwa COMMAND SCIENCE  SECONDARY SCHOOL (GIRL'S ONLY) suka fara jss1 abun su gwanin sha'awa tana fahimtar karatun ta har suka rubuta first term exam's lokacin cikin sadeeyah ya shiga watanni tara.


Wata rana ta tashi da nakuda aka dauke ta sai hospital dake cikin barrack d'in ta kwashe kusan awanni shida kafin ta samu ɗan ta namiji kato dashi mai cikakkiyar lafiya,dama suna da landline cikin gidan ya kira baby ta kawo ruwan zafi a flask da abinci..,ranta fari kal jin aunty nata ta samar mata K'ani ta haɗa komi cikin basket ta Kama hanya.., yazo giftawa ta wurin gidan yaya Mallam ta Ganshi yana shirin shiga mota,tace gud afternoon Papa..!,yace how u?
Ta amsa da am fine,yace ba hospital zaki ba.?,tace eh..,yace mu tafi nima zan zabi ya faɗa mun jin samu brother Yana murmushin,itama murmushin tayi ta aje basket a back sit ta shiga gaba "tana noticing nashi tunda ta gama primary school yaji itace Best female student of the year yake nan nan da ita yadda gift ya jawo mata na wani diamond necklace,Kuma ya ruki captain Sadiq gafara shike nan suka fara shiri har yana zuwa cin abinci gidan jefi jefi...

Da yake HOSPITAL din babue Nisa basu wani ɗauki lokaci ba suka Isa ta riga shi shiga sai gashi ya shigo yana ma Captain sadiq tsiya,,suka gaisa da nsadeeyah baby ta haɗa mata tea ta mika mata sanan ta dauki boy d'in dake faman baccin gajia...,yaya Mallam ya leka fuskar yaron, yace ahh wanan namu ne bahilace ne sak Mahaifin shi,deeyah ya akayi haka.?, wannan ya nuna ya fiki energy kenan"suka saka dariya duka!

Har zuwa yamma suna wurin aka sallame su,suka nufi gida,dama ya buga gidan su Yola kuma ya buga kaduna aka gobe zasu kamo hanya...

Matan abokan su cike da gida aka daura ruwan wanka akayi ma BABY'N,mai jego ma tayi,dama sun saba da baby Nan ta daura hurimin tuwon masara da tace tana son ci wanda yaji kanwa da miyar kuka.


Da safe baby ta gyara gidan sai kamshi yake ta kunna turaren wuta,ta haɗa masu breakfast,,zuwa goma na safe jirgin mutan yalo ya iso mutane biyu duka yanen shi mata,yan Kaduna da suka biyo mota suna hanya...,baby ta tarbe su da bai ci da kayan ciye ciye ta koma palour suna zaune d'akin sadeeyah lokacin Captain sadiq na office.

Aunty kubra(elder sister captain sadiq),ta dake rike da BABY'N ta kalli sadeeyah"wai yar aiki ce kika samu haka?

Aunty falmata tace wallahi naga yarinyar nada kokari..,sadeeyah tace ba yar aiki b'ace aunty Yar uwanta captain aminu CE yazo da ita daga ƙauyen su tayi karatu nan shine take taimaka mun (ta masu ƙarya).., Allah yasa bata da ɗan hali dai kin san ɗan adam yanzu ba'a shaidan shi saboda yaran kauye nan idan suka shigo birni idanun su na kan mijinka ko ɗan abunka"cewar aunty falmata.

Aunty kubra tace gaya mata gaskiya dai nasan halin ta da tausayi bata da wuyar sakin jiki da mutane...,tace ayyah baby ba haka take ba wallahi aunty tana da tarbiyya sosai!

Kuda Captain sadiq ya dawo office tare da yaya Mallam suka shigo ya zo gaishe da baƙi"aunty kubra tace KU zauna zan yi magana daku wanan zuwan naka ne bachelor Aminu (dama haka take kiran shi),suka sa dariya,amma kam sai da gaban shi ya fadi domin aunty kubra duk suka haɗu sai tayi mashi maganar aure..

Kafun nan sadiq abinda ya kawo mu tafiyar za muyi da matar ka Yola domin can zata yi wanka ayi bukin suna har tayi arba'in sai ta tafi Kaduna tayi kwana biyu a can zaka dauke ta ku dawo!!!

  ______""Yayi dai dai aunty kubra Dama muna saka ran zamu samu ƙarin girma ko canjin ma'aikata duk wanda ya yiwo ciki adu'a mu Allah ya zaba mana mafi alheri.

Ta kalli yaya Mallam kai kuma akwai yarinyar dana sama maka mai natsuwa. Nasan zaka yaba da zaɓi na idan kun samu lokaci Ku zo Yola ka ganta sai ka sanar da magabatan ka ayi Maganar aure..,sosa kai yayi, Allah ƙara girma aunty Babba sai kin gan mu, d'aki ta koma wurin mai jego.

Captain sadiq ya kira baby ta kawo masu abinci tare da sarving ɗin su,yaya Mallam yace lafiya kake ta kallon ta haka.?,uhmm baza ka gane bane wallahi ina matuƙar tausaya mata yanzu idan sadeeyah ta koma yola ina zata ci gaba da zama kasan akwai tafiya a gabana ruwa Canada training zai ɗauki lokaci kafin in dawo.

Na fahimta mai zai hana ta dawo gida na da zama  tunda kaga sadeeyah sai lokacin da ka dawo zaka dauko ta kuma lokacin nima zan tafi training ɗin sai ku amshe ta...,naushi ya kai ma yaya Mallam a kafada yana faɗin"na ki wayan ɗan iska ba cewa kayi baka sonta ba ka bani ita halak malak ina ruwan ka da wurin da zata zauna.?

Murmushi yayi Captain sadiq kenan kona maka bayani ba zaka taɓa gane wa ba,is just that dai ba'a canja wa tuwo suna ne ko wani irin kwaskwarima za'a mashi tuwo ne dai kuma naga taimako zan yi idan ka amince shike nan idan baka amince ba tabi sadeeyah yola asirin mu ya tonu..., point of correction"asirin ka ya tonu dai", Allah yasan ko lokacin da mike big guy dina ban aikata zina  ba bare yanzu da tsufa ya fara kama Ni,na zama baban boy"Yana dariyar muguntan...!,,,uhm ƙaddara ce ai kuma bata wuce ta faɗa kan kowa ba don uban ka.

Ɗan iska wane irin ƙaddara bayan har da yanzu kana nan matan banza,cikin matan ma kazamai , kabilun kafuran bariki...,wallahi tir da halin ka gwara ka tuba kayi aure tun lokaci bai kure maka ba Domin ga baby Nan ta fara nono saura period musha buki.(Lol)

Duka ya kai mashi,ya doje"gaskiya na faɗi AA tunda maman baby tayi aure ta yadda da ƙaddara kai ma kayi settling,abun kunya ina best man ɗin ka amma ban san sirrin ka ba,wai yaushe kayi wanan ciki kuma wace yarinya ka yaudara bani da labari???

Shiru yayi na ɗan Lokaci wanan is little secret,mu ci abinci kawai...

Da dare aunty falmata suka kwana tare da sadeeyah a d'akin ta,baby Kuma da aunty kubra, captain sadiq gidan yaya Mallam ya kwana sai da asuba yan kaduna suka iso aka tafi tasha dauko su...

Bayan sun yi wanka sun yi breakfast aka ƙara gagaisawa suka ga baby'nsu mai kyau kamar baban shi..,younger sister na sadeeyah ke tambaya inda ta samu yarinya.?,ce mata kawai tayi yar uwan Captain aminu ce take taya ta zama!

Da yake baby Hutu suke yi  idan ta gama aikin da ya kamata tayi zuwa 4pm take wuce wa islamiyya sai 6pm su dawo,bata wani sakin jiki da yaran Barrack d'in gudun tonon asiri da kana nan maganganu,iyakan ta dasu hiran makaranta.

Bayan kwana biyu Captain sadiq ya sanar da sadeeyah shawarar da Yan uwan shi suka yanke na wuce wa yalo da ita tare da yan uwanta da an gama bukin haihuwa sai sister's nata su koma Kaduna ita kuma taci gaba da wanka a Yola.

Tambayar data jeho Mai ya tayar mashi da hankali"ina baby zata koma da zama.?,,ya faɗa mata yadda suka yi da yaya Mallam d'azu..,tace gaskiya Ni fa ban yadda dashi ba tun farko yadda ya nuna ma yarinyar nan kiyayya yanzu yace wai sonta yake akwai alaman tambaya.?

Zargi babu kyau deeyah,koma menene shine ya haife ta bamu san dalilin shi na nuna mata kiyayya a farko ba haka yanzu ma bamu da tabbas akan soyayar da yake nuna mata,i believe ba zai aikata abinda zai regreting nan gaba ba tunda yana da hankali da tunani,kuma kinga bai kyautu mu zauna daga Ni sai ita cikin gida ba tunda ba muharamin ta bane ni.

Shike nan na amince Allah yasa babu wani mugun nufi a karkashin wanan soyayya da yake nuna mata,kuma don Allah ka dunga saka idanu akan lamuran ta koda ta koma can gidan kafin ka tafi Canada..!, kan shi ya daura a shoulder nata in Sha Allah my deeyah,,sallama sister ta suka yi(Babba) ya tashi kunya ce ya fita.

Tace sannu yar daɗi miji kowa na palour'n kina nan lake da miji.!,,kai yaya Zahra sak'o ya bani daga yan uwan shi na tafiyar mu yola Kuma can zan zauna...

Katse ta tayi saboda naki iyayen sun mutu ko baki da gata zaki tare gidan uwar miji wankan jego,ko raina arzikin gidan ku kika yi.?"tura baki tayi domin tasan halin Yaya Zahra da shegen fadan tsiya ga jinini",Yaya Zahra sak'o ne suka bashi ya bani yanzu ki tattauna dasu yadda kuke so!

Na fiki sanin hakan shashasha idan kika yi wasa yanzu zan sanar da inna (naman su,itama mafada ciya ce na gaske),shiru tay mata ,ta fita tana sababi har palour'n wurin su aunty kubra tana faɗa masu ba zasu yadda da hakan ba sai idan anyi suna su wuce da ita kaduna tayi wanka wurin iyayen ta...

Falmata tashe sakar mata magana,aunty kubra tace shike nan ai idan sun isa yola su tattauna da hajja (maman Captain).


Ana gobe zasu wuce Yola sadeeyah ta zaunar da baby a d'akin ta nasiha sosai tayi mata masu ratsa jiki ta bata kyautar kayayyaki da abubuwan buƙata na mata da alƙawarin zata ma Captain magana idan zai zo bukin suna yazo da ita.

Haka babu ta kwana jiki a sanyaye domin har ta hango ta cikin irin rayuwar data taso a baya muddin ta rasa aunty sadeeyah da uncle Sadiq asirin ta ya tonu a duniya,fatan ta ya ƙare sai yadda Allah yayi da ita,kuka taci cikin daren nan kafun ta samu bacci ya tafi da ita dama ta barma baƙi d'akin ta a palour take kwana.

Tunda safe ta tashi ta d'aura breakfast tayi sarving kowa ta gyara gidan,matan Barrack d'in not shigowa yima sadeeyah Allah ya kiyaye zuwa goma na safe yan uwanta sun gama shirya mata komi da zata tafi dashi cikin jakuna,goma da rabi suka fito baby sai sharan hawaye take yi..

Motaci biyu suka yi amfani dasu da land cruiser na yaya mallam wanda aunty kubra,falmata da zainab(sister sadeeyah) ke ciki,,range rubber na Captain sadiq wanda sadeeyah na gaba,baby riƙe da yaro a gidan baya, sai yaya Zahra suka nufi airport zuwa karfe 11:30am suka tashi zuwa yola jikin sadeeyah a sanyaye saboda tana ji a jikinta wani babban al'amari zai faru da baby.

Tun a hanya Captain sadiq yace da baby ta haɗa kayanta da duk abinda take da buƙata zata koma gidan papan ta saboda shi zai tafi...,kuka taci gaba da rairawa cikin motan domin sai data ayana a ranta za'a koma gidan juya idan har wanan bayin Allah basa tare da ita ",,hakuri ya shiga bata yana nuna mata Captain Aminu mutum ne mai daɗin zama sai dai yana da zafi sosai kuma shi ya nemi data dawo wurin shi na ɗan Lokaci saboda haka ta kwantar da hankalin ta babu abinda zai faru,idan ma ya faru ɗin wanan ya zama daga cikin ƙaddara ta a rayuwa,ya ɗauki alkawarin duk runtsi zai tsayaa mata a rayuwa kuma ya bata kariya sai in bashi a raye.

Shiru tayi tana sauraran shi har suka isa gida motar yaya Mallam na bayan nasu ta shiga ta haɗa komi nata ya rako ta cikin gidan.


Bata yi aikin komi ba domin Peter na nan iyakan ta kallon film idan ta gaji ta koma dakin ta kwanta har zuwa dare da yake ranar Friday ne babu islamiyya,su Captain suna wurin aiki.


Bayan sallar isha'i gidan yaya Mallam suka haɗu su biyar da abokan su nan suka ci dinner suna ta hira har zuwa 10pm kowa yace zai shige BLOCK nashi lokacin Captain sadiq yace wai ina daughter be.?

Yace inaga tayi bacci,ok ka faɗa mata da safe aunty'n ta sun sauka lafiya tana gaishe ta..yace ok mu kwana lafia gwauro.


Sai da ya rufe ko ina ya nufi d'akin baby fuskan Nan tashi a murtuk'e yayi banging door d'in,"kwance take saman bed tana bacci hankalin ta kwance..,ya riko kafafun ta duka biyu ya jawota feyt ya zubar da ita saman tyles ɗin.!

Baƙin ta ɗauke da sallati ta buɗe idanu,yaya Mallam ta gane tsaye kamar a mafarki..,don uwarki daina kallo na,shegiya kawai,babu yadda banyi ba kada kizo duniya amma sai da ta haife ki,kika zame mun ibilishiya,kin hana Ni jin daɗin rayuwa kullum da baƙin Cikin ki nake kwana nake tashi,ban taɓa son ki ko son ganin ki ba cikin duniya kina numfashi,wallahi tun da aka samu cikin ki duke wani farin ciki da walwala nawa ya kau,na zama abinda na zama ne(army) kawai don na wulakanta rayuwar ki idan kina raye,idan kin mutu in wulakanta rayuwar uwarki...,sai gashi Allah ya kawo ki cikin sauki ƙarƙashin kulawa na!

Now listen and listen Good,zan baki wasu task guda Uku idan har kika aiwatar da su yadda na umurta shine zai wanke mun zuciya yasa na yanta ki,ki zama Y'A KAMAR KOWA!!

Ita dai binshi take da idanu bata gane inda maganganun shi suka do saba,ashe dama yasan makircin da ya ke kulawa shine dalilin sake wani shi da ita?

_TASK NO-1:-za kiyi tarayya(having sex) da Captain sadiq kafun ya wuce Canada kuma ki tabbatar cikin shi ya shiga jikin ki!!_

Idanu ta ci gaba da zare wa tana girgiza kai alamar ba zata iya ba hawaye na zuba a idanun ta...

_TASK NO-2:-zaki shaidawa duniya Cikin shine ta hanyar shigar da ƙara kuma ki nuna so kike ki haifa masa cikin shi!!_

_TASK NO-3:-zaki zauna a duk inda yake da cikin nan har ki haihu duniya ta sani ki damk'a mashi abinda kika haifa shike nan ki dawo wurina!!_

_kuka ta shiga yi mai sauti tana faɗin"papa bani da kaman su a duniya sun yi mun abinda iyaye na da suka haife Ni basu mun ba,wallahi ba zan iya cin amanar aunty sadeeyah da UNCLE Sadiq ba!!_


Baby!,Baby!!,Baby!!!.., either u do as I said ko ki nemi wani uban bani ba,dama na gaji da ganin ki a duniya,wallahi i never wish for your success,I always pray for your downfall cuz u are a disgrace to me and entire family bastard kawai, na baki nan da gobe ki fara aiwatar da wanan aiki ko ina kashe ki na rantse da Allah Yadda na haifi shegiya kema sai kin haifi shege kin goya,d'akinya fice a d'akin...

Kuka mai cin rai ta fashe dashi tare da buga kanta da bangon d'akin"wayyo Allah na,Ni baby laifin me na aikata wa Allah da yasa na zo duniya ta wanan hanya..? kaifin me nayi Wa iyayena da basa sona..?,kowa gudu na yake babu wanda ke so na rab'e shi sai wayan'nan bayin Allah da suka mayar dani Y'A ,suka nuna wa duniya"NIMA Y'A CE  kamar kowa,,,wayyo ammie na kin cuce Ni da kika dawo dani wurin papa mugu ne azzalumi baya tsoron Allah,,ammie kin cuce Ni da baki mayar dani wurin mahaifiyata ba"YA ALLAH KA ƊAUKI RAYUWA TA A WANAN DARE KO ZAN HUTA DA WANAN BAK'AR UKU'BA(subhanallah)!!

~🅿35-36~


____""A daren ranar bata runtsa ba tayi kuka,kuka na fitan hankali,tayi adu'ointa na neman kariya Daga sharrin papan nata,ta saka wanan ta kwance wan can har aka kira Sallah asuba tana jin lokacin daya fita zuwa masjid kamar mutumin kwarai"tace cikin zuciyar ta",sallah ta gabatar itama ta jima cikin sujjada tana neman zaɓin Allah akan wanan masifa da yake son kunno Kai a tsakanin ta da mutanen da suka riƙe ta da gaskiya,ga Bacci tana aji amma ina bata da kwanciyar hankali.

Zuwa 9:40am ta fito bayan ta watsa ruwa  babu kowa a palour'n da alama har kitchen da alama yaya Mallam ya fita wurin aiki,break fast ta gani saman dianing table"shima Peter ya gama aikin shi ya fita",ruwan tea Kawai tasha ta rasa mafita wa zata faɗa ma damuwar ta ya fahimce ta.?

Saboda Allah yanzu da taga gaban mahaifiyar ta akwai wanda ya isa ya tunkaro ta da wanan mumunan aiki.?,wai uban daya haife ka shine ke da wanan irin buri akan ka,saboda shi ƙaddara ta afka mai ya aikata zina shike nan ita ma yake so ta aikata,zinah ma da aminin shi wanda ya rike ta da amana,kai gaskiya ba zata iya ba sai dai ya kashe ta wallahi.

Zuwa yamma ya dawo daga wurin aiki har d'aki ya same ta,tana zaune idan banda aikin tunani babu abinda take yi"wani irin tsawa ya daga mata.!,babu alamar tsoro a idanun ta  itama tayi mashi kuri domin tabar jin tsoron shi a yanzu...

Wane shawara kika yanke akan assignment Dana baki.?,ko dar babu cikin zuciyar ta tace"ba zan iya aikata wa ba!!

Bindiga ya zaro yayi pointing nata dashi kashe ki ba abu bane mai wahala a  wurin na kuma na kashe banza babu abinda zai faru....,hawaye na zirya a idanun ta tace"papa KA kashe Ni domin nafi bukatar mutuwa da rayuwa a wanan lokaci rayuwata bata da wani amfani wanda suka haife Ni basu dauke Ni a bakin komi ba  menene amfanin rayuwar tawa.?,na taso cikin wahala da ɓacin rai,ban taɓa sanin daɗin uwa ba domin ba tare da ita na tashi ba  ammie itace ma cen da ta raine Ni da daɗi babu daɗi kuna tare har Allah ya haɗa Ni daki,,wallahi kwara ita sau dubu da kai...

Tsawa da daka mata"yi mun shiru makira,kalaman ki ba zasu taɓa tasiri a zuciyata ba wallahi ko kukan jini za kiyi yau sai na kashe ki,sanan ita ammie tace maki ba itace mahaifiyar ki ba.?

Zan sanar dake abinda baki sani ba a yau  Hadiza(ammie) itace mahaifiyar ki uwar mu ɗaya uban mu ɗaya da ita,Ni aminu Mallam nine elder brother na ammie Kuma Ni ne nan nayi mata ciki taurin kanta yasa ta haife ki ,wallahi kashe ki zai fi komi faranta rayuwata a duniya....

Duke wa ƙasa tayi tana sallati duk wani addu'a daya zo bakin ta shi take yi"wanan wane irin kwamachala ne.?,yaya yayi wa kan wansa ciki???

Ai ko a garin arna ba'a wanan ɗan yen aiki,,papa don Allah kace ba gaskiya bane abinda kunnuwa na suke ji,wayyo naga rayuwa,wane irin jahilci ne haka,wane irin dabban ci ne wanan,iyayen ku suna ina kuka aikata hakan?

Suna gidan uban ki muka aikata yar iska ban yi niyyar sanar da kowa wanan sirri ba tunda har kika saka na fade abinda na sheka goma sha biyu ina boye wa wallahi kashe ki zan yi!

Ai kwara na mutu da wanan baƙin fenti da kuka shafa mun,gaske dai bana samun kulawa a wurin ammie ashe itama haushi na take ji,shi yasa bata so nayi mata maganan MAHAIFIYATA ashe itace ta haife Ni!wallahi tur da hali iri naku, Allah wadai da wannan kazamin rayuwa....,mari ya dalla mata,har kin isa ina fadi kina fadi!!!

Na isa har na wuce Captain Aminu ka kashe Ni nace ta riko hanuwan shi duka biyu tana ƙara tura bindigan a cikin ta....

Crut Peter dake labe a kofar d'akin tun da suka fara hayaniyar wanda haka ya nuna yaji komi da suka fadi,a guje ya fita zuwa block ɗin Captain sadiq dai dai zai fita cikin gari suka yi karo da Peter...!

Yana hakki yace oga Sadiq kazo babu lafiya ,oga aminu zai kashe yarinyar nan dake gidan ka.!!!

A guje ya bankade peter ya Isa gidan Captain Aminu,yana shiga ya jiwo hayaniyar su a d'akin baby, bankade kofar yayi a guje ya shiga tsakanin su sai da Captain Aminu ya faɗa saman bed,ITA kuma ta haɗa kai da bango,,,hanunta ya jawo ta tura ta waje yana faɗin"run for ur life baby,tsayawa   tayi kamar gunkie ko gezau bata yi ba..,tsawa ya kara daka mata" i Said get out!!!

Fita tayi zuwa gidan Captain sadiq tana hawaye domin zuciyar ta a bushe yake,da wanan mugun labari da taji wallahi gwara mutuwar ta!!

Kallon kallo suka shiga yiwa juna na Tsawon lokaci..,Captain sadiq yace yaushe ka fara shaye shaye ban sani ba.?,laifin menene ta aikata da har za kayi barazanar ɗauka rayuwar ta saboda Allah yarinya ƙarama kamar baby zaka nuna wa bindiga!!!!


Waya faɗa maka barazana nake mata sadiq..,kafi kowa sanin halina bana barazana da bindiga koda ga abokan gaba idan na nuna sai na harba,kafi kowa sanin idan na har ba sai rai ya fita...

Yanzu kana nufin kashe ta kake da niyyar yi.?,sosai ma kuwa and babu wanda ya isa ya hana Ni aikata hakan domin ba uban wani ne ya haifa mun ita ba!

Kada ka manta da hanun ka,ka danka amanar baby a hanuna na wanda  da bakin ka lokacin ka mallaka mun baby halak malak baka da wani sauran iko a kanta,idan ma wani laifi ta aikata ka sanar dani mana.

Sadiq laifin da baby ta aikata ɗaya ne"shine zuwan ta duniya a matsayin 'ya ta wanda idan ba kashe ta nayi ba hankali na ba zai taɓa kwanta wa,zuciyata ba zai taɓa samun farin cikin da nake nema ba,sadiq a dalilin haihuwar yarinyar nan na tafka Babban abun kunya wanda zai rinƙa bibiyata har karshen rayuwata,adalilin haihuwar ta na rabu da iyaye da kowa nawa,adalilin haihuwarta na kasa yin aure wanda inada bukatar haka...

Dafa shi yayi"Aminu ka yadda da ƙaddara ba'a kanka aka fara aikata zina ba kuma ba'a kanka aka fara haihuwa ba tare da aure ba,ka rungumi ƙaddara ka riƙe yarinyar nan ka bata kulawa da take da buƙata wanda bata samu a baya ba,ka nemi sulhu da yan uwanka Finally kayi aure kaci gaba da istigfari.


Sadiq cikin baby ba irin cikin shegen da aka saba yi bane a duniya"NI AMINU NAYI WA YAR UWATA HADIZA  CIKI TA HAIFI BABY,YAR UWATA WACCE MUKE UWA ƊAYA UBA ƊAYA ka fahimce Ni Sadiq"ya Fadi cikin Daga murya yana kuka....

Wa'iyazubillah,allahuma ajirni fii musibatii,auzubillahi minash shaidanin rajin,"zame wa ƙasa yayi ya zauna,, Are U kidding me aminu?,what come over u,how comes????


Shima zama yayi kusa da sadiq,wallahi sadiq abinda ya faru kenan banda masaniya ciki ɗaya muka fito dai da komi ya faru,ka faɗa mun wani irin zuciya nike dashi da zan amince da baby.?,da wani idanu zan kalli iyaye na.?,me duniya zata ce game da wanan kwaba....

Shiru na ɗan lokaci Captain Sadiq na nazari...koma menene Aminu ka yadda da ƙaddara kayi accepting baby a matsayin 'ya kamar kowa sanan ka tafi ga iyayen ka nasan hankalin su a tashe yake har sai lokacin da suka ganka.

Sadiq babban abun baƙin ciki wai kamar ammie yar UWATA zata haɗa kai da wani a dawo da baby wuri na saboda tana son tona mun asiri a duniya, recently ya Kira Ni Wai sunyi aure yana so muje kumbotso tare shine dalilin da yasa na canja layi.

Amma kayi kuskure koma ina ga baka fahimce ta ba,ita mace ce dole gidan wani zata ba lallai ne ya aure ta baby na hanun shi ba,babu inda ya fi dace wa da baby kamar gidan ka tunda kai namiji ne matar ka zata iya kula da baby ko don farin cikin ka,,yanzu nasan iyayen ku sun san komi kawai ka hakura ka tafi gida.

Sadiq ba zan tafi ba domin wanan ba ƙaramin abu bane,ita kuma ammie bata da hujjar dawo da baby wurina tun a farko na umurce ta da a zubar da cikin baby Bata aminta ba,yanzu menene nawa,,don Allah sadiq ja cire kanka cikin wanan rikicin ka bani baby Kawai in kashe ta kowa ya huta.

Yanzu kayi wanka ka huta za muyi magana anjima..,da kyar ya lalaba shi ya shiga wanka ya fito daga gidan nan ya haɗu da Peter,tambayar shi yayi abinda ya faru...,ya faɗa mai duk abinda ya sani.

Yace ya shiga ya kwaso mai kayan baby ba tare da sanin Captain Aminu ba yana jiran shi.

Koda ya shiga gida baby na aikin kuka,itama wanka yace ta shiga magrib ya gabato,,babu musu ta shige toilet kuka ya hana ta aikata komi har aka kira Sallah.

Koda Peter ya kawo kayan a parlour ya aje ya rufe gidan ta waje ya wuce masjid.

Daya fito cikin gari ya nufa inda manyan bus suke tsayawa na tafiya zuwa garuruwa masu tashi cikin dare da kyar ya samu ticket guda biyu ya biya zuwa 10pm motocin zasu tashi.

Sai bayan isha'i ya dawo gida koda ya buɗe tana zaune saman sallaya dama ya masu take away na abinci ya aje mata ya wuce d'akin shi ya kai minti talatin yana waya kafin daga bisani ya shiga wanka ya shirya cikin shirt da wando,ya haɗa brief case din shi .

Koda ya shiga d'akin tana wurin zaune bata ci abinci ba...!,baby ya Kira sunan ta.
Kallon shi tayi ba tare data amsa ba.

Inaso ki ɗauki abinda ya faru yau a matsayin kaddarar ki,ki sani ko wane bawa yana da nashi jarabawar na rayuwa,ke musulma ce wace ta yadda da ƙaddara mai kyau da mara kyau,ki yadda dani Wallahi ban taɓa sanin abinda Aminu ya aikata kenan ba wanda ya raba shi da kowa nashi sai a yau,tabbas zuciyar shi cike yake da kiyayar ki zalla babu soyayya"baby zaman ki ba zai yiwo a cikin Barrack d'in Nan ko garin nan ba,, Allah yasan nida sadeeyah muna son ki duk da bamu san komi a kanki ba yanzu ma sanin wanan sirri ba zai canja komi daga son da muke maki ba,,Kinga yanzu saura kwana biyu bukin suna,naso mu tafi Yola tare amma hakan ba zai samu ba saboda ba kowa zai fahimce Ni ba akanki zan so kiyi hakuri zuwa karfe goma zamu  bi luxurious bus mu bar Lagos...

A razane ta kalle shi,wallahi bazan koma wurin ammie ba kwara in zauna ya kashe ni gwara shi ya nuna mun soyayya duk da na karya ne,amma ita ban taɓa samun kwatan kwacin haka ba ta tsane ni,tsana mai girma tunda har ta iya boye dangan taka dake tsakani na da ita.!!

Wait baby,ba wurin ammie zan mayar dake ba,wurin da zan kai ki idan ba kece zaki nemi ammie ba har ki bar gidan ba zata taɓa ganin ki a  ba, kinga lokaci ya kusa ga waya nan karama (baby Nokia) akwai layi a cikin number na Dana sadeeyah duka na saka maki dashi zaki tafi idan kina da bukatar wani abu ko akwai abinda ya shige maki dubu ki neme Ni cuz bana son kowa ya san inda zaki koma,karatu zaki ci gaba a can tashi mu tafi kayan ki na palour.

Tashi tayi jiki a sanyaye ta fito yana rike da jakan ta da brief case nashi sai daya tabbatar babu mutane a BLOCK ɗin nasu yace ta shige mota da sauri a buɗe yake.

Da sauri ta faɗa mota, shima ya shiga tare da bata wuta suka bar Barrack din idanun baby na zubar da hawaye tana yiwa wurin kallon bankwana.




"""__Tunda ammie suka dawo wani sabon babin soyayya suka buɗe ita da alaji habu duk ranar girkin ta yana manne da ita aiki da Sallah kawai ke fitar dashi,shawarwari da umma ta bata tayi amfani dasu wanda ta fara da kiran hajia mariya a waya kullum safe su gaisa tun tana shan kamshi har ta sake fuska,,ko wane weekend sai ta kai mata ziyara da tsaraba niki niki kafin ka sani ta saye zuciyar ta suka zama damkar ciki ɗaya suka fito,yaran ma sun sake da ita har suna zuwa mata weekend basu da abokiyar shawara sai ammie.

Ta fannin ba'baa mairo ko wane ranar juma'a ammie na kai mata ziyara ta wuni har dare da girki mai kyau da alheri kala kala.

Hadiza karama kuwa ta samu admission a lupaloy secondary School kano inda ta fara jss1.

Ammie yanzu bata da wani buri da ya wuce ta kyautata ma umman su da ya hydar duk abinda ta samu su take tura wa ana mata kiyo,idan Kudi ne ya hydar ke juya mata har ta saya ma umma furnitures ta gyara mata palour da kayan kallo,ga chest freezer data saya mata har paint ta saka anyi wa gidan nasu,ita kuma nafisa engine na nika ta saya mata ta rinka sana'a...,yanzu ta gane uwar masu gida baƙin ciki take mata sam bata sonta da arziki shi yasa ma ta janye jiki da ita ko waya bata kiran ta,idan itace ta kira sai tace mata bata gari!

Tana ƙara gode wa Allah da ya bata gwarzon miji irin Alaji habu wanda ke sonta saboda Allah,ya tsaya mata akan komi na rayuwa duk wata matsalar ta nashi ne,baya nuna gazawar shi akan bukatun ta dana yan uwan ta yanzu haka suna shirye shiryen zuwa aikin Umrah ne tare dashi.

Bata da wata damuwa da ya wuce yawan mugayen mafarki da take yi da babya kwana kin nan hankalin ta a tashe yake,kusan kullum sai tayi mafarki da baby"ya zama Dole ta nemi baby domin sanin halin da take ciki!!!!


_Halimatus Sadiya Muhammad_

~🅿37-38~



 _____""koda suka isa Bus stop ɗin an gama loda kayan matafiya cikin mota har an fara amsan recept, wuri ya samu yayi parking motar shi tare da shaida masu yaci ke wani ɗan Form a gaban shi aka rufe motar da tempol..., recept nasu ya miƙa  suka shiga wuri ɗaya suka zauna gefen window,sauran mutane nata shigowa har kowa ya gama shiga,  a hankali motaci luxurious bus guda uku suka jera wanda hanya ɗaya za subi zuwa garuruwa daban daban....


Tafiya ta yi nisa wasu na bacci,wasu na hira,wasu na cin abinci,yanayin da wasu kallon tv dake lake cikin Bus ɗin suke yi...,idanun baby kyam Babu alama jin bacci,shi kuwa Captain Sadiq chatting yake da matar shi ta waya yana sanar da ita abubuwan da suka faru wanda ta bashi goyon baya akan hukunci da ya yanke.


Cin dare ya nisa ji kake tsit kowa yayi bacci ciki hadda baby da Captain sadiq....,kiraye kirayen sallar asuba ya sa aka tsayar da motar,musulmai suka fita zuwa sallah",da yake a wani ƙauye suka tsaya sallah maza suka shige masallaci,mata suka shimfida ɗan kwalayan su aka gabatar da sallah, wanda ke son cin wani abu ya saya suka ci gaba da tafiya....

Zuwa karfe goma sha ɗaya na rana suka iso Sokoto state babban garage motar tayi parking suka fito, adaidaita ya nema masu zuwa munirru road Wanda tafiya mai nisa ya kai su unguwar wanda akasari yan boko,yan siyasa da manyan yan kasuwa ke zaune wurin,shiru shiru har suka isa bakin wani tangamemen gida wanda ya amsa sunan sa.., kuɗi Naira ɗari biyar yaba mai adaidaita ya shiga kwankwasa gate ɗin...

Can mai gadi yazo ya buɗe wani matashi fuskar shi dauke da murmushi,ya sara ma Captain yana faɗin"ran maza ya dad'e kai ne tafe.?

Wallahi ko rabe,ya kwana biyu.?,sai alheri...,baby dai bata ce komi ba domin zuciyar ta aa bushe yake bata son ganin kowa bata bukatar taimako ko mu'amala da kowa kawai ba zata iyawa uncle Sadiq musu bane wallahi da babu inda zata sai yaya Mallam ya kashe ta.


Babban gida ne wanda ya amsa sunan sa,gidajen sama biyu ke cikin compound d'in,akwai local toilet da kitchen a harabar gidan sai wani corridor da zai sada ka da babban palour sai mai ɗauke da kitchen,store da ɗakunan Bacci guda biyu duka akwai toilet ciki sai dai dayan room and palour ne.

Da sallama ya buɗe kofar palour'n"wasu datajawa biyu mata ya gani ɗaya na goge goge, ɗaya na shirya kulolin abinci a wani gefe inda aka shimfida babban darduma mai kama da Centre carpet ga throw pillows Nan irin ba sarakai sun kai goma jere a gefe ɗaya.

Wacce ke goge goge ta duka har ƙasa,sannu da zuwa ranka shi dad'e...,Barka dai rafatu na same KU lafiya?

Lafiya klau ta amsa tana kallon baby..,SANNU yan mata.?,,ko kallonta baby bata yi ba!!!

Lale maraba da mutanen Legas baba sadiqu sannunka da zuwa"cewar matar dake shirya abinci..,fuskanta ɗauke da fara'a.

Yauwa halima ya aikin?,,da sauki ashe an samu k'aruwa Barka Allah sanya albarka...,ya amsa da Amin.

Baby ta kalla"sannu yar nan kun sha hanya..,banza tayi.!, Captain ya tsura mata idanu,sai dauke kanta tayi...,bai ji daɗin yadda tayi ba yayi gaba abunshi zuwa wani kofa tana biye dashi a baya ya kwada sallama....

Wata tsohuwa ce aƙalla zata kai shekaru saba'in da biyar a duniya,baka yar guntu wa sosai ko baby ta fita tsayi(saboda tsufa )ta washe baki"har kun iso.?,,,zama yayi akan ɗaya daga cikin kujeru palour'n ya kwashi gaisuwa...,sai lokacin baby ta duka a ƙasa ta gaishe da tsohuwar.

Fuska ɗauke da fara'a ta amsa tana faɗin sannu yar nan Allah ya ƙara hakuri komi lokaci ne ki kwantar da hankalin ki sadiqu ya mun bayanin komi a waya in Sha Allah za kiji daɗin zama dani,yar uban shi nake uwar mu ɗaya uban mu ɗaya da mahaifin shi,kinga girman gidan nan daga Ni sai mai gadi muke kwana,wa yan nan yan aiki da sun gama ko wace gidan ta take kwana da safe suke zuwa,,part din Nan biyu gidajen sama na yara na ne su uku dana mallaka shima sai sun zo hutu don ki saki jikin ki.

Gyada kai baby tayi domin ko kaɗan tsohuwar bata shiga ranta ba ..,tashi suka yi zuwa wurin cin abinci inda aka shirya kalolin abinci Kowa ya zuwa wanda zai iya ci har suka kamala.

Ta koma palour domin gidan ya fara cika da jama'a,,rafatu ta nuna ma baby d'akin ta madaidaici  Mai dauke da gado babba, wardrob da toilet.

Captain sadiq ya sake mata nasihohi sosai akan ta zauna lafiya da kowa domin gidan ya bambanta da barikin su zata haɗu da mutane kala kala yana so ya riƙe mutuncin kanta,ta zauna lafiya da kowa kamar yadda yasan halin ta,,ya tabbatar mata da gwaggon nashi nada faɗa sosai amma tana da daɗin zama,kuma maganar karatu yazo da komi nata zata ci gaba a nan insha Allah kafun ya wuce Canada zai dawo ya duba ta,,yanzu sauri yake zai Booking flight na zuwa Kano daga nan zai wuce yola.

A hankali ta bude baki,Nagode sosai uncle Sadiq da kulawa Allah ya saka da alheri insha Allah zaka same Ni bai biyayya da umurnin ka kamar yadda kace,idan ka isa na gaishe da aunty sadeeyah.

Shikenan baby zata ji don Allah kiyi ta hakuri da gwaggo,tana hawaye ta amsa da tau...,ya fito palour mutane ne sun kai biyar suna cin abinci gwaggo na zaune suna mata hira,zama yayi a hanun kujeran da take"ina driver a kai Ni airport zan yi Booking flight na 3pm zuwa kano bazan dawo nan ba zan shiga gari daga can zan wuce.

Da sauri haka sadiqu.?,eh gwaggo Yola zan isa sai mun yi waya dai..,sunan halima ta kira....,a guje ta amsa"dauko ƙaramin jakar nan a sakaden katifa,tau gwaggo.


Koda aka kawo ta ciro bandir na yan dari biyar biyar ta mika mai,ban samu tura kowa Yola a duba mun matar ka ba ga wanan dubu hamsin ne ka sayi ragon suna..., murmushi yayi"kai gwaggo ban kawo ba zan rage ki.?,ja'iri amshi don Allah,hannu biyu yasa ya amsa yana godiya.

Mamman driver KU tafi ka aje shi har ya kamala sai ka dawo,aje plate d'in yayi an gama hajia suka kama hanya.

Tunda Captain sadiq ya bar gidan baby na zaune cikin dakin shiru shiru har akayi sallah ta gabatar bata fito ba.

Gwaggo tace halima zo ki dubo yarinyar nan shiru shiru ko lafiya.?,koda ta shiga baby ta kifa Kai da guiwa tana kuka...yar nan lafiya kuwa.?,banza tayi da halima.

Ya dawo gwaggo kuka take yi fa.!,ce da ita ina kiran ta...,tare suka dawo da halima baby ta zauna gefe a palour'n dake cike da mutane.

Yar nan lafiya kike kuka.?,banza tayi da gwaggo..,wani daga cikin mutanen dake zaune wurin yace rashin sabo ne da kewan gida sai a hankali zata saba hajia.

Ran gwaggo ya Fara ɓaci tace ,duka nan babu bare yan gida ne yan uwan mijina sune ke ci a karkashin mu tun yana da rai,wanan sunan shi Mamman driver mijina ne yana zaune a boys quarters din gidan nan dake  bayan layin nan,halima mai dafa abinci itama a boys quarters take da mijinta,rafatu bayar ba ce  taimakon ta nayi tana mun shata da wanke wanke ina bata abinci gidan Sarkin yoruba wa take zaune, ɗan kambari driver na ne,sani kanin miji na duka a boys quarters suke zauna sai rabe mai gadi dake zama na gidan kowa nen su na zaune da matar shi da yara don haka ki saki jikin ki babu bare duk iyayen ki ne bana son rab'e rab'e.. ,baby ta amsa da tau badon ta amince zata saki jiki dasu ba.

Mutane iri iri keta shigowa gidan yan neman taimako maza da mata wanda sai sun cika cikin su da abinci a basu kuɗi su tafi har zuwa yamma ƙafa ya fara ɗauke wa rafatu da halima sun gama aikin su ko wace ta nufi gidan ta,sauran mazan ma sun fita sha'anin gaban su ya rage daga baby sai gwaggo cikin gidan,zaman ya koma masu na kurame gwaggo keta faman ba baby labarin garin da irin gwagwarmayar da suka sha ita da mijin ta kafin Allah ya basu haihuwa har su tara dukiya...,baby ko ci kanki bata  ce da ita ba har aka kira magrib ko wane ya nufi wurin sallah.



*WACECE GWAGGO?*


  Hajia Aisha mangal(Wacce aka fi sani da gwaggo) Yar Yola ce kamar yadda ta fadi ciki ɗaya suka fito da mahaifin Captain sadiq itace Babba a gidan na su mijinta marigayi Alhaji abu na mama ɗan asalin Sokoto state ne , ƙaramar hukumar yabo, ɗan  kasuwa ne sosai  wanda ke yawo gari gari fatauci lokacin su yayi tashen kuɗi saboda kasuwan ci,a ibadan suka fara zama wanda sai da suka shekara uku a can ,suka koma Niger state,Minna daga Nan   suka nufi Kano  lokacin suna da shekaru goma da aure Allah ya basu haihu fatima(wace suke kira da ladi), Mustapha (wanda suke ce ma ɗan Gusau domin a Gusau ta haife shi) daga nan ya ƙera gida na gani a fadi cikin garin Sokoto suka dawo da zama nan aka haifi auta mu'azu,shekarun su biyar da dawo wa Sokoto Allah ya mai rasuwa ta hanyar hatsarin mota yabar duniya da properties sosai a duniya,,Duk da gwaggo bata yi karatun boko ba amma ta tsaya kai da fata yaranta sun samu wadatacen ilimin boko dana addini,ita da kanta ke kula da dukiyar mijinta  har yaran suka mallaki hankalin kansu aka raba gado kowa ya ɗauki nashi;,Fatima na auren wani babban doctor a Birnin Kebbi yaran ta biyu mata, Mustapha na zaune Abuja da matar shi da yaran  uku, sai mu'azu dake Kaduna banker ne yaran shi biyu ,,,suna yawan zuwa wurin gwaggo idan anyi hutu ko lokacin bukin Sallah.



Gwaggo mafada ciya ce sosai,idan fahimce ta akwai dad'i zama....,koda goma na dare tayi baby taji bacci abunta.


Da safe koda ta taci rafatu har ta gama wanke wanke tana gyaran palour'n,halima na kitchen Ina kwana kawai ta masu ta koma d'akin ta....muryan gwaggo taji,har yanzu baki tashi baccin bane.?,na tashi ina kwana.?

Lafiya lau ya bakunta,Bata amsa ba fito kiga yadda suke aikin kema mace ce gidan aure zaki kina koyo zai fi maki....,banza tayi da ita sai data ga dama don kanta ta fito lokacin har an haɗa kunun tsamiya,ruwan tea na flask Ana soya doha da kwai...,baby tace sannu da aiki me zaka kama maki.?

Tace ba zaki iya ba sauce d'in kyaan ciki zan yi wa gwaggo dashi za taci doya...,kallon sama da ƙasa baby tayi mata irin'"sauce ɗin ne bazan iya ba.?

Ki bani zan iya,,jim tayi na dan lokaci tace,dama ga kayan ciki nan tafasasshen na ciro daga freezer ,na yanka komi haɗawa baya rage.

Gas cooker baby ta kula ta dauko ƙaramin tukunya ta zuwa kayan cikin da already anyanka ƙanana ta kara maggi, Curry da salt ta rufe ,,can ta d'aura frying pan ta zuwa olive oil kaɗan yana yi zafi ta zuba slice tomatoes,pepper,green pepper da oninon ta zuba seasoning Yana yi da kanshi har ya tsane ruwan shi sanan ta kwashe a wani abu dabam ta zuwa mai kuma a frying pan yayi zafi ta fara egg uku ta saka dan gishiri da farin maggi ta kada sanan ta zuba tana ta juyawa har ya nuna alamar yayi sanan ta zuba wannan can su tomato din da cabbage Wanda ke gefe a yanke ta chakude dai dai kayan ciki sun yi laushi ta haɗe duka a frying pan tana ta juyawa na wani lokaci gida ya dau kamshi"halima nata kallon ikon Allah ace yar yarinyar nan ta iya girki haka...

Har ta kamala fuskan nata adaure ta kashe gas din ta fita zuwa wurin wanka ,halima ta kwashe komi ta shirya...,koda gwaggo ta  fito cin abinci baby na d'aki,aka aika kiranta atafau taki fitowa dole diba akayi aka kai mata.

Gwaggo tace halima yau girkin naki tayi kyau,gishiri bai yawa ba haka yaji"tace ai baby CE tayi sauce din yau...gwaggo sai kiran masha Allah take tana murmushi har ta gama.

Zuwa goma na safe gidan ya sake cika kamar jiya da tsofaffi,matasa samari da yan mata yan uwan mijinta dake zuwa taya ta hira ciki hadda wasu yan mata biyu yaran Mamman driver (farida  CE babba sai Karima sa'ar baby),bayan sun gaisa tace su shiga yarinyar da aka kawo mata tana cikin dakin can...,cike da farin ciki suka shiga d'akin amma abun da ya basu mamaki ko kallo basu ishi baby ba suka ƙari zaman su,suka fito suna gulma wai ita kowa yar gidan uban wane da bata kula mutane sai faɗin rai.?,ai ko A'isha(takwara gwaggo,diyar Alhaji mua'azu)da ubanta hamshakin mai kudin ne idan suka zo tana nan nan da mutane ...


A takaice dai cikin kwana biyu babu wanda bai san halin baby na Girman kai da ɗaukan mutane ba'a bakin komi ba tun gwaggo na daukan shi yaranta da kuma rashin Sabo har abun ya fara bata haushi itama.

Ranar monday da safe ta haɗa baby da Mamman driver a kai ta schl (Abdullahi fodio islamic SCHOOL wanda mallakin Mustapha yaron gwaggo ne)babu bata lokaci a ranar aka gama komi suka bata ajinta jss1 da yake an shiga second term,cikin ikon Allah karatun yazo mata a saukake domin mostly abubuwan da ake koya masu shine aka koya mata a Barrack lokacin tana primary6...,duk teacher daya shigo sai ya umurci baby data gabatar da kanta(dama gwaggo tace a saka mata Aisha Mustapha na mama),abun haushi idan baby ta tashi introducing kanta sai tace"baby AMINU",a register Kuma aisha Mustapha na mama...zuwa karfe biyu driver ya dawo daukar ta lokacin form master nasu yayi reporting iskancin da baby tayi.

Suna isa gida Mamman driver ya kwashe komi ya sanar da gwaggo ai kuwa ta inda take shiga ba ta nan take fita ba faɗa,masifa da bala'i babu wanda bata sauke ma baby ba,wacce ko a jikin ta tana kwance d'aki tana snake game da wayarta,,,a ranar kowa ya shigo gidan sai da ya san ran  gwaggo a b'ace yake domin sanar dasu take abinda baby tayi mata ..,babu mai bada hakuri.

Ranar har dare tana faman faɗa,da baby taga fadan ba zai kare ba taki fitowa ko cin abinci.

Da safe tana tashi bacci tayi wanka ta shirya ko  breakfast Bata tsaya yiba ta kama hanya a ƙasa zuwa SCHOOL"gashi da nisa zuwa gidan",koda ta isa har 8am teacher na ciki jin gidan data futo a tunanin shi diyar mai makaranta ne (Alhaji Mustapha),bai punishing nata ba ya bata wuri ta shige.

Gida kuwa Mamman driver ya fito daukan ta wayam bata nan gashi ba wanda yaga fitan ta hankalin gwaggo ya tashi koda aka buga makaranta suka ce ta iso,,sanan hankalin su ya kwanta gwaggo sai sababbin faɗa domin abubuwan baby sun koma bata haushi.


Koda suka tashi break fast yunwa ya cuɗa ta gashi babu kuɗi a hanun ta shi yasa bata fita ba taci gaba da copying note data ara wurin wani yoruba..,dama bata magana da kowa a class d'in sai shi saboda sit nasu ɗaya kuma ta lura  yana da ilimi sosai.

Koda aka tashi motar mamman drive na jiranta a parking space Amma Kiri kiri yarinyar nan ta wuce shi taci gaba da tafiyar ƙasa zuwa gida....,,ran shi ya ɓaci sosai yaja mota ya dawo gida ya sanar da gwaggo.

Tace da sani ya dauko waya ya kira mata sadiqu yazo ya ɗauki ajiyar shi ba zata iya da wanan iskanci ba yarinya ƙarama sai faɗin rai da girman kai,bata iyawa kowa magana kamar itace mai gida!

~🅿39-40~



*ADAMAWA STATE,YOLA*

_____Around 8:00pm aunty sadeeyah CE zaune saman family size bed tana ba Umar FARUK(babyn ta)nono hankali kwance,tsakiyan gadon kaya ne makil na baby,sabulai,turaman zanuwa da sauran tarkace wanda yan uwa da abokan arziki suka tara mata, ƙasa wurin chest of drawer Kuma set na akwatuna ne wanda Captain sadiq da iyayen shi suka mata na haihuwa sallama da taji ne yasa ta ɗago kai fuska ɗauke da murmushi ta amsa.

Captain Sadiq ne sanye cikin track suit saboda yanayin sanyi da ake ciki hanuwan shi zube cikin aljihun wandon ya janyo kujera dake gaban dressing mirror ya zauna tare da amsan faruk,,,Barka da dare honey..!
Gyad'a Kai kawai yayi domin ranshi a b'ace yake...tashi tayi zata fita"yace ina zaki kuwa?

Abinci zan dauko maka,barshi kawai ba zan iya ci ba dawo magana za muyi dake saboda kin san gobe zan koma Lagos daga can jirgin mu zai tashi zuwa Canada.

A ƙasa ta zauna tare da tataru dukan natuswan ta tana kallon yanayin fuskan shi wanda ya rikide don ɓacin rai.

Deeyah zan sanar dake wani sirri wanda baki sani ba game da baby,ki gafarce Ni na boye maki komi lokacin da zan tafi da ita Sokoto nace dake gwaggo ta nemi da a kawo mata yarinya da zasu zauna tare tana ɗebe mata kewa wanda na zaɓi baby saboda natsuwa ta...,maganar gaskiya Captain Aminu ya tashi kashe baby shi yasa na ɗauke ta daga wurin shi(nan take ya sanar da ita asalin labarin baby kamar yadda yaji a bakin yaya Mallam har zuwan baby Sokoto ).

Kafin yakai karshen labari sadeeyah kuka take na fitan hankali har tana shesheki saboda tashin hankali,mamaki da kuma rudewa,tunda Allah ya halicce ta ko a Film bata taɓa ganin irin wanan kwaba ba ,wai yaya yayiwa kan wanshi ciki har ta haihu abinda aka haifa ta rayu a tsakanin su..!,tarin tambayoyi ya cika mata zuciya wanda tasan babu mai amsa mata shi a yanzu sai shi Captain Aminu!

Hannu ya miko mata,jiki a sanyaye ta miko nata ya matse cikin nashi, hannu ɗaya yana share mata hawaye"kiyi shiru haka nan ƙaddara ce da tsautsayi wanda ɗan adam ba zai iya wuce ƙaddara shi ba kin san bana son kuka fa kada ciwon ki ya tashi,shiru tayi ba tare da ta ce komi ba.

Nanauyar ajiyar zuciya yayi,kin san menene matsalar yanzu.?

Ta girgiza kai!,baby ta canza completely,da zu gwaggo ta kira Ni tana faɗa lalai sai inzo in tafi da Baby ba zata iya da mugun halin ta ba,bata magana da kowa,bata hira da kowa,abinci sau ta ga daman ci, schol bata amsa sunan da aka saka mata abubuwa iri iri,kin san gwaggo tsohuwa ce  bata da isasshen lafiya,bana so baby tayi abinda zai tayar da ciwon gwaggo idan yaran ta suka sabo labari baby ba zata kwashe lafiya dasu ba....

Katse shi tayi,yanzu me kace da gwaggo.?

Ce mata nayi tayi hakuri yau ake taron suna zan biya can gobe kafin in Koma Lagos!

Abban faruk nifa ban ga laifin baby ba wallahi ko ɗaya,kafi kowa sanin halin baby ba haka take ba yanayi ne ya canja ta,wallahi koda nice baby ba zan saki jiki da kowa ba wanan ai ba ƙaramin abu bane ace iyayen ka ciki ɗaya suka fito babban matsalar ma sun nuna kiyayya a gare ta ba dole tayi taka tsantsan da mutane ba,ta zauna a matsayin ta kada ta jawa kanta gori da cin fuska a cikin mutane,,kwata kwata baby guda nawa take da za'a damu da canjawan halin ta.?,yarinya karama wace kwata kwata bata wuce 12years old ba,Dame zata ji.?,halin da iyayenta suka saka ta ciki ko yawon bin gidajen mutane zaman rik'o.?

Gaskiya abban faruk ban amince ta tafi Sokoto saboda abinda baby ta aikata ba,kawai ka kira gwaggo a waya ka bata hakuri idan ba zata iya zama da baby ba a dawo da ita wurina koda a daki ɗaya zamu iya zama tare,ita kuwa baby Ni da kaina zan kiranta in rarrashe ta.

Tunda ta Fara magana kallon ta kawai yake yi domin akwai alamar gaskia a tattare da maganganun ta...,na fahimce ki aunty baby sai dai bai kamata a dauke ta daga wurin gwaggo ba zata ga kamar rashin kunya akayi mata yanzu zan bata hakuri sai kiyi magana da baby akan haka.

Koda ya duba agogo karfe 8:30pm,nasan gwaggo bata yi bacci ba Bara in kirata yayi dialing number.....

Har ya kusa katsewa ta dauka da sallama wake magana.?,kai gwaggo duk tsufan ne yau kuma murya na ne ba'a gane ba.!

Sadiqu bana son sakarci ba kakan ka nake ba haihuwar ka nayi"cikin zuciyar shi yace na jawa kai na masifa da daren nan.

Ayi hakuri gwaggo dama na kira ne akan maganar baby....,katse shi tayi bana son jin komi Allah sadiqu kazo gobe ka tattara ta ku tafi ba zan iya ba yarinya ƙarama da mugun hali sai baƙin rai,gida nawa ne ba nata ba amma sai shan kamshi da dagin kai.!

Ayi hakuri dai gwaggo yarinya ce ƙarama a hankali zata sake rashin sabo ne kwata kwata kwana nawa tayi tare dake.?,wallahi ba halin ta bane canjin yanayi ne nayi alkawari zan mata faɗa kuma za kiga canji.

Rufe mun baki makirin banza ita wanan ce karamar yarinya,wace tasan ta tako ƙasa daga makaranta zuwa gidan nan mai nisa,wacce tasan ta dauke kai bata gaisawa da kowa sai taga dama take cin abinci,bazan iya ba aga kamar kuntata mata nake yi nan kuwa ita ke azabtar dani.

Allah huci zuciyar ki hajia gwaggo uwar gida kuma amarya a gidan marigayi alhaji abu na mama.

Murmushi tayi kamar yana ganin ta,sadiqu ba kakan ka nake ba zan saɓa maka,yanzu dai na hakuri Allah ya bamu hakurin zama.

Yauwa hajiyata kin ga tafiyar da zan yi karo ilimi  Canada ya tabbata INSHA ALLAH nan da kwana biyu zan tafi bazan samu daman zuwa maki sallama ba a gafarce mu a saka mu a addu'a Allah ya bamu nasara.

Shike nan sadiqu a riƙe ibada da addu'a shine takobin Mumini nan gida zamu taya ku Allah ya kai lafiya ya bada Sa'a da nasara akan abinda aka sa a gaba na alheri, Allah ya kare ku daga sharrin mutum da aljan Amin.

Amin ya ALLAH gwaggo ayita hakuri don Allah da baby marainiya CE bata san daɗin uwa ba bare na uba kome tayi a Daura ta akan hanya,shike nan sadiqu domin farin cikin ka zan yi komi"suka yi sallama.

Dama hands free ya saka sadeeyah taji duk yadda suka yi da gwaggo,,yanzu ki kira baby mu ji ko.?

Dialing number nata tayi..,lokacin Baby na kwance saman bed nata tana duba littafan ta taji wayan na ƙara wanda tunda aka bata babu wanda ya taɓa kiranta kuma babu wanda ta kira duk da akwai credit a ciki.

Ganin number aunty sadeeyah yasa ta ɗauka da sauri tare da fashe wa da kuka(itama handsfree ta saka),kallon juna suka yi da Captain wanda ranshi ya ɓaci....,baby.!

Na'am,menene na kuka ko baki so na kira bane?,, a'a aunty nayi MISSING naki ne sosai..,common stop crying idan ba so kike kanin ki faruk ya maki dariya ba,,,,dariya ta saka ashe anyi suna aunty.?,eh Umar faruk aka saka,ya ku ke?

Lafiya lau aunty,,Karatu ba wata matsala kina gane wa ko.?,eh aunty Babu Ina Jin daɗin zama amma ba kamar gidan ki ba...,ayita hakuri zaki saba ne a hankali ki saki jiki gwaggo matsayin grandmother naki take KU zauna lafiya komi kike so ki faɗa mata kuma ki rinƙa jin magana bana son rashin ji.

INSHA ALLAH aunty zan kiyaye ina Umar faruk.?,gashi nan yana jin ki yayi missing naki plenty...,dariya mai sauti ta sake itama "haushi ya cika Captain sadiq shida yace ta kira yarinya tayi mata faɗa shine suke shiririta da shirmen su na mata",tsaki da ya jane ya dawo da hankalin ta gare shi,baby me yasa baki kiran mu a waya.?uhm dama ban san da kudi a ciki ba kuma ina zuwa school a gida nake barin wayar ban wani iya amfani dashi ba"ta shirya ƙarya".

Ok kiyi kokari kina kira ana gaisawa muna jin lafiyar ki and gobe idan kin dawo schl ki kira uncle ɗin ki,ki mashi fatan alheri zai wuce Canada..,shike nan zan kira a gaushe shi Nagode "suka aje wayar.

Amma dai sadeeyah baki da hankali abinda nace ki kira kiyi kenan.?,dama duk kece kika sagarta yarinya haka ake faɗa babu wani abu da kuma faɗa mata dangane da iskancin da take ma gwaggo naba..!

Kayi hakuri idan na bata maka rai shi yaro sai da rarashi, faɗa ko duka ba shi zai saka yaro ya gyara mistake nashi ba,a hankali zaka bi yaro cikin Hikima da jan shi a jiki...

Rufa mun baki don Allah,U ruined everything...,I Said I'm sorry honey...,ok nayi saboda faruk"ya fadi cikin sakin fuska",zan bar ATM dina na currently account saboda buƙatar ki na yau da kullum tunda Yaya Zahra ta ce sai kin yi watani biyu a kaduna kin ga idan akwai abinda kike buƙata ki cire kuɗi kiyi,akwai wani gida da Mallam bello na nuna mun na sayarwa nan bayan su hajja,kafin ku dawa zan turo kuɗin gidan agyara komi ki dawo ciki har inga yanayin zamana a can kinga da faruk yayi six months sai a barshi wurin hajja ki same Ni a canada..., dariya take yi sosai,amma abban faruk baka da dama na tabbata baka kai Ni son faruk ba,Wallahi ban manta da wahala dana sha wurin haihuwa ba ƙafa na ƙafan faruk koda birnin sin zaka tafi shi ɗan wata shida za'a cire a nono!

Wasa nake yi,gobe zaku je ki duba gidan idan ya maki sai in bada half payment and mota na dake Lagos zan saka sergent sagir ya kawo maki saboda hidma .,, Allah Kara budi ya sanya albarka honey.

Amin Amma ba anan ya kamata kiyi GODIA ba sai mun haɗu a Canada...,dariya ta saka tare da ƙara rungumo shi cikin Hikima suka samu natsuwa ba tayi da sun yi sex ba.




*SOKOTO STATE*


_____Baby na tashi Bacci da asuba tayi wanka tare da alwala ta gabatar da Sallah dama ta zo da Alkur'ani izo goma nata dashi take karatu bayan sallamar asuba,tana ganin 6:30am tayi ta shafa adu'oi ta sanan ta saka uniform abunda Bata tsaya jiran kowa ba ta ɗauki schol bag nata ta Kama hanya zata fita,mai gadi na kallon ta tana kallon shi ta buɗe gate ta fita a hankali take tafiya a bunta domin tayi alkawarin ba zata sake shiga motar kowa ba Allah ya halicce ta ne a matsayin kaddarar wasu,ta taso cikin wahala da baƙin ciki yanzu tasan gaskiya ba zata taɓa yadda kyale kyalin duniya ya rude ta ba wanda komin dad'e wa idan har aka san ko ita wacece ba zata ƙara samun wanan gatan ba,gwara tun yanzu ta saba da wahala wanda dama ciki ta taso...,koda 7:30am yayi ta isa schol ta shige class din su ta zauna"dama bata ma kowa magana har corper da ke masu integrated science ta shigo.

A gida gwaggo najin kwaramniya baby har ta fita domin itama data tashi sallar asuba bata komawa bacci, ɗaukar ma kanta alkawarin tayi ba zata sake shiga lamarin baby ba duk abunda taga dama tayi har Lokacin da zata gane gaskia.


Koda gari yayi haske sosai mutanen gwaggo suka fara taruwa kamar yadda suka saba kawo mata gaisuwa a taya ta hira har Lokacin fitan su aiki yayi ta tara su akan kowa ya saka ma baby idanu tayi abunda take so.

A schol sun gama First amsa second period Wanda kusan dukan abinda aka koya masu baby ta sani kuma tana bada amsa idan akayi tambaya har aka tashi break, ɗan sit nata Sherif Ya fita ya sayo egg roll guda biyu da soya milk ya mika mata ɗaya"dama yunwa take ji sosai,babu musu ta amsa taci suna hira cikin harshen turanci yake tambayanta wane schol tayi.?

Ta faɗa mashi COMMAND schol tayi a Lagos,,yace shi yasa kike da brain saboda ku na da kuɗi sosai ,ni government schol nayi domin iyayen na ba hali ne dasu ba ko yanzu wani uncle dina ya saka ni a wanan schl ɗin,,cikin zuciyarta tace gwara da kai kana tare da iyayen ka Cikin jin daɗi,idan da zaka ji labari na kila ko magana baba zaka sake mun .,suka ci gaba da hira har aka dawo break sauran yan class d'in nata gulmar sheriff Wai ya lake ma baby don yaga suna da hali...

Haka suka ci gaba da ɗaukan darasi har zuwa 1:30pm da aka tashi,tare suka jera da Sherif zuwa gida a ƙasa yana ta mamakin Yadda yar gidan masu kuɗi zata yi tafiyar ƙasa mai nisa haka,ana kai wa titin gidan su baby sherrif yabi hanyar shi ta tsallaka ta shige gida lokacin mutane sun rage yawa sallama kawai tayi har zata shige d'akin ta,sai ta tuna da maganar aunty sadeeyah ta dawo baya ta gaisa da mutanen dake palour'n,ta shiga kitchen ta gaushe dasu halima da aiki,ta wuce d'akin gwaggo ta gaishe ta,ba yabo ba falasa ta amsa mata,baby ta dan jima zaune ta tashi ta shige d'akin ta...,wanka ta fara yi tare da gabatar da Sallah sanan ta fito zuwa inda ake jera abinci ta diba a plate ta koma d'akin taci abunta,bayan ta gama Bacci ya kwashe ta sai la'asar ta tashi tayi sallah ta fito palour (dama abinci rana gaba ɗaya ake yi zuwa dare), Lokacin su halima sun wuce gwaggo ce zaune da wasu mata suna hira,gaishe su tayi ta zauna gefe hankalin ta na kan program da ake yi a TV...

Can dai ta tuna bata kira uncle Sadiq ba kamar yadda aunty sadeeyah ta umurce ta..., d'aki ta Koma tayi dialing number nashi har ya gama ringing bai Daga ba,ta sake kira shiru.

Lokacin data kira yana can suna rigima da yaya Mallam akan ba zai daga ko nan da can ba sai ya fito mashi da yar shi domin ba shine ya haifa mashi ita ba,,yace bashi da iko a kanta don haka bai isa ya tsare shi sai ya fitar da baby ba...,ranar ran maza ya ɓaci kamar zasu buga sai da aka raba su yaya Mallam yana kumfar baki wai Captain sadiq ya saka baby ta Raina shi Wallahi abun kunyar nan da suke gudu sai ta aikata shi a duniya,yadda ya ma wata ciki ta haife baby,itama baby haka za'a mata ciki ta haihu wanan alkawari ya ɗauka masu tunda suka ɗauke ta aka hana ta bin umurnin shi...,,ran Captain sadiq a b'ace ya haɗa dukan abinda zai buƙata ya saka driver ya kai shi hotel ya kwana domin saboda ɓacin rai ba zai iya kwana cikin Barrack d'in ba...,cema sergeant sagir yayi ya dawo da safe ya kai shi airport daga nan ya wuce yola da motar sai ya dawo a motar kasuwa.


Yaya Mallam baƙin ciki da hasada ke cin shi domin dai Captain sadiq ya fishi komi a duniya kuɗi,ilimi,daukaka da kuma kwanciyar hankali...,wanan yasa ya dauki alwashin sai ya wulakanta rayuwar Captain sadiq a duniya wanda dole sai baby na kusa dashi ya zama dole ya nemo baby ko don cikar burin  shi!.


*KANO STATE*

  ammie an zama babban mace domin ta samu soyayya da kwanciyar hankali a gidan mijinta,suna zaman lafiya sosai da uwar gida hajia mariya,ko wace na ganin mutuncin yar uwanta,yaran suna girmama ammie kamar itace ta haife su basu da abokiyar shawara sai ita musamman faiza da take babba lokacin da za'a zo nan auren ta gidan ammie akayi komi.

Alaji habu ga gama masu shirye shiryen zuwa aikin hajji wanda ammie,umma da faiza zasu tafi tare lokaci ɗaya domin akwai tafiya a gaban shi yaso kwarai su tafi da ammie sai dai ba zai samu ba saboda bata jin daɗin jikanta.

Bayan tafiyar shi rashin lafiya ya tsananta hajia mariya ta wuce da ita Hospital akayi dukan gwaje gwaje da ya kamata sakamako ya nuna tana dauke da juna biyu,,,murna wurin hajia mariya da yara ba'a magana,ita kam ammie ba haka taso ba domin wani dalili nata na daban!!!.


_Halimatu Sadiya Muhammad_
