____Cikin na ammie yazo da laulayi matuƙa wanda hakan yasa da alaji habu ya dawo daga tafiyar shi ya rarrashe ta akan tayi hakuri da tafiya aikin Hajji har wani shekara lokacin ta haihu.

Kuka ta saka masa ita dole sai ta tafi idan kuma ya hana ta wallahi zubar da cikin za tayi...,ranshi ya ɓaci sosai har ya furta duk abinda ya faru da cikin nan a bakin auren ta.!,kuka ta fashe mashi dashi wanda ya ƙara tunzura  shi ya fita ya bar mata gidan,tun daga ranar bata sake gane kanshi ba da abun ya ishe ta,takai ƙara nshi wurin ba'baa mairo wacce ta kira su duka ta masu nasiha sosai,ta bashi umurni akan idan da yadda za'a yi ya bari ammie ta yi tafiyar wanda dama ta faɗa mata kudirin ta nason zuwa kasa mai tsarki domin neman gafarar Ubangiji akan abubuwan data aikata na sab'on Allah,ta nuna ma ammie idan har taga bata samu zuwa ba awannan shekara Allah ne bai kira ta ba,GODIA suka mata sosai suka tafi gida.



Cikin ikon Allah a hankali laulayin yayi sauki sai shirye shiryen tafiya suke yi har lokaci tafiya yayi  dole umma kano tazo saboda ta nan jirgin su zai tashi, cikin yan rakiya yadda ya hydar da mama ladi murna makar zai kashe ammie yau ga umman ta da ɗan uwan ta a gidan ta,basu wani yi baccin kirki ba a ranar saboda farin ciki ammie wurin su ta tare wanda ba haka alaji habu yasu ba dole ya daga mata k'afa ,da safe tunda wuri suka gama shiri hajia mariya da kanta ta kawo ma su umma abun karyawa wanda hakan ya faranta ran umma sosai,basu wani bata lokaci ba suka nufi airport bayan an gama abinda ya kamata jirgin su ya daga zuwa ƙasa mai tsarki.
Hadiza karama gidan hajia mariya ta kowa saboda karatun ta.



____Baby bata da damuwar komi yanzu data tashi tayi sallah zata yi shirin schl bata jiran kowa ta kama hanya idan sherrif yazo da abinci tare suke ci,idan anyi darasi wurin da bata gane ba ya ƙara fahimtar da ita,inda bai gane ba ta fahimtar dashi lokacin cin abinci idan yazo da kuɗi ya saya masu abu su ci lokacin tashi tare suke jerawa da an kai titi ta shige gida yaci gaba da tafiya.

Wata ranar juma'a ta dawo daga schl kenan tana zaune d'aki taji sallamar mutane ana Barka da zuwa,Barka da zuwa ana shigo da kayan abinci niki niki,sai da taji hayaniyar ya lafa ta fito,"wata farar mace mai jiki da matsakaici tsayi ta gani tasha kwalliya cikin duguwar free gown na less Mai tsadar gaske,ta yane kanta da mayafi madaidaici,gefenta wata budurwa ce wace sanye cikin riga da skirt na atamfa ta yane kanta da just cover ko ba'a faɗa ba baby ta gane ta itace aunty ladi(babban diyar gwaggo),da yarta ta fari,mutane zazaune a saman carpet d'in suna kwasan gaisuwa..

Karasowa baby tayi tare da gaishe da aunty ladi,kallo tabi baby dashi sama da ƙasa,"wace mara kunya ce wanan dake gaishe ni tsaye.?

Gwaggo ta dubi baby tare da dauke kai,sadiqu ya kawo ta wai marainiya ce zata zauna tare damu na tsawon wani lokaci kafin ya dawo....,katse gwaggo tayi,don tana marainiya shine yasa bata da tarbiyya ko sadiq din da ya kawo ki idan ya gani har ƙasa yake kai wa ya gaishe Ni koda a cikin kasuwa ne bare ke  da dukan alamu baki samu tarbiya yadda ya kamata ba idanun ki a tsaye suke,gwaggo ki bani ita koda sati ɗaya ne tayi a wuri na zan koya mata hankali.

Gwaggo tace wallahi dana huta ladi,yarinya ƙarama sai taurin kai da mugun zuciya.?,ai gwara ki mayar da ita domin da alama ba zaki iya da ita ba"cewar Jamila diyar aunty ladi...,baby dai bata tanka masu ba tana tsaye ko gezau bata yi ba har suka ƙare cin mutuncin su ta koma daki.


Gwaggo tace ban san yau zaku zo ba shi yasa ba'a gyara shiyar ku ba...,aunty ladi tace tunda gobe zamu koma bari mu kwana wurin ki!

Jamila tayi karaf ita a dole saman su zata kwana ta kwala wa baby Kira Wai tayi maza ta gyara masu wuri...,bounch of keys aka ba jumma  ta haura gidan sama dake gefen dama shine nasu.

A hankali take haura steps ɗin har ta isa kofar ta buɗe Babban palour'n ne dogo an zagaye shi da furnitures da kayan kallo,sai tafka tafkan hotuna na aunty ladi da yaranta ɗaya kuma gwaggo ne da mijinta,, d'akuna uku ke cikin palour'n reras wanda duka sun sha tyles..,tsaye baby tayi ta rasa ta inda zata fara saboda girman wurin,daure wa tayi ta fara gyara Parlor'n,sai dakuna da toilet Wanda sun Yi kura sosai ta dauki kusan awa ɗaya kafin ta gama.

A gajiye ta fito jikin ta duk futu futu da ƙura.

Bayan tayi wanka zata huta ne aka sake kiranta domin buɗe kayan tsaraba da aunty ladi suka zo dashi wanda ya haɗa da sabulai na wanka da wanki,kayan abinci,sai albasa mai yawa sosai.

Gwaggo ta umurci rafatu data kwashe kayan abinci ta kai store,halima ta raba albasa da sabulai baby takai boys Quarters ba Yan uwan mijinta da sauran mutane dake ƙarƙashin ta.

Hijab baby ta saka ta ɗauki sakon ta nufi boys Quarters da yake ba sakar masu fuska take ba,ko wane gida sallama tayi kawai ta mika masu,data fita a bita da zagi wai yar cin arziki sai nuna isa da girman kai.


Zuwa dare duk wanda aka kaiwa tsaraba sai da matar shi ta zo godiya ta kawo abinci kala kala,wasu tuwo,ɗan wake,alala,dambu,da sauran su...,Jamila ta kira baby Wai ta dafa mata indomie ba zata iya cin tuwo ba,babu musu ta shiga kitchen ta dafa mata indomie da kwai ta kai mata..,duk yadda taso ta tozarta baby bata samu dama ba domin dai baby ta iya girki sosai.

Bayan ta gama cine ta Kira baby ta kwashe plate d'in,wani abun takaici wai ba ta kwana part din su ba d'akin gwaggo zata kwana,itama aunty ladi tace shike nan ba zata iya kwana ita ɗaya a can ba su kwana a nan tunda bed d'in nada girma.

Gwaggo ta kwala wa baby Kira Wai ta kwashe tarkacen dake saman gadon ta canja bedsheet tare da sharo masu d'akin...!,ranta a b'ace ta gama aikin ta koma d'akin ta.

Tunda safe baby ta tashi ta kama masu halima aikin BREAK fast saboda bak'i abinci kala kala suka yi irin wanda su halima suka sanar da ita aunty ladi na so har suka gama baby  ta dibi Naya taci sanan  tayi wanka.

Gwaggo da aunty ladi suka fito wurin breakfast Wanda sosai aunty ladi ta yaba da girkin na yau yasha banban dana ko wane lokaci.

Zuwa 10am Jamila ta tashi bacci sai sa tayi wanka ta fito cikin shiga ta alfarma tana yatsine yatsine,gaisawa tayi da gwaggo da momynta,halima ta gaishe ta tare da gabatar mata da abinci...

Sai cewa tayi babu abinda zata iya ci a wanan kwaba nasu a kira mata baby ta dafa mata vegetable indomie.

LOKACIN da sakon ta ya riske baby tana karatu domin ranar Monday zasu fara third term exam's,babu musu ta aje littafan ta tare da fitowa wanan lokacin har ƙasa ta kai ta gaishe dasu"aunty ladi tace koke fa, amma ace kina yarinya karama baki da respect,ita dai kitchen ta nufi cikin fridge ta ɗauko cabbage,carrot,green beans da green pepper;busashen kifi ta fara cire wa kaya ta zuba mai ruwan zafi da gishiri ta aje gefe,gas cooker ta Kuna tare da daura green beans d'in yayi laushi,gefe ɗaya kuma ta yanka carrot, cabbage da green pepper ta ɗauka tukunya karami tare da zuba oil kaɗan da yayi zafi ta zuba duka abinda tayi slicing hadda onion da spaces tana juyawa sanan ta wanke kifin ta zuba ciki kafin ta tsayar da ruwan lokacin ta duba green beans yayi laushi ta wanke ta haɗa ta rufe,can ta buɗe ta zuba indomie sai da ya shanye ruwan ta zuba seasoning d'in shi ta kwashe a plate"tun kafin ta gama kamshi ya cika hanjin Jamila ta matsu bata ci ba..koda baby ta kawo hada masifa wai bata ce da kifi ba tafi son egg.

Baby tace idan ba zaki ci ba ki ajiye tare da barin wurin..,ba Jamila ba hatta gwaggo sai da maganar baby ya bata mamaki!

Aunty ladi tace wallahi nasan ba zaki iya zama da wanan yarinya ba kwara ki tattara ta kada ta kashe ki,shiru kawai gwaggo tayi domin abubuwan baby na matukar bata mamaki yadda take zuwa makaranta a ƙasa ko breakfast Bata TSAYAWA yi,ta dawo a ƙasa.

Zuwa karfe goma sha biyu suka shiga kasuwan sokoto sayayya Wanda sai 2pm suka dawo basu wani tsaya cin abinci ba suka kama hanyar komawa Kebbi..


Cikin Sa'a su baby suka fara jarabawar wanda sai da suka kwashe sati biyu suka kamala aka fara hutu lokacin zaman gidan ya ƙara fita a ranta saboda yawan baƙi da suke samu jikokin gwaggo da diyanta ko wane sai kushe ta yake yi.


___Tafiyar su ammie Saudiya da kwana hudu ta zame cikin toilet Wanda haka yayi sanadiyar zubewar cikin dake jikinta,hankalin umma ya tashi sosai nan take aka kwashe ta sai hospital domin bai fita duka ba akayi mata wankin mara domin samun sassauci...,umma da kanta ta saka faiza ta kira ba'baa mairo a waya ta mata bayani saboda a kira alhaji a sanar dashi.

Da labari ya riske shi ran shi ya ɓaci domin ya kalafa ranshi a kan cikin nan sai dai haka Allah ya ƙaddara.


Saboda yanayin jikin bata samu yin aikin hajji yada ya kamata ba har aka kamala suka juyo gida.




*BAYAN WATANI UKU*


__Aunty sadeeyah ta fara shirye shiryen zuwa Canada domin Captain sadiq ya samu muhali mai kyau a can,ranar Sunday jirgin su ya sauka a  canada  hanun ta dauke  da faruk sun sha kwaliyya,a hankali take sauko wa daga jirgin nan take Captain sadiq ya hango murmushi ya sakar mata,koda suka karasa inda yake haɗe su yayi ya rungume na wani lokaci sanan ya amshi faru ita kuma tana yanke da babban troly nasu da handbag d'in ta har suka isa wurin shiga taxi.

Bakin wasu madaidaitan  gidaje ya aje su wanda bashi da maraba da irin Quarters na ma'aikata,suka nufi ɗaya daga cikin gidan Wanda room and palour ne sai kitchen da toilet.

Wanka ta fara shiga koda ta fito abinci da yayi order ya iso suka ci ya shirya masu kayan su cikin wardrob,da dare bayan sun gama hiran su na gida suka buɗe sabon babin soyayya...

 Da safe idan ya shiga schl ta gyara gidan ta shirya faruk  ta girka masu abinda ya sawaka haka rayuwar su ke tafiya cikin kwanciyar hankali da natsuwa.


  Cikin ikon Allah ammie ta sake samun wani cikin bayan watanni uku da barin wan can cikin koda suka tafi hospital likita ya bata bed rest na watanni uku har cikin ya fara kwari saboda mahaifarta naɗa matsala a sakamakon kwayoyin hana daukar ciki da tayi amfani dasu ya wuce ka'ida lokacin da take barikanci.

Manta wa suka yi da wasu sharudan na Hospital suka ci gaba da mu'amala su na aurata ya wanda a sanadiyar hakan aka sake rasa cikin a karo na biyu.


Tasha wahala matuƙa tare da jinya mai tsayi wanda hakan yasa likita ya bata hutun watani shida ba tare da aikin komi ba ciki hadda kwanciyar aure da mijinta.
Shawara ba'baa mairo ta yanke na ammie ta koma wurin umma tayi kwana biyu domin ta fuskanci idan har tana gidan mijinta ba zasu iya kiyaye dokokin da likita ya basu ba,haka kuwa akayi ba don yaso ba ta shirya tare da Hadiza karama saboda hutu suke yi suka nufi kumbotso.



    Baby sun koma schl yanzu jj2 suka shiga har yanzu bata kula yan matan ajin nasu duk da yaran masu hannu da shuni sun fi yawa kuma suna da sha'awar Kula abota da ita amma bata amince ba ko wane lokaci suna tare da sherrif.


Yau koda suka wayi gari gwaggo ta tashi da shirye shiryen baƙi ɗan Gusau(Mustapha founder na schl din su baby),sai zo hutu da matar shi,, rafatu da wata mama kulu suka gyara gefen su dake hanun hagu shima tsarin shi irin nasu aunty ladi ne,halima da Baby be a kitchen suna ta shirye shiryen abinci na tarban bak'i.

Da yake jirgi suka biyo daga Abuja zuwa Sokoto basu ɗauki lokaci ba suka iso wanda Mamman driver ya ɗauko su daga airport.

Tare yake da matar shi aunty Balki da ƙaramin danta Abubakar wanda suke kira da(boy),har palour'n gwaggo mutane suka rako su sanin halin shi irin na mahaifin shi mutum ne mai alheri da son jama'a ba kamar mu'azu ba.

Gaisawa suka yi da kowa lafiya ya masu alheri sanan suka gaisa da gwaggo, baby ta fito har ƙasa ta gaishe su kamar yadda aunty ladi ta koya mata...,tsareta da idanu yayi ko giftawa baya yi domin kyauta da natuswar ta sun saka yaji wani abu ya doki zuciyar shi,Balki ta amsa gaisuwa fuskar ta dauke da fara'a,gwaggo bak'uwa muka samu?

Eh sadiqu ya kawota ta taya Ni zama marainiya ce,,nan take tausayinta ya mamaye zuciyar su...,tace kin ji Allah jikan iyaye Amin.

Shi dai ɗan Gusau bai ce komi ba sai bin baby yake da kallo,gata yarinya ƙarama amma akwai natsuwa,kayan su ta shiga jida tana haura wa dasu saman su har ta gama.

Nan wurin gwaggo suka ci abinci,boy ya lake wa baby duk da ba wani son yara take ba haka ta ɗauke shi dai suka shige d'akin.

Tun daga lokacin aunty Balki ke jan ta a jiki sosai saboda yadda take kula mata da boy data dawo schl yana tare da ita,wani abinda ke ba baby haushi yadda yaya dan Gusau ke tasata gaba da kallon tsiya koga matar shi a wurin komi yace baby,aike goma yace baby idan aiki ne ita yake sakawa ko girki su halima suka yi da baby ta dawo sai ya saka ta dafa mashi wani abun,,aunty Balki ko a jikinta domin bata kawo komi a rai ba tasan halin mijin nata na tausaya wa da son jama'a.

Ganin aunty ladi su kayi kwatsam tazo gidan a cewar ta wani training na kwana biyu ya kawo ta Sokoto wanda zasu Fara gobe.

Hadda tsarabar doguwar riguna biyu ta kawo ma baby ta amsa da godiya,da dare bayan ta gama abubuwan da ta saba boy yayi bacci a hanunta aunty balki tace taje sama ta kwantar dashi mana kada ta takura,ta amsa da tau tana fita yaya dan Gusau yabi bayanta bata lora ba 
Har ta haura zuwa d'akin nasu ta kwantar dashi zabura yayi tare da riƙe rigar ta zai fara kuka ta kwanta saman bed d'in da kyau tana shafa bayan shi har ya koma bacci tana mike wa zata fita taci karo da Mutun tsaye bakin kofa,ratsawa tayi zata fita ya riko hanayen ta duka biyu yana kallon ta kamar yadda ya saba...


Shirun suka ji yayi yawa aunty Balki tace zata shige ta kwanta,itama aunty ladi tace zata kwanta Dakin gwaggo,sai da safe tayi masu ta haye saman su koda ta tura kofa mai zata gani mijinta rungume da baby tsam a jikin shi da alama baya cikin hayacin shi a guje ta sauko ƙasa ta janyo hanun aunty ladi tana kuka koda suka iso yana kokarin haɗe bakin shi dana baby fuskanta sai hawaye ke zuba shata shata,jikinta na kad'uwa saboda tsoro da fargaba domin irin haka bai taɓa faruwa da ita ba,bata san dalilin ta na kasa hana shi ba!

Alamar buga kofa ya dawo dashi cikin hayacin shi,aunty ladi ya gani tana watsa mai wani irin mugun kallo,matar shi Balki na faman kuka.!!!

NASREEN
NIMA 'YA CE LOADING..



~ALHAMDULILLAH FOR EVERYTHING~



~🅿43-44~




   

     Wani irin kallon banza aunty ladi ta kwatsa Mai sai da hanjin cikin shi suka kada"follow me",shine Kawai abinda tace dashi tare da barin d'akin..,sum sum sum kamar munafuki yake Binta a baya har d'akin gwaggo.

Aunty Balki ta shak'e baby iya karfin ta,for how long ki ke seducing mijina?,eyee tell me!

Kuka mai ƙarfi ya kwace wa baby tana faɗin aunty kiyi hakuri banyi komi ba kuma bani da laifi,is not what u are thinking...,Mari ta kai wa bakin baby Yar iska bana son jin komi yar karama  dake kin san hanyan da za kibi ki ja ra'ayin mijin wata ko?,zan koya maki hankali yau.

Tana riƙe da hanun baby har zuwa wani corridor ta  murd'a jam lock wani madaidaicin d'aki mai kama da store wanda ya kai shekaru ba'a buɗe shi ba saboda kayan da ba'a amfani dasu ke ciki sai duhu,nan ta tura baby tare da rufe D'akin gam.

Kofar shigowa part nasu ta rufe domin bata buƙatar ganin mijinta a wanan dare komi na iya faruwa,zaman dirshan tayi saman carpet tana sharan hawaye zuciyar ta na zugi kamar wuta..., Mustapha menene na raga ka dashi?,menene ka nema ka rasa a wuri na?,da me wanan yarinyar ta fini da har zata ɗauki hankalin ka haka for Goodness sake!


  A palour'n gwaggo kuwa aunty ladi wuri ta samu kawai ta zauna ranta a b'ace, ɗan Gusau na zaune saman carpet jikin shi yayi sanyi domin bai san irin hukuncin da gwaggo zata yanke ba.


Gwaggo tace menene ke faruwa kun tasa Ni gama kowa yayi shiru.?

Gashi nan ya faɗa da bakin shi abinda ya aikata!
Ɗan Gusau me ka aikata?
Ba wani abu bane fa gwaggo, yarinyar nan ce ta kwantar da yaro so tazo fitowa muka yi karo ina shiga d'akin bata san da zuwa na ba,shike nan aunty Balki suka shigo suna zargin wani abun dabam"ya faɗa hankalin shi kwance'.

Katse shi aunty ladi tayi, ƙarya yake gwaggo wallahi koda muka shiga baya cikin hayacin shi, rungume da ita muka same shi yana kokarin haɗe bakin shi da nata Allah ya kama shi.

Gwaggo ta dube shi ranta a b'ace,haka ne ɗan Gusau.?

Ba zan ƙarya ta ba domin tana gaba dani,amma ba abinda take tunani bane,kawai ina son yarinyar ne tun ranar da na fara ganinta,sam ba abinda ku ke tunani bane!

Hanun zagi gwaggo ta nuna mai"ka ci gidan ku nace",don ka zama shashasha wanda bai san kunya ba wanan yar jari riyar zaka runguma kaji me a jikin ta.?,wallahi ka kiyaye Ni!

Aunty ladi duka ne kawai ya rage bata kai masa ba domin iskancin nashi ya wuce sanin ta,ya zama dole yarinyar nan ta bar gidan nan gwaggo domin zaman ta fitina ne a tsakanin mu.

Babu inda zata"cewar ɗan Gusau",,baka Isa ba wallahi ladi itace babba tamkar uwa take a gare ku duk hukuncin data yanke dai-dai ne.

Gwaggo da ranki aunty ladi tana a matsayin tana Yayan mu amma ba uwa wa,namiji komin k'ank'anta shi uba ne akan mace gaskiya yarinyar nan ba zata je ko ina ba saboda marainiya ce,idan kuma kun matsa zan tafi da ita a d'aura mana aure!


Inna lillahi wa'inna ilaihi raji'un,Dan Gusau yaushe ka zama tantirin ɗan iska ban sani ba.?

Kyale Ni dashi gwaggo tunda yace haka ga fili ga Mai doki dole sai ta bar gidan idan ka isa yanzu da daren nan ka ɗauke ta a d'aura maku aure!

A'a ladi ba za'a yi haka ba,inaso kai ɗan Gusau tunda safe ka ɗauki matar ka ku bar mun gida,baby amana ce a hanu na ba zan bari ka cutar da ita ba,daga yau ba zaka sake kwana a gidan nan ba idan har baby tana gidan ,duk lokacin da zaku zo ka aje matar ka kawai idan mun gaisa ka koma gida.

Shike nan gwaggo na gode Allah ƙara girma da lafia,ke uwace a gare Ni bani da tamkar ki ba zan so aikata abinda zai bata ranki ba,insha Allah gobe zamu tafi"ya tace ya bar wurin.

Aunty ladi ba haka taso ba cikin ranta tace" a juri zuwa rafi...


Babu irin magiya da ɗan Gusau bai yi ba akan balki ta buɗe kofar amma keme me taki,hankalin shi ya tashi domin bai san halin da baby take ciki ba,alkawari ya ɗauka mudin wani abu ya faru da baby sai ya bata ran Balki.



Baby jingine da kofar d'akin tana rere kukan ta ahankali,k'asan mak'oshinta ta furta,menene yasa na zaki jiki dasu har muka shaku hakan ta faru bayan nayi Wa kai na alkawari ba zan taɓa sakin jiki da kowa ba kasan cewa ni ɗin bai baƙin jini ce,babu wanda ya ɗauke Ni a matsayin ɗan adam mai daraja,duk da nasan cewa NIMA 'YA CE kamar kowa,menene dalilin yaya ɗan Gusau na rungoma na?,gashi ya janyo mun masifa ina zama na lafiya,ya Allah ka kawo mun dauki!

Motsin beraye ya saka ta zabura domin tana tsoron so,sai k'arawa suke kamar ana koro su, adu'oi kawai take yi Allah ya kawo mata ƙarshen wanan rayuwa nata mai cike da sark'ak'iya!

Kiran sallar asuba ya tasar dashi domin saman steps d'in ya kwana kwance,gate ya buɗe ya wuce masjid dake jikin gidan.

Baby na zaune inda ta raɓa taji alamar tashin aunty balki,bugun kofar taci gaba dayi a hankali...,wani tunani ya fado ma aunty Balki bayan ta idare da Sallah ta buɗe d'akin tare da rik'o hanun baby har d'akin gwaggo da sallama har ƙasa ta kai suka gaisa dasu gwaggo sanan tace,tun kiran sallar farko nake ta jin bugun gate Koda na duba rabe na bacci,na tashe shi ya buɗe baby muka gani tsaye alamar ba gidan ta kwana ba,domin jiya da abin nan ya faru kuma fita itama ta fita a guje ina ce ko d'aki ta shiga ashe waje tayi.

Fuskan su ɗauke da mamaki har Balki ta kamala bayanin ta,ran kowa idan yayi dubu ya ɓaci!

Baby girgiza kai take cikin zuciyarta kuwa adu'oi take karanto wa na neman tsari daga sharrin mutum da aljan,hawaye na zirya a kumatun ta.

Aunty ladi tace ke anya iyayen ki ba zina suka yi suka haife ki ba.?,wanan wane irin akuyan cine.?

Gwaggo tace na gaji wallahi na gaji ba zan iya ba zan buga wa sadiqu yazo ya dauke ki wallahi ba zan iya dake ba!

Nan take aunty ladi tayi dialing number captain Sadiq..,lokacin it was around 2:30am a Canada, ringing na phone d'in ya tada sadeeyah daga bacci wanda taga number Nigeria sai da gaban ta ya bada daram,kiran ya katse nan take wani kiran ya sake shigowa da bak'uwa number bubuga shi tayi cikin bacci yace deeyah menene.?
Receiving call d'in tayi tare da kara mai a kunne,muryan gwaggo da yaji ne yasa ya tashi zaune a hanzarce...,hello .hello gwaggo ce.?

Itace ce sadiqu wallahi na gaji da wanan masifa daka kawo mun gida na ka gagauta zuwa ka ɗauke ta idan ka bata lokaci zaka samu mumunar labari...

Da yake handsfree aunty ladi ta saka duka suna jin abinda ke faruwa!

Sai ta kanshi yayi,meke faruwa kuma gwaggo,wani abun baby tayi.?,"jin an ambaci baby yasa sadeeyah ta kara kunnen ta jikin wayar.

Eh tana neman hadasa mun fitina agida ta kasa wanda zata yi soyayya dashi sai ɗan Gusau,Allah ya tona asirin ta shine ta gudu tun jiya sai yanzu ta dawo gidan nan, sadiqu ba zan dauki abun kunya ba idan ya kunsa mata ciki a ga laifi na ko.?,ka hanzarin zuwa ka ɗauki amanar ka!

Ya ma rasa mai zai fadi domin dai gwaggo ba zata taɓa faɗa mashi ƙarya ba,a wani bangare yana kokonta akan abinda ake zargin baby ta aika domin dai yasan ba halin ta bane idan ba yanzu ta canja ba.

Gwaggo kiyi wa darajar annabir rahama Sallallahu alaihi Wasallama kiyi hakuri in sha Allah hakan ba zata sake faruwa ba zan tsawata wa baby Kuma nayi alkawari idan na dawo Nigeria zan ɗauke baby,Amma yanzu Ni da mata ta muna kasar waje ba zan samu daman zuwa daukar ta ba.

Aunty ladi ta karɓe wayan Sadiq ba'a son iskanci ka ɗauko yarinya mara asali ka kawo wa gwaggo wahala tana nan zama mana masifa ta janyo mana abun kunya gaskiya ba zai yiwu ba.

Aunty babba ayi hakuri dai bana kusa ne idan na samu zuwa zan ɗauke ta kuma zan tsawata mata akan haka.

Ba haka taso ba amma dole ta hakura dai,shike nan Allah kawo ka lafiya ta kashe tana hararan baby.

Zo nan tace da baby,jiki a sanyaye ta jawo jiki zuwa wurin kafafun su,rankwashi mai Lafiya ta naɗa wa baby,don uwar ki da uban ki idan kin cika rik'ak'kiyar  karuwa don Allah kada ki fasa bin maza tunda kin zama karya,tashi ki ba mutane wuri!

Ranta a b'ace ta bar d'akin zuwa wani corridor inda shuke shuke suke ta zauna karkashin wani bishiya,cikin zuciyarta take magana tana mamaki yadda uncle Sadiq ya yadda da maganar aunty ladi ba tare da jin ta bakin ta ba,wanan wace irin rayuwa ce,babu mai sonta da gaskiya kowa na zargin ta akan laifin da bata taɓa aikata wa ba,Allah yana gani koma zai mata maganin komi.

Ɗan Gusau ya dawo daga masjid ya hangota baya son mata magana kada ya janyo mata wata matsalar domin yasan  halin aunty ladi Sarai Bata da mutunci amma ya zama dole ya sanar da ita sirrin dake cikin zuciyar shi.

A hankali ya tako har inda take ya risina Wanda Bata San yazo ɗin ba sai muryan shi ta tsinta,baby komi yayi tsanani maganin shi Allah,ko wane ɗan Adam da irin nashi jarabawa a rayuwa,ke musulma ce ki yadda da ƙaddara mai kyau ko mara kyau domin yana yadda da ƙaddara na  ɗaya daga cikin shika shikan musulunci,Har ga Allah ina son ki,sona gaskiya wanda babu cuta ko ha'incin a ciki zan so ki riƙe wanan a zuciyar ki Ni Mustapha mai kaunar kone domin Allah kuma nayi alkawari komin daren dad'e wa sai na aure ki idan Allah ya saka ke mata tace.

Kukan ta kawai taci gaba dayi domin tsayawa sauraren shi ma wani bata lokaci ne ba zai taɓa fahimtar halin da take ciki ba a dalilin abinda ya aikata jiya!

Ci gaba yayi da surutan shi bata amsa ba ya tashi ya wuce ciki.

Gani kawai Balki tayi yana haɗa kayan shi ko kallon inda take bai yi ba , daɗi taji a ranta itama ta shiga haɗa nasu kayan,"ki shirya an jima zamu wuce,ya fita da niyar zuwa ya gaida su gwaggo daga nan ya tafi ya masu Booking flight.


Baby na zaune a wurin nan har suka wuce sanan ta koma cikin Dakin ta lokacin ta gabatar da sallah asuba around 9am Koda ta duba phone d'in ta missed calls kusan goma duka na uncle Sadiq da aunty sadeeyah ne.

Kashe wayan tayi gaba ɗaya domin bata da abun ce wa tunda har shima ya yadda da zargin da ake mata.

Tunda su yaya ɗan Gusau suka bar gidan bata sake samun sakin fuska wurin gwaggo ba har aunty ladi ta gama abinda ya kawo ta ta koma kebbi.




*BAYAN SHEKARU UKU*

   Captain sadiq suna ta shirye shiryen dawo wa domin ya kamata abinda ya kai shi don ma ya samu delay ne da sun dawo tun da dade wa,aunty sadeeyah tayi fresh da ita gwanin sha'awa tana dauke da ƙaramin ciki,zaman su a Canada   tayi  course itama na shekaru biyu on reproductive health,faruk Kam yayi wayau sosai yana tafiya zuwa ko ina abun shi.


Yaya Mallam tun bayan tafiyar su wani tsautsayi ya ritsa dashi akwai wata budurwa shi Justina diyar general nasu ne suna soyayya sosai da yaya Mallam har ya mata ciki wanda yaso su zubar ta nuna ma zata aikata haka ba dole sai ya aure ta,ya nuna ba zai iya auren kafura ba idan ta amince a zubar da ciki shike nan zai aure ta,bata tsaya bata lokaci ba ta amince da hakan, wani private clinic suka tayi akayi aikin successfully sai dai me.?,jini yaki tsayawa kullum sai ƙara yawa yake tun suna iya controlling abun har yafi karfin su gashi a gidan shi ya aje ta gudun tonon asiri,da taga abun ya wuce wuri ta nemi su koma hospital a duba ta,ya ki amince wa saboda tana son shi tayi hakuri sati ɗaya da faruwar haka ta mutu a d'akin shi saboda ƙarancin jini a jikinta.

Rasa yadda zai da gwani ta yayi a ganin shi babu wanda yasan wanan sirri bata Peter dake mai hidima,bai sani ba ashe tana waya da wata sister nata dake anambra ta kwashe komi ta sanar da ita tun daga lokacin da ta samu ciki har zuwa zubar da cikin amma bata san da batun mutuwar ta ba.

Rufe gawan yayi cikin daki ya shirya tafiya na gaggawa zuwa kano domin shine kawai mafita a gare shi.

Kwana ɗaya da yini ɗaya gawan Justina ya fara wari cikin gidan ga karnin jini dake tashi duk ya bushe a jikinta Peter dake shigowa yana gyara gidan kamar yadda ya saba abu ya ishe shi dole ya kai report wurin babban su aka zuba gidan searching cikin ikon Allah aka bude d'akin sai ga gawan Justina Yana wari a d'akin.

Ba'a bata lokaci ba aka fitar da ita zuwa mutuary sanan labari ya isa kunnen General Emanuel,mamakin duniya ya cika shi domin Dai sunyi da Justina zata tafi anambra wurin sister nashi yanzu ace mai an tsinci gawan ta a gidan Captain Aminu"ya impossible!

Mrs Emanuel(maman ta) KUKA take Yi sosai domin taci buri akan Justina tana son yar ta kamar me sai gashi wai an tsinci gawan ta a gidan  musulmi.


Shirya wa suka yi zuwa mutuary domin tabbatar da gawan nata,ganin irin mutuwar wulakanci da tayi ai nan kuka ya kwace wa mrs emanual tana faɗin wallahi duk wanda ya kashe mata Justina Shima hukuncin kisa ya hau kanshi...,General Emanuel ma kuka ya shiga yi sosai saboda tausaya wa yar shi,likita yace su fita akwai bincike da yake son yi akan gawan domin tabbatar da zargin shi.

Damke Peter akayi sai ya fadi yadda haka ta faru,,yana kuka ya na magiya akan bashi da masaniyar abinda ya sani oga Aminu na dating madam Justina Wanda kusan kowa cikin Barrack d'in yasan da relationship nasu.

Mutane General Emanuel ya tayar aje Kano a taho mai da yaya Mallam domin ya fuskanci hukuma.




     Babban damuwar ammie a yanzu bai wuce rashin haihuwa ba domin tunda tayi b'ari na biyu ko bayar wata bata sake yi ba abun na damun ta har  fara tunanin ko dai hakkin baby ne ya fara kamata tun a duniya.

Hajia mariya dama likitar mata ce tana iya kokarinta wurin ganin ta taimaka wa ammie ta samu ciki sai dai duk gwaje gwaje da suka yi ya nuna mahaifarta yayi weak sosai zai wuya ya iya riƙe kwana haihuwa,daurata tayi akan magunguna masu inganci kuma tana kan bincike wurin yan uwanta likitoci ko za'a dace.


Tun bayan auren faiza babban diyar alaji habu Usman ya dawo hanun ammie,dama ita sarah mommy's girl ce,ammie ta haɗa Hadiza karama da Usman ta rike tamkar itace ta haife su babu abinda suka nema suka rasa;,Ya hydar da inna ladi ma alaji ya biya masu Umrah sun tafi watanni uku da suka wuce koda suka dawo ammie ta saka an ƙara gyara gidan su ya koma flat guda uku masu kyau,tana da manyan shaguna biyu a ƙauyen ana sayar da kaya irin na birni wanda ba'a samu a ƙauyen cikin farashi mai sauki,ga noma anayi mata dama ta buɗe gidan gona ana kiwon dabbobi manya da k'ana na,umma kullum cikin sanya mata albarka take babu abinda suka na suka rasa a Wurin ta,itace ke ɗauke da karatun yaran ya hydar,babban damuwarta a yanzu rashin haihuwa.


    Baby yanzu an shiga ss2 wanda an raba Yan class nasu wasu na SCIENCE class,while wasu na art,wasu commercial class.
Baby na ɗaya daga cikin wanda suka zaɓi commercial saboda personal interest nata,sherrif na SCIENCE Babu yadda bai da baby ta zaɓi science ba tunda she's intelligent Amma fafur taki har yaso zama masu faɗa,hakura yayi ya kyale ta.

Babu laifi tana gaisawa da yan matan class d'in nasu  yanzu SAMA, duk da samari na damun ta sosaia babu wanda take sake wa fuska ko mace yar uwarta bata samu wani sakin  fuska ba bare ɗa namiji,ta ƙara girma sosai da kyau'n jiki baƙin ta mai kyau da shek'i  duk da ba wani kyau'n fuska ne da ita ba amma ALHAMDULILLAH structure nata mai ɗaukan hankali.


Shekaru uku da tayi a Sokoto state gidan gwaggo tamkar a gidan yari tayi shi saboda masifa irin na gwaggo banda kazafi,sharri da makirci Wanda aunty ladi da diyata ke haɗa mata a ko wane lokaci suka zo gidan ko gwaggo ta aike ta birnin kebbi(da yake babu wani nisa daga Sokoto zuwa Kebbi).

Shi mu'azu bai cika zuwa ba domin wani lokaci ma sai dai taji labari yazo ya koma a gurguje saboda yanayin aikin shi sau ɗaya suka taɓa ganuwa wani zuwa da yayi gaisuwa rasuwar ɗan uwan mahaifin shi har ya bata kyautar dubu Uku,matar shi aunty Hausa mai mutunci itama sai lokacin bukin Sallah suka haɗu da baby Kuma ta yaba da natuswar ta.

Ya ɗan Gusau kam tunda abun nan ya faru bai sake zuwa gidan ba sai waya kuma ya hana matar shi zuwa wanda da gangan yayi hakan  saboda ya gane makircin da suka kula wa baby ita da aunty ladi.

Kwana kin Nan biyu gwaggo bata jin daɗin jiki baby ce mai kula da ita data dawo daga schol tana d'akin ta har lokacin baci yanzu ba laifi tana sake mata fuska saboda kyautatawa da take mata,ciwon sai gaba yake aka yanke shawara a kai ta teaching hospital Sokoto(UDUTH),WNAN umurnin mu'azu ne kafin su iso,akayi rushing nata domin numfashin ta ya fara sama da ƙasa dole intensive care unit aka amshe ta.


Hajia mariya ta gama duk wani bincike ɗaya kamata akan matsalar ammie aka haɗa ta da wani babban specialist dake Nan teaching hospital Sokoto,da kanta ta yi magana da alaji habu aka saka lokaci ya shirya masu tafiya su uku zuwa Sokoto domin matsalar ammie.


_kaka ƙara kaka!, Captain sadiq na shirin dake wa Nigeria,yaya Mallam ya shiga trap,ammie za'a Sokoto ko zasu haɗu da baby_?


_Halimatu Sadiya Muhammad_

~🅿45-46~


   


       Lokacin da yaya Mallam ya iso Lagos ba ƙaramin tashi hankalin shi yayi ba saboda abinda yake boye wa ya bayana,sanin halin General Emanuel ya saka hanjin shi kad'uwa.

Ranar monday manyan sojoji suka yi zama  na musamman akan abinda ya aika ta duba da dimbin shaidu da aka samu,bincike da likita yayi akan gawan ya tabbata asanadin cikin da ta zubar ne ta rasa ranta.
Yaya Mallam bai bata masu lokaci ba  ya amsa laifin  shi inda aka cire uniform din shi wanda ya nuna an kore shi a aiki,sanan za'a tsare shi cikin Barrack d'in na wani lokaci!

Sai da ya zub da hawaye saboda yadda aka tuzarta shi cikin yan uwansa shi manya da ka'nana,yana ji yana gani aka rufe shi.

Mrs Emanuel da ta samu labari kamar za tayi hauka domin bata dauka za'a sasauta mashi ba,taso yadda ya kashe mata yarinya a kashe shi...,hakuri mijinta ya bata akan babu yadda za suyi da hukuma amma yayi alƙawarin yadda Justina ta wahala tare da mutuwar wulakanci haka zai bada dama a wahalar da Aminu!



      Captain sadiq ya samu labarin abinda ya faru da yaya Mallam wanda hakan bai mashi daɗi ba ko kaɗan duk da basu rabu cikin sallama ba amma ya tausaya mai matuƙa akan rank nashi da aka cire yanzu shike nan wahalar da suka sha na karatu da training ya tashi a banza!

Sadeeyah bata wani ji tausayin shi ba domin abinda ya shuka ne ya fara girba,idan yayi wa yar uwarshi ciki ya wulakanta ta ya kwashe lafiya ba zai ma wasu ya tafi free ba.

Dama saura sati ɗaya su dawo sai ya dawo da tafiyar nan da kwana ki uku saboda ya taimaki aminin shi,tsaraba sosai sadeeyah tayi wa baby na kayan sawa,takalma da jakuna dama suna waya lokaci zuwa lokaci.

Ranar Sunday da misalin 8:30pm jirgin su yayi landing  a lagos Dama ya saka an gyara masu gida,bai tsaya bata lokaci ba ya saka wani sergent ya kai shi wurin da aka tsare yaya Mallam.

Sanin matsayin Captain sadiq ya saka bai mu sa mashi ba yayi directing nashi har cell d'in Wanda bashi da maraba da akulkin kaji babu window sai duhu kofar shiga ko yaro dan shekara shida sai ya dune Kai kafin ya shiga.
Kukan yaya Mallam ya fara ji da isan su  wurin wanda crut da sergent hudu ke jibgan shi wasu na bugu da rodi,wasu da kata ko,wasu da hannu sai jini ke fita ta ko ina a jikin shi!,, Captain sadiq ya dakatar dasu da kyar domin sun nuna umurnin general Emanuel ne,koda yace a bari ya fito suyi magana amma me.?,baya iya tafiya saboda an bubuge kafafun sai da aka chicchibo shi zuwa harabar wurin daga shi sai vest da boxer duk sun ɓaci da jini,fuska ya kumbura idanu sun kumbura,baki  da hanci sai zuban jini,kafafu sun kare idan ba ka sanshi sosai ba,ba zaka taɓa gane shi ba.

Kuka Captain sadiq ya fashe dashi tare da rungumo yaya Mallam yana faɗin"ka kyauta ma kanka kenan,yanzu irin wanan rayuwa da ka zaba ma kanka kana ganin ya dace da kai kenan,yanzu wa gari ya waya saboda Allah kana namiji mai ilimi,jarumi jajir tace wanda ya san kanshi ka mayar da kanka naka sashe,ka zubar mana da mutunci a idanun duniya wallahi kayi asara ka cuci iyayen ka Aminu!!!

Koda ya buɗe baki zai magana jini ya cika mai baki tofar da dunkulen jini yayi tare da haƙora shi uku yace"sadiq Abun da na shuka ne yanzu nake girba hakika naci amanan iyayena da yan uwana ga baby nasan Allah ba zai bani ba dole sai hakkin su ya kamani,idan na mutu Ni dai ka nema mun gafarar su.....

Stop it aminu ba zaka mutu yanzu ba sai ka gana da iyayen ka da yan uwanka kuma sai ka nemi gafarar su, INSHA ALLAH zan tsaya maka sai an fitar da kai daga wanan zalunci da suka kula ai ba haka ya kamata ayi ba idan an sallame ka a aiki wanan ba laifi ne ba amma basu da Right na tsare ka domin kai ba ƙaramin ma aikaci bane.

Yana nishi sama sama ya sake zubar da wasu jini,"kayya aminu kada ka wahalar da kanka akan abunda na aikata domin yana iya shafar aikin ka,babban burina ka kula mun da baby kamar yadda ka ɗauka a farko,ka nema mun gafarar ta dana ammie ko zan samu rahamar Ubangiji,zan baka address ɗin gidan Mallam saminu a Kano a nan za suyi directing naka zuwa ƙauyen mu.

Bana son Jin wanan magana insha Allah tare da kai zamu wurin su kowa zai yafe maka kaci gaba da rayuwa kamar kowa.

Muryan wani crut suka ji yana sanar dasu General Emanuel na neman Captain sadiq a office nashi.

Yace su mayar dashi sanan su tabatar da sun bashi abinci mai kyau yaci ya huta ,kada wanda ya sake dukan shi  anjima zai kawo doctor ayi treating nashi.


Yana Isa office din ya nemi izinin zama aka bashi.

General Emanuel ya fara da mashi Barka da dawo wa kuma yana taya shi murnar kamala course din da suka tafi da sauran yan uwan shi,gobe monday za'a ƙara masu girma.

Cikin girmamawa ya amsa yana GODIA...,yace abu na karshe yana ganin mutuncin shi baya so ya saka kanshi cikin rigimar su da Aminu domin ba hurumin shi bane.

Yace a mashi AFFUWA amma yana ganin abinda akayi wa Captain Aminu ba'a mashi adalci ba domin yayi wa ƙasa hidima...

Katse shi yayi da kar ya ƙara kiran Aminu da Captain domin yanzu baya tare dasu an sallame shi a aiki!

Captain sadiq yace idan an sallame shi daga aiki a bashi dama ya bar Barrack din shine adalci.

Cikin zafin rai General yace sai lokacin da ya ga dama domin har yanzu ba'a rufe Justina ba sai ya kashe Aminu hankalin shi zai kwanta.

Shi kuwa Captain sadiq ya mashi alkawari sai ya fitar da Aminu domin zalunci yayi yawa,rai a b'ace ya bar office d'in.

General Emanuel yace ya san ta inda zai bulo wa Captain sadiq!




   Baby SUNA zaune a harabar Hospital din domin tunda aka shiga da gwaggo babu wanda aka ba daman ganinta kowa hankalin shi a tashe yake domin basu san halin da take ciki ba,aunty ladi ta jima da isowa dama ta na wurin mu'azu da ɗan Gusau suna hanya da matan su.



   Misalin ƙarfe 3:30pm jirgin su ammie ya sauka a Sokoto dama yayi waya da wani abokin shi kafin su iso ya turo driver ya ɗauko su zuwa gidan shi.

Cikin mutunci aka tarbe su a gidan Alhaji Ahmed suka shi abinci suka yi sallah la'asar sanan suka nufi hospital din.

Tunda suka shiga harabar Hospital d'in gaban ammie ke faduwa sai maimaita innalillahi wa'inna ilaihi raji'un take har suka fito domin zuwa office d'in doctor.

LOKACIN su mu'azu da ɗan Gusau suka iso hospital din,bayan sun gaisa aunty ladi ta tambayi matan su.?

Mu'azu ya amsa da suna gidan gwaggo da yara domin anyi hutu, shi ɗan Gusau hankalin shi na kan baby data kara girma  komi ya ciciko kar wata babban mace; Ganin haka aunty ta umurci baby tabi Mamman driver zuwa gida domin a dafa abinci da za'a kawo Hospital d'in tasan na gida dasu halima suka yi baƙi sun cin ye.

Sai lokacin babu ta daga kai  cikin Sa'a  suka hada ido da ɗan Gusau wanda keta kallon ta cikin  Sa'a suka haɗa ido ya sakar mata kayatacen murmushi.

Mikewa tayi jiki a sanyaye ta kama hanya lokacin su ammie suka doso hanyar da baby ta biyo domin a corridor din office na doctor da zasu gani yake.....

~🅿47-48~




      Baby wace kanta na ƙasa zuciyarta cike da tunani iri iri akan rashin lafiyar gwaggon nasu ji kawai tayi taci karo da rodi wanda ke jere a gefen corridor d'in, Tuni ta dawo hayacin ta domin jin Muryar da ba zata taɓa manta da ita ba a duniya"baiwar Allah tunanin me kike yi haka.?

Baby na ɗago idanu sai cikin na ammie alamar mamaki da tsoro ya bayana a fuskanta,ita kuwa ammie farin ciki ne fal ranta domin ta jima tana kai kukanta wurin Allah ya bayyanar da baby idan tana raye!

Pretending baby tayi kamar bata gane ammie ba ta raɓa gefen su zata wuce ammie ta rik'o hanunta gam tana faɗin ko a mafarki na ganki ba zan taɓa manta wa da kamanin ki ba koda kuwa shekaru dubu mu kayi Bama tare bare kwata kwata shekaru bakwai kenan rabon mu da juna!

Hajia mariya ta sake baki tana kallon ikon Allah,shi kuwa alaji habu da farko bai gane baby ba saboda girma da tayi,sai daga baya da ammie tayi magana ya gano bakin zaren.

Baiwar Allah Ni fa ban sanki ba kuma ban san inda kika fito ba  don Allah ki sake ni cuz I have something urgent to do.


Hajia Maria tace ikon Allah sai kallo wanan wace ce kuma ammie.?,,tsuru tsuru suka yi daga ita har alaji domin sanar da hajia mariya wacece baby kamar buɗe wani sirri ne na ammie!

Ammie wacce ta dad'e da gane gaskiya a kullum burin ta bai wuce ta hadu da baby a raye ba domin neman gafara akan abubuwan data aikata mata,tabbas tasan hakkin baby ke bibiyanta tun a duniya Allah ya bata komi na jin daɗin rayuwa sai ya hana ta haihuwa wanda tasan hakan wani hikima ce ta Ubangiji domin ta samu gyara kura kuranta na baya,tasan ta zalunci baby ko shakka babu wanda Allah ba zai kyale ta ba.

Cikin kuka mai cin rai tace aunty baby diyanta CE ta cikina,nice na haife ta wallahi rabo na da ita shekaru bakwai kenan sai yau Allah ya haɗa mu!

Hajia mariya da mamakin duniya ya cika ta rasa abun cewa tayi,iya binciken da tayi akan ammie bata taɓa sanin ta  taɓa haihuwa ba duk yawon barikin ta sai yau.

Baby tace baiwar Allah nifa ban sanki ba haka ban taɓa ganin ki ba,ni marainiyace iyayena sun jima da rasuwa don haka ki nemi yar ki tun dare bai maki ba"kwace hanunta tayi ganin Mamman driver na nufo inda suke,a hanzarce ta bar wurin...,ammie ba kiran baby!,baby!!,baby!!!,,ko waigo wa bata yi ba bare ta amsa tana isa wurin mota ta rushe da wani irin wahaltacen kuka!

Wurin da su ammie suke tsaye naman driver ya iso tare da mike wa alaji HABU hannu suka gaisa yake tambaya ko baby ta masu wani laifi ne.?

Girgiza kai yayi yana faɗin ko ɗaya mun jima muna neman ta yana nuna ammie wanan itace mahaifiyar ta shekaru bakwai kenan tana rokon Allah ya bayyanar da baby.

Ikon Allah amma wanda ya kawo ta wurin mu cewa yayi marainiya ce!,,zai iya yiwuwa domin koda ta bar hannun mahaifiyarta bata san itace ta haife ta ba....,wanan magana babba ce,yanzu abinda za'ayi zan baku address na inda muke zaune zuwa dare lokacin hankali ya kwanta kowa ya dawo gida kwa iya zuwa ayi magana a natse.

Gaskiya da ka kyauta mana kuwa"cewar ammie, faɗa masu yayi tare da bada number wayar shi suka yi sallama.

Koda ya isa wurin mota baby na kuka bai ce da ita k'ala ba ya buɗe motar ta shiga suka kama hanyar gida shiru kowa da abunda yake tunani cikin zuciyar shi har suka isa gida ta fice ba tare da tace dashi komi ba zuwa cikin gidan.

Hayaniyar mutane ya kara bak'anta ranta domin bata son damuwa  a wanan lokaci,sallama kawai tayi cikin Sa'a babu kowa a babban  palour'n  duka suna palour'n gwaggo ta nufi d'akin ta tare da fashewa da sabon kuka bata lura da mutum saman bed d'in ba ta faɗa tana  ci gaba da kukan ta.

Subhanallah"shine abunda aysha ta faɗa,wace basu taɓa haduwa da baby ba sai ranar domin a Egypt take karatu (aysha itace babban diyar mu'azu ce suna zaune a Kaduna bayan ta kammala Nigeria Turkish Kaduna ta samu admission a Egypt tana shekara ta na biyu kenan tazo hutu,kwata kwata bata wuce shekaru ashirin ba a duniya).

Ɗago baby tayi cikin firgice tana faɗin gwaggon ce ta rasu ko wani abun ne ya faru da ita.?,shaf baby ta manta daga hospital ta fito nan take ta share hawayenta tana girgiza kai...

Nanauyar ajiyar zuciya aysha ta aje tana hamdallah, still idanunta na kan baby, what's wrong with you.?,"nothing,ta Fadi a takaice tana ci gaba da share hawayen ta tare da tashi daga saman bed d'in zata fita zuwa kitchen Aysha ta rik'o hanunta,kada ki boye mun komi baby tun kafin kizo gidan nan na samu labarin ki wurin Grandma (haka take ce da gwaggo),ba yabon kai ba tunda aka haife ni duk a cikin jikokin gwaggo tafi ji dani Allah ya haɗa jinin mu sosai,bata iya boye soyayyar ta a gare Ni Ko a gaban wanene tana matukar kokarin kyautata mun hakan ya jefe hadasa da kyashi a zukatan wasu daga cikin jikokin nata musamman jamila da rilya (yaran aunty ladi),wanan dalili yasa Daddy na  saka Ni a boarding school tunda na gama primary school ko hutu akayi baya cika zuwa dani saki lokacin sallah ba don baya don kasancewa tare dani ba sai don gudun ɓacin zumunci, grandma tayi faɗa har ta gaji haka ta hakura...,inaso ki saki ranki duk abinda ya samu bawa Mukadari ne daga Allah kiyi hakuri.

Maganganun aysha ya sanyaye zuciyar baby a karo na farko taji wani don ta da kaunarta kuma zuciyarta ya aminta da aysha a karo na farko,a hankali ta furta Nagode da shawara zan kiyaye.

Murmushi ta saki shike nan my baby Allah ya barmu tare,murmushi baby tayi itama tace amin...,yanzu ina zaki kuma.?, kitchen zan dafa masu aunty ladi abinci!

Uhmmm moma matsala duk masu aikin gidan nan sai ta saki.!,na saba ai babu matsala...

Katse ta tayi"U mean kin iya girki ?,nodding tayi.

Lallai Ni kam daga  indomie, macaroni sai snacks kawai na iya,shima saboda ummi na tana matsawa idan NAZO hutu mu shiga kitchen tare shi yasa na iya, snacks is my favorite shi yasa na koya varieties.

Murmushi kawai baby KE saki domin abinda ke zuciyarta daban ne ganin ammie da tayi yau ya fama mata tsohon gyambon dake zuciyarta...maganar aysha ya katse ta"kin gaida dasu Ummi na.?

Ban gansu ba"shine amsa da baby taba aysha,riko hanunta tayi muje suna dak'in grandma...,baby Bata so zuwa ba saboda matar ɗan Gusau kwanan duniya suka haɗu karuwa take kiranta saboda abinda ya faru tsakanin ta da mijinta shekarun baya da suka wuce.

Excuse aysha tace suka shiga tana riƙe da hanun baby,aunty Hauwa(ummi'nsu aysha)ta faɗada murmushin ta,maraba da yan matan gwaggo .., murmushi baby tayi ta zube yar ƙasa tana kwasan gaisuwa.

Farat aunty Balki matar ɗan Gusau ta amsa da,"karuwar gidan gwaggo kina lafiya.?

Magananta yama aunty hauwa ciwo matuƙa domin tun ba yau ba take kiran baby da wanan suna tasha mata nasiha akan itama uwace tana da d'iya mace wace ba zata so a danganta husna da irin wanan mumunar kalma ba amma sai gashi itace ki kiran diyar mutane da wanan suna.

Ammm aysha ku koma palour kun ji...,aysha riƙe da hanun boy za su fita aunty Balki tace wallahi hauwa idan kina sakar wanan shegiyar yarinya fuska wata rana sai kin koma da kin sani ai da haka muka fara na ɗauke ta tamkar yar dana haifa a cikina ashe butulu ce sai ne na kamata rungume da mijina,shegiyar mara asali...,gani Hauwa tayi wanna ba maganar da za'a yi a gaban yara bane tace da aysha ta haɗa kan su Khalifa da husna su koma palour cikin Daga murya!

Murmushi wanda yafi kuka ciwo baby tayi domin bata ga laifin aunty Balki Akan bakaken maganganu data yaba mata ba,laifin iyayenta ta gani wanda suka fara wulakanta ta a duniya, Basu ɗauke ta bakin komi ba,how will she expect someone to respect her!

Kitchen ta shige ranta a dagule,aysha ta kunna masu Khalifa tv ta biyo baby kitchen tana kwantar mata da hankali tare da bata labarai na ban dariya tana koyon girki har baby ta haɗa faten dankalin turawa da wake,sanan ta fara haɗa peppe soup na d'anyen kifi,"dama su halima sunyi tuwon shinkafa da miyar kuka na dare.

Zuwa biyar na yamma su kamar komi ta shirya a foodflask ta zuba cikin basket Dama tana fashin sallah wanka kawai tayi ta saka riga da zani na shadda tana jiran aysha ta fito a kai su Hospital,domin su aunty Balki bayan sun gama hutawa suka nufi hospital din da sauran yara...,aysha na idar da sallah ta dauko Arabian gown marron colour ta saka tare da yane kanta ta veil fari,kallon sama da ƙasa tayiwa baby tare da tuntsirewa da dariya har da hawaye,"wane irin dressing ne haka kamar tsohuwa.?,kin san Allah ko ummi na tabar daura zani bare ki sweet 17.

Uhmmm Kawai baby tace domin bata da wata natsuwa nayin wani kwalliya a yanzu babban addu'ar ta kada Allah ya sake haɗa ta da ammie a hospital d'in.

Common wear something sexy my dear"ta FAƊI cikin sigar tsokana,babu yadda baby ta iya haka ta koma ta saka duguwar riga itama na bak'ar jalabiya da mini hijab fari ta fito...aysha tace ko ke fa yan mata suka nufi wurin mota inda Mamman driver KE jiran su sai hospital.


   Can kowa bayan su baby sun tafi ammie ta ci gaba da raira kukanta wanda da kyar alaji HABU ya rarasheta suka isa wurin office din doctor Raji,wata nurse alaji habu ya tambaya ko yana ciki.?,ta tambaya ko suna da appointment ne dashi.?
Ya mata bayani shiga tayi ta sanar dashi yace su ɗan jira...,koda ta fito ta sanar dasu  akwai patient su bari ta fita sai su shiga,GODIA ya mata suka samu wuri suka zauna.

Hajia mariya na kwantar mata da hankali har aka basu daman shiga,,bayan sun gaisa ya yiwa ammie wasu yan tambayoyi ta bashi amsa yana rubuce-rubuce zuwa wani lokaci ya mika wasu takadu guda uku wanda biyu kwaje kwaje ne da za'a yiwa ammie a d'akin gwaji (blood,urine and sputum test),ɗaya kuma hoton ciki(ultrasound, pelvic USS) za'a mata...,wanda sai gobe da safe zata dawo aata domin yamma yayi yanzu  GODIA suka mashi sannan suka fita.


Ammie taso su wuce gidan da baby take kai tsaye,alaji habu yace tayi hakuri har da dare kamar yadda Mamman ya faɗa masu....,dole ta hakura ba don taso ba suka nufi gidan da suka sauka.


Lokacin da baby suka iso hospital gwaggo ta su sauki sosai dama nauran ƙarin numfashi aka jona mata da ƙarin ruwa(dama tana da chronic asthma sai high blood pressure),awanni goma  tayi a dakin taimakon gaggawa aka mayar da ita amenity word domin ta Kara samun isasshen hutu.

Koda suka isa d'akin cike yake da mutane, gwaggo tana shan shayi,ganin su baby ta faɗada murmushin ta tare da ware hanunta ma aysha,hawaye cike da idanun aysha ta faɗa jikin gwaggo tana faɗin,"Grandma I miss u so much,I can't bier to see u in pain..., shiiiii I'm feeling much more better now,In Sha Allah ba zan mutu ba sai naga auren ki eeshanle na.!,aka sa dariya banda su Jamila da haushi ya cika su.

Baby tace sannu gwaggo ya ƙarfin jikin.?,ta amsa naji dama sosai baby sannu da kokari Allah ya maki albarka ya jikan iyayen ki ,aysha ta amsa da amin ya Allah.

Addu'ar da gwaggo tayi ya saka Mamman driver ya tuna da mutanen da ya haɗu dasu d'azu wanda suka ce sune iyayen baby.

Aunty ladi ta harari baby Zaki yi serving abincin ko sai an faɗa maki aikin ki.?,,cikin rashin k'urazi ta ciro plate tare da tambayan gwaggo abinda za'a zuba mata.?,tace tafi don faten,aunty ladi ma faten Tasha,jamila da rilya kuwa peppe soup aka zuba masu,jin gwaggo tayi girkin yasa yaya ɗan Gusau yace ta zuba mashi peppe soup d'in ai kowa aunty Balki sai aika wa baby da uwar harara take kamar idanun ta zasu fado....,wata nurse ta shiga tare da cewa kowa ya fita mara lafiya na bukatar hutu sun cika d'aki da surutu.

Babu musu suka fita kowa ne da plate  nashi na abinci suka baje a babban darduma da aka kawo daga gida maza na gefe,mata na gefe da yaran,"aysha ko kallo su rilya basu isheta ba taja hanun baby SUKA koma gefe tana nuna mata pics dinta na Egypt a babban wayarta.

Mamman  driver wanda shi ɗaya ya rage a d'akin ya sanar da gwaggo abinda ya faru dazu ai kuwa mamaki ya cikata  domin dai sadiqu yace mata iyayen yarinya sun rasu,su wanene wanan haka kuma.?,ya zama dole ta koma gida yau domin ayi komi a gabanta da dare,idan har karya sadiqu ya shirya mata zai ga ɓacin ranta kuwa....,cewa tayi a kira mata ɗan Gusau ya amsa mata sallama gida zata koma.

Babu yadda likita bai da ita ba tayi hakuri har gobe fafur ta amincewa, dole ya basu sallama ya jaddada masu a kula da shan magungunan ta cikin lokaci,duk abinda aka san zai tayar da ciwon ta a kiyaye.

Nana suka haɗa kaya suka nufin gida kowa na mamakin wanne dalili ya saka GWAGGO ke son komawa gida!

A gida ta tara su akan da karfe takwas na dare kowa ya same ta a cikin babban palour akwai Magana!


      Ranar Monday da misalin karfe goma na safe aka k'arawa Captain sadiq girma ya koma General Sadiq mangal,cikin karamawa aka gama komi duk wanda suka tafi training an ƙara masu girma...,Nana aka ci gaba da bushe bushe,ciye ciye da shaye shaye har biyu na rashin aka kwatse.

General Emanuel ya shirya mak'ark'ashi ya akayi wa General Sadiq mangal transfer zuwa imo state domin cikar burin shi akan yaya Mallam wanda yayi alƙawarin sai ya saka an kashe shi da azaba kamar yadda ya kashe mashi justina,sai gashi sadiq nason zama mashi barrier.


Jin wanan bayani a bakin manya General Sadiq mangal ya gane makircin General Emanuel ne kuma yasan ta inda zai bulo mashi dama an bashi hutun sati biyu yaje yaga gida kafin ya koma imo state.

Sadeeyah Koda ya sanar da ita wanan labari bata ji dad'i ba ko kadan a ganinta bai kamata mijinta ya saka kanshi cikin rigimar Aminu ba domin bashi da mutunci ko kaɗan bare tausayi.

Wurin babban su Gaba ɗaya General Sadiq ya nufa tare da niman alfarma a bashi Aminu tunda ya amsa laifin shi kuma an amshi uniform nashi babu wani hujja da zai saka a mashi wanan tsattsauran hukunci haka.

Nana take aka rubuta takarda na sallamar aminu daga Barrack d'in aka bashi ya kai wa General Emanuel...,cike da farin ciki ya nufi office d'in ya bashi,rai a b'ace ya karanta tare da nodding Kai cikin baƙin ciki ci bashi da damar hukunta sadiq domin yanzu matsayin su ɗaya.

Saka hannu yayi a takardan ya mika wa General Sadiq,cikin matsakaiciyar farin ciki ya mika wa wani crut aka fito da yaya Mallam wanda yake kamar matacce ko kwakwaran motsi baya iya yi saboda azaba...,a hanzarce aka saka mai shi a mota ya nufi hospital dashi...


_Halimatu Sadiya Muhammad_

*_A meaningful silence is always better than a meaningless words[be wise my people]_*



~🅿49-50~



    Ƙarfe takwas na dare a kofar gidan gwaggo yayiwa su ammie,dama an san da zuwan su mai gadi ya wankale masu gate suka shiga harabar gidan....,Mamman driver ya masu iso har babban palour'n gidan dake shak'e da mutane.

Da sallama suka shiga aka gagaisawa,gwaggo tace,"Balki(matar ɗan Gusau), Hauwa (matar mu'azu),aysha,Khalifa(yaran mu'azu),Jamila,rilya(yaran aunty ladi),husna da boi(yaran ɗan Gusau) duka su shiga palour'n ta  na ɗan lokaci zasu tattauna magana kai muhimmanci da baƙi.

Ko wanne fuskan shi ɗauki da mamaki domin dai basu taɓa ganin wa yan'nan bayin Allah ba sai yau.

Palour'n ya rage daga gwaggo,aunty ladi,dan Gusau, mu'azu sai Mamman driver(kanin mijin gwaggo), Daga murya gwaggo tayi"Baby......

Na'am ta amsa tare da fitowa da hijab nata"dama idan da baƙi a gidan bata fitowa haka nan saboda tana da manyan boobs",da sallama ta shigo palour'n nan take gabanta ya bada darammm.!,lokaci ɗaya suka haɗa ido da ammie a hanzarce ta sauke nata idanun ƙasa tare da samun wuri gefe ta zauna.

Gwaggo tace bayin Allah zan so jin abunda ke tafe daku domin ina a gadon asibiti na samu labari dalilin da yasa na dawo gida kenan domin jin gaskiyar magana a bakin ku.

Alaji habu ya fara,GODIA ya tabbata ga Allah subhanahu wata'ala mai kowa amai komi,mai yin yadda yaso a lokacin da yake so kuma a wurin da yake so abun ya tabbata,suna na Alhaji Abubakar,wanan itace uwar gidata hajia mariya,ga amarya hajia  Hadiza (ammie),daga Kano muke;,kamar yadda Mallam Mamman ya sanar dake babu tantama baby d'iya CE ga hajia Hadiza(ammie) itace ta  dauki cikinta na watanni tara,ta haife ta har ta shakara goma sha ɗaya a hanunta a lokacin da zan aure ta kenan sai zaman baby ya koma hannun mahaifinta mai suna Captain Aminu Lagos yake aiki...,tun daga lokacin da muka danka baby a hanun mahaifinta bamu sake samun labarin ta ba sai yau da muka haɗu a hospital" bayan shekaru shida kenan....

Baby ta katse shi wallahi gwaggo ban san su ba Ni Ko ganin su ban taɓa yi ba sai yau,iyayena sun jima da rasuwa"tana kuka mai cin rai.!

Baby yau Ni ammie'n ki zaki ce baki sani ba.?,in raini cikin ki wata tara zuwa haihu,rainon ki,ci da shan ki har sutura babu wanda na gaza amma yau kina nuna wa duniya baki sanni ba.???

Mamaki ya cika kowa a palour'n musamman aunty ladi wacce bata ɗauki baby a bakin komi ba ashe tana da asali da gata fiye da tunanin ta,su yaya ɗan Gusau sanyi jikin su yayi, yayin da gwaggo ta gano wani abu cikin wanan muhawara nasu!

Alaji habu mamaki yake yadda baby ta girma ta za mace mai taurin kai da kafefen zuciya,ita kuwa hajia mariya da bata san gaskiyar magana ba shiru tayi.

Gwaggo yai gyaran murya,ku dakata dukan ku,"ke baby menene dalilin ki na cewa ba mahaifiyar ki bace bayan kina yanayi da ita sufar jikin ku ɗaya banbanci launi fata da fuska kawai ya banbanta ki da ita.?

Gwaggo wallahi da gaskiya ban santa ba, kin san dai ban taɓa maki ƙarya ba ko.?,idan baki yadda bama a kira UNCLE Sadiq idan ya sansu shima(Baby bata da masani akan General Sadiq yasan abinda ya faru tsakanin yaya Mallam da ammie).

aunty ladi uwar gulma tayi saurin dialing number General Sadiq tare da sakawa a speaker har yayi RINGING ya katse bai daga ba saboda lokacin suka isa Hospital da yaya Mallam ana ƙoƙarin ya saka hannu a shige dashi emergency unit.


Gwaggo ta kalle su,bayin Allah a gaskiya ba zamu amshi uzurin ku ba har sai mun tattauna da wanda ya kawo baby amana hannu na,yanzu kuyi hakuri ku tafi kwa dawo wani lokaci!


Kuka ammie ta fara tana faɗin HAJIA don girman Allah ki mun rai ba zan iya tafiya ba tare da baby ba,ITA kenan kwal dana mallaka a duniya daga ita har yau Allah zai sake bani haihuwa ba nasan na tafka kuskure a baya wanda son zuciya ne da sharrin shaidan sai zuwa Quruciya dake dawainiya dani wallahi bayi nadama na gane gaskiya,ku bani Baby mu tafi tare zan nuna mata gata fiye da yadda kuke tunani,zan nuna wa duniya itama 'yace kamar kowa...

Wallahi ba zan bi ki ba na gwamace in ga ranar mutuwa ta da koma gidan ki sai yanzu kika san NIMA 'YACE kamar kowa saboda Allah ya fara nuna maki Ishara tun a Nan duniya,ai kaɗan kika gani ma sai Allah ya fitar mun da hakki na akan ku!

Saukar mari Mai lafiya taji a kumatun ta wanda sai da wuta ta ɗauke mata ji kake tsit wurin yayi shiru,ba kowa bane ya natsar da ita illa mu'azu ya lura tana neman wuce gona da iri,shi mutum ne da baya ɗaukan raini ko kaɗan musamman daga na ƙasa dashi haka baya bada dama a raina babba a gaban shi wanan ne dalilin da yasa bai cika shiga tsabgar kowa ba iya kanshi da mutane gaisuwa.

Da kyau mu'azu Allah ya maka albarka domin na lura wani tashen balaga take ji dashi yan kwana kin nan"cewar gwaggo!

Su aunty Balki dake palour'n gwaggo duk abinda ake tattauna wa a kunnen su har marin da kanin  mijinta ya dank'arawa baby.

Kawu mamman sai faɗin yake"ayi hakuri dai Alhaji mu'azu yarinya ce..., ɗan Gusau ma ranshi ya ɓaci da kalaman baby a kan wa yan nan bayin Allah wanda akwai alamar gaskiya cikin maganar su.

Lokacin da General Sadiq ya dawo wurin motar shi ya ɗauki ATM card din shi saboda biyan Bill ɗin da aka rubuta masu wanda idan ba'a biya a yanzu ba babu DOCTOR da zai taɓa yaya Mallam cuz is a criminal Case.

LOKACIN da ya ɗauki phone nashi yaga missed calls da dama da text message ciki akwai na  matar shi sadeeyah,aunty kubra elder sister'n shi sai na aunty ladi...,hankalin shi ne ya tashi domin yasan gwaggo na Hospital"kada ace mai gwaggo ta rasu.?,shine abinda ya fara zuwa ranshi.!

Number aunty ladi yayi dialing a gagauce ringing biyu ta daga tana faɗin yauwa ga sadiq ɗin ma...,shiru wurin ya ɗauka kowa na sauraren yaji abinda sadiq zai fadi game da Baby.!

Gwaggo tayi sallama, nanauyar ajiyar zuciya ya sauke tare da faɗin tsohuwa mai ran karfe kina lafiya dai ko.?,murmushi tayi duk da zuciyarta a dagule yake"sadiqu na Fika lafiya ma yanzu.

Ya amsa da alhamdulillah..,ba wanan ba sadiqu wata magana mai muhimmanci zan yi da kai yanzu wanda inaso kaji tsoron Allah ka sanar dani gaskiya hakan zai sa ka tsira da mutuncin ka a idanun mutane da dama wanda ya zuwa suna tare dani.!

Mamaki ya cika shi,ok gwaggo nayi alkawari faɗin gaskiya komin dacin ta.

Iyayen baby suna raye ko sun mutu.?

Ki gafarce Ni gwaggo suna raye amma...


Ya isa haka ba dogon bayani nake nima ba...,suna wane gari ne,sanan menene sunan su.?

Tau mahaifinta dai babban amini na ne yana nan Lagos sunan shi Aminu,mahaifiyar ta na kano Hadiza anfi sanin ta da ammie.


"Allahu akbar",shine kalmar data fito daga bakin kawu mamman, ɗan Gusau ya faɗada murmushin shi,su ammie ma sai hamdala a cikin zukatan su.

Sadiqu kaci gidan ku amma menene yasa ka boye mun a farko.?

Gwaggo ki gafarce Ni lokacin da na kawo ta hankali na ba'a kwance yake ba, wallahi sai daga baya na gano gaskiyar lamarin.

Yanzu dai uwar yarinya na son daukar diyata idan ka amince zan bata suyi ta tafiya...

Baby ta kwala Kara wallahi UNCLE sadiq ba zan bita ba na faɗa masu ban santa ba...,"shiru yayi domin an ci zarafin baby da yawa a rayuwa ba don Allah yasa ta faɗa hannu na gari ba ai da tuni rayuwarta ta zama tarihi...


Gwaggo zan so kici gaba da kuka mun da Baby har hankali na ya kwanta sai mu zauna ayi maganar nan once and for All cuz yanzu haka ina hospital da baban baby bashi da lafiya seriously an shiga dashi emergency ma,ITA ammie tayi hakuri har wani lokaci idan Aminu yaji sauki  zamu neme ta a tafi gana iyayen su can a sulhunta,Ni zan tafi sai na kira anjima"kit ya kashe wayar!

Gwaggo tace dasu alaji habu suyi hakuri har zuwa wani lokaci kamar yadda sadiqu ya faɗi insha Allah baby zata  dawo wurin su.

Ba haka ammie ta so ba amma dole ta hakuri domin taurin kai da zafin rai ba nata bane dole ta bisu a hankali har ta cimma burin ta.

Exchanging contact akayi tare da yin sallama da juna suka tafi jiki a sanyaye.

Baby Kam d'aki ta Kuma tare da rufe kofa taci gaba da kusar kukanta jin uncle Sadiq yace zata koma hannun azalumar matar nan Ammie wacce bata ɗauke ta a bakin komi ba sai k'ask'antaciya mara y'anci....,can Kasan zuciyar ta kuwa farin ciki ne fal jin papanta na gadon asibiti ba lafiya sai taji kamar ya zuba ruwa a ƙasa tasha nan take ta tashi ta dauro alwala ta gabatar da sallar nafila raka'a biyu na musamman domin addu'ar Allah ya ɗauki ran yaya Mallam a yau ɗin nan ba gobe da,tana mai addu'ar Allah yasa yayi mutuwar wulakanci,yadda yasa taji kunya a duniya Allah ya saka yaji kunya na duniya da lahira....,ita kuwa ammie kada Allah Ubangiji ya cika mata burin ta na samun haihuwa,Allah ya tozarta ta kamar yadda ta tuzarta Baby, Allah ya wulakanta ta  duniya da lahira kamar yadda ta wulakanta baby!!!

Ranar tsakanin baby,ammie da General Sadiq babu wanda yayi baccin kirki!

Tunda safe ammie suka koma hospital akayi duk gwaje gwajen daya kamata nan aka gano mahaifarta keda matsala kamar yadda aka sanar da ita a baya wanda ba zai iya riƙe kwana halitta ba har ta samu ciki,sai dai likita ya basu shawarwari sai magunguna daya daurata a kai na sati ɗaya bayan ta gama sha za'a mata aiki a sati na biyu wanda bayan kwana ki goma idan aikin yayi kyau zata iya daukar ciki,idan bai kyau ba sai dai ta hakura har ta Allah ta kasance.

Kuɗi sosai alaji habu ya zuba aka kamala komi da alkawari zasu dawo Sokoto Bayan sati ɗaya.

 Ranar da zasu tafi har gidan gwaggo ta koma suyi sallama da baby Amma fafur taki sauraren ta a karshe ma toilet ta shige ta rufe har ammie ta gaji da zama ta tafi tana sharar kwala...


Su aunty ladi yanzu an gano baby nada gata kamar sauran yara an rage mata habaici da cin fuska.

Ɗan Gusau kam sai dai yasan yadda yayi ya amshi number baby a wurin aysha domin yana suna shan inuwa ɗaya.

Bayan kwana biyu duka suka tattara ko wane ya koma inda ya fito saboda hutu ya ƙara za'a noma schol mondaya,da zasu tafi aysha ta kwashe kusan rabin kayan sawa ta ta mallaka wa baby domin tana matukar sonta haka suka rabu cikin kewar juna.



    General Sadiq ya biya duka Bill ɗin da aka rubuta da yake teaching hospital ne duk abunda ake bukata akwai anan ba sai ya sayo ba shiru shiru yaya Mallam bai farfaɗo ba,an gyara raunuka da ya samu a jikin shi,duk wani taimako na gaggawa da ya kamata  a bashi likitoci sun yi iyakan kokarin su amma har yanzu numfashin sa bai koma dai dai ba dole suka juna mashi oxygen machine aka mayar dashi ICU ( intensive care unit),kowa ba'a bari ya shiga inda yake saboda halin da yake ciki, nurse's Kawai ke kula dashi.

Sadeeyah ta rasa gane kan mijin nata domin kullum yana hanyar Hospital Dan hutun da aka basu shi bai tafi gida ba ita ya hana ta tafiya,dole idan hutun ya ƙare zasu koma imo state ne,ga tarkacen yaya Mallam da yasa aka kwaso daga gidan da yake ana jibge mata a palour saboda yanzu bashi da muhali a cikin barikin.

Ranar da aka cika kwana na uku likita ke mashi bayani patient nashi ya farfaɗo sai fizge fizge yake yana yunkurinyin magana amma ya kasa sai da suka mashi Allurar Bacci ya samu relief,ya bashi shawara mai zai hana idan da hali su fitar dashi waje domin case ɗin nashi na nan fin karfin su....,GODIA ya wa doctor akan zai shawara da sauran yan uwa kafin ayi abinda ya kamata.

Gida ya dawo yana hawaye abun ya mashi yawa shi ɗaya duk ya fita hayacin shi saboda ganin halin da yaya Mallam yake ciki lokaci guda ya ƙare ya koma kamar yaro Dan shekaru goma.

Sadeeyah ta fito cikin kwalliya nanta samu mijinta cikin wani hali kwantar mashi da hankali ta shiga yi har ya samu natsuwa tace,"abban faruk mai zai hana ka nemi yan uwan aminu su gana dashi kafin ta Allah ta kasance a gare shi.?,nasan kana matukar kokari a kan shi amma wanan faɗa ba na mutum ɗaya bane,kai da bakin ka kake sanar dani Aminu bashi da wani buri da ya wuce ya nemi gafarar yan uwan shi kafin ya bar duniya tabbas idan har kayi nasarar sada shi dasu ba zai taɓa mantawa da kai a duniya ba.

Haka ne deeyah Nagode maki shaf na manta da wanan magana wallahi shaida hankali na ya dauku don haka akwai wayar shi a hanuna kuma ina da address na gidan su dake Kano zan tafi can ina sha Allah ba zan dawo Lagos ba sai da yan uwan shi.

Bai tsaya bin jirgi ba ranar da dare yabi luxurious bus zuwa kano wanda da safe suka isa babu bata lokaci ya gane gidan Mallam saminu inda aka kiri yaya Mallam cikin Sa'a babban ɗan shi Kabiru na gida ya mashi bayanin komi wanda ya jinjina maganar sosai a ranshi musamman ƙarfin hali da zalunci iri na yaya mallam akan baby da sauran mata daya yaudara har Allah ya kawo karshen zaluncin shi.

Yanzu dai kaci abinci na san har gidan da ammie take aure zamu fara zuwa office ɗin mijinta daga nan mu wuce kauyen su.


General Sadiq yace ba zai iya cin komi ba domin hankalin shi ba'a kwance yake ba su tafi Kawai....,a office d'in alhaji HABU bayan an masu iso ya mashi bayani shine sadiq Wanda baby ta fara zama ganin shi kafin ta koma Sokoto...,cike da farin ciki ya tarbe su domin tunda  suka dawo daga Sokoto bai sake gane kan ammie ba kullum sai kuka da surutai a dalilin  baby!

Kamar yadda General Sadiq yayi wa Kabir bayani haka yayiwa Alhaji habu wanda ko kaɗan bai ji tausayin yaya Mallam ba amma dole zai yi yadda sadiq ɗin yace watau a shawo kan ammie su tafi kumbotso tare ayi shirin zuwa Lagos su gana da shi.


A gida kuwa da fafur ammie tace ba zata ko nan da can ba sai dai yaya Mallam ya mutu domin bata manta da cin zarafi da yayi mata a rayuwa ba.

Kallon tsaf sadiq ya mata tare da gano yanayin ta da baby tabbas taurin kan su iri ɗaya,yanayin magana da structure nasu ɗaya.

Yace shike nan idan ba zaki yafe ma ɗan uwan ki ba wallahi ba zan taɓa Mallaka maki baby ba,idan ba'a yafiya a duniya iyayen ki zasu manta da abinda kika aikata har su gafarta maku.?, Allah Ubangiji zai gafarta maku ne.???

Lokacin jikinta yayi sanyi ta shirya suna nufi kombotso dama ammie tayi wa umma waya akan haɗu nan zuwa da baƙi ayi masu abinci...

Da yake hanya nada kyau basu ɗauki lokaci mai tsayi ba suka isa ƙauyen wanda aka cika su da kayan ciye ciye ciye da shaye shaye sai dai hura kawai General Sadiq ya iya sha suka nufi masallaci domin gabatar da sallar azuhur.

Tun a masallaci Alaji habu yayi magana da liman akan ya biyo su gida akwai tattaunawa da za suyi mai muhimmanci.

Bayan sun dawo daga masallaci ne alaji habu yaba General Sadiq Dama ya sanar dasu dalilin zuwan shi...

Nan umma ta kare da kukan farin ciki ɗan ta yana raye..,shi kam ya hydar kamar zai kama da wuta don bala'i yana faɗin ai kwara ya mutu su hutu tunda ya aikata barna ya gudu ya zaɓi duniya akan iyayen shi da yan uwan shi gashi ga duniya ta ishe shi riga da wando...!,sai da liman ya janyo ayoyi da hadisai ya masu nasiha mai ratsa jiki sanan kowa ya natsu ya karbi ƙaddara zuwa la'asar suka shirya zuwa Kano inda zasu kwana,alaji HABU da General Sadiq zasu yi chiku chiku nema masu visa na zuwa Lagos gobe.

Waya General Sadiq ya bugawa gwaggo yana so a nema wa baby Visa yau ɗin nan na zuwa Lagos gobe akwai muhimmiyar abu da zai kai ta...,bata tsaya dogon bincike ba ta saka sani ya amshi passport na baby Bata son a Sanar da ita har sai an kamala komi sanan.



Washe gari da misalin 9:30 AM jirgin su General Sadiq,ammie,alaji habu,umma,ya hydar ya tashi zuwa birnin Lagos wanda alaji HABU ya ɗauki nauyin tafiyar...

Itama baby gwaggo tayi mata nasiha mai ratsa zuciya sanan ta saka Mamman driver ya Kai ta airport har sai da yaga tashin jirgin nasu sanan ya juyo gida.


Jirgin su General Sadiq ya fara sauka a Lagos wanda suka jira kusan minti shirin sanan nasu baby ya sauka....farin ciki tayi sosai na ganin uncle Sadiq bayan shekaru uku kenan shima yana mamakin girma da tayi cikin ɗan kankanin lokaci.

Ganin ammie wani irin baƙin ciki ya ziyarci ci zuciyar tana ayanawa a ranta ba dai zamanta ya ƙare a Sokoto ba kenan zata koma hannun Ammie.?, shi menene ma ya kawo ammie'n Lagos..???
