      General Sadiq ganin baby ya faɗada murmushin shi tare da faɗin masha Allah har ta iso inda suke zaune direct inda yake zaune ta nufa tana murmushi"welcome back home sweet uncle and congratulations on your great achievement General Sadiq.!

Baki da dama baby,u changed alot..,anyway mu Isa gida ku  huta kafin visiting hour yay sai mu tafi hospital d'in.

Dukan su kallon baby suke ammie babban baƙin cikin ta yadda baby ta nuna halin ko in kula  a kan su,ko da bata san umma da ya hydar ba ai ta santa da alaji habu!

Already driver na jiran su da mota ƙirar Siena ammie,baby da umma suka shiga gidan baya,ya hydar da alaji habu suka shiga tsakiya gaba General Sadiq ne da driver sai Barrack....,tun daga gate na farko ake sara mashi har zuwa compound nashi  ya fito.

Baby da tunda suka shiga motar bata sake cewa komi ba har suka isa tunani ne iri iri cikin zuciyarta bata taɓa tunanin zata sake dawo wa cikin Barrack din  nan ba da irin abubuwan da suka faru a baya.


Da yake aunty sadeeyah ta San da zuwan su cikin shiga na alfarma ta fito rike da hanun faruk ta masu iso cikin palour'n aka sake gagaisawa ...,Baby rungume ta tayi cike da farin cikin sake ganinta bayan wasu shekaru.

Tare suka wuce kitchen baby na rike da faruk sai fara'a yake kamar ya santa,cikin kankanin lokaci suka shirya abinci"tuwon semovita da miyar yauk'i...,ammie sai bin baby take da ido wanda umma na lura da zallar kauna da soyayya dake cikin idanun ammie akan baby,Wanda baby Bata San ma tana yi ba..,shi ya hydar tunda yaga baby a karu na farko bai sake duban inda take ba domin bai dauke ta wata tsiya ba.

Cikin natsuwa suke cin abincin kowa da abunda yake sakawa cikin zuciyar shi...,Baby daukan plate nata tayi zuwa d'akin da tayi zama tare da faruk,General Sadiq kuw d'aki Ya koma da matar shi domin ya basu daman cin abinci yadda ya kamata.

Kallon sadeeyah yayi,"kinga wacce ke sanye da abaya red itace ammie'n baby,wanan mai babban jiki shine mijinta,dayan namijin shine kanin aminu,dattijuwa kuwa mahaifiyar su ce!

Allah mai iko gasu da kamala kuwa daga ganin su akwai mutunci da natsuwa a tattare dasu....,deeyah na yadda wanan al'amari ƙaddara ce kawai domin Aminu ya fito babban gida,  gidan mutunci masu kuka da Addini,,yanzu babban matsala Baby taurin kai taƙi bani haɗin kai a wanan tafiyar wallahi ko arzikin gaisuwa basu samu ba.

Ran sadeeyah ya ɓaci,ita ɗin banza ai ba itace ta haifi kanta ba,akwai wanda ya  isa ya wuce ƙaddarar shine a  duniya.?,wallahi tsawata mata za kayi lokaci yayi da za'a bar lalabata ka nuna mata dole tayi biyayya ga iyayenta da yan uwan su.!

Shiru yayi yana cin abincin shi har ya kamala  ya fita palour lokacin sun gama suna zaune shiru...,ce da sadeeyah yayi ta Kira baby su fito lokaci na tafiya.


Gaisawa suka ƙara yi,yace kamar yadda na sanar daku a baya suna na General Sadiq mangal asalin ɗan adamawa Yola ne tun muna makaranta Allah ya haɗa Ni da Aminu kuma munyi  zama na amana da mutunci har kuma kamala kowa ya samu aiki cikin Sa'a akayi posting ɗin mu wuri ɗaya a nan  Lagos;duk da ba wani abu na sani game dashi ba Allah dai ya haɗa jinin mu...,wanan itace matata sadeeyah yar Kaduna ce tare muka riki baby(ya nuna Baby), tunda zaman ta ya dawo nan wurin Aminu basu samu jituwa ba;bayan wani lokaci da zan tafi karatu Canada na damka amanar ta hanun gwaggo na a Sokoto state wanda a nan kuka haɗu da ita kwanakin baya.


Bayan tafiya na Aminu ya haɗu da wata ƙaddara wanda yayi sanadiyar mutuwar diyar ɗaya daga cikin manyan mu a dalilin haka aka sallame shi daga aiki bayan horo mai tsanani da suka bashi yanzu haka yana hospital Rai a hanun Allah;,sanin bashi da wani buri da ya wuce ya gana da iyayen da yan uwan shi domin neman  gafarar su akan abubuwa da ya aikata a bisa kuskure da rashin sani,wanan dalili ya saka na tafi har Kano na taho daku idan Allah ya saka kuna da rabon ganawa zamu tafi hospital d'in,"TSAYAWA yayi na yan mintuna yana karantar yanayin fuskokin su kafin yaci gaba..,na haɗa ku da girman Allah kowa ya manta da abubuwan da Aminu ya aikata a baya a rungumi ƙaddara domin komi ya kare mashi yanzu ne yake da bukatar ku fiye da ko wane lokaci,yanzu yake bukatar afuwan ku da addu'a domin gamawa da duniya lafiya...!

Umma ta fashe da kuka,ka sanar damu gaskiya ko mai sunan Mallam ya rasu ne kake boye mana.?

Ko ɗaya umma yana tsakanin mutuwa da rayuwa dai Allah shi e mafi sani akan komi. ..babban aalfarma da nake nema a wurin ku duka shine;umma,hydar,ammie da baby ku dubi girman Allah da darajar Manzon Allah Sallallahu alaihii Wassalam ko cire abubuwan da Aminu ya aikata a cikin zukatan ku,ko wane ya yafe mashi da zuciya ɗaya!

Baby ki nemi gafarar ammie'n ki akan abunda kika mata a Sokoto domin ta fini iko da kusan ci dake.

Ko wane hawaye yake banda alaji habu wanda yace; INSHA ALLAH komi ya wuce kamar yadda ka nemi alfarma nasan umma ta jima da yafe wa  mai sunan Mallam suma sauran yan uwan shi nasan za su aikata hakan..,muna godiya sosai a kan namijin kokari da kayi wurin kula da baby da tarbiyar ta har ta kawo wanan lokaci,tabbas ka zama aboki na kwarai wanda ke rik'o da gaskiya da amana Allah ya saka maka da mafificin alheri tare da matar ka, Allah ya raya maku zuri'a ya sanya albarka Amin aka amsa duka.

Baby kallon su kawai take domin ta riga da tayi alkawari babu wanda zata yafe wa a cikin su.

Inaga tunda mun gama tattaunawa sai mu Isa Hospital d'in lokaci na tafiya...

Sadeeyah ta katse shi,kafin mu bar gidan nan ya zama dole baby ta nemi gafarar su akan halin ko in kula data nuna masu tun a airport har zuwa yanzu domin ba irin tarbiyyar da muka bata kenan ba!

Ganin yadda ran aunty sadeeyah ya ɓaci yasa ta rusuna"kuyi hakuri Don Allah ban san ku bane...,buge bakin ta sadeeyah tayi,gidan ku don baki san su shine dalilin da yasa ba zaki gaishe su ba.?

Girgiza kai tayi,ba zan sake ba.

Umma tayi murmushi ranshi sani ne ai babu komi,ya hydar dai ikon Allah yake kallo domin fuskar ta sak yaya Mallam ikon Allah kenan.

Ammie dai jikinta a sanyaye tana fargaban yadda zata haɗa ido da yaya Mallam bayan shekaru da dama.

Tashi su kayi dimbin zuwa hospital;alaji habu,ya hydar motar General Sadiq suka shiga yana driving...
Baby,ammie da umma motar aunty sadeeyah suka shiga(ta miƙa faruk gidan friend nata),aunty sadeeyah ke driving sai uban faɗa take zabgawa Baby har suka isa Hospital d'in nurse tace ba zasu shiga inda yake ba sai da izinin doctor Kuma ba'a shiga duka sai mutum ɗaya ya shiga ya fito,wani ya shiga.

General Sadiq da kanshi yaga DOCTOR ya nemi alfarma a bari su shiga duka family nashi ne daga Kano suka zo.

Nurse ya kira yace ta bari su shiga kawai...ai kuwa tsakanin umma da sadiq ba'a san wanda yafi wani saurin isa wurin bed d'in ba.

Ko wane jikin shi a sanyaye yadda baby da take jin tsanar shi sai data tausayi mashi,ya hydar ganin dan uwan shi haka bai san lokacin daya fashe da kuka ba,domin ya tuna da abubuwa da dama lokacin QURUCIYAR su yadda yaya Mallam keda ilimi,hakuri,tausayi da kula da yan uwan shi.


Yaya Mallam kwance a gadon asibiti rai a hanun Allah hanun shi na dama dauke da ƙarin ruwa,hanun hagu kuwa ƙarin jini ne,kafafun shi duka biyu nad'e da bandage,an taushe hancin shi da na'ura jawo numfashi,idanun shi rufe kallo ɗaya zaka mashi kadan yana cikin mawuyan cin hali.

Umma duk'e a gefen shi tana kuka sai maganganu take"Mai sunan Mallam haka Allah yayi da kai,wanan wace irin tsautsayi ne.?,mutum mai nagarta da tausayi ya koma haka .,a kullum maganar Mallam kafin ya rasu shine Allah ya karkato da hankalin ka zuwa gida domin yasan ko bayan ranshi kai ne zaka kuma da yan uwan ka,zaka zama uba a gare su, d'a na gari wayyo duniya mai sunan Mallam ashe burin Mallam ba zai cika ba ?

Ammie da ya hydar ma kuka suke sosai na tunawa da mahaifin su,musamman ammie da Allah bai nufa sun gana ba har ya rasu!

Baby dake kusa da aunty sadeeyah jingine da bangon d'akin zuciyarta a bushe sai kallon su take ɗaya bayan ɗaya...,alaji habu da jikin shi yayi matukar sanyi fita yayi ya basu wuri, General Sadiq kukan zuci kawai yake yi!


Sun jima a d'akin amma bai ko san suna yi ba bare su saka ran zai farfaɗo har aka kira sallah maza suka nufi Masjid,Baby da ke fashin sallah wuri ta samu a harabar Hospital d'in ta zauna tana zancen zuci,su ammie kuwa suka nufi gefen da mata ke Sallah suka gabatar.


Suna hospital d'in har 8pm lokacin visiting hour ya ƙare amma bai farfaɗo ba suka nufi gida kowa da zancen zuci sai alaji habu ke ƙarfafa masu guiwa yana umurta kowa ya taya yaya Mallam da addu'a ko Allah zai kawo mai ɗauki.

A daren ranar babu wanda ya runtsa hadda baby jam'i suka yi cikin palour'n tare da gabatar da sallar nafila raka'a goma sha biyu bayan sun idan ko wanne ya buɗe Alqur'ani mai girma ya karanta iya abunda zai iya har zuwa lokacin sallar fajr General Sadiq ya kwararo adu'oi SOSAI na nema wa aminin shi Lafiya sanan suka nufin Masjid.

Ƙarfe Goma na safe a hospital ya musu cikin Sa'a suka samu ya farfaɗo amma baya iya motsa ko ina na sassan jikin shi sai bin kowa da idanu duk da ya gane su amma ina bai iya magana harshen shi yayi nauyi sosai.

Umma cikin kuka ta rungume shi;wallahi mai sunan Mallam na yafe maka duniya da lahira dama baka mun komi ba ƙaddara ce,ban taɓa riƙe ka azuciya ta ba akan abunda ka aikata,a kullum addu'a na Allah ya kare ka a duk i da kake ya dawo mana da kai lafiya,na yafe maka mai sunan Mallam Allah ya baka lafiya ya sanya maka albarka, Allah ya tashi kafad'un ka"kanta na kwance saman jikin shi take wanan maganganu",jin dumin hawayen shi yasa ta ɗago kai suka haɗa idanu,hannu tasa tana share mai hawayen tare da girgiza ka...,bana son ganin hawaye a idanun ka daga yau baƙin ciki ya ƙare a zuciyar ka sai farin ciki, INSHA ALLAH zaka samu lafiya mu koma gida zan baka kulawar da baka samu a wuri na ba a baya,wallahi tsabar jahilci ne da son zuciya ya saka Ni kunyar ɗan fari har haka ta faru......,ya hydar ya dawo kusa da ita tare da dafa kafad'un ta",umma ki bar faɗin haka wanan abun da ya faru ƙaddara ce kuma ya riga da ya wuce sai fatan Allah ya tsare gaba ya bashi lafiya,tabbas ɗan uwana hakika a baya na kulace ka akan abunda ka aikata wa yar uwar mu amma yanzu komi ya wuce ya zama tarihi bani da tamkar ka,mu uku iyayen mu suka haifa sai gashi ƙaddara ta raba mu baku rayuwa a tare ba ko wane yana a wani wurin dabam,yanzu da Allah ya ƙaddara saduwar mu zan su mu riƙe juna da amana kowa ya yafe ma ɗan uwan shi domin Ni kam na jima da yafe maka ina fata kai ma zaka gafarce Ni akan abubuwan da nayi maka a baya.?"kuka ne yaci ƙarfin shi ganin ɗan uwan nasu ko motsi baya yi sai Kada idanu alamar yana don yin magana amma babu baki sai hawaye dake zirya a idanun shi.

Aunty sadeeyah ta rik'o hanun ammie da baby Tare Da rada masu a kunne'ku je ga ɗan uwan ko domin nema nashi rahamar Ubangiji!

 Jiki a sanyaye suka isa jikin gadon ko wane idanu cike da hawaye tsabar tausaya wa,da kyar ammie ta iya buɗe bakin ta da yayi nauyi",yaya Mallam na yafe maka duniya da lahira nasan abu da muka aikata a baya tsautsayi ne kuma a bisa rashin sani,fatan Allah ya baka lafiya ya yafe mana baki ɗaya ɗan uwana...kukan da yaci ƙarfin tane ya hana ta ƙara sa maganganun da tayi niyya!

Baby kuwa maganganun da yaya Mallam ya taɓa furta mata ne suka dawo cikin kwakwalwar ta",i never pray for ur success,I always pray for ur downfall,u are a disgrace to me AND my entire family,nayi maki alkawari ba Zaki bar duniya ba sai kin ɗauki cikin shege,kin raini cikin shege kuma ki haifi shege!!!

Wani wawan tsaki taja zata fita aunty sadeeyah ta rik'o ta...,Sadiq yace let her be plz!

Cikin kankanin lokaci idanun yaya Mallam suka kafe,yana don yin magana harshen yayi nauyi babu hali already dama kanshi ya kare,numfashin shi ke fita da sauri sauri yana gurnani kamar wanda yaga wani abun tsoro!!

A guje Alaji habu ya Kira nurse tazo taga abinda ke faruwa ta nufi office na doctor suka dawo tare tana janye da oxygen mashine, doctor yace su fita zai duba shi...,umma ta Fara kuka da firge firge ita a barta Wurin shi babu inda zata!

Ammie ta rikota suna kuka suka fito waje kowa yayi cirko cirko...zuwa wani lokaci doctor ya fito jiki a sanyaye,da sauri suka karaso inda yake...,yace I'm sorry we lost him, Aminu lokaci yayi sai HAKURI!


A guje umma da ammie suka bangaje shi tare da faɗa wa dakin umma ta faɗa jikin gawan tana sambatu", innalillahi wa'inna ilaihi raji'un shike nan ba uwa ba Uba,ba miji Babu babban d'a, Allah bai nufa burin Mallam ya cika ba na ganin ka zama uba ga yan uwan ka shike nan ka tafi ka bar mu....,ammie ma d'aura kanta tayi saman chest nashi tana kuka mai cin zuciya ..!

General Sadiq ya shiga girgiza doctor Yana faɗin don Allah ka sake duba wa da kyau I'm pretty sure dogon suma yayi,plz  examine him again he will regain his conciouse"idanun shi ya rufe sai kuka yake kamar ƙaramin yaro,aunty sadeeyah ta Mike jiki a sanyaye tare da riƙe mijinta,abban FARUK ka dawo cikin hayacin ka ko wane mai rai sai ya d'and'ana zafin mutuwa,kowa lokaci yake jira babu mai wuce lokacin shi, addu'a shi yafi buƙata a gare mu ba kuka ba!

Ya hydar da tunda likita ya fito ya zaune a ƙasa dirshan kaman ƙaramin yaro wanda ya samu tabin hankali sai muzurai yake domin ya kasa furta komi,alaji habu ke bashi baki,nurse ya fito tare da fadin don Allah su fitar masu da matan nan biyu dake ciki za suyi aiki ne.

Alaji habu da keda sauran ƙarfi ya shiga tare da rik'o Ammie yana basu magana dole umma ta fito suka zube a waje kowa na kuka...,baby dake zaune gefe ta fashe da dariya,,ai kwara kuyi kuma domin dai mutum wuta direct zai tafi...

Bata rufe baki ba aunty sadeeyah ta wanka mata marigayi biyu masu lafiya zata kai na uku General Sadiq ya rik'e hanun ta!

Dif kiwa ya dauke wuta a na mamakin lamarin baby domin abun ya wuce wuri yana neman komawa rashin imani da tawali'u.

Aunty sadeeyah tace Ashe baki da imani Babu.?,yaushe kika zama kafura ban sani ba.?

Uhmm wanan shine a dake ka a hana ka kuka, yanzu aunty laifi na kike gani bayan abunda wanan azalumin ya aikata a gare ni,wani irin wulakan tani ne bai yi ba a Duniya,wane irin suna ne bai kirani dashi ba,wane irin mugun baki ne bai samun ba.?,don na faɗi son Raina shike Nan na kafurta na Koma mara imani.!,wanan suna ye ddashi suka dace domin shine ya mutu kafiri mara imani ta hanyar sab'on Allah!

Umma sai data rumtse idanun ta jin wanan zafafan kalamai akan ɗan data haifa,ammie ko ji tayi kamar ta shak'e baby har lahira,ya hydar da baya cikin hayacin shi bai san wani irin waina suke toyawa ba.

Cikin daga murya aunty sadeeyah tace Allah ya Isa tsakani na dake baby,irin tarbiyyar dana baki kenan?,kina son janyo mun zagi da tuzarci a idon duniya.?,wallahi ban yafe maki ba....

Nurse be ta iso wai likita na son ganin su, Alaji habu ya riko hanun Sadiq suka nufi office d'in...,yace dama akan maganar gawan ne sun gama duk wani necessary arrangement suyi signing Kawai su ɗauke shi!

General Sadiq yace mai zai hana gawan ya kwana a hospital  tunda bashi da muhalli a Barrack d'in idan suka ce zasu tafi dashi kano wahalar zai masu yawa,tunda akwai makabarta cikin Hospital d'in gobe zasu zo ayi jana'iza a nan.

Duba da irin mutunci dake tsakanin General Sadiq da doctor Adebayo yace shike nan ba Matsala,shima Alaji habu yaji daɗin shawarar.

Haka suka nufi gida jiki ba kwari cikin mota sai koke koke suke,shi dai ya hydar kamar mara hankali har yanzu ko komai bai ce ba.


Duk wanda ya kamata su san da arasuwar yan uwa da abokan arziki an sanar dasu gobe da ƙarfe 9:30am za'a yi jana'iza a teaching hospital.

Matan abokan aikin su sai shigowa suke suna masu umma gaisuwa...,lokacin da labari ya isa wurin mr and Mrs Emanuel hada taka rawar farin ciki burin su ya cika,nan take tace zasu shirya zuwa garin su domin gabatar da bukin mutuwar Justina.

Baby hankalin ta kwance tana d'aki tayi bacci ta har gari ya waye bayan sallar asuba aunty sadeeyah ta raba masu Alkur'ani domin a karanta bude wa kawai baby tayi amma cikin zuciyarta tana addu'a kada Allah ya sada shi da rahamar shi,azabar kabari,quncin kabari Allah ya tabbatar dasu a gare shi, Allah yasa wutar jahannama ta zama makoma a gare shi.


Ƙarfe 9:30am aka gama shirya gawan shi a hospital suka sallace shi aka nufi makabarta dashi ana saka shi a rami lokacin ya hydar ya saki wani irin ihu wanda ya razana sauran Mutane,yana sambatu don Allah a cire dan uwan shi a cikin wannan rami bai mutu ba da ranshi,kada su azabtar dashi da ɗan uwa da ranshi..., General sadiq sake sharan hawaye ya riko hanun shi suka nufi wurin mota mutane sai tausaya masu ake har aka rufe shi aka shafa addu'a sanan suka koma gida.

Su umma na ganin dawo War su nan wani sabon kuka ya barke babu mai rarashin wani.

Bayan kwana biyu General Sadiq ya fito da duk wani kaya da yaya Mallam ya Mallaka ya damk'a wa umma,ya sanar da su akwai kuɗaɗe MILLION huɗu cikin account nashi zai basu ATM d'in a tafi dashi.

Umma tace babu abinda take so cikin kayan ,shima ya hydar yace baya so aka sadaka dasu.

Kuɗi kuma aka yanke shawarar za'a siya wuri a nan kauyen su a gina Masallaci ana sallah ciki ta zame mashi sadakatul jariya.

Sanan General Sadiq yace yana so daga yau riƙon baby ya koma hannun Ammie domin itace mahaifiyarta tafi cancanta ta riƙe ta.

Daga cikin d'aki baby ta kurma wani uban ihu tana faɗin NOOOOOOOO!!!

~🅿53-54~



   
    Daga cikin d'aki baby ta kurma wani ihu tana faɗin"NOOOOOOOO!

Hankalin kowa ya koma gareta amma banda General Sadiq da aunty sadeeyah,sai da ya gama karantar su tsaf sanan yayi gyaran murna;kada ku wani damu da duk abinda zata aikata,tafiya dai ya zama dole a gare ta idan har ta ɗauke Ni da Uba lalai ne tabi umurni na FINAL.

Umma tace sadiq a wannan lokaci irin ku sun yi ƙaranci,mutane masu amana,gaskiya da sadaukar wa,tabbas kai aboki ne na hakika ka zame wa marigayi uwa da uba kuma ɗan uwa haka baby kun bata tarbiya,Ku nuna mata so da kauna Allah kadai zai iya biyan ku ladan wanan hidima da ku kayi ina fata zumunci ya daure a tsakanin mu na har abada, Allah ya sanya maku albarka, Allah ya kare ka da kariyar sa a duk inda kake a faɗin duniya, Allah ya raba ku da sharrin mahassada,shaidan,mutum da aljan Amin.

Ya hydar yace,umma ta gama faɗin komi nima ina ƙara gode ma ku tare da fatan alheri a duk inda kuka kasance....,aunty sadeeyah ta rik'o hanun ammie,"Ina ƙara jajanta maku akan rashi da muka yi na ɗan uwan mu fatan Allah ya sada shi da rahamar sa amin,don Allah kuyi hakuri da baby wallahi ba haka take ba zaman ta Sokoto duk ta canja wanda ban san dalilin ba,na tabbatar maku za ku ji daɗin zama da ita.

Murmushi ammie tayi,uhmm na fahimci haka dama yaro idan ya kai wani certain age(adolescent stage) idan ba ANYI da gaske ba tankwasa shi zai wahala ku taya mu da addu'a na yi maku alkawari zan kula da baby fiye da tunanin ku,zan daura daga inda kuka tsaya wurin bata tarbiyya,INSHA ALLAH zata samu ingantaccen rayuwa wace ta rasa a baya...,duka suka amsa da Allah ya bada ikon Amin.

Alaji habu yace maganar karatun ta yanzu a wani mataki take.?,ss2 dai take kuma son nuna tana da kokari sosai,"cewar General Sadiq.

Shike nan dama zamu koma sokoto ganin likita zan je har can gidan gwaggo a dauko kayanta sai a amsa mata transfer letter taci gaba da karatu wurin mu.....,hakan ma yayi zan yi kokarin yin waya da gwaggo kafin ku tafi.

Suka ci gaba da tattaunawa dai domin ranar za subi jirgin 3:pm zuwa Kano,su  General Sadiq kuwa washe gari zasu nufi Yola.

Zuwa karfe biyu kowa yayi Sallah sun ci abinci, General Sadiq ya shiga d'akin babu wace ke zaune saman bed da alama taci kuka har ta gaji.


Ya jima tsaye yana nazarin ta daga bisani yace BABY!,shiru ba amsa..., ɗago idanun ki ki kalle ni., still Bata ce komi ba kuma bata kalle shi ba"I SAID LOOK INTO MY EYES,ya FAƊI cikin ɗaga murya!

A hankali ta ɗago suka haɗa ido,nan da nan ta sauke nata idon domin nashi kamar garwashin wuta ya koma saboda kad'uwa.


Kin ga alamar wasa a idanu na.?, girgiza kai tayi,ya sake daka mata tsawa"OPEN YOUR MOUTH AND TALK,tace a'a....

Listen and listen Good,yanzu ba tare da bata lokaci ba za kibi ammie'n ki da yan uwanta zuwa kano,wacce ta fi kowa iko da kusan ci dake, Allah yasan na riƙe ki bisa gaskiya da amana,i did all my possible best to make u happy and who you are today!zama na dake ban taɓa daukan ki a matsayin bare ba sai d'iya wacce na haifa,haka mata ta kin su kulawa, soyayya,kauna da yarda a wurin ta,dispite all that,we ask for nothing in return,lokaci yayi da zan baki umurni kibi ko kina so ko bakya so ya zama dole ki koma hannun Ammie,ki zama mai biyayya a gare ta da duk wani na gaba dake wanda rashin yin haka a gare su zai janyo rashin jituwa a tsakanin mu,na rantse da Allah idan kika sab'a mata BAN YAFE MAKI BA,Allah ya kai ku Lafiya"ya juya ya fita.

Tashi tayi ta shiga toilet ta watsa ruwa dama bata zo da wani kaya ba sai tsarabarta na wurin aunty sadeeyah babban jaka ciki ta saka abaya  ta fito lokacin duk suna palour ita ake jira,ko kallon ta aunty sadeeyah Bata yi ba suka fito domin masu rakiya airport....,koda suka Isa passenger basu gama taruwa ba saboda sun fita da wuri.

General Sadiq gefe tare dasu ya hydar suna ƙara tattaunawa akan matsalar baby...,gefe ɗaya kuwa aunty sadeeyah CE ke ƙara kwantar masu da hankali har aka fara kiran passenger faruk na hanun baby ta zo har wurin da su General ke tsaye ai nan take ta fashe da kuka tare da rungumo aunty sadeeyah tana faɗin"aunty kiyi hakuri don Allah ba zan sake ba...., rungumo ta tayi tana tapping bayan ta cikin tsigar rarashi,it ok baby,idan kun tafi ki zauna da kowa lafiya kin ji, always be a good girl Allah ya maki albarka Amin.

General Sadiq da jikin shi yayi sanyi yace ku tafi suna jiran ki sai mun yi waya ko.?,tayi nodding suka tafi.


Suna tsaye har jirgin su ya tashi sanan suka nufi gida cike da kewar su.....,basu tsaya bata lokaci ba suka shiga haɗa kayan su(gado da kujeru dama nan suka same su),komi nasu wanda suke so suka haɗa aka zuba cikin motar shi dana sadeeyah wanda driver zasu ja tun da asuba zuwa yola domin su General Sadiq jirgin safe zasu bi.....,tun a daren suka fara sallama da yan uwa da abokan arziki.

Da misalin ƙarfe 9am suka kama hanyar Yola by air..


    Around 4:10pm jirgin su ammie ya sauka a kano,gidan ammie suka nufa direct inda su Hadiza da saratu suka shirya masu abinci aka yi serving kowa Banda baby wace tace ba zata ci ba,ammie tace da Hadiza ta kai kayan baby d'akin da take kwana nan zata zauna,da yake gidan ba bakon baby bane ta gane d'akin da ba'baa mairo ta zauna ne amma an gyara shi sosai da babban gado ga plasma tv manne a bango....,wanka ta shiga tayi sallah.

Su umma ma sallah suka yi domin ranar zasu koma gida,da suka kebe ta ƙara yiwa ammie nasiha sosai akan rayuwa kuma ta riƙe baby akan amana da gaskiya.

Driver alaji habu ya basu ya kai su gida cike da  arziki haka suka rabo da ammie jiki a sanyaye.

Da yake ranar ba gidan ammie ya kamata ya kwana ba sallama ya masu ya nufi gidan hajia mariya tare da saratu.

Tunda baby tayi sallah tana zaune bakin gado ta haɗa kai da guiwa maganar duniya Hadiza tayi amma bata amsa ta ba dole taje ta faɗa ma ammie,da kanta ta shigo Dakin tare da zama kusa da baby ta dafa ta,"meke damun ki baby.?,shiru babu amsa,ko zama dani ne ba kya so.?,ta amsa da eh...,Ni kuma ina son ki a na son zama dake har ga Allah domin nice na haife ki duk duniya baki da tamkar Ni...

Oh yanzu kika san _NIMA 'YACE_.?,ko ada can ke y'ata ce baby Quruciya da rashin yadda da ƙaddara yasa na wulakanta ki sanan abinda ya faru a baya ya wuce ki daina tuna shi ina jin ciwon hakan.!

Abu ɗaya zaki mun a duniya wanda zan yadda kina sona kuma ba zan sake tuna abinda ya faru a baya ba...,fadi ko menene idan bai saɓa wa Shari'a ba zan maki shi.

Ok daga yanzu kada ki sake shiga tsabgata,kada ki sake kira na diyar ki koda wani ya tambaya ban haɗa komi dake ba,idan kika mun haka kin gama mun komi a duniya ba zan sake tuna baya ba zamu zauna lafiya!

Idanun ammie sharaf da hawaye tayi nodding kai,shike nan idan har hakan zai faranta ranki consider it done,ta tashi ta fita jiki a sanyaye tana iya duk nata tayi banging door din ta faɗa saman gado tare da fashe wa da wani irin kuka mai cin rai,"Ya Allah why am I suffering for someone's mistake.?,why me.?,Allah ka agaza mun yaya Mallam ya tafi ya bar i da baƙin ciki a duniya Yau gashi sanadin Abunda ya aikata nice KE wahala..., Hadiza da tunda suka fara magana da baby tana tsaye tana ji har suka gama ammie ta koma d'aki gashi shiru bata fito ba, shine dalilin da yasa ta bita d'akin nan ta same ta tana kuka tana sambatu....,itama kuka ta shiga yi domin tasan komi mamanta ta sanar da ita duk abinda ya faru tsakanin ammie da yaya Mallam har haihuwar baby.

Rarashin ammie ta shiga yi tana kwantar mata da hankali domin su ba Baby LOKACI su gani ko zata saki jiki dasu,da kyar ammie tayi shiru domin bata so Hadiza ta sani ba sai gashi anyi komi a kan idanun ta.


Haka gidan ya kasance shiru ammie na d'aki bata fito ba,baby na d'aki hadiza ne kawai a palour tana kallo itama gajiya tayi ta nufi d'aki dauke da plate na abinci da bottle na ruwa ta kai wa baby wacce har ta fara bacci Hadiza ta tashe ta,da kyar da magoya ta ci abincin kaɗan sanan suka kwanta.


Da safe tare da Hadiza suka gyara gidan suka haɗa breakfast,domin baby ta lura tana da hakuri da sauƙin kai...,ammie da zazzaɓi ta tashi saboda kuka da damuwa da ta saka wa kanta,Hadiza ta taimaka mata tayi wanka ta ɗan sha tea kaɗan sanan tasha magani taci gaba da bacci....,koda Hadiza ta faɗa wa baby ta shiga ta duba ammie banza tayi da ita domin ba damuwar ta bane.

Kallo suke a palour amma hankalin Hadiza na kan ammie,jim kaɗan sai ta shiga ta duba ta shiru bata tashi ba,waya ta daga ta kira Daddy (alaji habu) yace gashi nan zuwa.

Baby kuwa ta kira aunty sadeeyah suka sha hira domin ta shaida mata sun isa Yola lafiya faruk na can yana ta wasa da yan uwan shi....,murmushi tayi,ina gaida uncle nasan fushi yake dani aunty ki bashi hakuri Allah mun shirya da ammie.!,iba fushi yake dake ba abunda ki yine Bai ji daɗin shi ba kuma INSHA ALLAH idan na tafi Kaduna zan biyo Kano in gan ku.

Yauwa aunty na sai kin zo suka yi sallama....,koda alaji habu ya iso baby ta gaida shi kamar yadda taga hadiza tayi har ƙasa,ya amsa masu ya haura saman.

Babu laifi ta tashi kuma taji dama, rungumo ta yayi a jikin shi yana shafa jikinta a hankali tare da kwantar mata da hankali...,tayi luf ajikin shi ganin haka yasan abinda take so domin rabon shi da ita kwana hudu kenan,bai bata lokaci ba ya haɗe bakin shi da nata Yana kissing cikin kwarewa tare da shafa dukan sassan jikin ta...,nan take ta rashin lafiya ya tafi ta biya mashi suka faranta wa juna rai.

Bayan komi ya lafa suka yi wanka tare ya shirya ya  fito har wurin mota ta raka shi ya nufi wurin aiki.koda tadawo ciki suna kitchen ta dan leka su tare da faɗin sanun ku da aiki.?,Hadiza tace ah aunty har kin fito ya jikin.?..,ta amsa na warware me kuke dafa mana ne baby.?,"idon matambayi,shike amsar da ta bata.!,murmushi tayi ta koma palour kawai domin ranta ya ɓaci da maganar baby,iqrah chennal ta saka inda karatun Alqur'ani ke tashi..., relax tayi saman three seater  karatun na ratsa ta har aka kira Sallah ta haura sama.

Kamala aikin suka yi aka zuma a foodflask ko wace tayi wanka suka yi sallah baby tabi lafiyar gado abunta .

Wuraren la'asar tace su shirya zasu fita wanda baby bata so ba amma Hadiza ta matsala  suka shirya suka fita cikin motar ammie sai Nasarawa G.R.A tayi parking mota gefe suka shiga da sallama,"madaidaicin gida ne mai ɗauke da dakuna manya uku gefe sai babban palour shima da daki ɗaya ciki da toilet ,muryan wata mata suka jiyo daga kitchen Wanda ko a mafarki baby ba zata manta muryan ba"ba'baa mairo shine abinda babu ta furta tare da shiga kitchen din a guje ta rumgume ta tana faɗin ba'baa mairo ashe kina nan.?,wacece wanan haka zata k'arasa ni.?

Tana dagowa suka haɗa ido da ammie gefenta Hadiza ne sai wace ke rungume da ita,"nice baby ba'baa mairo baki gane Ni bane.?...,baby dama kina raye Shekara da shekaru babu labarin ki muna ta cigiya da Ammie shiru!

Ina nan ba'baa yanayin rayuwa ne,ya bayan rabo.?,sai alheri baby mu shiga daga ciki mana...,suka isa palour'n aka gaisa tace Hadiza dauko maku zobo a fridge Nan take tayi wa ammie gaisuwa rasuwan yaya Mallam,sai da ta bari hankalin su baby ya dauku wurin kallon film taja hanun ammie zuwa kitchen Dama DAMBUN shinkafa take yi.

Kallon ammie tayi,ashe an samo baby kuma.?,,kuka ta fashe mata dashi dama abinda ya kawo Ni wurin ki kenan ba'baa mairo ki taimaka baby na Nan saka mun ciwon zuciya.!

Cikin sanyin jiki tace saboda mene?.,ammie ta kwace komi ta sanar da ita tun haɗuwar su a Sokoto har dawo wannan su.!, ba'baa mairo ta jinjina maganar sosai duk da tasan  ba'a kyauta wa baby ba Amma bai kamata tayiwa iyayenta haka ba",tau ammie kin san yaran yanzu ka haife sune baka haifi halin su ba Musamman ya'ya mata idan ba'a tsawata masu da wuri ba ,zai zame masu matsala a rayuwar su na gaba,zan so kiyi duba da rayuwar ki a baya yadda kika taso da irin soyayya da yaya Mallam ya baki tare da hana kowa tsawata maki, menene hakan ya haifar a gaba.?, BABBAN abun kunya,rugujewar farin cikin ki, ɓacin suna,lalacewar tarbiyya ga tsarabar haihuwa.

Tun wuri ki tsawata wa baby  wallahi,bilahil azim idan har kika mayar da kanki uwar banza wai kina daukan wani banzan alkawarin yarinya ƙarama sai kin yi kuka a gaba,ba baki nayi maki ba,kada ki yadda soyayyar d'iya ya rufe maki idanu ki ƙasa ganin gaskiya,kada ki sake Tarihi ya maimaita kanshi Ni dai na baki shawara a matsayi na ta uwa a gare ku baki daya daga ke har mijin ki shawara ce.

INSHA ALLAH ba'baa mairo zan kula Nagode,,yauwa taci gaba da bata shawarwari Yadda za ta zauna lafiya da baby har suka kamala aikin ba'baa ta kira baby ta kwashe kayan zuwa palour cikin plate ɗaya ta haɗa masu suka ci sai magrib suka biya gidan hajia mariya dake lamid'o  crescent har zuwa isha'i suka nufi gida.



Haka rayuwa ya kasance tsakanin ammie da Baby sai kallon kallo domin ammie ta ƙasa tabuka komi cikin shawarwarin da ba'baa mairo ta bata saboda tana tsoron rasa baby a karo na biyu,har lokacin komawa su Sokoto yayi ayi mata aiki.

Ana gobe zamu tafi ta tara su a palour tayi masu nasiha mai ratsa jiki which is directly referring to baby saboda abubuwan da take aikatawa a gidan is getting out of hand.

*CONGRATULATIONS MY SISTER*



~🅿55-56~


    LOKACIN da zasu wuce Sokoto ko fitowa baby baya yi ba har suka wuce airport Hadiza sai binsu take da addu'a Allah yasa a dace ,Baby kam ko a jikinta addu'a da take yi cikin zuciyar ta shine Allah kada ya bada Sa'a...


  Can Yola kuwa walima aka haɗa na cin abinci musamman domin General da matar shi akan ci gaba da ya samu na rayuwa,yan uwa da abokan arziki ne suka haɗu a babban palour'n gidan da misalin 8pm akayi addu'a sosai daga bisani kowa ya zuba irin abincin da yake son ci aka ci aka sha zuwa 9:30pm taro ya watse cikin farin ciki...,hajja mahaifiyar shi ya samu da maganar yana so su wuce gidan su tunda an gyara komi.

Tace babu matsala kuna iya tafiya ɗan tsaurun ido daga zuwa yau kace yau zamu tafi gidan ko baka hakuri har gobe yan uwa su raka ta.?

Ayi hakuri hajja kin san dole zata Kaduna kuma nan zan barta har sai naga yanayin wurin aikin nawa kafin mu tafi tare.!,,ku tafi ina.?

Yarinya da ƙaramin ciki zaka ta yawo da ita cikin duniya babu inda zata sai ka rinƙa zuwa idan ka samu sarari ta cicibi faruk dake Bacci ta nufi hanyar d'akin ta.

Baffan sune ya shigo wanda ya gama tattaunawa da baƙi da yake ta samu,"me kuke jira har yanzu baku tafi gida ba.?, dama walima ta tsayar damu baffa yanzu zamu tafi..., Yafi dai ku huta gajiya ya haura part nashi.

salama suka yi da sauran sisters nashi suka nufi gidan su wanda ya saya kafin ya wuce Canada.

Suna isa tun daga gate sadeeyah ta fara santin gidan domin yashi gyara an kashe kuɗi sosai,"Gaskiya gidan nan tayi kyau honey,hanun shi dake waist nata ya gyara zaman shi tare da faɗin duk kyawun gidan bata kai ki ba deeyah na.

Murmushi tayi tare da sagala hanunta biyu saman wuyan su ta haɗe fuskan su wuri ɗaya suna kallon juna cikin ido for a moment,softly ta furta"U Are the best husband ever,I love u a million times...,lower lip nata ya dan ciza sai da tayi ƙara cikin tsigar shagwaba,"And I love U a trillion Dollar times wifey.

Dariya ta saka mai sauti"U too funny,suka shige cikin palour'n ɗaya sha furnitures barakallah  sai Masha Allah take fadi har suka isa bed rum Nan ta sake baki saboda ya mata kyau sosai,cikin wasa yace ki ma bar jin daɗi d'aki nane nan ba naki ba sauran biyun kuwa ɗaya na faruk da sister nashi"yana shafa cikin ta, ɗaya kuma na amarya ne tunda akwai babban fili ta baya a hankali zan saka a gina maki naki ko uwar gida.?,,Kumbura tayi tana ƙoƙarin fita,"tunda AM not welcome here barin koma wurin hajja,ai magani na kenan tace kada in biyo ka naki ji ashe ci mun fuska za kayi.....,gaban ta yasha da gudu tare da riƙe kunnen shi yana faɗin affuwan na tuba kece amarya,uwargidan INSHA ALLAH ,idan har Kinga na Kara aure mu ɗauka yana cikin ƙaddara ta amma ba don ina so ba...,tsale tayi tare da rungume shi,"INSHA ALLAH ƙarin aure baya Daga cikin ƙaddara ka, kishiya bata daga cikin ƙaddara ta.!

Uhmm mata kenan,mu tafi kig d'akin ki dana faruk ya riko hanun ta...,kinga filin dake bayan nayi magana da Umar za'a fara ginawa saboda baƙi haka kinga nan zai mana kaɗan.!

Haka yayi kyau Allah ya sanya albarka ya ƙara rufa asiri Amin...., d'akin faruk suka fara shiga wanda aka saka gadon yara madaidaita guda biyu sai wardrobe da kayan wasa na yara yayi kyau sosai suka wuce d'akin ta wanda sai da ta zubar da hawayen farin ciki best colour nata aka saka a d'akin lemon green da orange "shiiiii bana son kuka addu'a kawai nake bukata a gare ku sai soyayya da kulawa,sadeeyah kin mun komi a duniya wallahi bana da damuwa zama na dake alheri ne sosai.

Dakin shi suka koma nan suka yi wanka suka yi shirin bacci wasa ta canja salo....


    Sun isa Sokoto cikin lokaci sai dai basu samu ganin likita a wanan rana ba saboda yana da appointment da dama kawai alaji habu ya kama masu hotel domin ba tare da hajia mariya suka zo ba,Nan aka buɗe sabon babin soyayya.

Da safe ya masu order na breakfast suka yi domin su fita akan lokaci.

Zaune suke a office d'in doctor Raji Yana mata yan tambayoyi tana bada amsa,daya gama yaci ta hau gado zai examining nata..., scanning ya fara yi wanda ya jima yana maimaita domin tabbatar da abinda ya gani fuskar shi ɗauke da murmushi.

Bell ya danna wata nurse ta shigo ya umurce ta data dibi jinin ammie ta nufi lab dashi ayi urgent pregnancy test,tayi abinda ya umurta a hanzarce.


Wurin alaji habu ya dawo yana faɗin, I think she's pregnant....,are u kidding me.?
Am not alhaji,kasan idan yana early stage Baya detecting through ultrasound,dole sai mun yi urine ko blood test,domin scanning da nayi wound d'in dake a wurin mahaifar nata yayi healing a dalilin magunguna da na baku last time,yanzu ba sai anyi aikin ba kawai wani taimako zan mata wanda zai supporting cikin har ya kai lokacin haihuwa ba tare da an sake samun matsala irin lokacin baya ba.

IKON Allah kenan,sannu da kokari doctor...,shigowar nurse ne na dakatar dasu.

Koda ya duba result ɗin it was positive.

 Congratulations sir your wife is pregnant...,Allahu Akbar shine kalmar data fara fitowa daga bakin alaji habu ya miƙa wa doctor hannu cike da tsantsar farin ciki kamar yau aka fara samun ciki a gidan shi.

Ammie ma dake kwance cikin wani daki tana jin bayanin su hawaye ne kawai ya fara ambaliya a fuskarta,tabbas ta yadda hakin baby KE bibiyanta shine dalilin rashin zaman cikin data samu a baya,yanzu data yafe mata komi ya fara daidaita.!

DOCTOR yace da alamu cikin bai wuce 2weeks ba,"that means Koda muka zo hospital the other time tana da shigar ciki .?,shine tambaya da alaji HABU ya jefa wa likita.!
Yes sir,ban yi figuring out ba saboda ba Ni  nayi scanning ba and one thing with early pregnancy idan ba kasan aikin ka sosai ba ba zaka gane akwai shi ba through scanning sai blood test.

  Kamar yadda na faɗa a farko uterus nata is very weak dole sai  an yi supporting cikin yadda zai zauna da kyau har ta haife shi,"ya ɗauko wani abu kamar kwai round Wanda har kana hango ruwan dake cikin shi,wanan shine zan saka mata yanzu.
wata nurse ta sake shigowa yace tayiwa ammie alura yana zuwa...,bayan anyi Alurai na kashe zafi ya kwaso karafuna ,nurse d'in ta ɗaga kafafun ammie ta daura saman wasu karfe nan ya ɗauki abun da wani ƙarfe ya tura cikin gabanta sai daya tabbatar ya shiga ya zauna,ya daura hanun shi saman marar ta yana feeling abun sanan ya cire Safar hanun,yace ta sauko ta fito.

Nan take ya rungume ta gaban likita yana hamdallah,murmushi take ga hawaye sun ki tsayawa a idanun ta....,likita yace alhamdulillah alaji da izinin Allah an dace tunda har ciki ya shiga,komi lokaci ne dama idan akayi hakuri sai Aci riba,za taci gaba da tayi hutu na sati biyu har abinda muka saka ya zauna da kyau sanan taci gaba da aikace aikacen ta,alaji kana iya ci gaba da mu'amala aure da matar ka babu wata matsala hakan zai taimaka ma aikin mu sosai.


Godiya yayi wa likita,aka basu wani takarda, ya tafi ya biya kuɗi ya dawo suka yi salama.

 Tun a hanya na komawa Hotel din  ya shiga kiraye kirayen waya  yana shaidawa mutane labari mai daɗi,umma hada kukan daɗi da ta  samu labari wurin ya hydar,su hajia mariya da sauran yara ma sai murna suke at longest last zasu samu little brother/sister.

 Abinci ya masu order Wanda yayi feeding nata cikin tsantsar so da kauna...,ammie abun nema ya samu sai langwabe wa take tana zuba shagwaba...!,kuka ta fashe dashi nan take hankalin alaji habu ya tashi rarashin duniya taki shiru.

Abinda ta tuna ya saka ta kuka shine lokacin da aka sanar dasu tana da cikin baby ITA da yaya mallam yadda ta wahala da cikin babu wani kulawa ko gata irin wanda ta samu yanzu, ƙara godewa Allah tayi daya haɗa ta da jarumin namiji wanda ke sonta saboda Allah kuma yana hakuri da ita a ko wane yanayi...,tabbas alaji habu ya zama haske a rayuwar ta domin shine silar fitar ta daga wanan mugun rayuwa.


Zuwa dare yace ta shirya su nufi gidan gwaggo domin gobe yake son su koma gida akwai tafiya a gaban shi.

A bakin gate ya Parker motar daya amsa wurin friend d'in shi suka salama rabe mai gadi ya gode tare da gaishe su,"already alaji habu yayi waya da Mamman driver akan zasu shigo tun suna gida...,ciki suka shiga lokacin gwaggo na palour tare da mamman ɗin aka gaisa tare da yiwa ammie gaisuwa rasuwar yaya mallam.

Hadiza tace wallahi yau ƙafa ta ƙafar ki babu inda zaki domin ban yadda da lesson d'in nan Na ƙarya da kike tsayawa.

Baby tace shike nan Meenah wuce abubuwan ki na fasa zuwa gidan ku and ba zani gida yanzu ba sai ki bi idan kin cika....

Rai a b'ace ta kama hanya tana tafiya Hadiza ta soma Binta a baya....!

For everything ,kada ki sake min haka  U know I need u more than anything in my life.

Tana fita sai da tai tafiya mai ɗan nisa ta samu napep ta hau na ta ciro Humran ta,ta shafa a jikinta mai napep dai na aikin shi bai san tana yi ba.

Sai dai me yana jin kamshin turaren nan ya rikice kamar wanda ya haukace tun a kofar apartment d'in ya fara romancing nata har suka shiga ciki inda ya fara shafa jikinta,yana tsosan boobs nata ,yana saka yatsa a ƙasan ta hankalin shi ya ƙara tashi ganin yadda take tsiyaya bai bata lokaci ba ya shigeta sambatu babu irin wanda bai yi ba a ranar ita kuwa ta murzu domin bai saurara mata ba tun 9am ake abu ɗaya sai ya huta yaci gaba har 12pm Baby ta galabaita  tun tana jin daɗi har ta fara gajiya da wahala ta saka mai kuka dole ya hakura ba don ya so ba.

Rayuwa yaci gaba da tafiya baby duk wani hanya da zata bi ta gana da uncle Christ tana bi cikin ikon Allah babu wanda ya gano ta har lokacin exams din su yazo...,shi da kanshi ya bata shawara ta mayar da hankali wurin karatu domin ta samu sakamako mai kyau suyi aure.

Jin haka dole ta rage zuwa wurin shi tana samu lokacin karatu.

Sun fara zana jarabawar waec ɗin cikin Sa'a wanda suna karatu sosai da mayar da hankali,ammie ta masu alkawarin wayoyin Android idan sun Kamala jarabawar sanan zata haɗa masu walima.

Suna cikin hutun ne aka baby tayi waya da aunty sadeeyah Amma ba ita ta daga ba aka sanar da ita suna hospital jiya da daren ta haihu baby boy ta samu.

Cike da farin ciki baby tace a gaishe ta,ta sanar da ammie itama tayi farin ciki da haka amma tasan da wuya alaji habu ya bari ta tafi saboda cikinta watanni shida yana batun shiga na bakwai sai fama take da nauyin ciki yayi girma sosai bata ko iya tsayuwa kan kafafunta,sai yawan jiri da kumburi take fama dashi a yanzu.

Last time da taje wurin awo likita ya tabbatar mata da ta jininta ya hau sosai saboda ywan damuwa da ta sama kanta shine ya kawo mata kumburin nan,idan har bata rage damuwa ba zata iya rasa babynta ko a rasa ta wurin haihu,yanzu haka an hanata cin farin Maggi da gishiri a abinci.

Yanke shawara akayi baby da hadiza su bi flight zuwa yola wurin bukin haihu saboda suna hutu.

Farin ciki wurin baby ba'a magana babban matsalar ta bata son yin nisa da uncle Christ gashi ya kusa tafiya,amma idan ta tuna zai dawo gareta suyi aure bayan ya gana da iyayen shi ya musulunta sai taji daɗi.

 Ana saura kwana ɗaya buki suna ta sanar da UNCLE Christ Wanda Shima give zai wuce amma bai sanar da ita ba,ya nuna mata zai jira ta dawo suyi ban kwana kafin ya sake dawo wa.

Daɗi taji sosai suka yi sallama.washe gari da safe suka  bi jirgi zuwa yola.

Already General Sadiq ya iso shi da kanshi ya tafi airport dauko su,baby sai zumudi taga baby, Shima yayi farin cikin ganinta cikin natsuwa da kwanciyar hankali ga kyau na musamman da ta ƙara sai shek'i take yi.


Suna isa gidan tun a bakin gate ta tabbatar ba ƙaramin kuɗi iyayen General Sadiq suke dashi ba,aka fara kai su part d'in hajja suka gaisa aka cika masu gaba da kayan ciye ciye...,ita dai hankalin ta na wurin aunty sadeeyah yace lalai su sun gama ci zai wuce dasu can gidan.

General Aunty sadeeyah ta Kira a waya,yace Hadiza ta kamata gashi nan iso wa gidan su kai ta Hospital.

   Bayan kamar minti talatin nurse ta shiga office na doctor da wani envelope daga lab Wanda ke ɗauke da sakamakon gwajin da akayi wa Baby.

Are u sure is her result?

Murmushi yayi na irin kaji wanan zai koya mun aiki na,Yes sir.

No plz check it again.

I have done that sir,ok lemme have it.!

Baby is pregnant,How,when,why and where?

Hi Mr general,are u alright.?

Nodding kanshi kawai yayi.

Ok Dama ba wani abu ya kawo mata high fever ba sai early sign of pregnancy Wanda idan ya kai wani lokaci zai tsaya. Hadiza taimaka mata ta samu tana ina zuwa.

Hadiza dai na Binta da kallo har ta kammala,"I need more,shine abunda tace zufa na keto mata ko ta ina duk da AC dake aiki.

Yanzu dai ki tashi ki watsa ruwa kinga ana kiran sallar isha'i gashi ko magrib baki yi ba.

Kamar za tayi kuka ta tashi jiki ba kwari domin wani irin mugun kwadayin tea ba sugar ya taso mata da daren nan.


Aunty sadeeyah na fitowa daga d'akin da Baby take ta nufi hanyar d'akin mijinta da sallama ta tura d'akin sai dai abun mamaki sallah yake yi wanda zata ce tunda ta aure shi idan ba matsananciyar rashin lafiya ba jam'i bata wuce shi.

D'akin ta kawai ta nufa domin ba Aminu Junior nono har ya kamala sallah.

Baby na fitowa wanka ta saka doguwar riga mara nauyi wanda ya amshi jikinta boobs Bata suka fito da kyau sai hijab har ƙasa ta kabarta sallah wanda da kyar ta kai raka'a ɗaya a tsaye sauran a zaune tayi. Tana idar wa bacci ya kwashe ta a wurin.

Hadiza dai sai mamakin ikon Allah take abubuwan baby kamar na masu ciki,da sauri ta kawar da wanan tunani tare da yin istigfari.


A d'akin mai jego kuwa hira ne ya kaure tsakanin ta da yan uwa da abokan arziki can taji wayarta na ƙara koda ta duba text ne daga mijinta kamar haka:-
*_Ki amso wayoyin baby da hadiza ki zo daki na dasu yanzu_*

Gaban ta ne ya fadi,zuciyarta na nasa mata abubuwa da dama ta daure ta fita.

Koda ta shiga Hadiza na zaune saman bed suna taɓa hira da sister nata amirah,Baby na kwance saman sallaya tana kwasan bacci duk tayi zuba..,ammm hadiza ku bani Aron phone naku na ɗan lokaci.!

Tau aunty Dama yanzu nake shirin kiran momi ko kin sanar da ita rashin lafiyar baby ne.?

Yanzu dai zan sanar da ita saboda haka ba sai kin kira ba...,dariyar amirah ya katse aunty sadeeyah, KE kuma lafiyar ki.?

Kai aunty wallahi naji kunya ace yan mata kaman su ƙarama waya suke riƙe wa shine muke hira ashe ko ɗan chat ɗin nan da ake yayi basa yi.

Itama aunty sadeeyah tayi mamaki ganin tun wayar da mijinta ya ba baby shekaru hudu kenan har yanzu dashi take amfani.

Hadiza kun ci abinci ko.?,eh naci tare da amirah Baby CE dai taki tashi tana bacci tunda ta idar da sallah..,ok ki tashe ta tahau gado mana ta fita abunta.

Da sallama ta shiga d'akin sai dai waya yake amsa wa taji yana faɗin,evening flight nake so zuwa Kano;around what time Kenan a goben.?,ok 5pm..,yes 3passengers thank u so much air vice Marshal Eric.

Wurgi da wayar yayi saman bed ya mayar da kanshi saman guiwar shi kamar yadda yake a zaune tunda ya idar da sallah.

Zama taya a ƙasa kusa dashi tare da talafo kanshi ta d'aura saman chest nata tana shafawa a hankali kanshi zuwa wuyar shi har fuskar shi.

Lumshe ido yayi yana shaƙar kamshin ta wani irin natsuwa na shigar shi,he believed a duniya ba zai samu mace  wacce tasan damuwar shi da kuma maganin damuwar  shi irin sadeeyah ba.

Can ƙasar mak'oshinta take magana,dukan tsanani yana tare da sauki haka komi yayi tsanani maganin shi Allah kamar yadda mai hakuri yana tare da Allah,ka sanar dani damuwar ka insha Allah ina tare da kai komin runtsi komin wuya da daɗi babu daɗi.

Shiru tanna kusan  minti biyu, ya ɗago kanshi tare da tsura mata ido na ɗan lokaci,"lemme have their phone, Mika mashi tayi duka biyu nan take ya gano na baby Wanda ya bata.

Contact ya fara shiga yaga basu wuce shida ba"best uncle(number shi),best aunty(na sadeeyah),sweet sis (na hadiza),Daddy (na alaji habu),ammie(na momi),lastly sarah dear (2nd daughter na alaji habu). Text massage ya shiga babu ko ɗaya,dial call ma empty, received call Empty,miss call Empty.

Tunani ya fara yi domin dai babu number wani saurayi a cikin wayar,"ba dai alaji habu ya yiwa Baby cikin ba.?
Astagfirullah ya furta a bayane,ya shiga duba na Hadiza wace itama kusan haka ne babu number wani saurayi cikin wayar.

Kallon matar shi yayi sosai yana nazarin ta kamar yadda itama take nazarin shi,wani zuciya na bashi ya sanar da ita wani zuciya na kwaban shi domin yasan halin ta bata iya shiga damuwa ba.

Mikewa yayi batare da bode chest of drawer ya ciro pen da memo ya fara rubutu wanda bai jima ba ya gama.

Inaso kiyi dialing number ammie duk abinda na rubuta a cikin paper Nan shi zaki tambaye ta.

Amsa  tayi ganin rubutun kamar zuciyarta ya fashe don tsoro...., ringing wayar ya fara yi wanda ta saka a handsfree zuwa can ammie ta amsa da sallama,aunty sadeeyah ta kafe paper da ido.

Ya kewar yan matan ki.?

Daga ɗaya bangaren ta amsa da akwai kewa kam musamman da dare haka muke zama a palour muna kallo,baby cema Bata damu da kallon ba tana d'aki.

Lallai kam domin har tayi Bacci ma,naga yan mata sun girma da an gama Neco sai batun aure ko.?

Matso wa yayi sosai domin yaji amsar da ammie zata bayar...,dariya mai sauti tayi auntyn su kenan a can Yola suka samu mijin.?

Yola kuma yaran da suka zo jiya jiya.!,,a tau nan dai basu da kowa domin daga schl sai islamiyya basu fita ko ina musamman baby Koda na shirya muje shopping bata zuwa ita dai kullum zaune d'aki kinga ko ba maganar aure.

Ikon Allah ai banga laifin ta ba domin ko da take Lagos daga schol sai islamiyya amma dai gwara su samo musha buki zaman mace haka bai yi musamman a wanan lokaci da maza basu da tabbas.

Allah dai yasa mu dace bara na barki ki kwanta nasan oga na gida,dariya ta saka suka kashe.

Baby Bata fita zuwa ko ina daga schl sai islamiyya a ina ta samu cikin?

Tabbas idan ba cikin gidan nan aka mata ciki ba either a schol ko islamiyya akwai wanda ya rude ta.kaddara Kenan Wanda ya riga fata.

Kallon matar shi yayi da kyau,sadeeyah.!

Ta amsa da na'am.

 Muna da shika shikan musulunci guda biyar wanda akwai akwai yadda da ƙaddara mai kyau da mara kyau ciki,a duniya ko wane bawa yana da irin tashi jarabawar ta tsiga dabam dabam domin Allah ya gwada imanin mu.

Jikinta yayi sanyi matuƙa ta kagu taji musababbin wanan dogon huduba.

Sadeeyah rashin lafiya da baby KE yi mun tafi hospital d'azu likita ya tabbatar mana tana ɗauke da juna biyu...

Dafe kirjin ta tayi da hanuwan ta biyu Tare Da zaro idanu bakin ta ɗauke da sallati.!

Juna biyu da abban faruk.?

Kwarai kuwa baby na ɗauke da ciki wanda yanzu nake so mu haɗa Karfi da ƙarfe mu gano wanda ya mata cikin nan.

Babban magana Ni sadeeyah garin yaya Baby ta samu ciki tunda ba fita take ya ba.?

Shine abunda na ke so mu gano a yanzu don haka wani boye boye,kuka ko canza mata fuska ba shi zai solving MATSALAR ba,ina so ki jata a jiki daga daren nan zuwa gobe ta sanar da ke komi idan Allah ya kai mu gobe kano zani tare da su ayi komi ta ƙare.

Shike nan zanyi iyakacin kokari na abban faruk daga goben saboda yanzu tayi bacci.

Shiru suka yi duka kowa da abunda yake tunani cikin zuciyar shi har wani lokaci suka yi sallama ta koma Dakin ta.

Bayan sun idar da sallar asuba General Sadiq ya bar gidan zuwa gidan iyayen shi domin a nan za'a yi zanen suna da karfe 8am.

Dajin da su baby suka kwana ta shiga dama Hadiza da amirah sun fito har sun gaisa da ita suka nufi cikin gida wurin aiki,baby CE ke sharar Bacci...,bubugata aunty sadeeyah tayi ta buɗe ido,"tashi haka nan rana ta fito.

Kai aunty wallahi bacci nake yi..,ke bana son Shashanci ki tashi magana za mu yi.Zaube ta tashi bakin ta sauke da sallati sai a lokacin ta lura da canjawan baby sosai irin na masu shigar ciki.

Oya taso mu je,tana biye da ita har d'akin General Sadiq suka shiga ta zauna baby na zaune ƙasa amma bacci bai sake ta ba.

Shiga toilet ki wanke fuskar ki Yanzu...,babu musu ta shiga ta fito riƙe da ciki aunty wallahi yunwa nake ji sosai kuma tea ba sugar nake son sha.

To hell with you and your tea,sit down my friend.

Ganin yada aunty sadeeyah ta buga mata tsawa ya saka ta zauna a razane.

Buɗe idon ki da kyau da kunuwan ki magana zan yi dake na fahimta na rantse da Allah i baki sanar dani gaskiya ba sai na shak'e ki a d'akin nan.

Baby kina ɗauke da ciki na wata ɗaya, wanene ya maki CIKI?

Zaro idanu tayi waje,ciki kuma Aunty wallahi bani da komi.!
Ok shi doctor ne yayi ƙarya ko mijina ne makaryaci.?

Zan so ki kwantar da hankalin ki baby ki faɗa mun gaskiya tun lokaci bai kure ba.

Shiru tayi domin a lokacin ta dawo hayacin ta komi ya dawo mata dala dala tun daga ranar da suka fara magana da uncle Christ har wayarta su ta ƙarshe.

Cikin kuka ta Fara faɗin Papa ka cuce ni, Allah ya isa tsaka nina da kai azallumin mugu Kawai,shike nan bakin ka ya kama ni dama kayi alkawari sai nayi cikin shege,sai na haifi shege na raine shi..,nima na maka alƙawarin zan raini cikin nan har na haife shi domin cikon burin ka.

Keee na kawo ki nan ne don yafi sirri!
Ta faɗa cikin tsawa ba abunda na tambaye ki kenan ba.
 WANENE Ya Maki CIKI BABY.?
UNCLE CHRIST ta FAƊI cikin ihu.

Mun shiga uku wanne kuma UNCLE Christ.?

Cikin kuka ta kwashe komi ta sanar da Aunty sadeeyah,rungume ta tayi tana rarashin ta.!

Kayya baby kin yi wauta sosai wallahi namiji ba abun yadda bane baby kazantar tayi yawa Christian mushrikai wanda ya haɗa Allah da wani,kayyah mai afkuwa ta afku ki daina ganin laifin marigayi Aminu duk da an san bakin iyaye kamar dafi take Amma kuma haɗe da ƙaddara.

Kwantar mata da hankali sadeeyah taci gaba da yi har ta koma bacci a jikinta, gyara mata kwanciya tayi cike da matukar tausayin ta sanan ta bar d'akin.

A ranar ko Hadiza bata samu ganin baby ba domin bacci ta yini yi sai da aka kira sallah lokacin gida ya cika da yan suna  aunty sadeeyah ta tashe ta wanka ta fara yi ta sha tea Wanda ya zame mata ruwan sha a yanzu wanda idan ba shi tasha ba komi taci sai amai.

Bayan koma ya lafa yan suna sun fara rage wa around 4pm Aunty sadeeyah ta keme da mijinta nan take ta sanar dashi komi wanda ranshi ya ɓaci matuƙa amma ya suka iya da ƙaddara ga bakin yaya Mallam.

Yace ta sanar dasu su shirya 5pm zasu isa airport.

Ciki ta koma ta haɗa masu kayan buki ta miƙa wa Hadiza tare da sanar da ita sak'on General...,hadiza bata ji dad'i ba amma ta dangana hakan da rashin lafiyar baby,gashi ba'a basu wayar su ba bare ta kira ammie.

4:30pm suka yi sallama da kowa suka bar gidan zuwa airport wanda Baby KE faman kuka rarashin Duniya Aunty sadeeyah tayi amma bata yi shiru ba.


Already tun a jiyan yayi waya da alaji habu akan yana so su hadu yadda ya hydar a gidan nan kano yana nan tafe.


5:40pm a palour'n gidan alhaji Abubakar ya masu,alaji habu,ya hydar da General Sadiq suna zaune saman three seater,ammie na gefe sanye da hijab,baby na zaune ƙasa taci kuka har ta gaji.

General Sadiq ya fara da sallama sanan yaci gaba,dukan ku nan iyaye ne a wurin baby Wanda Koda ran mahaifinta ko babu i believe zaku riƙe ta to the best of your ability,ba sai na mayar da hanun agogo baya ba cikin ku babu wanda bai san ta hanyar da Baby tazo duniya ba Wanda Yana ɗaya daga cikin ƙaddara na iyayen ta.Bazan boye maku ba zaman baby da Yaya Mallam babu sauki ko kaɗan abubuwa da dama sun faru sai dai yanzu babu wani fa'idar dawo da abinda ya wuce tunda ya rasu fatan Allah ya jikan shi Amin.


Ammie zan fara dake misali an baki amanar mutum ya zauna ƙarƙashin ki,cin shi da shan shi haka kuma da tarbiyar shi kina matukar kokarin ki sai wani ƙaddara na rayuwa ta afka mashi ya za kiji.?

Ba zan ji dad'i ba domin amana ne aka bani zan ga kamar na ci amana ko ban kula da amanar kamar yadda ya kamata ba.

Alhaji Abubakar gare ka,na saka yarinya ta a makaranta wace ba musulma b'ace rana tsaka a matsayin ka na mallakin makaranta musulmi ka mata wayau ka lalata mata tarbiya ba tare da saba'in iyayen ta ba shin kayi mata adalci kenan.?

Wa'iyazubillah mai zai haɗa musulmi soyayya da Christian kuma har ya kai ga wani alaƙa.!, Allah sawake.
Nagode sosai da fahimtar ku.
Baban abunda ya tara mu a nan shine ƙaddara rayuwa dai ta afka wa baby dukan wanan tambaya da na maku a kanta ya faru Allah ya haɗa ta da wani ɗan suka Christian ya yaudare ta yanzu haka tana ɗauke da ciki (watau tarihi ne ta maimaita kanta).

Kalmar nan na karshe ya fara yawo cikin kwakwalwar ammie"yanzu haka tana ɗauke da ciki (tarihi ya maimaita kanshi).

Wani irin ihu ta kurma tare da shako wuyar baby da ƙarfin gaske"baby baki mun adalci ba tarbiyyar dana baki kenan.?,menene kika nema kika rasa a wuri na ashe daman lesson d'in ƙarya da kike tsayawa kenan.!

Alaji habu da ya hydar suman zaune suka yi suna juya maganar cikin ranau domin wanan abu yayi muni da yawa.

General Sadiq ya mike Yana faɗin don girman Allah hajia Hadiza kiyi hakuri wani zagi,cin mutunci da tonon aisir bashi zai kawo mafita ba a yanzu.

Tari ne yaci ƙarfin ta hanunta na riƙe da wuyar baby Nan ta FAƊI ƙasa sumamiya.

Hadiza dake labe tana jin komi ta fado palour'n da gudu tana kuka tare da rufe baby da duka tana faɗin yar iska ashe daman abinda kika kula kenan tare da uncle Christ shi yasa kike zuwa staff room wurin shi bakya so kowa ya sani.?,lesson d'in ƙarya da kike tsayawa kenan.?,na rantse da Allah idan wani abu ya faru da momi ba zan kyale ki ba.

Wanke ta da mari ya hydar yayi,uban wa ya saka bakin ki a maganar manya.?,wallahi zaman ki ya ƙare a kano kema bokon banza da wofi mu gidan mu babu abunda bako ya haifar mana sai abun kunya da nadama.

Alaji habu ya zuba wa AMMIE ruwa taja wani wahaltacen nunfashi wanda ke fita da kyar ai nan take ya sungume ta zuwa hospital tare da su ya hydar baby Kawai aka bari a palour'n tana kuka da shure Shure kamar wacce farfadiya ta kama.

Suka isa aka shigar da ita emergency unit ana ƙoƙarin ceto rayuwar ta amma ina sun kasa dole aka kira doctor on call, Allah ya saka shine doctor nasu wanda ake kula dasu.

Examine nata ya fara yi sosai da sosai amma ina ba haske ya rasa gane komi  saboda jinita  ya hau  sosai  ,ga bleeding tana Yi SOSAI ba gagautawa, numfashi ya ki daidaita haka bugun zuciyarta shawara ya yanke after magrib  za'a mata aiki saboda ceto rayuwar ta domin bai saka ran babyn da take ɗauke da shi yana lafiya ba saboda bai kwanta dai dai ba ga zubar jini.

Koda ya fito yace dasu jikin da sauki su shiga Masallaci,haka dai suka yi Sallah hankali ba kwance ba suna fito wa ya kira su OFFICE nashi domin an riga an shirya ta shi ake jira.

Bayani ya masu halin da take ciki yana so alaji habu ya saka hannu ayi mata aiki domin ceto rayuwar ta.

Kuka kawai ya fara yi domin daga babyn har matar shi yana so duka ba zai mancewa wahalar da suka shiga kafin Allah ya bata cikin nan ba.

Rarashin su kawai General keyi domin ya hydar ma ya fara zub da hawaye na tausayin yar uwar shi , tausayin umman su idan ta samo labari ita da bata da isasshen lafiya.

Karfe 7:30pm aka shiga da ita theater likitoci uku ke saman kanta aka fara aiki cikin sauki suka ciwo pre matured baby girl aka  saka ta cikin infant rediant warmer domin da alama tana da rai sai dai tayi ƙarama sosai, she's weighing 2kg.

Aka dukufa akan ammie bayan an dunke wurin da aka ciru babyn an gyara ta sai dai har Lokacin numfashin ta baya fita dai dai.

Zuwa wani lokaci numfashin ya fara fita da sauri sauri tana fizge fizge can aka ji shiru alamar rai yayi halinsa.

Innalillahi wa'inna ilaihi raji'un ammie lokaci yayi sai fatan Allah ya jikan ta da Rahama ya kyauta makwancin ta, Allah ya gafarta mata kura kurenta amin😭

~🅿65-66~




    Alamar buɗe glass door ya saka hankalin su ya dawo wurin theater room d'in,sai ga doctor  ya fito fuska ba yabo ba fallasa domin ba haka yaso ba kwarai yaso samun nasara akan aikin ammie.

Wurin shi suka nufi General Sadiq yayi ƙarfin halin tambayar mai jiki.?

Ajiyar zuciya ya sauke tare da faɗin bismillah mu tafi office d'in.....

Wuri kowa ya samu ya zauna jikin su a sanyaye ya shiga nazarin fuskokin su daga bisani ya fara,"Godiya ta tabbata ga Allah subhanahu wata'ala da ya halicce mu ta jinsi dabam dabam,ya bamu rai da lafiya a lokacin da wasu ke neman lafiya wasu kuma ke kwance a gidan su na gaskiya,alhamdulillah mun yi aiki Allah ya azurta ka da samun 'ya mace yar wattani bakwai saboda haka bata da quzari dole zamu aje ta cikin kwalba ana kula da ita a nan har tsawon watani biyu.Alhaji sai dai muyi hakuri Hajiya Hadiza lokaci yayi muna mata kyakkyawar zato domin tayi mutuwar shahada sai addu'a Allah ya sa aljanna ce makomar ta Allah kuma ya raya abinda ta bari ya bamu hakuri rashin ta Amin.

Innalillahi wa'inna ilaihi raji'un shike kalmar da suke maimaita wa a lokaci ɗaya hawaye na zuba a idanun ko wane daga cikin su domin mutuwar ammie ya zo masu a bazata,lafiya klau suka yini da ita a yau ɗin da mijinta ko irin yawan nauyin cikin da take complain yau bata yi ba da kanta ta gyara gida,tayi girki ta biyawa mijinta bukatar aure sanan suka tsunduma hiran duniya har ta zaɓi suna da take so idan mace ta haifa"NAZIYA,idan namiji ne"NAZIR,cikin wasa yake ce mata insha Allah yan biyu zata haifa saboda girma da nauyin cikin yayi yawa ashe hiran bankwana suka yi.

Kuka mai sauti ya fara yi a hankali lips din shu ke motsawa yana furta"AMMIE.

Ya hydar ma nisa yayi cikin duniyar tunani domin ya tuna hiran su na karshe a Hospital inda take Sanar dashi damuwarta akan baby da irin zaman da suke yi da ita,ya kwantar mata da hankali tare da bata shawarwari Yadda zata ja baby a jiki ta nuna mata soyayya ta hanyar daura ta a kan turba na gaskiya,idan kuma ba zata iya tsawata mata ba Ni zai dauki baby ta dawo wurin su har sakamakon jarabawar su ya fito.

A lokacin murmushi tayi tace,ya hydar a duniya bayan umma babu wani ɗan adam da nike so da kauna da burin kyautata wa kamar baby ba zan so a takura mata ba domin daga Ni sai ita muka san matsatsin rayuwa da muka fuskanta a baya shi yasa ko tana wasu abubuwa bana ganin laifin ta,amma nayi maka alkawari ko bayan rai na mallaka maka baby halak malak ka Kuala mun da ita kamar yadda zaka kuma da Hadiza,inaso ta samu ingantaccen ilimi da muka rasa har Allah ya kawo mata miji wanda zai iya zama da ita a halin da take ciki; ya hydar ka mun alkawari duk wanda yazo neman auren baby aka sanar da shi gaskiya akan abinda ya faru da iyayen ta domin ta haka ne kawai zamu gane masoyin ta na gaskiya....

Allahu Akbar ya furta" shike nan  yar UWATA kin tafi kin barni tun muna mu uku na koma Ni ɗaya,Ni Aliyu da wani baki san sanar da umma wanan mumunar labari.?,umma ba lafiya ba zata jure wanan baƙin ciki ba,zuciyarta ba zai dauka ba!

Likita yace ya kamata a samu mota ku tafi da ita gida, General ya nemi alfarma a Basu ambulance likita yace ba Matsala zai ma driver magana.

Alaji habu da ya hydar suka koma kamar mata saboda kuka da sambatu har aka shigar da ita cikin ambulance din, General Sadiq ne mai ƙarfin halin driving motar da suka zo da ita zuwa gida.

Mai gadi ya wangale gate aka shigo da mota suka buɗe aka talaffo gawan ta zuwa ciki wanda baby na zaune inda suka barta nan tayi sallah,taci kuka sosai tare da adu'oi Allah ya ba ammie'n ta lafiya tayi alƙawarin ba zata sake Sab'a mata ba zasu zauna lafiya,zata yi mata biyayya kamar yadda ko wace 'ya cewa iyayen ta.

Gani tayi an shigo da mutum rufe  General na gaba an nufi sama da ita,alaji habu da ya hydar na biye ko wanne na kuka....,zaran idanu ta fara yi tana faɗin ammie.!,ammie.!!,ina ammie na.!!!?

Riko hanuwan ta ya hydar yayi yana girgiza mata kai a hankali ya furta" AMMIE lokaci yayi.

NOOOOOOOO.!

Na shiga uku na lalace asirina ya gama tonuwa a duniya na rasa gata na shike nan haihuwa na babu abinda ya haifar sai tashin hankali da sanadin rayuka...,ta zube saman tyles tana buga kanta da curshian.

Jin hayaniyar su ne ya fito da Hadiza wace tunda suka tafi take zaune bisa sallaya tana adu'oi da lazumi,sai ganin babanta tayi tsaye yana sharan hawaye tare da kwantar da Baby da hankali.

Da baya ta ke ja tana sallati wani Karfi ya zo mata ta nufi baby a guje ta shak'o ta...,ai na faɗa maki duk abinda ya samu momi wallahi ba zan kyale ki ba mara imani da tausayi Allah ya wadaran haihuwar ki domin babu abinda ya janyo wa zuri'ar mu sai tashin hankali da ɓaci suna.

Bana son Shashanci hadiza ki bari muji da ɗaya mana koda baby ko babu baby idan lokacin ta yayi akwai wanda ya isa ya hana ta mutuwa ne.?, addu'ar mu kawai take nema a halin yanzu mu godewa Allah likita ya tabbatar mana tayi mutuwar shahada sanan ta samu diyar ta mace tana hospital.

Kalaman ya hydar da suka ji ya sanya zuciyar Hadiza ya ɗan yi sanyi amma ba haka aka so ba yadda ammie taci buri akan abunda zata haifa taso Allah ya barta domin ta raini abunta.

  Saman gadon ta da aure aka kwantar da ita General ya fito tare da mutanen, Alaji habu zamewa yayi bakin gadon kamar ƙaramin yaro a hankali ya buɗe fuskarta wanda yake shek'i kamar wacce take bacci hankali kwance hancin ta an taushe da auduga fari haka kunen ta,sake zanin yayi cikin kuka yana faɗin shike nan mata ta ashe zaman mu ka'ida de ne Allah ba zai nufa ki raina abunda kika haifa ba wanda mun ci buri akan haka,kin jure wahalhalun rayuwa da dama,kin yi hakuri da nadama kin kyautata mun da duk wanda ke tare dake ashe zaki riga mu tafiya hadiza.!,tabbas nayi rashin mace ta gari,idan har kyautata wa miji na saka mace shiga aljanna bani da haufi akan ki Hadiza domin kin kyautata mun matuƙa Allah ya jikan ki ya bamu hakuri rashin ki.

Ya jima zaune a wurin ya fito palour suna zaune jigum.

_text ya tura ma uwar gidan shi yana sanar da ita mutuwar ammie kuma yana so ta kira kawun Shi dake nan cikin Kano da ba'baa mairo ta sanar dasu yasan zasu aikawa sauran yan uwan shi dake ƙauye_.

Shima General Sadiq text ya yiwa matar shi sanan ya kashe wayar gaba ɗaya.ya hydar ya kira nafisa yace gobe da safe yana so suyi shatar motar kasuwa ita da umma da yara su zo Kano ammie ta haihu...,cikin zumudi ta tambayi abinda aka haifa yace d'iya mace suka yi sallama.


Tun da asuba hajia mariya ta iso gidan tare da ba'baa mairo domin ta biya ta dauko ta,haka mutane keta shigowa yan uwa da abokan arziki domin ammie mace ce mai son mutane akwai jama'a.

Sun aje jana'iza karfe goma na safe saboda masu iso wa daga nisa suna kan zuwa.

Ƙarfe 9am a gidan ammie yayiwa su umma domin tunda aka sanar da ita haihuwa kuma akace ta zo hankalin ta bai kwanta ba shiru kawai tayi,abinda ya ƙara tayar mata da hankali ganin dandazon mutane a kofar gidan wanda babu tantama rasuwa ammie'n ta tayi ake boye mata.

Tana shiga palour'n kuwa ammie ce kwance an mata sutura dama su ake jira a fita da ita,nafisa ce ta fara kuka tana sallati umma kam wani irin karfin hali da dauriya yazo mata ta zaune gefe tare da yaye mayafin fuskar d'iyar ta ya bayyana ta shiga kwararo adu'oi ana shafawa Har ta kammala ta rufe.

General da ya hydar suka shigo suka ɗauke ta ai nan mata suka rud'e wuri da kuka baby hadda birgima a ƙasa wayyo ita Allah shike nan ta rasa ammie'n ta Hadiza ma kuka take amma bata ko bin takan babu domin yanzu haushi take bata.

Haka aka sallace gawan ammie aka nufi gidan ta na gaskiya da ita.

Umma kam dauriya take domin zuciyarta kamar zai fasa kirjin ta ya fito saboda zafi da rad'adi.

Can makabarta bayan an rufe ta Alaji habu ya rinƙa kuka yana surutai haka suka nufo gida dashi baya cikin hayacin shi sai sambatu,shima ya hydar kukan zuci kawai yake domin hawayen sun kafe sun ki zuwa.

Mutane daga sassa dabam dabam ke zuwa gaisuwa ana kawo abinci da abun sha sai dai babu mai kwanciyar hankalin cin wani abu har aka yi sallah lokacin aunty sadeeyah ta iso da elder sister nasu General Wanda daddy'n shi ya basu dama su tafi...,ganin su ya saka mutuwar ta dawo ma baby sabuwa ai kuwa ta faɗa jikin aunty sadeeyah tana kuka da surutai duk ta galabaita ga yunwa na cin ta,amai take ji amma ko tayi yunkuri baya zuwa saboda bata ci komi ba.

Lura da haka ya saka aunty sadeeyah ta ce da Hadiza ta haɗa mata tea Mara sugar ta cika flask yanzu,jiki a sanyaye ta ratse mutane zuwa kitchen ta kusa electric kettle ta joner har ya tafasa ta haɗa ta zuba a Flask ta nufo d'akin.

Baby kamar mayya cup biyu ta sha a take tayi bacci.

Zuwa yamma mutane na rage wa wasu na shigo wa zuwa da sallama wata mata ta shigo palour'n da hijab har ƙasa tayi masu gaisuwa tare da zama can tace ina baby ne.?

Aka nuna mata dakin da suke ta mike ta shiga tayi ma su aunty sadeeyah gaisuwa tana tambayar Baby.?

Baby dake kwance da babban hijab kanta na saman jikin aunty sadeeyah ta Kara rumtse ido domin ko a mafarki taji muryan ba zata taɓaantawa da shi ba saboda azabar da tasha a hanun su(uwar masu gida kenan)

A hankali aunty sadeeyah ta bubugata"tashi ana maki gaisuwa,ta tashi tare da haɗa ido da ita...,uwar masu gida wacce bata gane baby ba sai da taji an ambaci sunanta cikin zuciyarta tace lallai Allah mai girma shi yasan hikimar dake cikin haihuwar Baby.

Tace baby uwar masu gida ce a TV naji Sanar wa shine NAZO gaisuwa duk da dai ammie ta yada Ni a dalilin mugun shawara da na bata ta kashe mijinta bata sake waiwayen na ba,Dana zo gidan nan taci mutunci na tare da nasiha shine dalilin da yasa na tuba ga Allah na samu miji na yi aure yanzu ma tare muke yana wurin maza.

Shiru kawai baby tayi mata domin bata ga dalilin tonon asiri a gidan mutuwa ba,shike nan Allah bada lada mun gode ko.

Gajiya tayi da tsayuwar tace sai anjima ku.

Da dare bayan sallah Isha'i da kyar hajia mariya da ba'baa mairo suka yi dasu suka ci wani abun  haka sai babu dad'i.

Ranar addu'a kwana uku mama ladi taso aka tadda sabon kuka ranar ne kuma yaya ɗan Gusau ya zo daga Abuja gaisuwa rasuwa domin ya samu labari wurin General Wanda shi ya sanar da su gwaggo itama taso zuwa sai dai babu lafiyar kafafu,aunty ladi Kuma Bata Kasar.

Har ciki ya shiga yawa baby gaisuwa Wacce taji daɗin haka sosai,domin aysha diyar mu'azu wace ke karatu a Egypt sau biyu tana kiranta tana mata gaisuwa.

Lokacin da suka kebe da General Sadiq ne yake sanar dashi yana son baby Kuma auren ta take son yi.

Da mamaki sadiq ya kalle shi,Are u sure zaka iya yaya ɗan Gusau.?

Mai zai hana,tun ba yau ba nake son Baby lokacin da na gano iyayenta na raye nabi didigi babu abinda ban sani ba akanta tun daga haihuwarta har irin rayuwar da mahaifiyarta tayi duka na sani,so banda dalilin da zai hana Ni auren baby ba.

Amma Yaya...

Amma me?,ko menene ina sonta kuma zan aure ta.!

Shike nan nayi hakuri har hankali ya kwanta zan gabatar da bukatar ka wurin magabatan ta.

Suka yi sallama ya wuce masauki shi ya kwana.

Washe gari da safe yazo ya masu sallama tare da aje envelope Mai ɗauke da kuɗi a matsayin sadaka ya wuce.


Ranar da ammie ta cika kwana hudu da rasuwa suka nufi hospital domin ganin baby Wanda an riga an biya kudaden da Hospital ta nema domin kula da ita har na watanni biyu.

Tana cikin infant rediant warmer sai motsi take a hankali fara tasa da ita yar firit,komi na halittar jikinta ɗan Karami ne saboda haka baka iya tantace kamanin ta.

Sun jima tsaye wurin suna mamakin hikima irin na bature Kamar yada likita ya masu bayani wurin da suka sakata shine matsayin cikin mahaifiyar ta data fito lokaci bai ki ba kuma a nan zata samu duk abinda ya kamata ta samu kamar tana ciki ta na'urorin da ke haɗe da warmer d'in.

Ko wannen  su jiki yayi sanyi sai sharar hawaye suke musamman umma da bata samu yin kuka ba sai yanzu.!
Dama su General daga hospital za'a ake su airport zasu koma saboda aikin shi kwana uku kawai aka bashi gashi yana shirin yin sari ɗaya,nasiha sosai suka ma baby a airport kafun jirgin su ya tashi wanda General yaso ya sanar da umma baby nada jiki ganin ya hydar baya so ta sani yaja bakin shi yayi shiru. haka suka dawo gida jiki ba kwari.


Zama suka yi a palour'n gidan da umma,mama ladi,Mallam liman da ya hydar,alaji habu ya fito da wasu file da yasan da su wanda  ya kunshi abubuwan da ammie ta mallaka ne ciki akwai,gidan gona a garin su,babban shago a kasuwar sabon gari,motar hawa guda ɗaya,shaguna uku da aka bada haya da filaye manya biyu a kumbotso sai kuɗaɗen dake cikin account nata.

Kamar yadda ta bari a rubuce gidan gonan ta dake kumbotso ta mallaka wa Baby domin tasan bata da gadon ta,sauran abinda ta bari Mallam liman ya yi Kasafi aka cire wa umma nata a matsayin ta na mahaifiya,shima abokin haihuwarta aka cire nashi saura kuwa duka na abunda ta haifa ne.


Zaman da akayi alaji habu da kanshi cikin natsuwa ya masu bayanin cikin da baby keda shi wanda ƙaddara ce daga Allah ya jawo hankalin su baya so a nuna mata tsana ko tsangwama addu'a da fatan shiriya shi ya rage tsakanin su da Baby,bayan haka yana  so a bar Baby da Hadiza  gidan hajia mariya har su kamala jarabawar sai su koma kumbotso domin cika alkawarin ammie da ya hydar ya dauka har ya sauke baby Lafiya.

Kowa yayi na'am da shawarar domin a ranar su umma ZASU koma.

Akwatuna ammie aka sauke da duk gwala gwalai da take dasu akace umma ta zaɓi abinda take so,fafur taki dauka sai ma kuka da take yi wai hakkin abunda aka haifa ne su ake mata har ta girma,yace shike nan yayi alƙawarin zai ake komi na ammie har diyata ta girma.

Anyi koke koke da wani lokaci sanan su umma suka wuce zuciyarta cike da baƙin cikin wanan mumunar labari na Cikin baby babban abun tashin hankali kafiri ya mata ciki,oh ita kuma nata ƙaddara kenan.!



A ranar suka haɗa komi na ammie a d'aki ɗaya aka rufe su baby suka haɗa kayan su ya rufe gidan gaba daya bayan ya ɗauke duk abinda ya mallaka a ciki wanda zai amfani dashi suka bar gidan zuwa  lamid'o crescent.

D'akin da faiza ta zauna Kafin tayi aure aka gyara masu an zuba komi na mure rayuwa haka suka zauna kaman kurame ba mai magana da wani,Hadiza na jin zafin Baby,baby na son Bata hakuri amma ba fuska.

Hajia mariya taji daɗin zuwan su domin sarah na schol a katsina duk da tazo wurin gaisuwa during weekend, Usman ne kawai  a gidan.


Lokacin da za suyi bacci baby ta nemi gafarar Hadiza akan abubuwan da ta aikata mata a baya tana kuka sosai..,haka yasa Hadiza taji tausayinta sosai ta rungume ta suna kuka domin sun yi missing uwa ta gari (watau ammie).

Ta ɗago baby,me yasa kika boye mun alakar ki da uncle Christ.?

Ki gafarce Ni wallahi sharrin shaidan da son zuciya kuma soyayya ya rufe mun ido shi yasa.

Shike nan na fahimta dama can haka Allah ya rubutu zai faru bamu isa mu kauce wa wanan ƙaddara ba sai mu ci gaba da addu'a Allah ƙara kare wa ya rufa asiri Amin.

Yanzu shi uncle Christ ya San kina da ciki.?

A'a tunda muka je yola basu yi waya ba kuma da muka dawo ana cikin tashin hankali shi yasa.

Amma zan so ki kira shi muji ta bakin shi..,kuka taci gaba da yi Hadiza bana son bata maki Rai kuma amma naso rufe babin tarihin shi a rayuwata .

Na sani baby Kawai Kira muji...number ta saka tare da yin dialing

Can ya ɗaga da kakkausar murya yana faɗin baby.

Tace na'am idan ba zaka damu ba zan so muyi magana..,no ba sai kin bata bakin ki ba nasan kice na wuce ban sanar dake ba,maganar auren mu da sauran su,barin faɗa maki Babu sh yadda nasa Kafa nabar kano haka na saka ƙafa na shure soyayyar ki domin Ni ban taɓa son ki ba jikin ki nake so kuma na samu final.

Murmushi mai ciwo tayi,gashi yanzu ina dauke da cikin ka na wata ɗaya da sati ɗaya.

Cikin wa.?,God forbid ki nemi wanda ya maki CIKI ba dai Ni ba domin ban sanki ba kuma babu wanda yasan nayi soyayya dake,beside ma ina zaki sake gani na.?

Oh haka kace shike nan wallahi yadda kayi sanadin bakin ciki a zuciyarta da mom Dita har ta rasa ranta kai ma sai ka dandano baƙin ciki i promise ta kashe wayar.

Faduwa tayi jikin Hadiza tana kuka mai cin zuciya...,baby kinga illah soyayya da kafiri kenan ya yaudare ki yaci amanar ki domin ba auren ki zai yi ba,how stupid u are to think  that he'll definitely married u or come back for u baby.?

Baban abunda ya rage maki a yanzu shine dukufa da ibada, istigfari da nan zaɓin Allah, INSHA ALLAH General Sadiq zai bi maki hakkin ki kamar yadda yace zai binciko shi kuma ya gurfanar dashi a gaban kotu.

Tun daga ranar suka mayar da hankali wurin revision domin exams NASU dake zuwa suna haɗawa da adu'oi,zaman su gidan hajia mariya sai godiyar Allah domin babu abinda suka nema suka rasa, duk da tasan Baby nada ciki bai saka hajia ta kyamace ta ba illah janta a jiki da tayi sosai tana kyautata mata tare da kwantar mata da hankali.
 Mallami aka samo har cikin gida yake shigowa masu karatu da yamma,suna zuwa hospital a kai akai duba sister'n su.

Sati ɗaya da haihuwa aka yi zanen suna a masallaci yarinya taci suna NAZIYA kamar yadda ammie ta buƙata.


Sun Fara zana jarabawar NECO ranar Wednesday Wanda driver yake kai su before su fito yana waje yana jiran su ya dawo dasu gida.

Hadiza suna ci gaba da soyayya da Mallam munir wanda ke so ta bashi dama ya turo iyayen shi a masu baiko amma tana jin tsoro.



Komawar General Sadiq imo State ya saka wani abokin aikin shi dake aiki a river state ya binciko mai Christ Wanda Dama iyaye da kakanni shi ba boyayyu bane cikin Sa'a aka yi nasarar cafke shi yana dawo wa daga club da dare, General Sadiq ya bada dama a tsare mai shi a cikin Barrack na port hacourt tare da bashi horo mai tsanani har sai ya zo.

 Ai kuwa yasha ɗan banzar duka har an sauya mai kamani sai kuka yake yana tuba domin yaji jiki.

Kwana biyu a kulle ana azabtar dashi sanan General Sadiq ya iso wurin shida yasa  a hira shi sai da yaji tausayin shi.

Ya dube shi kasan dalilin da yasa aka dauko ka.?

Ya girgiza kai a wahalce,amma kasan baby a kano.?

Yace eh,ok me ya haɗa ku.?

Ba kunya ya bayyana komi kuma yana neman gafara.

General yace tunda aka nemi gafara yanzu zamu gaban iyayen ka sai ka fadi abinda kayi and ya zama dole kaje kano ka bata hakuri da alƙawarin zaka kula da ita da abunda zata haifa,hakan ba yana nufin idan ta haihu za'a baka abunda ta haifa ba.!,ko ɗaya ita keda iko dashi domin ba zamu ba kafiri ya bata ma jinin mu tarbiyyar ba.

Cikin sauri ya amince da duka sharudan General aka saka shi a mota jina jina sai gidan su.

Inda hankalin iyayen shi a tashe yake kwana biyu basu ga shi ba an saka cikiya babu labari..,ganin shi haka da jami'an tsaro hankalin su ya tashi sosai aka fara koke koke sai me jin abunda ya aikata yafi komi bata ma uban shi rai wanda ita uwarshi tasan ya saba ɓacin ranta kawowa da ya koma kan musulma duk yan matan su na kudu ya rasa wace zai wa ciki sai musulma.

Grandfather nashi doka shak'e sarki ne ya goyi bayan jikan shi yana neman kawo Maganar banza, General yace a bayar da christ Barrack aci gaba da hora shi har sai sun shirya amsan laifin ɗan su.

Uwar ta shiga kuka tana magiya har dai aka sasanta ya basu sati ɗaya su tafi kano idan ba haka ba zai saka a rufe mai christ har ta haihu.


Bayan ya koma imo state ne ya kira alaji habu ya sanar dashi yadda akayi da christ,yace kawai ya kyale shi ba sai sun zo ya tayar ma baby da hankali ba domin yanzu ta manta da komi karatu take yi,in dai maganar kula da ita da abunda zata haifa ne insha Allah ba zai basu kunya ba,suka yi sallama.

Bayan wattani biyu sun kammala Neco aka shirya masu walima haɗe dana saukar al-Qur'ani mai girma a cikin gida aka yi,su umma duka sun zo duk da tana fama da ciwo cikin zuciyarta taki magana ne kada hankalin kowa ya sake tashi,duniya babu abinda ta nema ta rasa yanzu,ta riga ta manyanta ƙarfi ya ƙare sai fatan gamawa da Duniya lafiya.

Walima  ya samu halartar yan uwa da abokan arziki inda aka ci aka sha akayi adu'oi na musamman ga marigayi ya Hajiya Hadiza (Ammie).

Alaji habu ya basu kyautar waya  kirar Ta android ko wace a cikin kwaliyin ta da layi(dana burin ammie kenan sai sun gama jarabawa ta basu kyautar waya babba)
 An dauko naziya daga Hospital yanzu madara aka rubuta mata shi take sha tayi kyau abun ta kamar ba haihuwar watani bakwai ba,fuskarta sak ammie sai shiga rai kowa na sonta da tausayinta.


Cikin baby nada wattani uku yanzu suka shirya zuwa kumbotso hutu har sakamako ya fito,driver aka saka ya kai su wacce baby ta kwashe komi nata domin umma tace nan zata ci gaba da zama har ta haihu.

Haka suka bar gidan cike da kewar baby naziya.

Yaya ɗan Gusau ya sake yiwa  General Sadiq tuni akan maganar auren baby da take son yi.

Yace ya bashi nan da watani shida(dai dai ta haihu) INSHA ALLAH zai sanar dashi halin da ake ciki.

Ranshi ya ɓaci yadda ake mai yawo da hankali kawai ya shirya zuwa kano domin magana da alaji habu!
