~🅿67-68~



       Zaman su a kumbotso ba ƙaramin natsuwa da kwanciyar hankali Baby KE samu wurin umma ba,a koda yaushe tana kan yi mata nasiha da bata shawarawari saboda halin rayuwa.

Idan yamma tayi bayan sun Kamala aikin abinci su kan ɗan zaga cikin garin ita da Hadiza tana nuna mata wurare,bayan sallar isha'i kuma suna leka makarantar islamiyya a biya masu karatu su dawo gida.

A yanzu hankalin baby ya kwanta saboda tana cikin yan uwanta na jini suna zama lafiya babu wani fargaba,gori ko tashin hankali ta ƙara sanin daraja da muhimmanci zumunci a kullum burin ta bai wuce Allah ya raba ta da cikin nan lafiya ba domin ta cika burin ammie na ƙarshe a kanta"samun ingantaccen ilimi.

Alkawari ta ɗauka duk abinda ta haifa zata bashi kulawa kamar yadda ko wane ɗa zai samu,zata bashi gata da tarbiyya kuma zata nisanta shi da uban shi sai idan shi har sai ya mallaki hankalin kanshi zata bashi labarin  komi idan ya nemi zuwa inda uban shi yake zata sada su domin sauke nauyin da Allah ya d'aura mata,idan kuma bata kai wanan lokaci ba zata bar wasiyya.


  Yaya ɗan Gusau zaune a babban palour'n gidan alaji habu ya xayane mashi buƙatar shi na son auren baby,ganin general Sadiq na mashi YAWO da hankali yasa yazo da kanshi.

Shiru ya biyu baya na ɗan lokaci Alaji habu ya nisa,Alhamdulillah mun gode wa Allah da ya kawo mu wanan lokaci da muka dad'e muna jira,dama burin marigayi biyu ne akan baby Wanda Allah bai bata ikon cika wa ba,sune:samun ingantaccen ilimi da muka miji na gari wanda zai riƙe mata Baby akan gaskiya da amana,sai gashi Allah ya kawo mijin  wanda ya san Baby yasaan wacece ita kuma ya amince da ita a haka.

Sai dai wani hanzarin ba gudu ba,gwaggo ta san da wanan magana.?

Sosai ma kuma itace ma ta bani kwarin gwiwa akan maganar domin a farko dana samu labari Baby jikina yayi sanyi matuƙa sai na ga ai ƙaddara ce kowa da irin tasa.

Akwai abu ɗaya da yasa  General Bai shigo da maganar ka wurin magabatan baby ba GASKIYA wanda nima ban ga laifin shi ba,magana ta Gaskiya baby na ɗauke da cikin watanni uku a yanzu haka wanda wani ɗan bautar ƙasa ya yaudare ta har Allah ya kawo rabo a tsakanin su.

Wani irin zufa ke keto mashi ta ko ina domin yaji maganar unexpected ne wani mugun kishi ya taso mai saboda gaskiya yana son Baby ba zai so wani abu makamancin zargi ya shiga tsakanin shi da ita ba.

Na fahimta babu Matsala zan iya auren ta a haka.

Masha Allah yanzu inaga muyi hakuri har ta haihu sai ka gabatar da kanka a wurin ta,idan kun daidaita ba za'a ɗauki lokaci ba ,zan samu lokaci in tafi can muyi magana da shi hydar.

Godiya ya mashi suka rabu ya koma masauki shi.


Waya Alaji habu ya fara buga wa General akan maganar domin yanzu sun zama family duka ba'a boye wa juna komi tare ake Shawara.

Sun tattauna sosai a kan maganar wanda General ya bashi assurance akan yaya ɗan Gusau zai iya riƙe masu Baby domin ɗan uwan shi ne kuma ya yaba da halayen shi.


Ranar asabar suka shirya da Hajiya mariya zuwa kumbotso domin ta kai na umma naziya ta gani.

Murna wurin su baby baa magana suka shirya abinci kala kala na tarban su hajiya.


Da wuri suka iso gidan saboda suna son komawa cikin lokaci, Baby ta amshi naziya ta rumgume tana mata wasa,Hadiza ta ɗauka,wanan ya dauka wanan ya ɗauka umma dai ta amshe yar jikan ta wace ke kama da mahaifiyar ta sak gata fara fol da ita.


Sun zauna da ya hydar an yi maganar yaya ɗan Gusau wanda shima yaji daɗin haka domin bai ga amfanin dogon karatun 'ya mace ba a ganin shi a dalilin karatun boko ammie ta samu ciki,yaya Mallam ya guje wa gida,ga baby da ciki suka a dalilin  boko.

Lokacin da zasu koma baby taso a bar mata naziya,Hajiya ta basu hakuri da alƙawarin zata rinka kawo ta a kai akai suna ganinta.


Haka dai rayuwa taci gaba su kan je Kano suyi kwana biyu,ana kawo naziya wurin su.

Wayoyin da aka basu ko wace  tana chat da nata da yake baby tafi yin face book Nan ta hadu da mutane da dama wa yanda ta sani irin su: sherrif class mate nata a sokoto,aysha na Sokoto har suka yi exchanging contact ta Bata shawara ta fara WhatsApp.

LOKACIN da ta buɗe WhatsApp ne taga aunty sadeeyah, general Sadiq, Meenah,aunty faiza,saratu,Yaya ɗan Gusau wanda aysha ta bashi sabon number baby.

Ta Nan ya fara cusa kanshi wurin Baby wace tun tana share wa har ta saki jiki suna chat sosai kuma yakan kirata a waya wasu lokuta.

 

*Bayan watanni shida*
Result ɗin WAEC ya fito Alhamdulillah sun yi making babu wa ta matsala wanda haka ya yiwa General Sadiq DAƊI sosai burin shi baby ta samu ilimi.

Tun bayan da baby tayi sallah asuba take fama da ciwon baya kafafunta sun riƙe ga nauyin ciki duk ya sauko mata ƙasa ko daga ƙafa bata iya yi sai daure wa take bata faɗa ma kowa ba tana zaune saman sallaya tana adu'oi har gari yayi haske tana wurin,umma ce ta fara lura da halin da take ciki ta aika yara aka taso ya hydar su tafi PRIMARY HEALTH Care dake cikin garin(dama shi ke amfani da motar ammie).

Umma da Hadiza suka riƙe ta zuwa mota domin ta galabaita,koda suna isa ma aikata basu fito ba saboda 7am lokacin,mai gadi ya kira metron ɗin a waya suka fito domin Nan cikin clinic d'in suke da gida.

Ɗaya nurse d'in ta tambayi Hadiza file ɗin Baby na awo fa?,tace ai bata taɓa zuwa awo ba.

Faɗa suka shiga masu wai yan ƙauye haka suke yi suna kashe kansu ta yaya mace zata dauke ciki daga wata ɗaya zuwa atara ace bata can me take ɗauke dashi ba bare lafiyar ta da abunda ke cikin ta.

Haushi abun ya ba Hadiza tece,mu fi ba yan ƙauye ba ne domin yadda kike takama kina da ilimi haka muke dashi condition ne yayi warranting namu zuwa wannan wuri.

Metron ɗin ta dakatar da hayaniyar ta hanyar shigar da baby labour rum Nan suka ɗauki vital signs nata,aka duba fundal height, presentation anda position na abunda ke cikin ta ana they are within normal range,Nan aka fara bata taimakon gaggawa domin koda suka zo tana 4cm.

Nurse d'in Yar bala'i ya fito tare da kallon Hadiza shekeke,amshi wanan list ɗin a sayo yanzu dasu zamu yi amfani.

Bata bita kanta ba ta amsa ta nufi wurin mota inda babanta yake ta bashi.

Umma tace ta bishi gida su hado kayan haihuwa tunda an ce da sauran Lokaci.


Gida suka fara zuwa har nafisa ta zuba masu ruwan zafi a babban Flask,gefe kuma ta d'aura tukunyar wanka.hadiza tace sannu da aiki mama!

Yauwa ya mai jikin dai?

Da sauki sun ce da sauran lokaci zan ɗauki kaya ne baba na waje.

Already ya hydar ya saya kaya unisex da setting baf na jarirai,cikin wani jakka ta ɗauki kala biyu kaya na mace da namiji,sau zanuwa uku da riga suka wuce.


Azaban duniya baby ta Sha shi kasancewa haihuwar fari ce gabanta a matse yake duk da ba scanning akayi ba sun ji alama babyn nada girma wanda dole sai an ƙara ta idan kuma ta kasa za su tura su babban Hospital ayi mata aiki.

Kuka, addu'a da nan gafara babu wanda Baby Bata Yi ba a ganinta mutuwa za tayi ta saka aka kira mata umma da Hadiza ta shiga rokon gafara tana barin wasiyya idan mace ce sunan aunty sadeeyah,idan namiji ne UNCLE sadiq.

Kwantar mata da hankali umma ta shiga yi tana bata adu'oi da zata riƙe,Hadiza dai kuka take yi ganin yadda yar uwarta ke wahala.

Ta dauki tsayin awanni  shida Da kyar da wahala kan jariri ya fara fitowa wanda sai da aka mata ƙari sanan ya fito duka,ta samo Baby boy fari fol mai kyau da shi.

Bayan an gyara ta an mata dunki inda suka ƙara suka  canza mata zani,nurse tace a kawo kunu mai zafi ko tea.

 Umma ta haɗa tea mai zafi wanda yaji sugar aka ba nurse ta kai mata domin zai taimaka sauran jinin daya daskare a cikinta zai fito.

Tana zaune saman wani bed na dabam tana shan tea a hankali gefe kuma nurse na gyara jariri ita dai kallon su take tana tuno da abubuwa da dama,can ta saki murmushi tunawa da yadda ta rinƙa haukar shan tea ba sugar lokacin da take da ciki gashi yau Allah ya raba su lafiya,ummm yau ita baby ce ta haifi ɗa nata na kanta bata hanyar aure ba.!

Wa'iyazubillah,ba zata butulce ba tana Godiya ga Allah da ya bata wanan babban kyauta wanda wasu na nema da kuɗi basu samo ba.alhamdulillah iyayenta basu ga wanan abun kunya ba da ambata san halin da zasu shiga ba.


Ana gama gyara baby ya hydar ya biya kuɗaɗen da aka bukata suka shigo ganinta kowa sai murna yake ana mata barka,basu jima ba aka sallame su suka koma gida.

Umma da kanta tayi wa baby wanka saboda jariri sai da yamma za'a mai,wurin bada nono aka sha fama da ita domin babu kai(nipple),Shima ko an saka mai a baki baya kama wa ga nonuwa sun ciwo sosai lokacin da ya fara kamawa sai data kwala ihu saboda azaba gashi ruwan yaƙi zuwa nurse tace a barshi yayita tostso ruwan zai zo a hankali.
Duk da bata samu wanan gata ba a lokacin da ka haife ta amma ta jinjina wa iyaye mata yadda suke wahala da ciki,haihuwa har zuwa shayarwan.

Waya ya hydar ya bugawa su Alaji habu da su General sadiq ya sanar dasu haihuwa.

Washe gari sai ga hajia mariya,aunty faiza,saratu da usman sun zo da abun arziki iri iri,sai bin naziya baby keyi da ido saboda tayi wayau sosai tana rarafe ko ina har tana kama kujera ta tashi.

Duk wani gata da ake yiwa mace idan ta haihu Baby ta samu ta ko ina kayan sawa jariri,nata abinci da sauran su duka an kawo.

General ya bata hakuri ba zai samu zuwa ba saboda aiki da ya mashi yasa sai dai matar shi zata zo ta kwana biyu.


Su Alaji habu sun zo da abun arziki sosai yadda rago babba  koda yayi magana da baby tace sunan UNCLE sadiq take so a saka,yayi wa yaro huduba da sunan


Ana gobe zanen suna Aunty sadeeyah taso da irin nata abun arzikin wanda sai da ya saka baby kukan farin ciki da kukan rashin ammie'n ta.

Washe gari aka rada suna a masallaci yaro yace Abubakar sadiq aka yanke ruguna biyu wanda ya hydar ya saya ɗaya,alaji habu ya saya ɗaya.

Anyi taro cikin gida tsakanin su kawai wanda an ci an sha sai godiyan Allah.

Bayan komi ya lafa Aunty sadeeyah taba baby shawarwari Yadda zata kula da honey(haka take kiran jariri),yadda zata kula da kanta wanda koda ta gama shayarwan boobs Bata ba zasu kwanta ba, haƙa gabanta ta kula dashi yadda zai koma normal size.

Umma ta aje kunya jefe tana kuka da baby sosai da sosai har suka yi kwana arba'in da haihuwa lokacin suka  tafi kano rubuta JAMB Wanda baby ta rubuta Thursday,hadiza ta rubuta Friday suka yanke shawarar yin weekend a kano tunda da LITTLE Sadiq tazo.

Ranar Saturday a gidan ba'baa mairo suka yini sai yamma suka koma gida kawai taga mutum ashe tun azuhur ya iso ya hana a kirata(yaya ɗan Gusau kenan).

Bayan sun gaisa ta dauko mai little Sadiq ya amsa tare da  yi mata barka.

Sun jima zaune ya katse shirun da cewa,baby kamar yadda kika sani a baya Ni masoyin kine Wanda ke sonki saboda Allah da niyyar auren ki,na dawo a karo na biyu ki bani dama mu fahimci juna,nayi magana da magabatan ki sun bani dama Saboda haka  ba zan so a ɗauki lokaci mai tsayi ba saboda na samu babban aiki a cotonou, republic of Benin zan koma can da zama,ina bukatar mace a kusa dani domin ita Balki ta nuna bata ra'ayin komawa kuma ta bani dabam ƙara aure.

Shiru tayi itama dole a halin da ake ciki soyayya ta fice mata a rai kwata kwata bare a kai ga maganar aure,sai dai ba zata iya bijire wa magabatan ta  ba.

Can Kasan mak'oshinta tace"Allah ya tabbatar mana da alheri.

Faɗada murmushin shi yayi tare da amsawa Amin.

Kada ki samu damuwa baby zan riƙe ki da sadiq amana haka maganar karatu akwai makarantu masu kyau a can ko wane course kike so Zaki samu zan tsaya maki da ƙarfi na da kuma aljihuna.

NAGODE tace.

Babban leda ya aje wurin ƙafan ta,ki shiga da wanan ba yawa sai na shigo gobe kafin in koma.

Godiya ta sake mashi ta amshi little ta shige.

Saman Hajiya mariya ta wuce direct ta aje mata ledan..,ah alhaji Mustapha hada dawainiya, Allah ya sanya albarka mun gode and baby ki tsayar da hankalin ki wuri ɗaya aure shine darajar ko wace d'iya mace AM sure alhaji Mustapha zai kula da ke sosai.

Tace tau, buɗe ledan Hajiya tayi taga turmin atamfan super wax vlisco da Holland biyu sai material ɗaya da kayan baby kala uku masu kyau da tsada.

Barin kayan tayi a d'akin domin a nuna ma Alaji habu idan ya shigo ta wuce d'akin su.

Har  little Sadiq ya fara bacci ta sanya mai pampas ta kwantar dashi sanan taba Hadiza labari.

Murna sosai tayi tana mata tsiya wai zata yi hijira zuwa ƙasar wasu.

Joke apart Hadiza na amince dashi ne kawai ba don ina sonshi ba sai don faranta ran iyayen mu.

Gaskiya banji daɗin maganan kin bBy amma muci gaba da addu'a Kinga Nima Mallam munir ya matsa zai turo zan faɗa ma hajiya ayiwa Daddy magana.

Uhmm yaran yanzu babu kunya suka saka dariya.

Da safe suka shirya Mashi breakfast Mai kyau duk da ba da wuri yazo ba ya tashi a makare lokaci ya kure idan ya tsaya zai iya MISSING flight nashi,kawai a tsatsaye ya ci abincin kaɗan ya masu sallama.

Baby da kanta ta gabatar da maganar mallam munir wurin Hajiya wacce tayi farin ciki da haka kuma aya ma alaji magana domin ya turo ɗin.

Sai da aka sake jin ta baƙin Hadiza Kafin a bashi dama,ai kuwa a ranar lahadin da dare yazo da abokan shi biyu suka gaisar da su alaji wanda ya yaba da hankalin shi matuƙa ɗan matashi wanda ba zai wuce shekaru ashirin da takwas a duniya ba amma ya samu Foundation mai kyau  Allah ya bashi Sa'a ya kamala degree nashi in HUMAN RESOURCES MANAGEMENT A GHANA ya dawo da sakamako mai kyau har ya fara aiki da ministry for local government and chieftency affairs Nan Kano.


Koda alaji habu ya bincika abun duk gida ne domin mahaifin munir makwabcin  shine a can shagari Quarters Kuma mutumin kirki ne,bayan ya sanar da ya hydar wanda yafi kowa farin ciki domin shi burin shi ya aurar da yaran shi cikin lokaci, yabar wuk'a da mama hanun alaji habu.

Ba'a ɗauki lokaci ba magabatan shi suka zo tambaya kuma aka basu,an a shi dama yaci gaba da neman auren ta idan anyi shawara za'a saka lokaci Gaba ɗaya dana Baby.


Haka suka dawo kumbotso cike da farin ciki wanda umma tafi kowa murna da jin wannan labari.

Bayan kwana biyu JAMB ya fito cikin ikon Allah sun samu score din da ake so domin ita Hadiza sociology take son Karatu Saboda science tayi, Baby dai bata da choice tunda ba'a Nigeria zata zauna ba.

Alaji habu da kanshi ya saya ma Hadiza Form na Bayero University Kano( BUK) suka fara neman admission.


Duk wani al'ada da bahaushe keyi wurin neman aure an gabatar both na Baby da Hadiza  aka yanke lokacin aure watanni shida masu zuwa.

Hajiya mariya da kanta ta rubuta madara masu kyau na yara da za'a fara ba little sadiq domin a cire shi a nono su samu dabam kyara ta yadda ya kamata.

Cikin ikon Allah kuwa yana shan madarar sosai ta amshe shi babu wani rama ko zazzaɓi da yayi saboda shan madarar aka ci gaba da bashi.

Aunty sadeeyah taso bada nata gudun kawa amma bata kusa tabi mijinta imo state sai dai tayi alkawarin zuwa da wuri Kafin a fara shagulgula buki.


Kano suka koma aka barma umma little Sadiq tana kula dashi domin Hadiza ta samu admission a faculty of social science sai dai public administration suka bata yanzu leacture ya rage ta fara.

Gidan ya masu daɗi domin saratu ta kammala degree Bata yanzu tana service ne a Dala local government wanda ba kullum take zuwa ba.


Shirye shirye ake ta ko wane bangare saura wata guda cur auren,Hadiza kai ya dau zafi ga karatu  an fara ga shirin aure anata sayayya,ita baby ba zata da kaya ba kuɗi zasu dashi a saya a can.


Waya suka yi da aysha mu'azu take sanar da ita ta kamalla karatu ta dawo gida wurin gwaggo ta samu labari mai daɗi baby zata dawo cikin family su.

Tace haka Allah ke lamuran shi Aysha sai ku taya mu da addu'a kawai.

Lallai kam ki dage domin wallahi aunty balki ba zaune take ba duk ta tashi hankalinta dana mutane a dole bata amince ya aure ki ba.

Jikin baby sanyi yayi sosai jin haka domin ta tsani tashin hankali musamman irin gwagwarmayar data sha a rayuwa yanzu lokaci yayi da zata samu natsuwa a gidan mijinta ma kishiya na neman jaza mata bala'i.


Haka suka rabu a waya Aysha na kwantar mata da hankali.

Ana buki saura sati biyu aunty sadeeyah ta diro da nata makaman yaƙi musamman domin gyara baby ciki da waje,wanda duk gyaran ya fice mata a rai kowa ya taso ayi kanta da gyara,umma tayi nata, Hajiya mariya tayi yadda su ba'baa mairo yanzu ga Aunty sadeeyah da nata.

Ango Mustapha ana can cotonou an kama aiki duk da yana ƙoƙarin kiranta a waya lokaci zuwa lokaci,sai  daya tabbatar ya  gama mata komi ta samu admission Sana ya sanar da ita ta samu admission a "ISM ADONAI UNIVERSITY (cotonou campus), faculty of art and humanities,sun Bata international relations.

Murna sosai tayi da jin wanan albishir,ya tabbatar ta sai saura kwana uku daurin aure zai shigo Nigeria jirgin su zai sauka a Lagos ya shigo luxurious zuwa Sokoto daga washe gari ya biyo jirgi da abokan shi zuwa kano daurin aure.

Godiya ta mashi tare da adu'oi samun nasara akan abinda ya saka a gaba na alheri ,sai amsawa yake da amin Amin alamar yaji daɗin adu'oi suka yi sallama.


Satin biki ya zagayo suka tarkata gaba ɗaya suka bar kano zuwa kumbotso wanda nan aka kai lefe ko wace set ɗaya kaya na gani a faɗa musamman na baby yaya ɗan Gusau ya buɗe bakin aljihun shi sosai.


Aunty sadeeyah ganin NAZIYA sai da ta zubar da hawaye Tunawa da ammie.
Naziya yar shekara ɗaya da watani uku amma kamar yar shekaru uku saboda samun kulawa,bata da samuwar komi ga sauri ta shiga zuciya.

Already sun gama da tailor tun a kano Basu da Matsala da wanan,ranar talata da safe aka sanya su a lalle ranar laraba akayi kamu da yamma a gidan gonar ammie wanda mallakin baby me a yanzu.

Bayan magrib sun dawo gida har ta idar da Sallah taga text din yaya ɗan Gusau don yata kiranta wayar na cikin purse,Yana shaida mata ya isa Lagos lafiya da karfe goma na dare zasu kama hanyar Sokoto.

Text ta tura mashi na fatan alheri sai ga kiran shi ya shigo.

AMARYAN ya fama da jama'a.?,ciki cik ta amsa alhamdlillah ya hanya.?

Lafiya kau muka Sauka  saura bin hanya, Allah ya tsare ya kai ku lafiya.
Ya amsa da Amin ya Allah.

Aunty sadeeyah ce ta isheta ta tashi taci abinci ta shiga wanka da ruwan lalle Hadiza har ta gama nata.

Duka yana jin su ya saki dariya mai sauti,amarya na atashi a gyara mun kaya na bana son wasa da wurin nan.

Kunya ne ya kamata domin bashi da kunyar sakin maganganun ta lura da haka tun da aka saka date kanshi na rawa  kullum jikin jadadda mata yadda yake jinta a zuciyar shi da yadda zasu rinƙa cin abinci,wanka da kwana tare.

Gud Dama wayau na maki kullum idan na e i love You sai kice wani thanks kamar wace za'a yiwa auren dole.

Dariya ya bata ta kashe wayar saboda aunty sadeeyah ta tsare ta da idanu.

Bayan sun gama komi har sun yi shirin kwanciya ta tuna da wayarta dake chargie a palour,zuwa tayi ta dauko ai kuwa missed Calls nashi plenty da text sun kama hanya daga Lagos zuwa Sokoto he need her prayer.

Koda ta Kira shi wayar out of service Area haka ta gaji ta tura text ta kwanta.

Da asuba ta tashi Sallah ta kira shi still a kashe.

Abinda ya faru bayan ya kira baby bai samu ba ya kira General Sadiq ya faɗa mai zasu kama hanya zuwa Sokoto,yace yayi hakuri har da safe yabi flight zai fi mashi domin hanyar nan babu kyau cikin kwana kin nan ana reporting rubbery case sosai.

Sai ya nuna mutuwa da rayuwa tana hanun Allah shi ba zai iya jira ba yana son Isa Sokoto on time ne saboda wasu shirye shiryen da zai yi kafin friday su wuce kano.
Addu'a kawai ya bishi dashi.

Sai gashi 2pm na dare aka kira shi (last dial call)ana sanar dashi  mai wayar yayi accident an wuce dashi  hospital a wani ƙauye.

Zufa ke keto mashi ta ko ina domin komi ya ƙare mashi ana saura kwana biyu daurin aure yayi accident idan baby ta samu labari sai yaya.?
Allah dai yasa da sauki.

Ƙasa bacci yayi cikin daren yayi waya da air vice Marshal a Nan cikin imo Yana son tafiya gobe da safe zuwa Lagos,yana ganawa ya kira tsohon Barrack nasu na Lagos a shirya mai mutane da mota da ya iso zasu bi hanyar.

Bai kira kowa ba don baya son tayar masu da hankali har sai ya ganshi tukuna.

7am a airport ya mashi suka nufi Lagos 7:40am daga nan basu tsaya ba suka kama hanya suna isa scene d'in da accident din ya faru cikin dare suka ga motar a gefe da alama mugun fashi aka masu domin akwai busashen jini a saman titi,basu wani tsaya ba suka wuce around 10am suna cikin Hospital din.

Bayan ya gabatar da kanshi a matsayin ɗan uwan ɗaya daga cikin wanda accident ya ritsa dasu cikin dare aka ɗauke shi zuwa word d'in da masu rai suke amma kam suna jin jiki sosai.!

Iya Binciken shi babu yaya ɗan Gusau a Cikin su nan take gaban shi ya fadi yana sallati suka nufi mutuary ana ta bubude mai gawawakin"innalillahi wa'inna ilaihi raji'un shine abunda ya furta ganin ɗan wani shi,ya'yan shi abokin shi kuma surukin shi  wanda ayiwa yankan rago kwance ba rai.

Da sauri ya rufe gawan domin ba zai juri ganin jijiyar wuyan dannawa shi a fili ba.

Suman zaune yayi saman floor domin wanan masifa tafi ƙarfin shi,da wani baki zai kai wanan labari.?,shi dai haka Allah ya halicce shi da kai mumunar labari.!

Rashin lafiyar yaya Mallam shi ya kai labari haka mutuwar shi,shine aka fara sanar wa,cikin baby shi ya kai ma ammie labari ga mutuwar ammie a dalilin labarin da ya kawo sama yanzu ya kai ma Baby labarin mutuwar wanda zata aura,!
Gaskiya ba zai iya ba sai  dai akai gawan Sokoto a mashi sutura ba tare da Sanin kowa a akano ba idan yaso a d'aura auren dashi,bayan daurin Aure ya sanar da kowa Gaskiya dole za'a ɗauki hakuri!

~🅿69-70~



   🅱a'a ɗauki lokaci ba suka bashi gawan zuwa Sokoto,dama mua'azu kawai ya sanar ma wa da rasuwar wanda yana Kaduna wurin aikin shi daga nan zai tafi kano daurin aure amma matar shi ta wuce Sokoto.


Ba tare da wani tunani ba ya buga wa Alaji habu waya yana sanar dashi rasuwar ɗan uwan shi domin su dakatar da shirin bukin, dama can ba son auren yake ba a ganin shi za'a gurɓata masu zuri'a.

Jikin alaji habu yayi sanyi matuƙa domin wanan ƙaddara ta baby sai addu'a ace ka rasu iyayen ka cikin shekara ɗaya a shekara ta biyu ta samu ciki kumanl rasa wanda zaka aura.!

Shiri yayi na zuwa kumbotso domin sanar dasu halin da ake ciki,koda ya isa suna wurin mother's day daga umma sai yan uwa na ƙauye ne kawai a gida.

Waya ya buga wa ya hydar ya tafi wurin event d'in a ɗauko Baby.

Zuwa wani lokaci ya dawo da ita suka zaune kuryan Dakin umma domin akwai jama'a cikin gida.

Wuri ya ɗauki shiru umma da ya hydar Suna sauraren suji abunda ke tafe da alaji habu ,Baby dama tun jiya take ji a jikinta wani babban al'amari zai faru da ita.


Katse shirun yayi,kamar yadda kuka sani gobe ne daurin auren baby da Alhaji Mustapha wanda dama Allah ya haɗa duk da haka a kullum muna kan addu'a Allah ya tabbatar mana da alherin da ke cikin wannan al'amari haka zalika matar mutum kabarin shi ce,mace bata auren mijin wata,haka namiji baya auren matar wani kamar yadda kowa da irin tashi ƙaddara.

Zan su mu rungumi wanan ƙaddara hanu biyu ko yadda da cewa komi ya samu bawa Mukadari ne daga Allah, ba'a jima ba Alhaji mu'azu ya mun waya Allah yayi wa Alhaji Mustapha rasuwa a hanyar shi ta dawo wa daga Lagos zuwa Sokoto yan fashi suka tsare su akayi mashi yankan rago!

Hawaye ne ke fita a idanun baby kamar an bude famfo,umma sai sallati take tana Kada kai,shi ya hydar rasa abun yi yayi sai shiru.

Don haka inaso ku yadda da ƙaddara dama can ba mijinta bane,mu bishi da addu'a tare da nema mai rahamar Ubangiji.

Ajiyar zuciya ya hydar yayi"har an rufe shi ne.?

A'a suna hanya ma Sadiq ya ɗauko gawan zuwa Sokoto inaga sai gobe za'a rufe shi,sai mu shirya da safe mu tafi ko Allah zai sa mu samu jana'iza.

Daurin auren Hadiza idan ka bani dama zan yi waya da iyayen shi a  daura bayan sallar asuba a masallaci sai ya ɗauki matar shi a gida na.

Umma tace haka ma yayi tunda an kai kayanta an gama jere me za'a jira.

Ba wani kuka baby tayi ba saboda mutuwar makusantan ta ba sabon abu bane a wurin ta wanda ta ɗauki hakan a matsayin babban kaddarar rayuwarta sai fatan Allah ya jikan shi da rahama ya sauya mata da mafi alheri.


Kafin wani lokaci labarin mutuwar yaya ɗan Gusau ya karade gidan buki sai tausayin Baby ake ana jajanta mata,aunty sadeeyah sai da tayi kuka sosai domin tafi kowa son baby tayi aure ta natsu gidan mijinta jita samu kwanciyar hankali.

Hadiza kuwa kamar mijin nata ne ya rasu domin a daren nan sai da akayi mata ƙarin ruwa saboda zazzaɓi da ya rufe ta a sanadiyar kukan da ta rinƙa dirzan baby na Bata hakuri....,umma ma tasha nata kukan a b'oye.

A daren alaji habu da ya hydar suka dawo kano domin magana da iyayen munir aka aje akan da safe hajia mariya zata wuto da hadiza a danka wa mijinta.



Gidan buki ya koma gidan mutuwa kowa ka gani ya kame kanshi shiru sai zancen zuci, Alhamdulillah iyayen munir mutane masu mutunci da fahimta suka aminci da bukatar su ya hydar ana idar da Sallah asuba aka daura aure cikin masallaci aka raba goro da minti,gari na fara haske suka bi jirgi zuwa Sokoto.

General Sadiq basu suka iso garin Sokoto ba sai karfe 3:0am na safe ,da yake kowa ya samu labari gidan cike yake da yan uwa na nesa da kusa duk suna nan saboda auren,aunty Balki sai sambatu tana zargin baby ta kashe mata miji burin ta ya cika ta raba ta dashi har abada,ba zata yadda ba sai ta dau mata ki a kan baby Wanda da kyar aka rarasheta.

 
Cikin ikon Allah aka shirya shi tsaf yan uwa suna ta zuwa wurin gawan ana addu'a har 9:0am tayi lokacin su alaji habu suka iso aka nufi makabarta dasu.

Ganin su ba ƙaramin tayar wa General Sadiq da hankali yayi ba saboda bai so a sanar dasu ba har sai ya share ma Baby hawaye amma haka Allah ya ƙaddara.

Bayan sun dawo daga makabarta su ya hydar suka shiga cikin domin yiwa mata gaisuwa, General ne ke gabatar dasu ai kuwa aunty Balki KAMAR ta shak'o su don baƙin ciki.

Tare suka zaune har yamma domin a ranar zasu juya sanin halin da suka bar gida gashi babu wani namiji babba a tare dasu,suka shigo yiwa GWAGGO sallama sai Godiya take masu tana miƙa sakon gaisuwa zuwa ga baby.


  Can Kano ango da kanshi yazo ya ɗauki amarya ya fara kaita gidan iyayen shi ta yini sai da dare suka wuce gidan su dake zoo road madaidaici.


 Idan kaga yadda baby ta Koma sai ka tausaya mata saboda daga ibada sai shiru ko cin abinci sai aunty sadeeyah ta matsa take ɗan tsakura wa har akayi kwana uku da rasuwan lokaci General ya baro Sokoto domin abban shi dasu hajja sun tafi,shi yasa ya nufo Kano saboda yana son magana da baby,Dama yabar matar shine ta kwantar mata da hankali.

Cikin ikon Allah koda yazo sai yadda ba abunda yake tunani bane,Baby ta saki ranta babu wani damuwa a tattare da ita.

Nasiha ya mata sosai tare da mika gaisuwa gwaggo a gare ta.

Wani envelope ya fitar wanda ke ɗauke da passport,visa  da duk  abinda  zata buƙata nata na karatu a  cotonou.

Kuka take yi sosai jin lalai sai ta tafi ƙasar da bata san kowa ba saboda karatu,wanda zata a dalilin shi ya rasu mai zai hana a barta taji da damuwar dake addabar zuciyarta ana neman tursasa ta.


Umma da ya hydar ma basu so tafiyarsa ba domin a yanzu burin su bai wuce Allah ya fitar mata da wani mijin ta yi aure kamar yadda yar uwarta tayi ba.

Jan hankalin su sadiq yayi yana nuna masu muhimmanci abun wanda yasan Baby ba zata basu kunya ba tunda shekara ɗaya da rabi zata yi ta kammala degree nata sai dawo wa gida kuma ya masu alkawari idan ta samu miji ba'a jinkiri ba za'a mata aure,da haka ya shawo kan su suka aminci Ta shiga haɗa kayanta domin tare zasu wuce Kano tayi sallama  da mutane daga nan su nufi Lagos a can jirgin su zai tashi zuwa Benin republic.

LOKACIN da zasu bar gidan da kuka ta rabo da umma ga little Sadiq tasan zata yi missing nasu sosai suka nufi kano.

Gidan Hadiza suka fara tsayawa wanda tayi matuƙar farin ciki da ganin Baby sai dai jin labarin bankwana tazo mata ya sata kuka sosai.

Nasiha dai Aunty sadeeyah ta Kara masu duka sanan suka bar gidan zuwa gidan Alaji habu shima basu jima ba  suka fito da zasu wuce ta rungume naziya tana kuka sai da tayi mai isar ta aka raba su.

Abunda bata sani ba ashe aunty sadeeyah ta nemi Alfarma su raka baby har cotonou su tabbatar da inda za tayi karatu da wurin zaman ta.

Suka isa Lagos suka nufi Barrack nasu na da,nan suka ci abinci suka yi wanka taga mutanen da ta jima bata gani ba sai da dare suka nufi airport ,basu jima ba aka fara kiran passenger tana batun fara kuka taga sun shiga jirgin tare dasu aunty sadeeyah Nan take ta rungome ta tana kukan farin ciki.!

   🅱abban airport dake cotonou jirgin su yayi landing around 9:0pm na dare suka yi shatar taxi gidan da marigayi ya zauna wanda staff Quarters ne gidajen irin ginin turawa ne(open apartment) gwanin sha'awa  suka shigar da kayan baby s cikin babban palour mai ɗauke da manyan ledar curshian.

Zama suka yi shiru kowa da abunda yake tunani,katse shirun yayi"Baby Kinga yadda Allah ke lamarin shi ko?

Kun gama tsara rayuwar ku sai ga mutuwa tayi mai yankan kauna,gaskiya ban so zuwa cotonou tare da ke ba saboda wurin aiki ana nema na, sai sadeeyah tayi insisting mu zo tare saboda ma akwai kayan shi da za'a koma gida dasu  mun yi magana da gwaggo tun a Sokoto aka nema mana visa Ni da sadeeyah shi yasa ta bar yara hanun hajja a Sokoto,so gobe da safe zamu raka ki cikin schl nan zaki zauna domin gidan nan na ma'aikata ne zasu amshi abun su kaya kawai zamu kwashe nashi.

Gyada kai tayi wasu hawaye Masu zafi suna sauka saman kumatun ta.

Kuna bukatar abinci ne?,kowa yace a'a,ok shiga ɗaya daga cikin ɗakunan sai kiyi wanka ki huta.

Haka ta tashi jiki babu kwari ta shiga bedroom d'in Wanda shine mallakin marigayi,kamshin turaren shi ya doki hancin ta nan take ta durkushe ƙasa ta fashe da wani irin kuka wanda ko kanar da aka ce ya rasu bata yi shi ba.!

Bin Dakin take da kallo,saman dressing mirror turaruka ne kala kala da body lotion nashi,saman Chest of drower Kuma ƙaramin frame ne mai ɗauke da hutun shi,matar shi da yaran shi  sun yi kyau cikin kana nan kaya sai dariya suke wanda kamar ma ba'a Nigeria suka ɗauka hoton ba.

Ɗaukan frame din tayi ta rungume a kirjin ta tana ci gaba da kuka har bacci ya ɗauke ta bata sani ba.

Da asuba bayan sun yi sallah ya fita ya samo masu abunda za suyi breakfast dashi wanda Baby baya wani ci ba saboda bata jin daɗin jikinta kwata kwata.

Zuwa 8am suka fito da kayan baby da suka zo dashi ya samo taxi da zai kai su carrifou kossi inda University ɗin yake.

Suna isa makeken gate na schl ɗin daga sama taga an rubuta"ISM ADONAI UNIVERSITY (l'universite prestigieuse"cotonou campus"),sai da security ya duba kayan su kafin ya bari suka shiga wanda tafiya mai nisa ne sosai daga gate zuwa  main building din, Baby kam ta zama ba kauya sai kale kale  take cuz schl  d'in ya haɗu sosai ga jinsi  iri iri tun daga baƙin fata har farin fata daga  ƙasa dabam dabam.
(SCHOOL OF ARTS AND HUMANITIES) driver yayi  parking suka fito yace da mai taxi aunty sadeeyah zata zauna ciki ta kira su kaɗan.

Wani steps duka hau wanda ya sada su da admin block yayi norking wani office,can aka bashi parmission suka shiga.
Bature ne zaune saman kujera a babban OFFICE d'in,bayan sun gaisa general ya mashi bayani cikin harshen turanci wanda ya fahimta ya bukaci ganin file na Baby aka bashi ya duba tare da jin signing a wani takada ya dauko ƙaramin recept da course outline ya basu.

massager dake zaune kofar office d'in yayi guiding nasu har faculty nata(department of international relations), already ta duba time table bata da lecture sai zuwa 10am.

Wurin mota suka koma driver ya yasu har gate na female HOSTEL,tare da aunty sadeeyah suka kwashe akwatin Baby zuwa ciki wanda faɗin tsaruwar HOSTEL din Bata lokaci ne domin three tower hill building ne ko wane faculty da HOSTEL block nasu wanda nasu baby KE hawa na uku rum 110 aka bata sauƙin su ɗaya akwai lifter ba sai kayi wahalan taka benen ka hau sama ba.


Norking door d'in suka yi,aka masa da "the door is open,turawa suka yi da sallama wani kamshi da sanyi ya ratsa zukatan su.

Wata farar mace tas ce zaune saman wani kujera da table a gabanta da gani karatu take yi,amsa sallamar su tayi tare da faɗa da murmushi.

Cikin harshen turanci tayi masu sannu da zuwa tana faɗa masu ai mr Frank Bailey ya faɗa mata za'a kawo fresh student d'akin ta.

Murmushi suka yi suma aunty sadeeyah tace eh baby CE student d'in ita aunty tane ta rako ta Daga Nigeria.

Hannu ta miko ma Baby tana fadin sunan ta janah al-Uzma daga ƙasar Libya tana final year d'in ta,baby tace"Aisha Mustapha daga Nigeria.

Dama bed da wardrob da table na karatu duka set biyu ke d'akin,janah tayi occupying ɗaya,aunty sadeeyah ta Kama ma baby suka shirya kayanta cikin wardrob dake facing bed nata.

Lokacin da zata tafi hada kuka baby tayi,ta kam aunty sadeeyah ganin jahah ta manyanta ta damk'a amanar baby a hanunta tare suka raka su har wurin taxi ɗin suka yi sallama.

 Koda suka dawo d'aki janah ta shirya domin a ranar zasu fara exams Wanda daga shi za suyi final exams nasu,tace ma baby idan anyi norking ta amsa masu abinci idan bata fita ba idan kuma ta fita ta rufe D'akin tana da spare key.

Zaune kawai tayi saman bed d'in ta tana tunanin duniya can ta shiga toilet nasu dake cikin rum d'in ta Kama ruwa ta dawo wayarta ta jawo ta kira aunty sadeeyah ko sun tafi.?
Tace sai da yamma suna haɗa kayan gidan ne za'a zuba motar haya ya wuce dasu sokoto su daga nan imo state suka yi.

Bayan sun gama waya ta kira Hadiza suka sha hira tana bata labarin yadda suke shan soyayya da munir ga karatu ya tasa ta gaba...,haka dai tayita kiran mutane suna gaisawa har 9:30am tayi.

Wanka ta shiga a gagauce ta shafa mai da turaruka,gefen da suka shirya abaya ta buɗe tare da ciro wani sky blue Arabian gown da yasha stones kala kala a gaban shi ya matse daga sama ƙasa ya buɗe sosai tayi Rolling ƙaramin veil dark blue sai kayan karatu da ID card nata ta zuba cikin ƙaramin handbag ta fito dagwas da ita sai shek'i take, sauƙin ta ɗaya babu ruwan wani da wani kowa na harkan gaban shi haka take tafiya a hankali har ta isa venue n leacture d'in.

Ba'a wani taro ba domin yau ne zasu fara leacture ɗin so wasu ma ba suyi resuming ba,wuri ta samu daga gefen window ta zauna tana kalle kalle har aka fara cika zuwa wani lokacin hall don ya cika makil da student FROM different countries and  region.


WATA baturiya ta shigo hall d'in tare da gabatar da kanta a matsayin leacture nasu da course d'in da zata ɗauke su,cikin tsararren British English nata ta fara masu da introducing Kafin su shiga main course d'in.

Baby dai na sauraren wasu abubuwan tana ɗan jotting a karshe tace su duba curriculum nasu suyi research akan next topic da zasu shiga ta fita around 12:30pm.

Time table nasu Baby ta duba taga  basu da wani lecture sai 4pm kawai tayi deciding ta Koma HOSTEL ta Dan kwanta kafin a kira sallah.

Mutane da rinƙa ratsa wa cikin hall d'in zata fita wanda wasu hira kawai suke maza da mata har ta fita tana tafiya ne taji anata kiran"ayeeshah,ayeeshah!
Bata amsa ba bare ta waiga domin bata san da wacce ake yi ba,ashe janah ce sun fito daga exams taga baby tana ta kiran ayeeshah yadda Larabawa ke faɗi...,har ta karaso wurin baby ta dafa ta sanan ta juyo da sauri tare da sakar mata murmushi.

Cikin harshen turanci tace,ina yuta kiran ki baki ji ba!
Tace ai ban san dani kike yi ba kuma ban ɗauki murya ba,tace ya exams?

Ta amsa da alhamdulillah,suka ci gaba da tafiya suna ɗan hira har suka isa HOSTEL janah ta shiga wanka baby kam lafiyar gado tabi sai bacci.

Tana fitowa wanka ta canja zuwa abaya baka da Jan veil Mai kauri ta rubuta short note ta aje wa baby ta fita.

Norking da ake tayi repeatedly ya tashi baby daga bacci ta buɗe kofar,wata datijuwa ce sanya da uniform irin na masu girki ta gaishe da baby tare da mata Barka da zuwa makarantar, foodflask babba ta shiga dashi Dakin ta aje ta fita.

Ganin har 2:20pm baby ta watsa ruwa ta gabatar da sallah yunwa ne ya isheta,bata yi yunkurin buɗe abincin ba ta ɗauko DAMBUN naman kaza da umma tayi mata shi taci ta kora da soft drink tana korarin gyara bed d'in be takada ya fado ta buɗe"janah ke sanar da ita ta tafi masjid ba zata shigo ba sai after 4pm idan ta tashi taci abinci za'a kawo.

Koda ta buɗe foodflask ɗin kawai ta rufe da sauri domin Italian recepy ne wanda ya spacies kala kala,ITA dai ba zata iya cin wannan kwabe kwabe ba gaskiya.

Zuwa 4pm ta nufi venue da za suyi leacture wanda wani wuri ne dabam haka dai leacture ɗin ya shigo wanda daga gani ɗan Nigeria ne amma ba musulmi bane ya gama surutu shi har 6pm tayi ya fita,mike wa tayi zata fita yaji sallama daga bayan ta wanda ko tantama babu muryan yar Nigeria ce kuma cikakkiyar bahausa.

Amsa wa tayi tare da juyawa suka haɗa ido da wata budurwa wace ba zata wuce sa'ar ta ba,hannu ta miƙa mata"Rahmatu B Ibrahim daga Nigeria a jahar kano.

Baby ta faɗada murmushin ta"Aisha Mustapha nima yar kano ce,rahma tace Wow gaskiya naji dad'i haɗuwa dake sabida tun last week ka kawo Ni sai garari nake a ƙasar mutane babu wacce na sani gashi su nan babu mai shiga sabgar wani.

Baby tace ayah Ni yau aka kawo ni yanzu ma hostel zan koma..,ok hostel kike?
Ni gida mahaifina ya kama mun a cikin gari kuma ina da driver,house maiden da gate man tare muke.

Yanke hiran nasu tayi Ni zan isa hostel,ah da wuri haka ko baki amince dani bane aysha kinga ina sonki kuwa wallahi tun da safe da kika shigo naji hankalina ya kwanta dake gaki yar garin mu.

Ko ɗaya kawai ina sauri ne,ok ban contact naki mana..,karanto mata baby tayi ta wuce abunta hostel domin bata son shiga tsabgar kowa tafi so tayi abinda ya kawo ta domin faranta ma yan uwanta.

Wani hadaden mota aka zo ɗaukar rahma dashi zuwa gidanta dake  opposite pharmacy alafia.


Da yamma tare da janah suka nufi  babban masjid dake cikin schl ɗin inda akwai ɓangaren mata dabam idan an isar da Sallah isha'i ana koyar da karatu ranar friday kuma tafsir ake yi.

Baby najin daɗin zama da janah domin mace ce wacce ta san abunda take yi ga kula da addini da faɗin gaskiya ta haka ne baby ta gano matar aure ce da yaron ta ɗaya mijinta ɗan France ne amma a Libya yake aiki,duk Bayan sati biyu yana zuwa ganinta wani lokaci yazo da yaron nasu.
Yanzu bata da wani damuwa da ya wuce rahma wace take matsa mata da kira a waya idan suna class ta isheta da kallo,babu yadda rahma bata yi ba baby ta bita gidan ta amma taƙi bada fuska domin bata yadda da dabi'u ta ba yadda take yawan kula samari ya kawaye birjik tana dasu.

Tana waya da mutanen gida akai akai kuma ana yi mata aiki idan an samu masu zuwa sayayya shi yasa bata wani damu ba sai kewar naziya da little sadiq KE adaban ta,haka dai har suka Kamala jarabawar zango na farko aka bada break na 1wk kawai lokacin baby tayi magana da General tana son zuwa gida ya bata hakuri tunda week ƊAYA ne ta zauna tayi karatu kowa na lafiya.

Haushi taji domin watani uku kenan tayi fa a garin mutane, babban  baƙin cikinta sister janah zata gida tayi hutu saboda da sun dawo zasu fara shirye shiryen final exams shike nan ta tafi ta bata.

Wata rana Baby na kwance a d'aki da dare taji norking koda ta duba Rahma ce ta sha kwaliyya sosai, fuska ba yabo ba falasa baby ta buɗe mata,wuri ta samu daf da baby ta zauna tana ƙare mata kallo tare da shanye idanun ta kamar wani jarababbe.

Dama sleeping dress KE jikinta wanda dashi da babu duk ɗaya,tace ya dai rahma.?
Dai dai na kawo maku ziyara ne kawai.

Tace nako gode dama Ni ɗaya ce sister janah ta tafi gida, Allah Sarki"rahma tace.

Ta jima tsaye tana kallon cute lips d'in baby domin suna ɗaya daga cikin abunda ke jan hankalin ta a jikin baby sai kuma boobs nata Wanda tayi alƙawarin sai ta sha su kota halin yaya.

Baki zauna ba!,ajiyar zuciya tayi ta zauna saman kujera Baby ta buɗe ɗan fidge nasu ta ɗauko mata farm pride youghurt da cup ta d'aura mata saman table nasu na karatu, lokacin ta dawo hayacin ta.

Sama sama suke ɗan taɓa hira wanda kusan jiran duka rahma ke yi wani abun sai dai ta amsa da eh, a'a har dare ya fara yi sosai gashi bacci take ji ita kuma taki tafiya.

Ƙarfin hali tayi,rahma ko nan zaki kwana ne?

Wani sanyin daɗi ya ziyarci zuciyarta domin daman da ta dad'e tana nema kenan su kebe da Baby Bata samu ba saboda tayi juyin duniya ta kawo mata ziyara gidan ta fafur taki.

Yace eh wallahi ba zan iya komawa gida yanzu ba saboda bada driver na fito ba kuma ban iya driving da dare,ok ki hau gado na zan kwanta a na sis janah..
Saboda me"ta katse ta,ai naga bed d'in da girma zai ɗauke mu ba sai kin kwanta a nata ba saboda ba kowa keson sharing bed da wani jinsi ba.

Sai kawai tayi reasoning da maganar rahma ta dauko mata rigar bacci ta vata, toilet ta shiga ta watsa ruwa ta saka,itama ta shiga ta dauro alwala ta kwanta abunta.

Rahma sai waya take yi da samari kala kala har bacci ya ɗauke baby,can cikin bacci take jin alamar kamar ana taɓa jikinta kamar mafarki haka dai koda ta buɗe ido bakinta haɗe da a rahama tana bata wani hot kisses,hanayen ta duka biyu tana wasa da boobs nata da alama ta fita hayacin ta.

Wani irin yunkuri tayi tare da hankade ta sai data fado daga saman bed d'in tana sauke nishi da sauri da sauri idanunta sun canza launi.

Cikin ɗaga murya tace wani irin iskanci kenan rahma.?
Dama abinda ya kawo ki kenan kuyi fasikanci da Ni?
Allah ya isa tsakani na dake Wallahi ban yafe hakki na ba,ki bar d'akin nan yanzu!

Riƙe kafafun baby tayi,don Allah ki taimaka mun zai iya shiga mawuyan cin hali akan ki wallahi tun ranar dana fara ganin ki Allah ya ɗauka mun son ki da sha'awar ki bana da kwanciyar hankali idan ba muryan ki naji ba,naso ki bani dama ko sau ɗaya ne amma kin ki fahimtar inda na dosa.

Daga bisani uncle's da auntie's nashi suna ta shigowa ganin baby ana gaisawa har suka gama shigowa sanan suka nufi part d'in grampa nashi wanda yana riƙe da wani traditional tittle bayan sun gaisa.

Yace sadat ya masu bayanin komi game da baby Wanda wanan ba matsala bane ta ɓangaren su amma basu san gefen Dad nashi ba tunda Bahaushe ne ko zai amince,sun yi assuring nata komi zai zo da sauki idan har tana son sadat Kuma ta amince dashi zasu tsaya masu har ayi komi lafiya.

Godiya tayi masu da zasu tafi aka haɗa mata sha tara na arziki ya ajeta makaranta.

Bata yi baccin kirki ba tana tunanin maganganun grandparents nashi domin ta ga alamar akwai Matsala maybe sun fara magana da Dad d'in ne oho.

Tashi tayi ta gabatar da sallatul istikhara sanan ta samu bacci mai cike da muna nan mafarki masu ban tsoro!

Lokacin haihuwa nayi ta sake samun baby boy ai kuwa mijinta ya rada mata suna uwar maza.

Shekaru na tafiya abubuwa da dama sun faru na ci gaba,farin ciki da tashin hankali wanda sai godiyar Allah.

Cikin ikon Allah sadat ya fito takarar gwamna kebbi wanda baby bata so haka ba amma mutane suka tsayar dashi,cikin ikon Allah ya samu kuwa ana rantsar da shi ya tataro matar shi suka bar Abuja aka dawo government house birnin kebbi da yaran su maza uku matar shi ya zaba a matsayin commissioner of chieftency affairs wanda Hakan ya kawo cece kuce cikin mutane ko a jikin shi da tafiya tayi nisa aka ga tana  da kwazo dole ya canza akayi shiru.

Ba zan fadi ainin sunan governor d'in ba da shekarar da yayi mulkin haka itama ba zan fadi shakarar da ta riƙe muƙamin ba saboda boye siriirn su.

*TAMMAT BI HAMDALLAH,A NAN NA KAWO KARSHEN WANNA LITTAFI MAI CIKE DA DUMBIN DARUSA NA RAYUWA DA FATAN ZAMU AMFANA DA ABUNDA KE CIKI KUMA MU GYARA TARE DA YADDA DA ƘADDARA A DUK TA YANDA TAZO MANA,INA YIWA DUKANIN MUSULMI BARKA DA ZUWAN RAMADAN ALLAH YASA MU SHIGE TA A SA'A MUYI IBADA KARBABBIYA AMIN SAI MUN HAƊU BAYAN SALLAH DA IZININ ALLAH*

_Halimatus Sadiya Muhammad_
